((tolkāppiyam, eḻuttatikāram, nacciṉārkkiṉiyam))

((ciṟappuppāyiram))

{{S PREF}}

TE0n


vaṭa vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ kumari
y-āy iṭi+
tamiḻ kūṟu nal= ulakattu
vaḻakku* um^ ceyyuḷ um āy iru mutaliṉ
eḻuttu* um^ col= um poruḷ um* nāṭi+
cem^ tamiḻ iyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu
muntu-nūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa v-eṇṇi+
pulam^ tokuttōṉ ē pōkku* aṟu paṉuval*
^nilam^taru-tiruviṉ+ pāṇṭiyaṉ aviyattu
aṟaṅ kari nāvil* ^nāṉ maṟi muṟṟiya
v-ataṅkōṭṭu* ācāṟku* aril* ^tapa+ terintu
mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu muṟi kāṭṭi
malku nīr varippiṉ aintiram* niṟinta
tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyar tōṟṟi+
pal pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimiyōṉ ē.

{{S NAC}} eṉpatu pāyiram.

e+ nūl urippiṉum a+ nūṟku+ pāyiram urittu urikka eṉpatu ilakkaṇam. eṉṉi?

{{S CIT}}
`āyiram* mukattāṉ akaṉṟatu* āyiṉum
pāyiram illatu paṉuval aṉṟu* ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ.

pāyiram eṉṟatu puṟam*v-uriyi. nūl kēṭkiṉṟāṉ puṟam*v-uri kēṭkiṉ+ $koḻu+ ceṉṟavaḻi+ tuṉ=ūci iṉitu cellum-āṟu pōla a+ nūl iṉitu viḷaṅkutaliṉ+ puṟam*v-uri kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. eṉṉi?

{{S CIT}}
`paru+ poruṭṭu* ākiya pāyiram^ kēṭṭārkku
nuṇ poruṭṭu ākiya nūl iṉitu viḷaṅkum.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṟār ākaliṉ.

a+ pāyiram^ tāṉ tali y-aminta yāṉikku viṉi y-aminta pākaṉ pōla v-um aḷappu* ariya ākāyattiṟku viḷakkam ākiya tiṅkaḷ um ñāyiṟu* um pōla v-um nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyā+ ciṟappiṟṟu* āy iruttaliṉ, atu kēḷākkāl+ kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kurīi+ pōla v-uṅ kuṟicci pukka māṉ pōla v-um māṇākkaṉ iṭar+paṭum eṉka.

a+ pāyiram potu v-um^ ciṟappu* um eṉa iru-vakittu.

avaṟṟuḷ+ potu+pāyiram ellā nūl%mukattu* um urikka+paṭum. atu tāṉ nāṉku vakittu.

{{S CIT}}
`īvōṉ ^taṉmi y-ītal iyaṟki
koḷvōṉ ^taṉmi kōṭaṉ marapu* eṉa
v-īr-iraṇṭu eṉpa potuviṉ ^toki y-ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉṉum itaṉāṉ aṟika.

īvōr kaṟkappaṭuvōr um^ kaṟkappaṭātōr um eṉa iru-vakiyar. avaruḷ+ kaṟkappaṭuvōr nāṉku tiṟattār.

{{S CIT}}
`mali nilam pū-v-ē tulā+kōl eṉṟiṉṉar
$ulivil uṇarvuṭiyōr.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉuḷ,

{{S CIT}}
`mali y-ē,
aḷakkal ākā+ perumi y-um arumi y-um
$aruṅkakalam uṭimi y-um ēṟaṟku* arumi y-um
porunta+ kūṟupa poccāppu* iṉṟi.'

`nilattiṉ iyalpu* ē niṉikkum^ kāli+
poṟi y-uṭimiyoṭu $ceypāṅku* amintapiṉ
viḷital vaṇmi y-um pōy+ cārntōri
y-iṭutal um eṭuttal um $iṉṉaṇam āka
v-iyiya+ kūṟupa v-iyalpu* uṇarntōr ē.'

`pūviṉatu* iyalpu ē porunta+ kūṟiṉ
maṅkalam ātal um* nāṟṟam uṭimi y-um^
kālattiṉ malartal um vaṇṭiṟku ñekiḻtal um
kaṇṭōr uvattal um viḻiya+ paṭutal um
uvamattu* iyalpiṉ uṇara+ kāṭṭupa.'

`tulā+kōl iyalpē tūkkum^ kāli
mikiṉum^ kuṟiyiṉum* nillātu* ākal um
aiyam tīrttal um* naṭuvunilimiyōṭu*
eyta+ kūṟupa v-iyalpu* uṇarntōr ē.'

{{S NAC}} eṉa nāṉku* um^ kaṇṭukoḷka.

iṉi+ kaṟkappaṭātōr um nāṉku tiṟattār.

{{S CIT}}
`kaḻal+ pey kuṭam ē maṭal+paṉi muṭa+teṅku
kuṇṭiki+ paruttiyōṭu* ivi y-eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉuḷ+ kaḻal+pey-kuṭam āvatu koḷvōṉ uṇarvu ciṟitu* āyiṉum^ tāṉ kaṟṟatu* ellām oruṅku* urittal. maṭal+paṉi y-eṉpatu piṟarāṟ kiṭṭutaṟku ariyatu* āki iṉitu* ākiya payaṉkaḷi+ koṇṭiruttal. muṭa+teṅku* eṉpatu oruvar nīr vārkka+ piṟarkku+ payaṉ-paṭuvatu pōla oruvar vaḻi-paṭa+ piṟarkku urittal. kuṇṭiki+ parutti y-eṉpatu coriyiṉum vīḻātu ciṟitu-ciṟitāka vāṅka+ $koṭukkumatu pōla+ koḷvōṉ uṇarvu peritu* āyiṉum^ ciṟitu-ciṟitāka+ kūṟutal.

iṉi,

{{S CIT}}
`ītal iyalpu* ē y-iyalp-uṟa+ kiḷappiṉ+
poḻippu* ē y-akalam* nuṭpam eccam eṉa+
paḻippu* il pal=uri payiṉṟa nāviṉaṉ
pukaḻnta matiyiṉ+ poruntum $ōriyiṉ+
tikaḻnta v-aṟiviṉaṉ teyvam vāḻtti+
koḷvōṉ uṇar-vaki y-aṟintavaṉ koḷvara+
koṭuttal% marapu* eṉa+ kūṟiṉar pulavar.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

iṉi+ koḷvōr um^ kaṟpikkappaṭuvōr um^ kaṟpikkappaṭātōr um eṉa iru-vakiyar. avaruḷ+ kaṟpikkappaṭuvōr aṟu-vakiyar.

avar tām,

{{S CIT}}
`taṉ makaṉ ācāṉ makaṉ ē $maṉmakaṉ
poruḷ naṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻi-paṭuvōṉ ē
y-$urikōḷāḷaṉōṭu* ivar eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} ivar taṉmi,

{{S CIT}}
`aṉṉam^ kiḷi y-ē $naṉṉiṟa $neyyari
yāṉi $yāṉē $ṟeṉṟivi pōla+
kūṟi+ koḷpa kuṇam* māṇṭōr ē.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

iṉi+ kaṟpikkappaṭātōr eṇvakiyar.

{{S CIT}}
`maṭi māṉi poccāppaṉ kāmukaṉ kaḷvaṉ
aṭunōy+ piṇiyāḷaṉ āṟā+ ciṉattaṉ
taṭumāṟu neñcattavaṉ uḷḷiṭṭu* eṇmar
neṭunūli+ kaṟkalātār.'

{{S NAC}} eṉa ivar.

ivar taṉmi,

{{S CIT}}
`$kuraṅkeṟi $viḷaṅkā $yerumi $yāṭē
$tōṇi y-eṉṟāṅku* ivi y-eṉa moḻipa.'

{{S NAC}} itaṉāṉ aṟika.

ivaruḷ+ kaḷam^ kaṭiyappaṭṭār:

{{S CIT}}
`moḻivatu* uṇarātār muṉṉiruntu kāyvār
paṭiṟu pala v-urippār pal-kāl nakuvār
tiritaru neñcattār tīyavi y-ōrppār
kaṭiyappaṭṭār aviyiṉ kaṇ.'

{{S NAC}} iṉi+ kōṭaṉ-marapu:

{{S CIT}}
`kōṭaṉ marapu kūṟum^ kāli+
poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭaṉ muṉiyāṉ
muṉ=um piṉ=um iraviṉ-um pakaliṉ-um
akalāṉ āki y-aṉpoṭu keḻīi+
kuṇattoṭu paḻaki+ kuṟippiṉ vaḻi-niṉṟu*
ācaṟa v-uṇarntōṉ vā v-eṉa vantāṅku*
iru v-eṉa v-iruntu* ēṭu* aviḻ eṉa v-aviḻttu+
col=eṉa+ colli+ cel=eṉa+ ceṉṟu
parukuvaṉ aṉṉa v-ārvattaṉ āki+
cittira+ pāviyiṉ $attaka v-aṭaṅki+
cevi vāy āka neñcu kaḷaṉ āka+
kēṭṭavi kēṭṭavi vallaṉ āki+
pōṟṟi+ kōṭal ataṉatu paṇpu* ē.'

`ettiṟam ācāṉ uvakkum attiṟam
aṟattiṉ+ tiriyā+ $paṭarcci vaḻipāṭu* ē'

`cevvaṉ terikiṟpāṉ mey+nōkki+ kāṇkiṟpāṉ
pal=uri y-um^ kēṭpāṉ mika+peritu* um^ kātalāṉ
teyvatti+ pōla matippāṉ tiripu* illāṉ
iv=āṟu māṇpu* um uṭiyāṟku* urippa v-ē
cevvitiṉ* nūli+ terintu.'

`vaḻakkiṉ ilakkaṇam iḻukku* iṉṟu* aṟital
pāṭam pōṟṟal kēṭṭavi niṉittal
ācāṉ+ cārntavi y-amivara+ kēṭṭal
a+māṇpu* uṭiyōr tammoṭu payiṟal
viṉātal viṉāyavi viṭuttal eṉṟiṉṉavi
kaṭaṉ ā+ koḷiṉ ē maṭa naṉi y-ikakkum.'

`aṉiyaṉ allōṉ kēṭkuvaṉ āyiṉ
viṉiyiṉ uḻappoṭu payaṉ tali+paṭāaṉ.'

`aṉiyaṉ allōṉ a+marapu* illōṉ
kēṭkuvaṉ āyiṉ+ koḷvōṉ* nallaṉ.'

{{S NAC}} ivaṟṟāṉ uṇarka.

i+māṇākkaṉ muṟṟa uṇarntāṉ ām āṟu,

{{S CIT}}
`oru kuṟi kēṭpōṉ iru-kāl+ kēṭpiṉ+
peruka nūliṟ piḻipāṭu* ilaṉ ē'

`mu+kāṟ kēṭpiṉ muṟi y-aṟintu* urikkum.'

`ācāṉ urittatu* amivara+ koḷiṉum^
$kāṟkūṟu* allatu paṟṟalaṉ ākum.'

`av viṉiyāḷaroṭu payil vaki y-orupāl+
cevvitiṉ urippa v-av=iru pāl-um
aiyaṟu pulami māṇpu naṉi y-uṭittu* ē.'

`piṟarkku* uri y-iṭattē nūl+kalappu* ākum^
tiṟa+paṭa v-uṇarum^ teḷiviṉōrkku* ē.'

{{S NAC}} ivaṟṟāṉ aṟika.

potu+pāyiram muṟṟiṟṟu.

iṉi+ ciṟappuppāyiram āvatu taṉṉāl urikkappaṭum nūṟku iṉṟiyamiyātatu. atu patiṉoru-vaki y-ām.

`ākkiyōṉ peyar-ē vaḻi y-ē y-elli
nūṟ-peyar yāppu* ē nutaliya poruḷ ē
kēṭpōr payaṉōṭu* āy eṇ poruḷ um
vāyppa+ kāṭṭal pāyirattu iyalpu* ē.'

`kālam^ kaḷaṉ ē kāraṇam eṉṟu* i+
mū-vaki y-ēṟṟi moḻinar um uḷar-ē.'

i+patiṉoṉṟu* um i+pāyirattu* uḷ=ē peṟappaṭṭaṉa.

iṉi+ ciṟappu+pāyirattu* ilakkaṇam^ ceppum-āṟu,

`pāyirattu* ilakkaṇam pakarum^ kāli
nūl* nutal poruḷi+ taṉ=akattu* aṭakki
y-āciriyamāṉ um veṇpāvāṉ um*
$maruviya vakiyāṉ* nuvaṟal vēṇṭum.'

itaṉāṉ aṟika.

nūlceytāṉ pāyiram^ ceytāṉ āyiṉ+ taṉṉi+ pukaḻntāṉ ām.

`tōṉṟā tōṟṟi+ tuṟi pala muṭippiṉum^
tāṉ taṟ pukaḻtal* takuti y-aṉṟu* ē.'

eṉpa v-ākaliṉ.

pāyirañ-ceyvār taṉ āciriyar-um^ taṉṉoṭu oruṅku-kaṟṟa oru-cāli māṇākkar-um^ taṉ māṇākkar-um-eṉa ivar. avar-uḷ i+nūṟku+ pāyiram^ ceytār tamakku oru-cāli māṇākkar-ākiya paṉampāraṉār.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṭa-vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ-kumari āy-iṭi --- vaṭakkiṉkaṇ vēṅkaṭam-um^ teṟkiṉkaṇ+ kumari y-um ākiya av= iraṇṭu* ellikku* uḷ=iruntu, tamiḻ kūṟum nal ulakattu vaḻakku* um^ ceyyuḷ um āy iru mutaliṉ --- tamiḻi+ collum nal=āciriyaratu vaḻakku*um^ ceyyuḷ-um-ākiya av=iraṇṭi y-um aṭi y-āka+ koḷḷukiyiṉāl-ē, cen-tamiḻ iyaṟki civaṇiya nilattoṭu muntu-nūl kaṇṭu - avar kūṟum^ centamiḻ iyalpu* āka+ poruntiya centamiḻ-nāṭṭiṟku iyinta vaḻakkōṭu* ē muṉṉi y-ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ iyinta paṭiyi muṟṟa+kaṇṭu, muṟi+paṭa eṇṇi --- av= ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ ellām^ cil vāḻ-nāḷ+ pal-piṇi+ ciṟṟu* aṟiviṉōrkku aṟiyal-ākāmiyiṉ yāṉ ittuṇi vari y-aṟuttu uṇarttuval eṉṟu a+ nūlkaḷiṉ+ kiṭanta v-āṟu* aṉṟi atikāram* muṟiyāṉ muṟimi+paṭa+ ceytali eṇṇi, eḻuttu* um^ col=um poruḷ-um nāṭi --- avvilakkaṇaṅkaḷ uḷ eḻuttiṉi y-um^ colliṉi y-um poruḷiṉi y-um ārāyntu, pōkku aṟu paṉuval --- pattu-vaki+ kuṟṟam-um^ tīrntu muppattiraṇṭu vaki uttiyoṭu puṇarnta i+nūl uḷ=ē, mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttu-muṟi kāṭṭi+ pulan-tokuttōṉ-ē --- a+mū-vaki y-ilakkaṇam-um mayaṅkā muṟimiyāṉ+ ceykiṉṟamiyiṉ eḻuttu* ilakkaṇatti muṉṉar+ kāṭṭi+ piṉṉar ēṉi y-ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi y-um^ tokuttu+ kūṟiṉāṉ, nilan-taru tiruviṉ+ pāṇṭiyaṉ aviyattu --- māṟṟāratu nilatti+ koḷḷum pōr+ tiruviṉi y-uṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉ mā-kīrtti aviyiṉkaṇ=ē, aṟaṅ-kari-nāviṉ nāṉ-maṟi muṟṟiya ataṅkōṭṭu* ācāṟku aril tapa+terintu --- aṟam-ē kūṟum nāviṉi y-uṭiya nāṉku vētattiṉi y-um muṟṟa aṟinta ataṅkōṭu* eṉkiṟa ūriṉ āciriyaṉukku+ kuṟṟam-aṟa ārāyntu-kūṟi, malku-nīr-varippiṉ aintiram niṟinta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyar tōṟṟi --- kaṭal-cūḻnta ulakiṉ-kaṇ=ē aintira-viyākaraṇatti niṟiya aṟinta paḻiya kāppiya+ kuṭiyiṉ-uḷḷōṉ eṉa+ taṉ peyari māyāmal niṟutti, pal-pukaḻ niṟutta paṭimiyōṉ ē --- pala pukaḻkaḷi y-um iv=ulakiṉ-kaṇ=ē māyāmal niṟuttiya tava vēṭatti y-uṭiyōṉ; eṉṟavāṟu.

iruntu tamiḻi+ collum eṉa+ koḷḷukiyiṉāl ē poruntiya nāṭu eṉa+ kaṇṭu eṇṇi ārāyntu i+ nūl-uḷ=ē tokuttāṉ, avaṉ yār eṉiṉ aviyiṉ-kaṇ=ē kūṟi ulakiṉ kaṇ=ē taṉ peyari niṟutti+ pukaḻi niṟuttiya paṭimiyōṉ eṉka.

i+pāyiram-um^ ceyyuḷ-ātaliṉ iṅṅaṉam $māṭṭuṟuppu nikaḻa+ kūṟiṉār. itaṟku iṅṅaṉam^ kaṇ=aḻittal uriyāciriyar karuttu* eṉpatu avar ururiyāṉ uṇarka.

iṉi maṅkala-marapiṉ+ kāriyañ-ceyvār vaṭakku* um^ kiḻakku* um nōkki y-um^ cintittu* um naṟ-karumaṅkaḷ ceyvār ātaliṉ maṅkalam-ākiya vaṭa-ticiyi muṟkūṟiṉār, i+nūl niṉṟu nilavutal vēṇṭi. teṉpulattārkku vēṇṭuvaṉa ceyvār teṟku* um mēṟku* um nōkki y-um^ karumaṅkaḷ ceyvār ātaliṉ teṉ ticiyi+ piṟkūṟiṉār. nilaṅ-kaṭanta neṭu-muṭi y-aṇṇali nōkki ulakam^ tavañ-ceytu vīṭu-peṟṟa mali y-ātalāṉ-um ellārāṉ-um aṟiya+paṭutalāṉ-um vēṅkaṭatti elli y-āka+ kūṟiṉār. kumari y-um^ tīrttam ākaliṉ elli y-āka+ kūṟiṉār. iv= iraṇṭiṉi y-um^ kāli y-ē ōtuvārkku nal-viṉi y-uṇṭām eṉṟu karuti ivaṟṟi y-ē kūṟiṉār. ivi y-iraṇṭu* um $akappāṭṭu* elli y-āyiṉa. eṉṉi? kumari y-āṟṟiṉ teṟku nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭu kaṭalkoṇṭatu* ākaliṉ. kiḻakku* um mēṟku* um^ kaṭal elli y-āka muṭitaliṉ vēṟu* elli kūṟār-āyiṉār. vēṅkaṭam-um^ kumari y-um yāṇṭiya eṉṟāl vaṭa-vēṅkaṭam^ teṉ-kumari y-eṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ ataṉi viḷaṅka+ kūṟiṉār.

ulakam-eṉṟatu pala-poruḷ-oru-col=ākaliṉ īṇṭu uyarntōri y-uṇarttiṟṟu, ulakam avari y-ē kaṇ=āka v-uṭimiyiṉ. eṉṉi?

`vaḻakku* eṉa+ paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟu* ē
nikaḻcci avar-kaṭṭu* ākalāṉa.' ((marapiyal//92))

eṉa marapu* iyal-uḷ+ kūṟutaliṉ. av=uyarntōr-āvār akattiyaṉār-um mārkkaṇṭēyar-um^ tali+caṅkattārum mutalāyiṉōr. ulakattu ulakattiṉ-uṭiya eṉa virikka.

vaḻakku* āvatu cila-coṟ piṟanta a+ kālattu iḵtu aṟatti uṇarttiṟṟu, itu poruḷi uṇarttiṟṟu, iḵtu iṉpatti uṇarttiṟṟu, itu vīṭṭi uṇarttiṟṟu eṉṟu uṇarvippatu.

ceyyuḷ āvatu `pāṭṭurinūlē' ((ceyyu//79)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattāṟ kūṟiya ēḻu nilam um aṟam* mutaliya mūṉṟu poruḷ um payappa nikaḻvatu. mutaliṉ eṉṟatu $mutalukiyiṉālē eṉṟavāṟu.

eḻuttu* eṉṟatu yātaṉi y-eṉiṉ, kaṭpulaṉ ākā uru v-um^ kaṭpulaṉ ākiya vaṭivu* um uṭittu* āka vēṟu-vēṟu vakuttu+koṇṭu taṉṉi y-ē uṇartti y-um^ coṟku iyintu* um niṟkum ōciyi y-ām. kaṭal-oli caṅk-oli mutaliya ōcikaḷ poruḷ uṇarttāmiyāṉ um $muṟku $vīḷi $ilati mutaliyaṉa poruḷuṇarttiṉa v-ēṉum eḻuttu* ākāmiyāṉ um avi īṇṭu+ koḷḷār āyiṉar. īṇṭu uru v-eṉṟatu maṉaṉ uṇarvu* āy niṟkum^ karuttu+ poruḷi. atu ceṟippa+ cēṟalāṉ um^ ceṟippa varutalāṉ um iṭiyeṟiyappaṭutalāṉ um iṉpam* tuṉpatti y-ākkalāṉ um uru v-um uru v-um^ kūṭi+ piṟattalāṉ um unti mutal āka+ tōṉṟi eṇ vaki nilattu* um piṟantu kaṭpulaṉ ām^ taṉmi y-iṉṟi+ cevikkaṇ+ ceṉṟu uṟum ūṟu* uṭimiyāṉ um vicumpiṟ piṟantu iyaṅkuvatu* ōr taṉmi y-uṭimiyāṉ um^ kāṟṟiṉ kuṇam āvatu* ōr uru v-ām. vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi kōṭalāṉ um uru v-ē y-āyiṟṟu. itaṉi+ kāṟṟiṉ kuṇam ē y-eṉṟal iv= āciriyar karuttu. itaṉi vicumpiṉ kuṇam eṉpār um uḷar. iv= uru `uru v-uru v-āki' ((eḻu//17)) eṉa v-um `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uru v-ākummē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉa v-um^ kāṭci+ poruṭku* um^ ciṟupāṉmi varum. vaṭivu* āvatu kaṇ+pulaṉ āki y-ē niṟkum. atu vaṭṭam^ caturam mutaliya muppattiraṇṭaṉ uḷ oṉṟi uṇarttum. maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇ= uṇarvu* illōr um uṇartaṟku eḻuttukkaṭku vēṟu vēṟu vaṭivam^ kāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṟ kaṇ+pulaṉ ākiya vari-vaṭivu* um uṭiya v-āyiṉa. itaṟku viti `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uru v-ākum= ē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉṉum^ cūttiram mutaliyaṉa v-ām. ivaṟṟāṟ perumpāṉmi meykku* ē vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. `ekara okarattu iyaṟki y-um aṟṟu* ē' ((eḻu//16)) eṉa uyirkku* um^ ciṟupāṉmi vaṭivu kūṟiṉār.

iṉi+ taṉṉi uṇarttum ōci y-āvatu taṉ piṟappi y-um māttiriyi y-um ē aṟivittu+ taṉṉi+ peṟa nikaḻum ōci.

coṟku iyiyum ōci y-āvatu {ōr eḻuttu* oru moḻi} mutaliya v-āy varum ōci.

avar kēḷaṉmiṉ eṉṟaṟku+ kāraṇam eṉṉi y-eṉiṉ, tēvar ellārum^ kūṭi yām^ cēra v-iruttaliṉ mēru+ tāḻntu teṉ ^tici uyarntatu, itaṟku akattiyaṉār ē āṇṭiruttaṟ kuriyar eṉṟu avari vēṇṭi+koḷḷa, avar um^ teṉ ^ticikkaṇ+ pōtukiṉṟavar kaṅki-yāru* uḻi+ ceṉṟu kāviriyāri vāṅki+koṇṭu, piṉṉar yamatakkiṉiyār uḻi+ ceṉṟu avar makaṉār tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyāri vāṅki+koṇṭu, pulattiyaṉār uḻi+ceṉṟu avar uṭaṉ piṟanta kumariyār ulōpā muttiriyāri avar koṭuppa nīr ēṟṟu irīi+ peyarntu, tuvarāpati+ pōntu nilam^ kaṭanta neṭu muṭi y-aṇṇal vaḻi+kaṇ aracar patiṉeṇmari y-um patiṉeṇ-kōṭi vēḷir uḷ= iṭṭāri y-um aruvāḷari y-um^ koṇṭu-pōntu, kāṭu keṭuttu nāṭu* ākki+ potiyiṉ-kaṇ iruntu, irāvaṇaṉi+ kantaruvattāṟ piṇittu, irākkatari āṇṭu iyaṅkāmi vilakki+ tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyār ākiya tolkāppiyaṉāri nōkki, `nī ceṉṟu kumariyāri+ koṇṭu varuka' v-eṉa+ kūṟa avar um em perumāṭṭiyi eṅṅaṉam^ koṇṭu varuval eṉṟārkku `muṉṉāka+ piṉṉāka nāṟ kōl nīḷam akala niṉṟu koṇṭu varuka v-eṉa avar-um aṅṅaṉam^ koṇṭu varu-vaḻi, viyi nīr kaṭuki+ kumariyāri īrttu+ koṇṭu pōka+, tolkāppiyaṉār kaṭṭaḷi y-iṟantu ceṉṟu ōr $vetirṅ-kōli muṟittu nīṭṭa, atu paṟṟi y-ēṟiṉār; atu kuṟṟam eṉṟu akattiyaṉār kumariyāri y-um^ tolkāppiyaṉāri y-um^ `cuvarkkam pukāppir' eṉa+ capittār; `yāṅkaḷ oru kuṟṟam um^ ceyyātu* irukka eṅkaḷi+ capittamiyāṉ emperumāṉ um^ cuvarkkam pukāppir' eṉa avar akattiyaṉāri+ capittār. ataṉāṉ avar vekuṇṭār ātaliṉ avaṉ ceyta nūli+ kēḷaṟka v-eṉṟār eṉka.

nāṉku kūṟu* um āy maṟinta poruḷ um uṭimiyāṉ `nāṉ maṟi' y-eṉṟār. avi tittiriyam um puṭikam um^ $talavakāram um^ cāma-vētam um ām. iṉi irukkum yacu v-um cāmam um atarvaṇam um eṉpār um uḷar. atu poruntātu; ivar i+ nūl ceyta-piṉṉar vēta-viyātar cil% ^nāḷ+ pal piṇi+ ciṟṟu* aṟiviṉōr uṇartaṟku nāṉku kūṟu āka ivaṟṟi+ ceytār ātaliṉ.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

iv= atikāram eṉṉa peyarttu* ō v-eṉiṉ eḻuttu* ilakkaṇam uṇarttiṉami kāraṇattāṉ eḻuttu* atikāram eṉṉum peyarttu. eḻutti uṇarttiya atikāram eṉa virikka. atikāram --- muṟimi.

eḻuttu uṇarttum iṭattu eṉittu vakiyāṉ uṇarttiṉār ō v-eṉiṉ eṭṭu vakiyāṉ um eṭṭu* iṟanta pala vakiyāṉ um uṇarttiṉār eṉka.

eṭṭu vakiya v-eṉpār kūṟum-āṟu: --- eḻuttu iṉittu* eṉṟal um iṉṉa peyariṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa muṟiyiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa aḷaviṉa v-eṉṟalum iṉṉa piṟappiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa puṇarcciyiṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa vaṭiviṉa v-eṉṟal-um iṉṉa taṉmiyiṉa v-eṉṟal um ām. ivaṟṟuḷ taṉmi y-um vaṭivu* um namakku uṇarttal ākāmiyiṉ āciriyar īṇṭu urittilar. ēṉiya itaṉuḷ+ peṟutum.

eḻuttu iṉittu* eṉṟali+ toki vaki viriyāṉ uṇarka. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu toki. uyir paṉṉiraṇṭu* um uṭampu patiṉeṭṭu* um^ cārpiṉ+ ^tōṟṟam mūṉṟu* um ataṉ vaki. aḷapeṭi y-ēḻu* um uyirmey y-irunūṟṟorupattāṟu* um avaṟṟōṭu* um^ kūṭṭi irunūṟṟimpattāṟu* eṉal viri.

iṉi eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟiy um^ toki y-um i+ cūttirattāṉ+ peṟṟām. vaki `aukāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//8)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//9)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avi tām^, kuṟṟiyalikaram^ kuṟṟiyalukaram' ((eḻu//2)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām. viri `kuṉṟici moḻi vayiṉ' ((eḻu//41)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām.

aḷavu `avaṟṟuḷ, a i u' ((eḻu//3)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `ā ī ū' ((eḻu//4)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē' ((eḻu//11)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avviyal* ^niliyum' ((eḻu//12)) eṉpataṉāṉ um peṟṟām.

piṟappu piṟappiyal uḷ+ peṟṟām.

puṇarcci `uyir iṟu col% muṉ' ((eḻu//107)) eṉpataṉāṉ um `avaṟṟu, ṇiṟutta colliṉ' ((eḻu//108)) eṉpataṉāṉ um piṟavāṟṟāṉ um peṟṟām.

iṉi eṭṭu* iṟanta pal vakiya v-eṉpār kūṟum āṟu: --- eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟivu um^ kūṭṭam um pirivu* um mayakkam um moḻi y-ākkam um nili y-um iṉam um oṉṟu pala v-ātal um^ tirintataṉ ^tiripu atu v-eṉṟal um piṟitu* eṉṟal um atu v-um piṟitu* um eṉṟal um niliyiṟṟu* eṉṟal um niliyātu* eṉṟal um niliyiṟṟu* um niliyātu* um eṉṟal um iṉṉōraṉṉa pala v-um ām.

kuṟivu `ari y-aḷapu kuṟukal' ((eḻu//13)) `ōr aḷapu* ākum' ((eḻu//57)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟṟām.

kūṭṭam `meyyōṭu* iyiyiṉum' ((eḻu//10)) `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟṟām.

pirivu `mey= uyir nīṅkiṉ' ((eḻu//139)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

moḻi y-ākkam `ōr eḻuttu* oru moḻi' ((eḻu//45)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām, av= eḻuttukkaḷi moḻi y-ākkaliṉ.

nili `paṉṉīr uyir um' ((eḻu//59)) `uyirmey= allaṉa' ((eḻu//90)) `uyir au' ((eḻu//69)) `ña ṇa na ma ṉa' ((eḻu//78)) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟāṉ moḻikku mutal ām eḻuttu* um īṟu* ām eḻuttu* um peṟṟām.

iṉam `val= eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//19)) `mel= eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//20)) `iṭi y-eḻuttu* eṉpa' ((eḻu//21)) `aukāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//8)) `ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy' ((eḻu//9)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ peṟṟām. iv= uruvu* ākiya ōcikku āciriyar vaṭivu kūṟāmi uṇarka. iṉi vari-vaṭivu kūṟuṅkāl meykku* ē perumpāṉmi y-um vaṭivu kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.

oṉṟu pala v-ātal `eḻuttōraṉṉa' ((eḻu//141)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

tirintataṉ ^tiripu* atu v-eṉṟal `takaram varuvaḻi' ((eḻu//369)) eṉpataṉāṉum piṟāṇṭum peṟṟām.

atu v-um piṟitu* um eṉṟal `āṟaṉ urupiṉ akara+kiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

niliyiṟṟu* eṉṟal `avaṟṟu* uḷ niṟutta colliṉ īṟu* āku' ((eḻu//108)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

niliyātu* eṉṟal nilimoḻiyatu īṟṟukkaṇ ^niṉṟum varumoḻiyatu mutaṟkaṇ ^niṉṟum puṇarcci tam= uḷ ilavātal. atu `maruviṉ ^tokuti' ((eḻu//111)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

niliyiṟṟu* um niliyātu* um eṉṟal `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar um' ((eḻu//226)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya akaram `irā v-eṉ kiḷavikku* akaram illi' ((eḻu//227)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟām.

i+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ karuvi y-um^ ceyki y-um eṉa iru-vakiya.

avaṟṟu* uḷ+ karuvi puṟa+puṟa+ karuvi y-um puṟa+karuvi y-um akappuṟa+ karuvi y-um aka+karuvi y-um eṉa nāl-vakittu. nūṉ-marapu* um piṟappu*iyal um puṟa+puṟa+ karuvi. moḻi-marapu puṟa+karuvi. puṇar iyal aka+puṟa+karuvi. `ekara okaram peyarkku* īṟu* ākā' ((eḻu//272)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa aka+karuvi.

iṉi+ ceyki y-um puṟa+puṟa+ceyki y-um puṟa+ceyki y-um aka+puṟa+ ceyki y-um aka+ceyki y-um eṉa nāl-vakittu. `ellā moḻikku* um uyir varu-vaḻi y-ē'(eḻu//140) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa puṟa+puṟa+ceyki. `la ṉa v-eṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar' ((eḻu//149)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa puṟa+ceyki. `ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi y-iṟuti' ((eḻu//163)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa aka+puṟa+ceyki. toki marapu mutaliya ōttiṉ uḷ iṉṉa īṟu iṉṉa v-āṟu muṭiyum eṉa+ ceyki kūṟuvaṉa v-ellām aka+ceyki. iv vikaṟpam ellām^ toki y-āka uṇarka.

iv= ōttu* eṉ ^nutaliṟṟu* ō v-eṉiṉ, atu v-um ataṉ peyar urippa v-ē aṭaṅkum.

iv= ōttu* eṉṉa peyarttu* ō v-eṉiṉ i+ tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūṟku marapu* ām^ tuṇikku vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi+ tokuttu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ nūṉ-marapu* eṉṉum peyarttu* āyiṟṟu.

nūl eṉṟatu nūl pōṟaliṉ oppiṉ āyatōr āku-peyar ām. av= oppu* āya v-āṟu* eṉṉi y-eṉiṉ, kuṟṟam^ kaḷintu eḵkiya $paṉṉuṉi+ pañcikaḷi y-ellām^ ki val% makaṭūu+ tūymi y-um nuṇmi y-um uṭiya v-āka ōr iḻi+ paṭuttiṉāl+pōla `viṉiyiṉ* ^nīṅki viḷaṅkiya v-aṟivaṉāl' ((marapiyal//94)) ē vaḻu+kaḷintu eḵkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi y-ellām mutal um muṭivu* um māṟukōḷ iṉṟu* āka v-um^, tokiyiṉ um vakiyiṉ um poruṇmi kāṭṭi y-um, uri y-um^ kāṇṭiki y-um uḷ niṉṟu akala v-um, īr aim^ kuṟṟam um iṉṟi īr aintu* aḻaku peṟa, muppattiraṇṭu tantira v-uttiyōṭu puṇara v-um,

`oru poruḷ* ^nutaliya cūttirattāṉ um
iṉa moḻi kiḷanta v-ōttiṉāṉ um
potu moḻi kiḷanta paṭalattāṉ um*
mūṉṟu* uṟuppu* aṭakkiya piṇṭattāṉ um' ((cey//168))

oru-neṟi+paṭa+ puṇarkka+paṭūum^ taṉmi y-uṭimiyāṉ eṉka. marapu, ilakkaṇam, muṟimi, taṉmi eṉpaṉa oru-poruḷ+kiḷavi.

āyiṉ nūl eṉṟatu īṇṭu mūṉṟu* atikārattiṉi y-um aṉṟu* ē? iv=ōttu mūṉṟu* atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam āya v-āṟu* eṉṉi y-eṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟi y-um iv= atikārattiṟku* um^ ceyyuḷ iyaṟku* um oppa+ kūṟiyatu. īṇṭu+ kūṟiya muppattumūṉṟaṉi+ patiṉintu* ākki āṇṭu+ toki kōṭalil toki vēṟu* ām. aḷavu ceyyuḷ iyaṟku* um iv= atikārattiṟku* um otta aḷavu* um ovvā aḷavu* um uḷavāka+ kūṟiyatu. kuṟiṟku* um neṭiṟku* um^ kūṟiya māttiri iraṇṭu* iṭattiṟku* um otta aḷavu. āṇṭu+ kūṟum^ ceyyuṭku aḷavu kōṭaṟku īṇṭikku+ payaṉ tārāta aḷapeṭi kūṟiyatu ovvā aḷavu. aḵtu `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattōṭu āṇṭu māṭṭeṟiyum-āṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉṉum^ kuṟil um neṭil um mū-vaki y-iṉam um āytam um vaṇṇattiṟku* um iv= atikārattiṟku* um oppa+ kūṟiyaṉa. kuṟivu* um iraṇṭaṟku* um okkum. kūṭṭam um pirivu* um mayakkam um iv= atikārattiṟku* ē uriyaṉa v-āka+ kūṟiyaṉa. `a+ mū v-āṟum' ((eḻu//22)) eṉṉum^ cūttira mutaliyaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭi+ col= ām āṟu kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ+ col= atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār āyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu* atikārattiṟku* um ilakkaṇam^ kūṟutaliṉ iv= ōttu nūliṉatu ilakkaṇam^ kūṟiyatu* āyiṟṟu. nūl eṉṟatu tolkāppiyam eṉṉum piṇṭatti. iv= ōttilakkaṇaṅkaḷ* ^tām eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟi y-um^ toki y-um aḷavu* um^ kuṟivu* um^ kūṭṭam um iṉam um mayakkam um ām. ēṉiya iv= atikārattu* uḷ ēṉi y-ōttukkaḷ uḷ uṇarttupa.

aṟṟu* ēl aḵtu* āka, i+ tali+cūttiram eṉ ^nutaliṟṟu* ō v-eṉiṉ eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar um muṟi y-um toki y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuttu* eṉa+paṭupa --- eḻuttu* eṉṟu ciṟappittu+ colla+paṭuvaṉa, akaram* mutal ṉakaram* v-iṟuvāy muppaḵtu* eṉpa --- akaram mutal ṉakaram īṟu* āka+ kiṭanta muppatu* eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar, cārntu varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭi y-ē --- cārntu varutali+ tamakku ilakkaṇam āka v-uṭiya mūṉṟu* um allāta iṭattu; eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, a+ mūṉṟu* um^ kūṭiya vaḻi muppattu mūṉṟu* eṉpa. a-ā-i-ī-u-ū-e-ē-ai-o-ō-au-k-ṅ-c-ñ-ṭ-ṇ-t-n-p-m-y-r-l-v-ḻ-ḷ-ṟ-ṉ eṉa varum. eṉa+paṭuva v-eṉṟu ciṟappittu* uṇarttutalāṉ aḷapeṭi y-um uyirmey= um i+tuṇi+ ciṟappu* ila; oci y-uṇarvārkku+ karuvi y-ākiya vari vaṭivu* um^ ciṟappu* ilā eḻuttu* āka+ koḷḷa+paṭum.

akaram mutal ātal āriyattiṟku* um okkum ēṉum īṇṭu+ tamiḻ eḻuttu* ē kūṟukiṉṟār eṉpatu uṇartaṟku ṉakara v-iṟuvāy eṉṟār.

paṭupa, paṭuva. paṭupa v-eṉpatu paṭuttal-ōciyāl toḻil+peyar āka+ kūṟa+paṭum. pakaram um vakaram um īṇṭu niṟṟaṟku+ tam= uḷ otta urimiya v-ēṉum eḻuttu* eṉa+paṭupa v-eṉa+ $tūkkaṟṟu niṟkum^ col+cīr aṭikku+ paṭupa v-eṉpatu iṉ=ōcittu* āy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu+ paṭupa v-eṉṟu* ē pāṭam ōtuka. iḵtu aṉ peṟāta akara v-īṟṟu+ pala v-aṟi col.

akara ṉakaram eṉa v-ē peyar um^ kūṟiṉār.

eḻuttukkaṭku* ellām akaram mutal ātaṟku+ kāraṇam `meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//46)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟupa. vīṭu pēṟṟiṟku uriya āṇmakaṉi uṇarttum^ ciṟappāṉ ṉakaram piṉ vittār. iṉi eḻuttukkaṭku* um^ kiṭakki muṟi y-āyiṉa v-āṟu kūṟutum.

kuṟṟu* eḻuttukkaḷi muṉ= āka+ kūṟi avaṟṟiṟku iṉam otta neṭṭu* eḻuttukkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ piṉ= āka+ kūṟiṉār. oru māttiri kūṟi y-ē iraṇṭu māttiri kūṟa vēṇṭutaliṉ. aṉṟi iraṇṭi muṉ+ kūṟiṉ āl ō v-eṉiṉ, ākātu; oṉṟu niṉṟu ataṉōṭu piṉṉarum oṉṟu kūṭi y-ē iraṇṭu* āvatu* aṉṟi iraṇṭu* eṉpatu* oṉṟu iṉṟu* ātaliṉ. itaṉāṉ oṉṟu tāṉ pala kūṭi y-ē eṇ virintatu* eṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaram eṇṇum piṟappu* um poruḷ um ottaliṉ vittār. ikarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaram vittār, piṟappu ovvātu* ēṉum `a-i-u a+ mūṉṟu* um^ cuṭṭu' ((eḻu//31)) eṉa+ cuṭṭu+poruṭṭu* āy niṟkiṉṟa iṉam^ karuti. avi aim pāl+ kaṇ= um perum-pāṉmi varum-āṟu uṇarka. ekaram ataṉ piṉ vittār, akara ikaraṅkaḷōṭu piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi. aikāra aukāraṅkaṭku iṉam ākiya kuṟṟu* eḻuttu iṉṟu* ēṉum piṟappu oppumi paṟṟi ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉar aikāra aukāram vittār. okaram no eṉa meyyōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu* allatu tāṉ āka ōr eḻuttu* oru moḻi y-ākā ciṟappu* iṉmi nōkki aikārattiṉ piṉ vittār. a-i-u-e eṉṉum nāṉku* um a+koṟṟaṉ i+koṟṟaṉ u+koṟṟaṉ e+koṟṟaṉ eṉa meyyōṭu kūṭāmal tām iṭiccol= āy niṉṟu* āyiṉum mēl varum peyarkaḷōṭu kūṭi+ cuṭṭu+ poruḷ um viṉā+ poruḷ um uṇarttum. okaram meyyōṭu kūṭi y-ē taṉ poruḷ uṇarttuvatu* allatu tāṉ āka+ poruḷ uṇarttātu* eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉṉum a-ā-u-ū-e-ē-o-ō-au eṉpaṉa tam= uḷ vaṭivu okkum. i-ī-ai tam= uḷ vaṭivu ovvā. iṉṉum ivi aḷapeṭukkuṅkāl neṭṭu* eḻuttōṭu kuṟṟu* eḻuttiṟku ōci iyiyum āṟṟāṉ um uṇarka. iṉi+ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu ākāra īkāra ūkāraṅkaḷ ātalāṉ um poruḷ okkum. puṇarcci oppumi uyirmayaṅkiyal uḷ+ peṟutum. i+ muṟi vaḻuvāmal mēl āḷum āṟu uṇarka.

iṉi+ kakāra ṅakāram um^ cakāra ñakāram um ṭakāra ṇakāram um^ takāra nakāram um pakāra makāram um^ tamakku+ piṟappu* um^ ceyki y-um ottaliṉ val= oṟṟu* iṭi y-ē mel= oṟṟu+ kalantu vittār. mutal* ^nā v-um mutal aṇṇam um iṭi nā v-um iṭi y-aṇṇam um nuṉi nā v-um nuṉi y-aṇṇam um itaḻ iyital um āki+ piṟakkiṉṟa iṭattiṉ muṟimi nōkki av= eḻuttukkaḷi+ ka-ca-ṭa-ta-pa-ṅa-ña-ṇa-na-ma-ṉa v-eṉa i+ muṟi y-ē vittār. piṟappu oppumiyāṉ um ṉakāram ṟakāram āy+ tiritalāṉ um ṟakāram um ṉakāram um^ cēra vittār. ivi tamiḻ eḻuttu* eṉpatu aṟivittaṟku+ piṉṉar vittār. iṉi iṭi y-eḻuttukkaḷil yakāram muṉ vittār. atuvum uyirkaḷ pōla miṭaṟṟu+ piṟanta vaḷi aṇṇaṅkaṇ= uṟṟu aṭiya+ piṟattaliṉ. rakāram ataṉōṭu piṟappu ovvātu* ēṉum^ ceyki ottaliṉ ataṉ piṉ vittār. lakāram um vakāram um^ tammiṉ+ piṟappu* um^ ceyki y-um ovvā v-ēṉum^ kal valitu col valitu eṉṟāl+pōla+ tammiṉ+ cērntu varum^ coṟkaḷ perum-pāṉmi y-eṉpatu paṟṟi lakāram um vakāram um^ cēra vittār. ḻakāram um ḷakāram um oṉṟāṉ um iyipu* ila v-ēṉum `iṭi y-eḻuttu* eṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa' ((eḻu//21)) eṉṟāl+ $canta v-iṉpattiṟku iyipu* uṭimi karuti+ cēra vittār pōlum.

akaram uyir akaram um uyirmey= akaram um eṉa iraṇṭu. iḵtu ēṉi y-uyirkaṭku* um okkum. eṉa v-ē, ōr-uyir patiṉeṭṭu* āyiṟṟu.

iv= eḻuttu* eṉappaṭṭa ōciyi $aruvu* eṉpār aṟiyātār. ataṉi uruvu* eṉṟu* ē kōṭum. atu ceṟippa+ cēṟalāṉ um^, ceṟippa varutalāṉ um, iṭi y-eṟiya+paṭutalāṉ um^, cevikkaṇ+ ceṉṟu uṟutalāṉ um, iṉpa tuṉpatti ākkutalān um, uruvu* um uruvu* uṅ kūṭi+ piṟattalāṉ un, tali y-um miṭaṟu* um neñcu* um eṉṉum mūṉṟu* iṭattu* um nili-peṟṟu+ pal= um itaḻ um nā v-um mūkku* um aṇṇam um uṟa+ piṟakkum eṉṟamiyāṉ um uruvu* ē y-ām. aruvu* ē y-āyiṉ iv= iṭattiṉ+ kūṟiyaṉa iṉmi uṇarka. allatūum, vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi y-eṉṟu ōtiyamiyāṉ um uṇarka. uṭampoṭu puṇarttal eṉṉum ilakkaṇattāṉ iv= ōci uruvu* ātal nilipeṟṟatu* eṉṟu uṇarka. ataṟku+ kāraṇam um muṉṉar+ kūṟiṉām.

iv= eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruviṟku vaṭivu kūṟār āyiṉār, atu muppattiraṇṭu vaṭiviṉ uḷ iṉṉa eḻuttiṟku iṉṉa vaṭivu* eṉa+ piṟarkku uṇarttutaṟku aritu* eṉpatu karuti. av vaṭivu ārāyum iṭattu+ $peṟṟapeṟṟa vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivu* ām, kuḻal akattiṉ+ kūṟiṉ+ kuḻal vaṭivum^ kuṭattu* akattiṉ+ kūṟiṉ+ kuṭa vaṭivu* um $veḷḷiṭiyiṉ+ kūṟiṉ ellā+ tici y-um $nīrttaraṅkamum pōla.

`ellā mey= um uruvu* uruvu* āki' ((eḻu//17)) eṉa v-um `uḷ+peṟu puḷḷi y-uruvu* ākum= ē' ((eḻu//14)) eṉa v-um `meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal' ((eḻu//15)) eṉa v-um^ ciṟupāṉmi vaṭivu* um^ kūṟuvar. atu vaṭṭam^ caturam mutaliya muppattiraṇṭaṉ uḷ oṉṟi uṇarttum. maṉattāṉ uṇarum nuṇ= uṇarvu illōr um uṇartaṟku eḻuttukkaṭku vēṟuvēṟu vaṭivam^ kāṭṭi eḻutappaṭṭu naṭattaliṉ+ kaṭpulaṉ ākiya varivaṭivu* um uṭiya v-āyiṉa. perumpāṉmi meykku* ē vaṭivu kūṟiṉār, uyirkku vaṭivu* iṉmiyiṉ. `ekara v-okarattu* iyaṟki y-um aṟṟu* ē' ((eḻu//16)) eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmi uyirkku* um vaṭivu kūṟiṉār. (1)

TE2n

avi tām^
kuṟṟu* iyal ikaram^ kuṟṟu* iyal ukaram
āytam eṉṟa
mu+ pāl+ puḷḷi y-um eḻuttu* ōr-aṉṉa.

itu mēl+ cārntu varum eṉṟa mūṉṟiṟku* um peyar um muṟi y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: --- avi tām --- mēl+ cārntu varum eṉa+paṭṭavi tām, kuṟṟiyal ikaram^ kuṟṟiyal ukaram āytam eṉṟa mu+pāl+puḷḷi y-um --- kuṟṟiyal ikaram um^ kuṟṟiyal ukaram um āytam um eṉṟa colla+paṭṭa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatu* ākiya puḷḷi vaṭivu* um ām; eḻuttu* ōr-aṉṉa --- avi y-um muṉ+kūṟiya muppatu* eḻuttōṭu oru taṉmi y-āy vaḻaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

muṉ+kūṟiya iraṇṭu* um ummi tokka niṉṟaṉa. ikara ukaram^ kuṟuki niṉṟaṉa, vikāram* vakiyāṉ+ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutaliṉ. ivaṟṟi iṅṅaṉam^ kuṟiyiṭṭu* āḷutal ellārkkum oppa muṭintatu. cantaṉam+kōl kuṟukiṉāl+ pirappam^ kōl ākātatu pōla uyiratu kuṟukkam um uyir ē y-ām. ivaṟṟi+ puṇarcci vēṟṟumi y-um poruḷ vēṟṟumi y-um paṟṟi vēṟu* ōr eḻuttu* āka vēṇṭiṉār.

ivaṟṟu* uḷ+ kuṟṟiyal ukaram nērpaci y-um niri paci y-um āka+ cīrkaḷi+ pala v-ākkum āṟu ceyyuḷiyal uḷ uṇarka.

āytam eṉṟa ōci tāṉ aṭuppu+kūṭṭu+pōla mūṉṟu puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu* eṉpatu uṇarttaṟku āytam eṉṟa muppāl+ puḷḷi y-um eṉṟār. ataṉi ikkālattār naṭuvu vāṅkiyiṭṭu* eḻutupa. itaṟku vaṭivu kūṟiṉār, ēṉi y-oṟṟukkaḷ pōla uyir ēṟātu ōci vikāram āy niṟpatu* oṉṟu* ākaliṉ. eḻuttiyal taḻā ōcikaḷ pōla+ koḷḷiṉum^ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟaṟku eḻuttu* ē y-ām eṉṟār. itaṉi+ puḷḷi vaṭiviṟṟu* eṉa v-ē ēṉi y-eḻuttukkaḷ ellām vari-vaṭiviṉa v-ātal peṟṟām.

muṉ ^niṉṟa cūttirattāṉ+ cārntu varaṉ marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭi y-ē eḻuttu* eṉappaṭupa muppaḵtu* eṉpa eṉa v-ē, cārntu varal% marapiṉ mūṉṟu* um ē ciṟantaṉa, ēṉiya muppatu* um avvāṟu ciṟantila v-eṉa v-um poruḷ* ^tantu niṟṟaliṉ ataṉi vilakki+ ciṟanta muppatu eḻuttōṭu ivi y-um oppa vaḻaṅkum eṉṟaṟku eḻuttu* ōraṉṉa eṉṟār.

i+ peyarkaḷ ē peyar. i+ muṟi y-ē muṟi. toki y-um mūṉṟu* ē. i+ mūṉṟu peyar um paṇpu+toki.

`avitām' `āytam eṉṟa' eṉpaṉa col+cīr-aṭi. (2)

TE3n

avaṟṟu* uḷ, a i u

itu muṉ+ kūṟiyavaṟṟu* uḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um uṇarttutal* ^nutaliṟṟu.

i+ kāraṇa+peyar mēl āḷumāṟu āṇṭu uṇarka.

TE4n

ā ī ū ē ai

TE5n

mū v-aḷapu* icittal ōr eḻuttu* iṉṟu* ē.

iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; ōr eḻuttu mū v-aḷapu* āy um icikkum^ kollō v-eṉṟu aiya+paṭutaliṉ.
