eṉa v-ē, pala eḻuttu+ kūṭiya iṭattu mūṉṟu māttiri y-um nāṉku māttiri y-um icikkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉa v-ē, perumpāṉmi mūṉṟu māttiri y-ē peṟum eṉṟār pulavar. pala eḻuttu* eṉa v-ē, nāṉku māttiri y-um peṟutal peṟṟām. (5)

TE6n

nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ av= aḷapu* uṭiya
kūṭṭi y-eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu māttiri nīḷum āṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ --- vaḻakku* iṭattu* um^ ceyyuḷ iṭattu* um ōci y-um poruḷ um peṟutal kāraṇam āka iraṇṭu māttiri peṟṟa eḻuttu a+māttiriyiṉ mikku olittali virumpuvar āyiṉ, av= aḷapu uṭiya kūṭṭi eḻūutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- tām^ karutiya māttiriyi+ tarutaṟku uriya eḻuttukkaḷi+ kūṭṭi a+ māttirikaḷi eḻuppuka v-eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar; eṉṟavāṟu.

kūṭṭi y-eḻuppum āṟu `kuṉṟu* ici moḻi' ((eḻu//41)) `ai-au v-eṉṉum' ((eḻu//42)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ eḻu vakittu* eṉa+ kūṟupa.

utāraṇam: āa, īi, ūu, ēe, aii, ōo, auu eṉa varum. ivi mūṉṟu māttiri peṟṟaṉa. ivitām `neṭṭu* eḻuttu ēḻu* ē y-or eḻuttu oru moḻi' ((eḻu//43)) eṉṟa a+neṭṭu* eḻuttukkaḷ ē aḷapu* eṭuttaliṉ+ collāmal eytiṉa. iṉi `aḷapeṭi y-acinili y-ākal um urittu* ē' ((ceyyuḷiyal//17)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷ-iyal+ cūttirattāṉ eḻuttu* ām^taṉmi y-um eytiṟṟu.

itu tāṉ iyaṟki y-aḷapeṭi y-um^ ceyyuṭku+ pulavar ceytu-koṇṭa ceyaṟki y-aḷapeṭi y-um āy+ col+ ^taṉmi eyti-niṉṟu alaku peṟum-āṟu* um^ kuṟṟiyal ikara+ kuṟṟiyal ukaraṅkaḷ pōla eḻuttu* ām^ taṉmi eyti alaku peṟātu niṟkum āṟu* um a+cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. eṉa v-ē, eḻuttu* ām^ taṉmi y-um uṭimiyiṉ aḷapeṭiyōṭu kūṭi eḻuttu nāṟpatu eṉṟal um poruntiṟṟu. oṟṟu* aḷapeṭi ceyyuṭku* ē varutaliṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟār āyiṉār.

av= aḷapu* uṭiya eṉa+ paṉmi y-āka+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivar um nāṉku māttiri y-um^ koṇṭār. eṉṉi? iv=āciriyari `muntu nūl kaṇṭu' eṉṟār ākaliṉ. māpurāṇattu,

`ceyyuṭkaḷ ōci citiyuṅkāl īr aḷapu* um
aiya+pāṭu* iṉṟi $yaṇiyumā $mitīroṟṟu*
iṉṟi y-um^ ceyyuḷ+ keṭiṉ oṟṟi y-uṇṭākku
kuṉṟu mēl oṟṟu* aḷapum^ koḷ.'

eṉṟa cūttirattāṉ avar koṇṭa nāṉku māttiri y-um iv= āciriyarkku nērtal vēṇṭutaliṉ. atu `ceṟāaay vāḻiya neñcu' ((kuṟaḷ//1200)) `tūuu+ tīm puki tol vicumpu' `pēeerttu+kol' `ilāaarkku* illi+ tamar' `virāaay+ ceyyāmi naṉṟu' `marīii+piṉṉi+ pirivu' eṉa+ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭku* ellām nāṉku māttiri peṟṟu niṉṟaṉa. aṉṟi mūṉṟu māttiri peṟṟaṉa v-ēl āciriya+taḷi taṭṭu+ ceppal-ōci keṭum āyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam aḷapu* eṭātu-niṉṟu āciriya+ taḷi taṭṭu niṟpaṉa kalikku uṟuppu* ākiya koccaka veṇpākkaḷ; ivi aṉṉa v-aṉṟu* eṉa uṇarka.

kōṭṭu nūṟu* um mañcaḷ um^ kūṭiya vaḻi+ piṟanta cev vaṇṇam pōla neṭil um^ kuṟil um^ kūṭiya kūṭṭattu+ piṟanta piṉṉar+ piḷavu-paṭā v-ōciyi aḷapeṭi y-eṉṟu āciriyar vēṇṭiṉār. ivi kūṭṭi+ colliya kālattu* allatu pulappaṭā, eḷ= āṭṭiya vaḻi y-allatu eṇṇey pulappaṭā v-āṟu pōla v-eṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi aḷapeṭi y-allāta ōcikaḷ ellām ici y-ōci y-ātaliṉ avaṟṟi `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa. (6)

TE7n

kaṇṇimi noṭi y-eṉa v-av= ē māttiri
nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟu* ē.

itu māttirikku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kaṇṇimi noṭi eṉa av= ē māttiri --- kaṇṇimi y-eṉa v-um noṭi y-eṉa v-um av= iraṇṭu* ē eḻuttiṉ māttirikku aḷavu, nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟē --- nuṇṇitāka nūl ilakkaṇatti uṇarnta āciriyar kaṇṭa neṟi eṉṟavāṟu.

`eṉa' eṇṇiṉ+ pirintu iraṇṭu* iṭattu* um^ kūṭiṟṟu. kaṇṇimi noṭi eṉṉum pala poruḷ oru coṟkaḷ īṇṭu+ toḻil% mēl um ōci mēl um muṟi y-ē niṉṟaṉa. āciriyar ellār um eḻuttiṟku ivi y-ē aḷavāka+ kūṟaliṉ ivar um^ kūṟiṉār. iyaṟki makaṉ taṉ kuṟippiṉ aṉṟi iraṇṭu* imi y-um oru kāl+kūṭi nīṅkiṉa kāla+kaḻivu* um a eṉa+ piṟanta ōciyatu tōṟṟa+ kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um okkum. i+ kaṇ=imiyiṉatu pākam meykku* um^ cārpiṉ+ ^tōṟṟattiṟku* um itaṉ pākam makara+ kuṟukkattiṟku* um^ koḷka. i+kaṇ=imi iraṭṭittu varutal neṭiṟku* um atu mūṉṟu* um nāṉku* um āy varutal aḷapeṭikku* um^ koḷka. atu pōla v-ē noṭittal+ ^toḻiliṉ+ piṟanta ōciyatu tōṟṟa+kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um a eṉa+ piṟanta ōciyatu tōṟṟa+kēṭṭu+ kāla+kaḻivu* um okkum. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku* um^ kūṟiya v-āṟē koḷka.

iṉi av= aḷavi tāṉ niṟuttaḷ attal peytu* aḷattal cārtti y-aḷattal nīṭṭi y-aḷattal teṟittu* aḷattal $tēṅka mukantu* aḷattal eṇṇi y-aḷattal eṉa eḻu vakittu. avaṟṟuḷ itu cārtti y-aḷattal ām.

kaṇ=imikku* um noṭikku* um aḷavu ārāyiṉ varampu* iṉṟi ōṭum eṉṟu karuti `nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭa v-āṟu' eṉṟu muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉṉum utti kūṟiṉār. iḵtu āṇi kūṟutal um ām. eṉavē, eḻuttiṟku* ē aḷavu kūṟi māttirikku aḷavu kūṟiṟṟu* ilar. noṭiyiṉ+ kaṇṇimi ciṟappu* uṭittu, uḷḷattāṉ niṉittu nikaḻāmiyiṉ. (7)

TE8n

aukāram* v-iṟuvāy+
paṉṉīr eḻuttu* um uyir eṉa moḻipa.

itu kuṟili y-um neṭili y-um^ tokuttu vēṟu* ōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aukāra iṟuvāy+ paṉṉīr eḻuttu* um --- akaram mutal-āka aukāram īṟu* āka+ kiṭanta paṉṉiraṇṭu* eḻuttu* um, uyir eṉa moḻipa --- uyir eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉi y-uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itu v-um āṭci y-um^ kāraṇam um nōkkiyatu*ōr kuṟi. mey patiṉeṭṭiṉi y-um iyakki+ tāṉ $aruvāy vaṭivu* iṉṟi niṟṟaliṉ uyir āyiṟṟu. ivi meykku uyir āy niṉṟu meykaḷi iyakkum ēl uyir eṉa vēṟu* ōr eḻuttu* iṉṟām piṟa v-eṉiṉ, meyyiṉ* ^niṟkum uyir um^ taṉi y-ē niṟkum uyir um vēṟu* eṉa uṇarka. eṉṉi? `akara mutala' ((kuṟaḷ//1)) eṉpuḻi akaram^ taṉi y-uyir um āy+ kakaram* v-oṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uyir um āy vēṟu niṟṟaliṉ. av= akaram^ taṉi y-ē niṟṟalāṉ um pala meykkaḷ% niṉṟu avva+ meykaṭku icinta ōcikaḷi+ payantu* ē niṟṟalāṉ um vēṟupaṭṭatu* ākaliṉ oṉṟu* ē y-āy um pala v-ē y-āy-um niṟpatu* ōr taṉmiyi y-uṭittu* eṉṟu kōṭum; iṟivaṉ oṉṟu* ē y-āy niṟkum^ taṉmi y-um pal= uyirkku* um^ tāṉ ē y-āy avaṟṟiṉ aḷavu* āy niṟkum^ taṉmi y-um pōla. atu a eṉṟa vaḻiyum ūra eṉa viḷi y-ēṟṟa-vaḻi y-um `akara mutala' eṉṟa vaḻi y-um mū v-iṉaṅkaḷil ēṟiṉa-vaḻi y-um ōci vēṟupaṭṭa v-āṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam icittu* uḻi y-um māttiri oṉṟu* ē y-ām. iḵtu ēṉi y-uyirkaṭku* um okkum.

aukāra v-iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇpu+toki. ummi muṟṟu* ummi. `akara mutal' eṉa muṉ+kūṟi+ pōntamiyiṉ īṇṭu īṟu* ē kūṟiṉār. (8)

TE9n

ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy+,
patiṉeṇ= eḻuttu mey= eṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu uyir allaṉavaṟṟi+ tokuttu ōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy+ patiṉeṇ eḻuttu* um --- kakāram mutal ṉakāram īṟu* āy+ kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttu* um, mey= eṉa moḻipa --- mey= eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉi y-uṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itu v-um āṭci y-um^ kāraṇam um nōkkiya kuṟi. eṉṉi? paṉṉīr uyirkku* um^ tāṉ iṭam^ koṭuttu avaṟṟāṉ iyaṅkum^ taṉmi peṟṟa uṭampu* āy niṟṟaliṉ.

ṉakāra v-iṟuvāy eṉpatu paṇpu+toki. ummi muṟṟu* ummi. muṉṉar ṉakāram* v-iṟuvāy eṉpuḻi muppatu* eḻuttiṟku* um īṟu* ām eṉṟār, īṇṭu+ patiṉeṭṭu* eḻuttiṟku* um īṟu* ām eṉṟār ātaliṉ+ kūṟiyatu kūṟiṟṟu* aṉṟu. (9)

TE10n

meyyōṭu* iyiyiṉum uyir iyal* ^tiriyā.

iḵtu uyir-meykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyir meyyōṭu iyiyiṉum --- paṉṉīr uyir um patiṉeṭṭu meyyōṭu* um^ kūṭi niṉṟaṉa v-āyiṉum, iyal tiriyā --- tam aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um eṇṇum^ tirintu nillā eṉṟavāṟu.

itu `puḷḷi y-illā' ((eḻu//17)) eṉpataṉi nōkki niṟṟaliṉ etiratu pōṟṟal ām. uyir um mey= um atikāra+paṭutaliṉ īṇṭu vittār. a eṉpuḻi niṉṟa aḷavu* um^ kuṟi y-um oṉṟu* eṉṉum eṇṇum^ kā eṉa niṉṟa iṭattu* um okkum eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. piṟa v-um aṉṉa. āyiṉ oṉṟu* ari māttiri y-um iraṇṭu* ari māttiri y-um uṭiyaṉa oru māttiri y-um iraṇṭu māttiri y-um āya v-āṟu eṉṉi y-eṉiṉ, nīr taṉittu aḷantu* uḻi y-um nāḻi y-āy ari nāḻi y-uppiṉ+ kalantu* uḻi y-um^ kūṭi oṉṟari nāḻi y-āy mikāta v-āṟu pōlvatu* ōr poruḷ+peṟṟi y-eṉṟu koḷvatu* allatu kāraṇam^ kūṟal ākāmi uṇarka. āciriyaṉ āṇi eṉpār um uḷar.

`viḷaṅkāy tiraṭṭiṉār illi+ kaḷaṅkaṉiyi+
kār eṉa+ ceytār um il' ((nālaṭiyār//103//107))

eṉpatu* ē kāṭṭiṉār uriyāciriyar um. (10)

TE11n

meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē y-ari y-eṉa moḻipa.

itu taṉi-meykku aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ aḷavu* ē ari y-eṉa moḻipa --- meyyiṉatu māttiriyiṉi orōvoṉṟu ari-māttiri y-uṭiya v-eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

av= arimāttiri y-um^ taṉittu+ kūṟi+ kāṭṭal ākātu, nā+ ciṟitu puṭipeyarum^ taṉmi y-āy niṟṟaliṉ. iṉi ataṉi+ cila moḻi mēl+ peytu kākki kōṅku kavvi y-eṉa+ kāṭṭupa. mey= eṉpatu aḵṟiṇi y-iyal+ peyar ātaliṉ mey= eṉṉum oṟṟumi paṟṟi ari y-eṉṟār. (11)

TE12n

avviya ṉiliyu mēṉi mūṉṟē.

itu cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟaṟkum aḷavu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi mūṉṟu --- cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattu mūṉṟum, avviyal niliyum --- muṟkūṟiya ari māttiriyākiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

kēṇmiyā nāku eḵku eṉa varum. (12)

TE13n

ari y-aḷavu kuṟukal% makaram uṭittu* ē
y-ici y-iṭaṉ arukum^ teriyum^ kāli.

itu meykaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ici iṭaṉ makaram ariyaḷavu kuṟukaluṭittu --- vēṟōr eḻuttiṉatu ōciyiṉkaṇ makaravoṟṟu+ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉ+ kuṟuki+ kālmāttiri peṟutaliyuṭittu, teriyuṅkāli arukum --- ārāyuṅkālattu+ tāṉ ciṟupāṉmiyāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pōṉm varumvaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa orumoḻikkaṇṇum irumoḻikkaṇṇum^ koḷka. itu piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟa leṉṉum utti. (13)

TE14n

uṭpeṟu puḷḷi yuruvā kummē.

itu pakarattōṭu makarattiṭi varivaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceykiṉṟatu.

makaram atikārappaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṭpeṟupuḷḷi --- puṟattu+ peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷāṟpeṟum puḷḷi, uruvākum --- makarattiṟku vaṭivām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē puṟattu+ peṟum puḷḷiyāvatu mēṟcūttirattāṉ meykaṭku+ kūṟum puḷḷi. īṇṭu uruveṉṟatu kāṭcipporuḷi uṇartti niṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: kappi kappi (kammi) eṉavarum. iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟal. (14)

TE15n

meyyi ṉiyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.

itu taṉimeykkum uyirmeykkum oppumimēl vēṟṟumiceytal kūṟukiṉṟatu. eṉṉi? uyirmeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkum^ taṉimeyyāṉa kakara ṅakaraṅkaṭkum vaṭivu oṉṟāka eḻutiṉavaṟṟi oṉṟākkutaṟku+ piṉpu puḷḷi peṟukaveṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ iyaṟki puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal --- patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷiṉ taṉmiyāvatu puḷḷipeṟṟu niṟṟalām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē uyirmeykaṭku+ puḷḷiyiṉṟāyiṟṟu. k-ṅ...ṟ-ṉ eṉavarum. ivaṟṟi+ puḷḷiyiṭṭu+ kāṭṭavē puḷḷi peṟuvataṟku muṉṉar akaram uṭaṉiṉṟatōr meyvaṭivē peṟṟu niṉṟaṉavaṟṟi+ piṉṉar appuḷḷiyiṭṭu+ taṉimey yākkiṉāreṉpatūum peṟutum. itaṉāṉē kakaram ṅakaram mutaliyaṉa puḷḷipeṟuvataṟku muṉṉar iyalpāka akaram peṟṟē niṟkumeṉpatūum puḷḷi peṟuṅkālattu avvakaram nīṅkumeṉpatūum piṉṉar a+ puḷḷi nīṅki uyirēṟumiṭattu+ taṉkaṇ akaram nīṅkiyē pōka varukiṉṟatōr uyir yātāyiṉum oṉṟu ēṟi niṟkumeṉpatūum peṟṟām. `meyyiṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//46)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉum ituvē itaṟku+ karuttātal uṇarka. (15)

TE16n

ekara okarattu iyaṟkiyu maṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ekara okarattu iyaṟkiyum aṟṟē --- ekara okaraṅkaḷiṉatu niliyum meypōla+ puḷḷipeṟum iyalpiṟṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē ēkāra ōkāraṅkaṭku+ puḷḷi yiṉṟāyiṟṟu.

e/-o/-eṉa varum.

iḵtu uyirmeykkum okkum.

makaram ārāyccippaṭṭatu kaṇṭu makarattiṟku vaṭivu vēṟṟumi ceytu, atikārattāṉ meyyiṉ taṉmi kūṟi, ataṉpiṉ māṭṭēṟṟaliṉ ekara okarattiyum^ kūṟiṉār. (16)

TE17n

puḷḷi y-illā v-ellā mey=um
uruvu* uruvu* āki y-akaramōṭu* uyirttal um
ēṉi y-uyirōṭu* uruvu tirintu* uyirttal um
āy īr iyala v-uyirttal āṟu* ē.

itu meyyum uyirum^ kūṭi+ puṇarumāṟum āṇṭu avi tiriyātum^ tirintum niṟkumāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷi illā ellā meyyum --- uyiri+ peṟutaṟku+ puḷḷiyi+ pōkkiṉa ellā meykaḷum, uruuruvāki akaramōṭu uyirttalum --- puḷḷi peṟukiṉṟa kālattu iyalpākiya akara nīṅkiya vaṭivē tamakku vaṭivāki niṉṟu piṉṉar ēṟiya akarattōṭukūṭi olittalum, ēṉiuyirōṭu uruvu tirintu uyirttalum --- oḻinta patiṉōruyirum^ kūṭi avvaṭivu tirintu olittalum, āyīriyala uyirttalāṟē --- eṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya avi olikkum muṟimi eṉṟavāṟu.

puḷḷiyillā meyyeṉavē muṉpeṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷiyi uyirēṟṟutaṟku+ pōkkiṉami peṟutum. uruvuruvākiyeṉavē puḷḷi peṟutaṟkāka iyalpākiya akaram nīṅkiya vaṭivē piṉṉar akaram peṟutaṟkum vaṭivāmeṉpatu kūṟiṉār.

ka-ṅa-ya eṉa varum.

uruvu tirintu uyirttalāvatu mēlum^ kīḻum vilaṅku peṟṟum kōṭupeṟṟum puḷḷipeṟṟum puḷḷiyum^ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟum uyirttalām. ki kī mutaliyaṉa mēlvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ku kū mutaliyaṉa kīḻvilaṅku peṟṟaṉa. ke kē mutaliyaṉa kōṭu peṟṟaṉa. kā ṅā mutaliyaṉa puḷḷi peṟṟaṉa. arukē peṟṟa puḷḷiyi ikkālattār kālāka eḻutiṉār. makaram uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyi vaḷitteḻutiṉār. ko kō ṅo ṅō mutaliyaṉa puḷḷiyum^ kōṭum uṭaṉpeṟṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉam^ tirintu olippavē uyirmey paṉṉirupatiṉeṭṭu irunūṟṟorupattāṟāyiṟṟu. ākavē uyirmeykku vaṭivum oruvāṟṟāṉ+ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē mey taṉakku iyalpākiya akaratti nīṅki niṟpatōr taṉmiyum piṟitōruyiri ēṟkum^ taṉmiyum uṭiyateṉpatūum, uyirmeykkaṇ+ pulappaṭātu iyalpākiya akaramāy niṟkum^ taṉmiyum mey puḷḷipeṟ ṟaḻintavaḻi avaṟṟiṟku+ takka uyirāy+ pulappaṭṭuvarum^ taṉmiyum uṭiyateṉpatūum peṟṟām. uyirmey yeṉpatu ummi+ toki. (17)

TE18n

meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu niliyē.

itu meyyum uyirum^ kūṭiyavaḻi avaṟṟiṉ ōci niṟkum muṟimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu --- meyyiṉatu ōci tōṉṟiya piṉṉatām, uyir tōṉṟu niliyē --- uyiriṉatu ōci tōṉṟum nili eṉṟavāṟu.

muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ mey muṉṉar niṟpa uyir piṉ vantu ēṟumeṉṟār. ammuṟiyē ōciyum piṟakkumeṉṟār. itaṉāṉē māttiri koḷḷuṅkāl uppum nīrum pōla oṉṟēyāy niṟṟalum vēṟupaṭuttu+ kāṇuṅkāl viralum viralum^ cēraniṉṟāṟpōla vēṟāy niṟṟalum peṟṟām. nīr uppiṉ kuṇamēyāyavāṟupōla uyirum meyyiṉ kuṇamēyāy vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi eyti niṟṟal koḷka.

utāraṇam: ka-ṅa-ya eṉa+ kūṭṭamum pirivum mūvakiyōciyum^ kāṇka. (18)

TE19n

valleḻuttu eṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa.

itu taṉimeykaḷuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa --- ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa eṉṉum^ taṉimeykaḷi, valleḻutteṉpa --- valleḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

iḵtu āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. oḻinta melleḻuttiyum iṭiyeḻuttiyam nōkki+ tām valleṉṟicittalāṉum valleṉa+ talivaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum valleḻuttāyiṟṟu. (19)

TE20n

melleḻuttu eṉpa ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa --- ṅa ña ṇa na ma ṉa eṉṉum^ taṉi meykaḷi, melleḻutteṉpa --- melleḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. melleṉṟicittalāṉum melleṉṟu mūkkiṉ vaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum melleḻuttāyiṟṟu. (20)

TE21n

iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ya ra la va ḻa ḷa --- ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum^ taṉi meykaḷi, iṭiyeḻutteṉpa --- iṭiyeḻutteṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi. iṭinikarttāy olittalāṉum iṭinikarttāya miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ+ piṟattalāṉum iṭiyeḻuttāyiṟṟu.

valliṉattu+ ka-ca-ta-pa eṉṉum nāṉkam melliṉattu ṅa-na-ma eṉṉum mūṉṟum iṭiyiṉattu ya-va eṉṉum iraṇṭum moḻikku mutalātal nōkki immuṟiyē vittār. ippeyarāṉē eḻutteṉṉum ōcikaḷ uruvāyiṉa. uyirkkum^ kuṟumi neṭumi kūṟaliṉ aviyum uruvāyiṉa. itu cārpiṉ+ ṟōṟṟattiṟkum okkum. (21)

TE22n

ammū vāṟum vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ
mey+mayaṅku* uṭaṉili teriyum^ kāli.

itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭum^ taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum uyirmey uyirmeyyōṭum^ taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum mayakkamum kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ammūvāṟum --- aṅṅaṉam mūṉṟu kūṟāka+ pakutta patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ --- vaḻakkiṭattum^ ceyyuḷiṭattum eḻuttukkaḷi+ kūṭṭi moḻippaṭuttu vaḻaṅkutal uḷatāmiṭattu, mey mayaṅkuṭaṉili --- taṉimey taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa piṟameyyōṭum^ taṉmeyyōṭum mayaṅkum niliyum, uṭaṉmayaṅkum nili --- appatiṉeṭṭum uyiruṭaṉē niṉṟu taṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭum^ taṉimeyyōṭum mayaṅkum niliyumeṉa iraṇṭām, teriyum^ kāli --- avi mayaṅkum moḻiyām^ taṉmi ārāyuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, taṉittuniṉṟa eḻuttuṭaṉ muṉṉiṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷ tām^ kūṭumāṟu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. kaṭka eṉṟāl iṭiniṉṟa taṉimey muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉiṉ vēṟāya kakaravoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kākki eṉṟāl iṭiniṉṟa kakaravoṟṟu muṉṉar niṉṟa taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. karu eṉa īreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ karutu eṉa mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum uyirmey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa uyirmeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. tuṇaṅki eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu. kal vil eṉa uyirmey niṉṟu taṉimeyyōṭu mayaṅkiṟṟu.

teriyuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ uyir muṉṉar uyirmeymmayakkamum uyir muṉṉar+ taṉimeymmayakkamum^ koḷka. avi aḷi āmpal eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa.

meymmayakkaṅkaḷuḷ taṉimeymuṉṉar+ piṟamey niṉṟu mayaṅkutal palavātaliṉ+ pala cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟi+ taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantu mayaṅkutali oru cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa. avi mayaṅkuṅkāl valliṉattil ṭakaramum ṟakaramum melliṉamāṟum iṭiyiṉamāṟum piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkumeṉṟum valliṉattiṉ+ kacatapakkaḷ taṉ meyyōṭaṉṟi+ piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkāveṉṟum uyttuṇara+ kūṟumāṟu uṇarka.

mūvāṟu meṉṉum ummi muṟṟummi.

iccūttiram mutalāka `meynnilicuṭṭiṉ' ((eḻu//30)) īṟāka mēṟkūṟum moḻimarapiṟku+ poruntiya karuvi kūṟukiṉṟateṉṟuṇarka; eḻuttukkaḷ tammiṉ+ kūṭi+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatātaliṉ. (22)

TE23n

ṭa ṟa la ḷa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
ka ca pa veṉṉu mūveḻuttu uriya.

itu taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkum mayakkam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- moḻiyiṭi niṉṟa ṭa ṟa la ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum nāṉku puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ka ca pa eṉṉum mūveḻuttu uriya --- ka ca pa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭka kaṭci naṭpu eṉavum^ kaṟka muyaṟci kaṟpu eṉavum^ celka valci celpa eṉavum^ koḷka. nīḷciṉi koḷpa eṉavum^ taṉimey piṟameyyōṭu mayaṅkiyavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭciṟār kaṟciṟār eṉpaṉa irumoḻi+ puṇarcciyākaliṉ īṇṭikkākā. (21)

TE24n

avaṟṟuḷ
laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavum^ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya nāṉkaṉuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar --- lakāra ḷakāramākiya puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yavavum^ tōṉṟum --- kacapakkaḷē yaṉṟi yakara vakaraṅkaḷum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

kolyāṉi celvam veḷyāṟu kaḷvaṉ eṉa varum.

ivaṟṟuḷ+ kolyāṉi eṉa viṉittokiyum veḷyāṟu eṉa+ paṇputtokiyum nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceyvataṟku iyiyāmiyiṉ `maruviṉpāttiya' eṉṟu kūṟuvarātaliṉ ivvāciriyar ivaṟṟi orumoḻiyāka+ koḷvareṉṟu uṇarka. ikkaruttāṉē mēlum viṉittokiyum paṇputtokiyum orumoḻiyākakkoṇṭu utāraṇam^ kāṭṭutum. aṉṟi ivvāciriyar nūl ceykiṉṟa kālattu viṉittokikkaṇṇum paṇputtokikkaṇṇumaṉṟi orumoḻikkaṇṇē mayaṅkuvaṉavum uḷavātaliṉ, avaṟṟi+ kaṇṭu ilakkaṇam^ kūṟiṉār. avi piṉṉar iṟantaṉaveṉṟu oḻittu utāraṇamillaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam^ kāṭṭāmal+ pōtalē naṉṟeṉṟu kūṟalumoṉṟu. (24)

TE25n

ṅañaṇa namaṉa veṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
tatta micika ḷottaṉa niliyē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tatta micikaḷ --- tamakkiṉamāy muṉṉiṉṟa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟakkaḷ, ottaṉa niliyē --- piṉṉiṟṟaṟku+ poruntiṉa mayaṅki niṟṟaṟkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṅkaṉ kañcaṉ kaṇṭaṉ kantaṉ kampaṉ maṉṟaṉ eṉa varum. teṅku piñcu vaṇṭu pantu kampu kaṉṟu eṉa+ kuṟṟukaramum^ kāṭṭupa. (25)

TE26n

avaṟṟuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar+
ka ca ña pa ma ya vav vēḻu muriya

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- mēṟkūṟiya melloṟṟu āṟaṉuḷ, ṇaṉaḵkāṉ muṉṉar --- ṇakāraṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, kacañapamayava ēḻum uriya --- ṭaṟakkaḷē yaṉṟi+ kacañapamayava eṉṉum ēḻeḻuttum vantu mayaṅkutaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṇku veṇcāntu veṇñāṇ paṇpu veṇmi maṇyāṟu eṇvaṭṭu eṉavum, puṉku puṉcey meṉñāṇ aṉpu vaṉmi iṉyāḻ puṉvaraku eṉavum varum. eṇvaṭṭu viṉittoki. eṇku puṉku peyar. (26)

TE27n

ña na ma va veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
yaḵkā ṉiṟṟaṉ meypeṟ ṟaṉṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- ña na ma va eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal meypeṟṟaṉṟē --- yaḵkāṉ niṟṟal poruṇmi peṟṟatu eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam āciriyar cūttirañceytaliṉ akkālattu oru moḻiyāka vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ uḷaveṉpatu peṟṟām. avi ikkālattu iṟantaṉa.

iṉi uriyāciriyar uriñyātu porunyātu tirumyātu tevyātu eṉa irumoḻikkaṇ varuvaṉa utāraṇamāka+ kāṭṭiṉārāleṉiṉ, āciriyar orumoḻiyāmāṟu īṇṭu+ kūṟi, irumoḻi puṇarttaṟku+ puṇariyaleṉṟu vēṟōr iyalum^ kūṟi, ataṉkaṇ `meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum' ((eḻu//107)) eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. kūṟiyavaḻi+ piṉṉum `ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti' ((eḻu//163)) eṉṟum piṟāṇṭum īṟukaṭōṟum eṭuttōti+ puṇarppar. ātaliṉ īṇṭu irumoḻippuṇarcci kāṭṭiṉ+ kūṟiyatukūṟa leṉṉum^ kuṟṟamām. ataṉāl avi kāṭṭutal poruntāmi uṇarka. (27)

TE28n

maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ vavvum^ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷimuṉ --- muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ makaramākiya puḷḷi muṉṉar, vavvum^ tōṉṟum --- pakarayakaramēyaṉṟi vakaramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṟkum utāraṇam ikkālattu iṟantaṉa. aṉṟi, varum vaṇṇakkaṉ eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kāṭṭiṉ `vakāra miciyu makāram^ kuṟukum' ((eḻu//330)) eṉṟa viti vēṇṭāvām. (28)

TE29n

yaraḻa veṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar
mutalā keḻuttu ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yaraḻaveṉṉum puḷḷi muṉṉar --- yaraḻa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar,mutalākeḻuttum --- moḻikku mutalāmeṉa mēṟkūṟum oṉpateḻuttukkaḷum, ummiyāṉ moḻikku mutalākāta piṟa eḻuttukkaḷum, ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum --- ṅakāramum vantu mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyka ārka āḻka, āytal ārtal āḻtal, āynar ārnar āḻnar, āypavi ārpavi āḻpavi, vāymi nērmi kīḻmi, eycili vārcili vāḻcēri, teyvam cērvatu vāḻvatu, pāyñekiḻi nērñekiḻi vāḻñeṇṭu, ceyyāṟu pōryāḷi vīḻyāṉi eṉa moḻikku mutalām oṉpatum vantu mayaṅkiṉa. ceyyāṟu eṉa yakarattiṉ muṉṉar yakaram vantatu taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantatām.

iṉi ummiyāṟkoṇṭa moḻikku mutalākātavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum^ cila kāṭṭutum: ōyvu cōrvu vāḻvu, ōyvōr cōrvōr vāḻvōr, āyñar cērñar āḻñar eṉavarum. piṟa eḻuttukkaḷōṭu varuvaṉa uḷavēṉum vaḻakkum^ ceyyuḷum nōkki+ kūṟikkoḷka.

iṉi vēyṅṅaṉam vērṅṅaṉam vēḻṅṅaṉam eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta ṅakaram iṭivanta coṟkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟuṇarka, āciriyar ōtutaliṉ. itaṉi `ṅakaramoṭu tōṉṟum' eṉa+ pirittōtiṉār akkālattum aritāka vaḻaṅkaliṉ.

iṉi vēykaṭitu vērkaṭitu vīḻkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avi irumoḻiyāka nilimoḻi varumoḻi ceytu mēṟpuṇarkkiṉṟaṉa īṇṭikkākā eṉa maṟukka. (29)

TE30n

meynnili cuṭṭi ṉellā veḻuttum^
tammuṉ+ ṟāmvarūum raḻavalam^ kaṭiyē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē taṉimey taṉṉoṟṟōṭu mayaṅkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meynnili cuṭṭiṉ --- poruṇilimiyi+ karutiṉ, ellā eḻuttum --- patiṉeṭṭu meyyum, tammuṉ tām varūum --- tammuṉṉē tām vantu mayaṅkum, raḻa alaṅkaṭiyē --- rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷallāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kākki eṅṅaṉam pacci maññi paṭṭi maṇṇi tatti venney appu ammi veyyar elli evvi kaḷḷi koṟṟi kaṉṉi eṉa varum.

meynnili cuṭṭiṉeṉṟataṉāl taṉimey muṉṉar uyirmey varumeṉṟu koḷka. ellāmeṉṟatu rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ oḻintaṉavaṟṟi+ taḻuviṟṟu. (30)

TE31n

a i u am mūṉṟum^ cuṭṭu.

itu kuṟṟeḻuttu eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: a i u ammūṉṟum^ cuṭṭu --- a i u eṉṟu kūṟiya ammūṉṟum^ cuṭṭeṉṉum^ kuṟiyiḷiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiyatōr kuṟi, cuṭṭi aṟiyappaṭum poruḷi uṇarttaliṉ. taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉpataṉāṉ ekaram viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉa varum. ivi peyari+ cārntu tattam^ kuṟippiṉ+ poruḷceyta iṭiccol. iccūttiram orutalimoḻita leṉṉum utti. ituvum mēliccūttiramum eḻuttāntaṉmiyaṉṟi moḻinilimippaṭṭu vēṟōr kuṟipeṟṟu niṟṟaliṉ moḻimarapiṉi+ cēravittār. (31)

TE32n

ā ē ō am mūṉṟum viṉā.

itu neṭṭeḻutteṉṟavaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟōr kuṟiyīṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ā ē ō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ammūṉṟum viṉā eṉṉum^ kuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.

ituvum āṭciyum^ kāraṇamum nōkkiya kuṟi, viṉāpporuḷ uṇarttaliṉ.

utāraṇam: uṇkā uṇkē uṇkō eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ākāram ikkālattu viṉāvāy varuta laritu. nīyē nīyō eṉpatu ikkālattu varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ē ō eṉpaṉa iṭiccollōttiṉuḷḷum^ kūṟiṉār, ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ tarum poruṭṭoki paṟṟi. itu moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉṉum uttikku iṉamām, yakara ākāramum viṉāvāy varutaliṉ. (32)

TE33n

aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um oṟṟu* ici nīṭal um
uḷa v-eṉa moḻipa v-iciyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟiya v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi icinūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟi, avvilakkaṇam innūṟkum^ koḷkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷapiṟantu uyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṟkūṟiya uyirum uyirmeyyum māttiriyi iṟantolittalum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ arimāttiriyiṉ nīṇṭolittalum yāḻ nūliṭattaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, iciyoṭu civaṇiya uḷaveṉa moḻipa --- aṅṅaṉam aḷapiṟantum nīṇṭum icittal ōciyōṭu poruntiya nālvaki+ ceyyuṭkaḷukkum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

eḻuttukkaḷ muṟkūṟiya māttiriyi iṟantolikkumāṟu kaṇṭu, aḵtu iṟantolikkum iṭam^ kūṟiṉār, eḻuttum^ collum poruḷum^ kiṭakkum iṭam^ ceyyuḷiṭamātaliṉ. atu mikkolittali+ ceyyuḷiyaliṉkaṇ `māttiri yeḻuttiya lacivaki yeṉāa' ((cey//1)) eṉa irupattāṟu uṟuppiṟkum^ ciṟappuṟuppāka muṟkūṟip, piṉṉar

`māttiri y-aḷavu* um eḻuttu* iyal vaki y-um*
mēl+ kiḷantu* aṉṉa v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.' ((cey//2))

eṉa iccūttirattōṭu māṭṭeṟintu, piṉṉum

`eḻuttaḷa veñciṉum^ cīrnili tāṉē

kuṉṟalu mikutalu milleṉa moḻipa.' ((cey//3))

eṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār. itu etiratupōṟṟaleṉṉam uttiyam^ kūṟiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: `varuvarkol vayaṅkiḻāay valippaṉyāṉ kēeḷiṉi' ((kali//11)) eṉpuḻi ḻakara ākāramum kakara ēkāramum māttiri iṟantolittavāṟu uṇarka. `piṭi yūṭṭi+ piṉṉuṇṇum^m^ kaḷiṟeṉavumurittaṉarē' ((kali//11)) eṉpuḻi ṅakaravoṟṟu aḷaviṟantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻinta mūvaki+ ceyyuṭkum ivvāṟē tattamakkuriya pāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉi naṭātti aḷavu mikumāṟu kāṇka.

civaṇiya eṉpatu toḻiṟpeyar. iciyoṭu civaṇiya eṉavē ceyyuḷātal peṟṟām. narampeṉṟatu ākupeyarāy yāḻiṉi uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟiyeṉṟatu nūli. moḻipa veṉṟum eṉmaṉār pulavareṉṟum irukāl+ kūṟiyavataṉāl, iṅṅaṉam poruḷ kūṟalē āciriyarkku+ karuttāyiṟṟu. eṉṉi? ceyyuḷiyaluḷ+ kūṟiya `māttiriyaḷavum' eṉṉum^ cūttirattil `mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa' ((cey//2)) eṉṉum māṭṭēṟṟiṟku ivvōttiṉuḷ vēṟōr cūttiram iṉmiyiṉ. ivvilakkaṇam^ kūṟākkāl+ ceyyuṭku+ pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻātu avi uricceyyuṭpōla niṟṟaliṉ ivvilakkaṇam^ kūṟalē vēṇṭumeṉṟu uṇarka.

`cūttirattu+ poruḷ aṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
viṉṟi yamiyā tiyipavi yellā
moṉṟa vurippa turiyeṉa+ paṭumē.' ((marapiyal//103))

eṉṉum marapiyal+ cūttirattāṉē ivvāṟē cūttiraṅkaḷi nalintu poruḷurippaṉavellām^ koḷka. (33)

((nūṉmarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((2. moḻimarapu))

TE34n

kuṟṟiyal ikara niṟṟal vēṇṭum
yā-v-eṉ ciṉi mici y-uriyaci+ kiḷavikku
āvayiṉ varūu makaram ūrntu ē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. mēl eḻuttu uṇarttiyapiṉṉar avi tammuḷ+ toṭarumāṟum uṇartti avveḻuttāḷum moḻiyatu marapu uṇarttukiṉṟamiyiṉ ivvōttu moḻimarapeṉa+ kāraṇa+ peyarttāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram muṉṉar+ cārntu varumeṉṟa mūṉṟaṉuḷ+ kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uriyaci+ kiḷavikku varūum --- tāṉ kūṟum poruḷi+ kōṭaṟku oruvaṉi etirmukamākkum^ colliṟku+ poruntavarum, āvayiṉ --- ammiyāveṉṉum iṭiccolli+ collumiṭattu, yāveṉ ciṉimici makaram ūrntu --- yāveṉṉum uṟuppiṉ mēlatāy mutalāy niṉṟa makaravoṟṟiṉi yēṟi, kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- kuṟṟiyalikaram niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉa varum. kēḷeṉṟatu uriyacikkiḷavi; ataṉi+ cārntu taṉakku iyalpiṉṟi niṉṟatu miyāveṉṉum iṭiccol. avviṭiccol mutalum ataṉiṉ+ piriyum yā ataṟku uṟuppumā meṉṟu karuti yāveṉciṉi eṉṟār. miyā iṭam; makaram paṟṟukkōṭu. yāvum ikaram ari māttiriyātaṟku+ cārpu. ivviṭiccol taṉittuniṟṟal āṟṟāmiyiṉ+ kēḷeṉpataṉōṭu cārntu oru collāyē niṉṟuḻi iṭiniṉṟa ikaram orumoḻiyiṭattu+ kuṟṟiyalikaramāy varutalāṉum āṇṭu uṇarttaṟku+ ciṟappiṉmiyāṉum īṇṭu+ pōttantu kūṟiṉār. ūrnteṉavē kuṟṟiyalikaramum uyireṉpatu peṟṟām, uyirkkallatu ēṟutaliṉmiyiṉ. (1)

TE35n

puṇariya ṉiliyiṭi+ kuṟukalu murittē
yuṇara+ kūṟiṉ muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum.

itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puṇariyal niliyiṭi+ kuṟukalum urittē --- akkuṟṟiyalikaram puṇariyaluḷ orumoḻikkaṇṇaṉṟi irumoḻi tammiṉ+ puṇartaliyaṉṟa nilimikkaṇṇum^ kuṟukutalurittu, uṇarakkūṟiṉ muṉṉarttōṉṟum --- ataṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇara+ kūṟattoṭaṅkiṉ avi kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaluḷḷē kūṟappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

kuṟukalumeṉṉumiṭattu ummiyi niliyiṭiyumeṉa māṟikkūṭṭuka. `yakaramvaruvaḻi' ((eḻu//410)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu yakaram iṭam, ukaram cārnta valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu.

utāraṇam: nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu eṉavarum. itu moḻivāmeṉṉum utti (2)

TE36n

neṭṭeḻuttu imparum^ toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum^
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē.

iḵtu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattukku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram vallāṟu ūrntē --- kuṟṟiyalukaram valleḻuttukkaḷ āṟiṉiyum ūrntu, neṭṭeḻuttimparum^ toṭarmoḻi īṟṟum --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉ piṉṉum aivaki+ toṭarmoḻiyiṉ iṟutiyiṉum niṟṟal vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

neṭṭeḻuttiṉatu piṉ toṭarmoḻiyiṉatu īṟeṉa nilattatakalampōla oṉṟiyaṟkiḻamippaṭṭu niṉṟatu, ammoḻiyiṉ+ ṟīrntu kuṟṟiyalukaram nillāmiyiṉ. vallāṟu paṇputtoki. muṟṟummi tokkuniṉṟatu. atikāra muṟimiyeṉṉum uttiyāṉ niṟṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu varuvikka.

utāraṇam: nāku varaku teḷku eḵku kokku kuraṅku eṉavarum. ivvāṟuvakiyum iṭam; valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu. eṉavē, moḻikku īṟātalum peṟṟām. perumuracu tirumuracu eṉpaṉa irumoḻikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. paracu iṅku ētu eṉṉum muṟṟukaraṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiccitivu. tarukku aṇukku eṉpaṉa viṉikkaṇ vanta muṟṟukaram. kuṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉar vanta uyirēṟimuṭiya arimāttiriyāy niṟṟalum, muṟṟukarattiṟku muṉṉarvanta uyirēṟi muṭiyāmiyum tammuḷ vēṟṟumi. (3)

TE37n

iṭippaṭiṉ+ kuṟuku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa.

itu kuṟṟiyalukaram puṇarmoḻikkaṇ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉ+ kuṟukivaru meṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭippaṭiṉ+ kuṟukum iṭaṉumār uṇṭē --- avvukaram orumoḻiyuḷaṉṟi+ puṇarmoḻiyiṭippaṭiṉ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉum^ kuṟukum iṭaṉum uṇṭu, kaṭappāṭu aṟinta puṇariyalāṉa --- ataṟku iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum yāṇṭu+ peṟuvateṉiṉ, ataṉ puṇarccimuṟimi aṟiyum^ kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaluḷ eṉṟavāṟu.

`valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi' ((eḻu//409)) eṉpataṉuḷ valleḻuttu+ toṭarmoḻiyum valleḻuttu varumvaḻiyum iṭam; īṟṟu valleḻuttu+ paṟṟukkōṭu.

utāraṇam: cekkukkaṇi cukkukkōṭu eṉavarum. ivi arimāttiriyiṉ+ kuṟukiyavāṟu ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttu uṇarka. iṭaṉumeṉavē itu ciṟupāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. (4)

TE38n

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāyta+ puḷḷi
yuyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē āytam orumoḻiyuḷ varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āytappuḷḷi --- āytamākiya oṟṟu, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micittu --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyirōṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟiṉ mēliṭattatāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

vallavāṟaṉ micitteṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭu+ puḷḷi yeṉṟataṉāṉum `āyta+ toṭarmoḻi' ((eḻu//406)) eṉa mēṟkūṟutalāṉum uyireṉṟatu īṇṭu+ perumpāṉmiyum^ kuṟṟukaramēyām. ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi yuyirkaḷiyum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: eḵku kaḵcu kaḵṭu kaḵtu kaḵpu kaḵṟu aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉavarum. kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉpaṉavaṟṟi meypiṟitākiya puṇarcci eṉṟataṉāṉum īṇṭu+ puḷḷiyeṉṟataṉāṉum āytamum meyyāyiṟṟu, aḵkāmi veḵkāmi aḵki veḵki aḵkam eṉa+ piṟavuyirkaḷōṭum vantatu. kaḵciyāteṉa+ tirintatuvum^ kuṟṟiyalukarattōṭu puṇarntatām. (5)

TE39n

īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉu micimi tōṉṟum.

iḵtu avvāytam puṇarmoḻiyakattum varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īṟiyaṉ maruṅkiṉum --- nilimoḻiyīṟu varumoḻi mutaloṭu puṇarntu naṭakkum iṭattum, icimi tōṉṟum --- ataṉ arimāttiriyē icikkum^ taṉmi tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu muḵṭītu eṉavarum. ivvīṟu iyalumāṟu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ+ peṟutum. īṇṭum iṭam kuṟṟeḻuttiṉmēl varum valleḻuttu. (6)

TE40n

uruviṉ um iciyiṉ um aruki+ tōṉṟu
moḻikkuṟippu ellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā
v-āytam aḵkā+ kāli y-āṉa.

iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ+ ceyyuḷiyali nōkki āytattiṟku eytiyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uruviṉum iciyiṉum aruki+ tōṉṟum^ kuṟippumoḻiyum --- niṟattiṉkaṇṇum ōciyiṉkaṇṇum^ ciṟupāṉmi āytantōṉṟum poruḷ kuṟittaliyuṭiya collum, ellāmoḻiyum --- aviyoḻinta ellā moḻikaḷum, eḻuttiṉiyalā --- oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷpōla arimāttiriyiṉkaṇṇum ciṟupāṉmi mikkum naṭantu, āytam aḵkā+ kāliyāṉa --- āytam^ curuṅkāta iṭattāṉa coṟkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, īṇṭu ārāycciyiṉṟēṉum^ ceyyuḷiyaliṉ+ kūṟum `oṟṟaḷapeṭuppiṉu maṟṟeṉamoḻipa` ((ceyyuḷiyal//18)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu+ `kaṇṇṭaṇṇeṉa+ kaṇṭuṅkēṭṭum' eṉpuḻi+ caṇṇṇeṉpatu cīrnili yeytiṉāṟpōlak `kaḵḵṟeṉṉuṅkallatarattam' eṉa niṟattiṉkaṇṇum `cuḵḵṟeṉṉum^ taṇṭōṭṭu+ peṇṇi' eṉa iciyiṉkaṇṇum vanta āytam orumāttiripeṟṟu+ cīrnili yeytuṅkāl, āṇṭu+ peṟukiṉṟa orumāttirikku īṇṭu etiratupōṟṟi viti kūṟiṉār, āytam atikārappaṭṭami kaṇṭu. `eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kiyarā yiṉṉuyir --- veḵḵ kuvārkkilli vīṭu' eṉṟu ēṉiyiṭattum vantaṉa. oṟṟaḷapeṭukkumāṟu ivvatikārattu+ kūṟiṟṟilar, aḵtu uyiraḷapeṭipōla+ cīrniliyeytutalum aciniliyāntaṉmiyu muṭiyavāy+ ceyyuṭkē varutaliṉ. itaṉāṉē oṟṟaḷapeṭiyum oru māttiri peṟumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

eḻuttiṉeṉṟa iṉ uvamapporuḷ. iyalāveṉṟatu ceyyāveṉṉum viṉiyeccam.

ivvāṟaṉṟi ikkuṟippuccoṟkaḷ āytam iraṇṭiṭṭu eḻutappaṭāveṉṟu poruḷkūṟiṉ+ ceyyuḷiyalōṭu māṟupaṭṭu māṟukoḷa+kūṟal eṉṉum^ kuṟṟam^ taṅkum eṉṟu uṇarka. (7)

TE41n

kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikku
neṭṭeḻuttu impa rottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.

iḵtu etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi+ ceyyuḷiyalinōkki `nīṭṭam vēṇṭiṉ' ((eḻu//6)) eṉa muṟkūṟiya aḷapeṭiyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṉṟici moḻivayiṉ niṉṟu ici niṟikkum --- aḷapeṭuttu+ kūṟākkāl+ kuṉṟuvatāṉa ōciyiyuṭiya avvaḷapeṭi+ coṟkaṇṇē niṉṟu avvōciyi niṟikkum, avi yāviyōveṉiṉ, neṭṭeḻuttimpar otta kuṟṟeḻuttē --- neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṉṉāka+ tamakku iṉamotta kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉavarum. kuṉṟici moḻi eṉṟataṟku icikuṉṟumoḻi eṉṟumām. iṉamottalāvatu piṟappum puṇarcciyum ōciyum vaṭivum ottal. īṇṭu moḻiyeṉṟatu `aḷapeṭiyacinili' ((ceyyuḷiyal//17)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyal+ cūttirattu eṭṭu iyaṟcīriṉ pāṟpaṭukiṉṟa eṇvaki aḷapeṭiccoṟkaḷiyum. avi āa kaṭāa āaḻi paṭāaki āaṅku āavatu pukāarttu virāayatu eṉpaṉavām. kaṭṭaḷikoḷḷā āciriyar ivaṟṟi+ taṉinili mutaṉili iṭinili iṟutinili yeṉṟum aṭakkupa. iṉi moḻiyeṉṟataṟku+ taṉinili ēḻaṉiyumē koḷḷiṉ, oḻinta iyaṟcīrppāṟpaṭum aḷapeṭi kōṭaṟku iṭamiṉmi uṇarka. (8)

TE42n

aiau veṉṉu māyī reḻuttiṟ
kikara vukara miciniṟi vākum.

iḵtu ottakuṟṟeḻuttu illātaṉa aḷapeṭukkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

ituvum etiratu pōṟṟal.

itaṉ poruḷ: aiau eṉṉum āyīreḻuttiṟku --- tamakku iṉamillāta aikāra aukārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum avviraṇṭeḻuttiṟku, ikara ukaram iciniṟivākum --- īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷi+ cārttikkūṟa, avi a+ kuṉṟicimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟu ōciyi niṟippaṉavām eṉṟavāṟu.

aii auu eṉa niraṉiṟiyāka+ koḷka. ivaṟṟi muṟkūṟiya iyaṟcīreṭṭiṟkum ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu utāraṇam^ kāṭṭikkoḷka.

ittuṇiyum nūṉmarapiṉ oḻipu. (9)

TE43n

neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē yoreḻuttu orumoḻi.

iḵtu ōreḻuttorumoḻi uṇarttutal nutaliyavaṟṟuḷ neṭṭeḻuttākum moḻiyākkam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē --- neṭṭeḻuttākiya uyirkaḷēḻum, ōreḻuttorumoḻi --- ōreḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟṟummi tokuttu īṟṟaciyēkāram virittār.

utāraṇam: ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉavarum. aukāram uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārātu. ū eṉpatu taci. iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum viti. kā tī pū cē ti kō ku eṉavarum. ivi tammiyuṇaraniṉṟa vaḻi eḻuttām. iṭiniṉṟu poruḷuṇarttiyavaḻi+ collām. neṭṭeḻuttēṟiyamey neṭṭeḻuttāyum^ kuṟṟeḻuttēṟiyamey kuṟṟeḻuttāyum niṟṟalēyaṉṟi meykku neṭumiyum^ kuṟumiyum iṉmi uṇarka. (10)

TE44n

kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu moḻiniṟi pilavē.

itu kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum moḻiyākā. avaṟṟuḷ+ cila moḻiyākumeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu aintum --- kuṟṟeḻuttākiya uyirintum, moḻiniṟipu ilavē --- tāmē niṟintu niṉṟu moḻiyātal ila; cila meyyōṭu kūṭi niṟintu niṉṟu moḻiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tu no eṉavarum. ivi uyirmeykkaṇṇallatu vārāmiyāṉum, uyirkkaṇṇum, ēḷi akaramum ekaramum, akkoṟṟaṉ epporuḷ eṉa+ taṉittu niṉṟu uṇarttalāṟṟātu iṭiccollāy+ peyari+ cārntu niṉṟu cuṭṭupporuḷum viṉāpporuḷum uṇarttutalāṉum `niṟipila' veṉṟār. muṟṟummi īṇṭu eccappaṭṭu niṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. (11)

TE45n

ōr eḻuttu oru moḻi y-īr eḻuttu oru moḻi
y-iraṇṭu* iṟantu* icikkum^ toṭarmoḻi y-uḷa+paṭa
mūṉṟu* ē moḻi-nili tōṉṟiya neṟi y-ē.

itu muṉṉar meymmayakkam uṭaṉilimayakkam^ kūṟalāṉum īṇṭu `neṭṭeḻuttēḻē' ((eḻu//43)) eṉpataṉāṉum eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu kūṟiṉār, ammoḻikku iccūttirattāṉ+ peyarum muṟiyum^ tokiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟār.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻi iraṇṭiṟantu icikkum^ toṭarmoḻi uḷappaṭa --- ōreḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākum orumoḻiyum iraṇṭaṉi iṟantu palavāṟṟāṉ icikkum^ toṭarmoḻiyuṭaṉē kūṭa, moḻinili mūṉṟē --- moḻikaḷiṉ nilimi mūṉṟēyām. tōṉṟiya neṟiyē --- avi tōṉṟiya vaḻakkuneṟikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ā kā nā ōreḻuttorumoḻi, maṇi varaku koṟṟaṉ īreḻuttorumoḻi, kuravu aravu mūveḻuttorumoḻi, kaṇaviri nāleḻuttorumoḻi, akattiyaṉār aiyeḻuttorumoḻi, tirucciṟṟampalam āṟeḻuttorumoḻi, perumpaṟṟappuliyūr ēḻeḻuttorumoḻi.

ōreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ toṭarmoḻiyu meṉṉātu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum ōtiṉār, cila pala eṉṉum^ tamiḻ vaḻakku nōkki.

āciriyar oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum eḻutteṉṟu koṇṭaṉarātaliṉ mā kā eṉa niṉṟa coṟkaḷ māl kāl eṉa oṟṟaṭuttuḻi oṟṟiṉāṉ vēṟu poruḷ tantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟi īreḻuttorumoḻiyeṉṟum, nāku varaku eṉṉum^ kuṟṟukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ+ kuṟṟukaraṅkaḷ colloṭukūṭi+ poruḷtantu niṟṟaliṉ ivaṟṟi īreḻuttorumoḻi mūveḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟum^ kōṭumeṉpārkku, āciriyar poruḷi+ karutātu māttiri kuṟintamipaṟṟi `uyirileḻuttu meṇṇappaṭā' ((ceyyuḷiyal//44)) `kuṟilēneṭilē kuṟiliṇi' ((ceyyuḷiyal//3)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyal+ cūttiraṅkaḷāl ivaṟṟi eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟā eṉṟu vilakkuvarātaliṉ, avaṟṟāl īṇṭu īreḻuttorumoḻiyum mūveḻuttorumoḻiyum^ koḷḷiṉ, māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉṉum^ kuṟṟam^ taṅkumeṉṟu maṟukka.

iṉi `neṭṭeḻuttu ēḻē yōreḻuttu orumoḻi' ((eḻu//43)) `kuṟṟeḻuttu aintu moḻiniṟi pilavē' ((eḻu//44)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ meykku+ kuṟumi neṭumi yiṉmiyāṉ uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya neṭilum^ kuṟilumē moḻiyāmeṉṟu kūṟi, mīṭṭum ataṉiyē iccūttirattāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻi yeṉṟeṭuttu ataṉōṭē īreḻuttiyum iraṇṭiṟantataṉiyum^ kūṭṭi moḻiyāka+ kōṭaliṉ, oṟṟiṉi+ kūṭṭi eḻuttāka+ kōṭal āciriyarkku+ karuttaṉmiyuṇarka. aṉṟiyum `moḻippaṭutticippiṉum' ((eḻu//53)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉum uṇarka. `akara mutal ṉakaraviṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa' ((eḻu//1)) eṉa oṟṟiṉiyum eḻutteṉṟatu eḻuttiṉ taṉmi kūṟiṟṟu. īṇṭu moḻiyāntaṉmi kūṟiṟṟu. (12)

TE46n

meyyi ṉiyakka makaramoṭu civaṇum.

itu taṉimeykaḷi akaram iyakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meyyiṉiyakkam --- taṉimeykaḷiṉatu naṭappu, akaramoṭu civaṇum --- akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē oruvaṉ taṉimeykaḷi nāvāṉ+ karuttupporuḷākiya uruvāka iyakkum iyakkamum mūvakiyāṉ+ kāṭci+ poruḷākiya vaṭivāka iyakkum iyakkamum akarattōṭu porunti naṭakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `valleḻuttu eṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa' ((eḻu//19)) `kakāra ṅakāra mutaṉā vaṇṇam' ((eḻu//89)) eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa nāvāl iyakkiyavāṟu kāṇka. eḻuti+ kāṭṭumiṭattu+ kakaram mutaliyaṉa uyirpeṟṟu niṉṟa vaṭivāka eḻuti+ piṉṉar+ taṉimeyyākkutaṟku+ puḷḷiyiṭṭu+ kāṭṭukiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ vaṭivi iyakkumiṭattum akaram^ kalantu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.

iṅṅaṉam meykkaṇ akaram^ kalantu niṟkumāṟu kūṟiṉāṟpōla+ patiṉōruyirkkaṇṇum akaram^ kalantu niṟkumeṉpatu āciriyar kūṟārāyiṉār, annilimi tamakkē pulappaṭuttalāṉum piṟarkku ivvāṟu uṇarttutal aritākalāṉumeṉṟu uṇarka. iṟivaṉ iyaṅkutiṇikkaṇṇum nilittiṇikkaṇṇum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāy niṟkumāṟu ellārkkum oppa muṭintāṟpōla akaramum uyirkkaṇṇum^ taṉimeykkaṇṇum^ kalantu avaṟṟiṉ taṉmiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu cāṉṟōrkkellām oppamuṭintatu. `akaramutala' eṉṉum^ kuṟaḷāṉ akaramākiya mutaliyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷellām, atupōla iṟivaṉākiya mutaliyuṭittu ulakameṉa vaḷḷuvaṉār uvami kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉuṅ, kaṇṇaṉ eḻuttukkaḷil akaramākiṉṟēṉ yāṉēyeṉa+ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum piṟanūlkaḷāṉum uṇarka.

itaṉāṉ uṇmittaṉmiyum^ ciṟitu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. itaṉi nūṉmarapiṉ+ kūṟātu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār, `valleḻutteṉpa ka ca ṭa ta pa ṟa'(eḻu//19) eṉṟa iṭattu+ tāṉ iṭiniṉṟu oṟṟeṉpatōr poruḷi uṇartti moḻiyāntaṉmi eytiniṟṟaliṉ. (13)

TE47n

tammiyal kiḷappi ṉellā veḻuttu
meynnili mayakka māṉa milli.

itu muṉṉar meykkaṇ uyir niṉṟavāṟu kūṟi avvuyir meykkaṇ ēṟi uyirmeyyāy niṉṟakālattu ammeyyāṉ+ peyar peṟumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellā eḻuttum --- paṉṉīruyirum, meynnili tam iyal mayakkam^ kiḷappiṉ --- meyyiṉ taṉmiyilē tammuṭiya taṉmi mayaṅkiṟṟāka+ peyar kūṟiṉ, māṉamilli --- kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

meyyiṉ taṉmiyāvatu vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimi; tammiyalāvatu uyirttaṉmi; eṉṟatu valleḻuttu melleḻuttu iṭiyeḻutteṉa uyirmeykkum peyariṭṭāḷutal kūṟiṟṟu. avi `valleḻuttu iyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//19)) eṉavum, `melleḻuttu iyiyi ṉiṟutiyō ṭuṟaḻum ((eḻu//20)) eṉavum, `iṭiyeḻuttu eṉpa yaralavaḻaḷa' ((eḻu//21)) eṉavum piṟāṇṭum āḷpa. eḻutti vaṉmi meṉmi iṭimiyeṉa vicēṭitta ciṟappāṉ ippeyar kūṟiṉār.

iḵtaṉṟi+ patiṉeṭṭumeyyiṉ taṉmi kūṟumiṭattu meymmayakkam^ kūṟiya vakiyāṉaṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu mayaṅkumeṉṟu kūṟi, `avaṟṟuḷ laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//24)) eṉpataṉi+ kāṭṭil aḵtu irumoḻikkaṇṇateṉa maṟukka. (14)

TE48n

yaraḻa veṉṉu mūṉṟu moṟṟa+
kacatapa ṅañanama vīroṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yaraḻa eṉṉum mūṉṟum oṟṟa --- yaraḻa veṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu puḷḷiyum oṟṟāyniṟpa, kacatapaṅañanama īroṟṟākum --- kacatapakkaḷum ṅañanamakkaḷum vantu īroṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vēykka vāycci pāyttal vāyppu eṉavum, pīrkku nērcci vārttal ārppu eṉavum, vāḻkki tāḻcci tāḻttal tāḻppu eṉavum, kāyṅkaṉi tēyñcatu kāyntaṉam kāympuṟam, eṉavum, nērṅkal nērñcili nērntili nērmpuṟam eṉavum varum. ḻakārattiṟku vāḻntaṉam ikkālattu nakaravoṟṟu varum. ēṉiya mūṉṟum ikkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉiṉ uṇarka.

iṉi+ tāḻṅkuli tāḻñciṉi tāḻntiraḷ vīḻmpaṭi eṉa akkālattu vaḻaṅkumeṉṟu ittoki+ coṟkaḷ kāṭṭalum oṉṟu. uriyāciriyarum irumoḻikkaṇ+ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟku avvīṟukaṭōṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷ piṉṉar vēṇṭāmi uṇarka. iḵtu īroṟṟuṭaṉiliyātaliṉ īṇṭu vittār.

iṉi neṭiṟkīḻēyaṉṟi+ palaveḻuttum^ toṭarntu niṉṟataṉ piṉṉum īroṟṟu varutal koḷka. avi vēntarkku aṉṉāykku eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavām. (15)

TE49n

avaṟṟuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram^ kuṟṟoṟ ṟākā.

iḵtu eytiyatu oru maruṅku maṟuttal kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ, rakāra ḻakāram --- rakāramum ḻakāramum, kuṟṟoṟṟākā --- kuṟiṟkīḻ oṟṟākā; neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟām; kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyām eṉṟavāṟu.

kīḻeṉṉum urupu tokuttu+ kūṟiṉār. ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇamiṉṟu.

utāraṇam: kār vīḻ eṉa neṭiṟkīḻ oṟṟāy vantaṉa. karu maḻu eṉa+ kuṟiṟkīḻ uyirmeyyāy vantaṉa. ivaṟṟi vilakkavē, yakaram poy eṉavum noy eṉavum iraṇṭiṭattum oṟṟāy varutal peṟṟām. pukar pukaḻ pulavar eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉavōveṉiṉ, moḻikku mutalām eḻuttiṉi+ colvaṉavaṟṟiṟkē īṇṭu ārāycciyātalāl avi vēṇṭiyavāṟē varumeṉṟu uṇarka. aṉṟiyum^ kuṟṟoṟṟeṉṟē cūttirañceytaliṉ+ kuṟiliṇi yoṟṟiṉi+ kāṭṭi+ kaṭāvalākāmi uṇarka. itu variyaṟiyiṉṟi uyirmeyyōṭu taṉimey mayaṅkuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ+ cila voṟṟiṟku variyaṟi īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu. (16)

TE50n

kuṟumiyu neṭumiyu maḷaviṉ+ kōṭaliṉ+
ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻuttu iyala.

iḵtu `aḷapu* iṟantu* uyirttal um' ((eḻu//33)) eṉṉum^ ceyyuḷiyali nōkkiya nūṉmarapiṉ+ cūttirattiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭiyāy ataṉkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟukiṉṟatu; eṉṉi? uyirum uyirmeyyum aḷapiṟantu icikkuṅkāl+ kuṟilō neṭilō icippateṉa māṇākkarkku nikaḻvatōr aiyam aṟuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟumiyum neṭumiyum --- eḻuttukkaḷatu kuṟiyataṉmiyum neṭiyataṉmiyum, aḷaviṟkōṭaliṉ --- māttiriyeṉṉum uṟuppiṉi+ cevi karuviyāka aḷakkiṉṟa aḷavu toḻilālē ceyyuṭku+ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ, toṭarmoḻiyellām --- ammāttiri tammuḷ+ ṭoṭarntu niṟkiṉṟa coṟkaḷellām, neṭṭeḻuttiyala --- neṭṭeḻuttu māttiri mikku naṭakkumpaṭiyāka+ toṭarnta collām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `varuvarkolvayaṅkiḻāay' ((kali//11)) eṉavum, `kaṭiyavēkaṉaṅkuḻāay' (kali11) eṉavum^ kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām neṭṭeḻuttiṉi māttiri mikuttaṟku+ kūṭiyavāṟu uṇarka. ēṉi+ ceyyaṭkaḷiyum ivvāṟē kāṇka.

eṉavē, māttiri aḷakkuṅkāl neṭṭeḻuttē māttiri peṟṟu mikku niṟkum eṉṟamiyāṉ, etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttipaṟṟi+ ceyyuḷiyali nōkki+ kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār, neṭṭeḻuttu iraṇṭu māttiriyiṉ ikantuvarumeṉpatu aṟivittaṟku.

aḷapeṉṟu māttiriyi+ kūṟātu aḷaveṉa+ cūttiram^ ceytamiyāṉ aḷavu toḻiṉmēṉiṉṟatu. atu ceyyuḷiyaluḷ `māttiriyaḷavum' ((ceyyuḷiyal//2)) eṉpataṉāṉum uṇarka. iyalaveṉṟataṉi+ ceyaveṉeccamākki+ paṭuttalōciyāṉ+ kūṟuka.

iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. `kuraṅṅkuḷi+ polinta koycuvaṟ puravi' ((akam//4)) eṉṟa kuṟuñcīrvaṇṇattiṟku uriya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām iṭiyiṉiṉṟa oṟṟeḻutti māttiri mikuttaṟku+ kūṭiniṉṟavāṟu uṇarka. eṉavē, kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷellām oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷōṭum neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷōṭum^ kūṭi avaṟṟiyē ōcimikuttu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē oṟṟici nīṭalumeṉṟa oṟṟici nīḷuṅkāl+ kuṟṟeḻuttāy nīḷumeṉṟār. iṉi uriyāciriyar pukar pukaḻ eṉa+ kuṟiliṇikkīḻ rakāra ḻakāraṅkaḷ vanta toṭarmoḻikaḷellām^ tār tāḻ eṉṟāṟpōla ōciyottu neṭṭeḻuttiṉ taṉmiyavām eṉṟārāleṉiṉ, pukar pukaḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi neṭṭeḻutteṉṟē evviṭattum āḷāmiyāṉum neṭṭeḻuttāka+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattāl orupayaṉ koḷḷāmiyāṉum^ ceyyaḷiyaluḷ ivaṟṟi+ kuṟiliṇi oṟṟaṭutta niriyaciyākavum^ tār tāḻ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi neṭṭeḻuttu oṟṟaṭutta nēraciyākavum^ kōṭalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmi uṇarka. (17)

TE51n

ceyyu ḷiṟuti+ pōli moḻivayi
ṉakāra makāra mīroṟ ṟākum.

itu ceyyuṭkaṇ īroṟṟilakkaṇamāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyuṭpōli moḻi iṟutivayiṉ --- ceyyuṭkaṇ+ pōlumeṉṉum^ colliṉ iṟutikkaṇ, ṉakāram makāram īroṟṟākum --- ṉakāramum makāramum vantu īroṟṟu uṭaṉiliyāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `annūli munnūlā+ koḷvāṉum pōṉm' `citiyum^ kalatti+ payiṉāṉ+ ^tirutti+ --- tici y-aṟi mīkāṉ um pōṉm' ((pari//10-55)) eṉa varum. pōlum eṉṉum^ ceyyumeṉṉum muṟṟu īṟṟumiciyukaram meyyoḻittu+ keṭṭu lakāram^ tirintu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṟutiyil muṟṟu iṭiyiṉ+ peyareccamākiya uvamavurupu. īṇṭu muṟṟeṉpār iṟutimoḻi eṉṟār. (18)

TE52n

ṉakāri muṉṉar makāram^ kuṟukum.

iḵtu ariyaḷapu kuṟukumeṉṟa makārattiṟku+ kuṟukum iṭam ituveṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāri muṉṉar makāram^ kuṟukum --- muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattiṉ muṉṉar vanta makaram^ taṉ arimāttiriyiṉ+ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pōṉm eṉa muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉām. ṉakāriyeṉa iṭiccol īṟutirintu niṉṟatu.

iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ ṇakāravoṟṟiṉ muṉṉum makāram^ kuṟukutal koḷka. `maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇm' eṉa varum. (19)

TE53n

moḻippaṭuttu icippiṉum^ terintuvē ṟicippiṉu
meḻuttiya ṟiriyā veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum īṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷōṭu kūṭṭi eṇṇappaṭṭu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ eṇṇappaṭātu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: terintu --- oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷtaru nilimiyi ārāyntu, moḻippaṭuttu icippiṉum --- collāka+ cērttu+ colliṉum, vēṟu icippiṉum --- ceyyuḷiyaluḷ oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum poruḷ tarumēṉum māttiri kuṟintu niṟkum nilimiyi nōkki eḻutteṇṇappaṭāveṉṟu āṇṭikku vēṟāka+ kūṟiṉum, eḻuttiyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avviraṇṭiṭattum arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum oṟṟum^ kuṟṟukaramum muṟkūṟiya eḻuttām^ taṉmi tiriyāveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉāṉ oṟṟum āytamum^ kuṟṟukaramum eḻuttāki niṉṟu poruḷ tantum, eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāveṉpatu kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. terintu vēṟicittal kuṟṟukarattiṟku iṉṟātaliṉ ēṟpuḻikkōṭalāṉ oṟṟiṟkum āytattiṟkum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: al il uṇ eṇ ol eṉavum, kal vil muḷ cel col eṉavum, āl īr ūr ēr ōr eṉavum, kāl cīr cūl tēṉ kōṉ eṉavum uyirum uyirmeyyumākiya kuṟṟeḻuttiyum neṭṭeḻuttiyum oṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ aṭuttuniṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. kaṭam kaṭām uṭiyāṉ tiruvārūr akattiyaṉār eṉa īreḻuttiyum mūveḻuttiyum nāleḻuttiyum aiyeḻuttiyum iṟutiyilum iṭiyilum oṟṟaṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷ tantavāṟu kāṇka. eḵku teḷku kokku kuraṅku eṉpaṉavum eḻutteṇṇavum alakiṭavum peṟāta kuṟṟukaram aṭuttu niṉṟu poruḷtantavāṟu kāṇka. `uyiri leḻuttu meṇṇappaṭāa --- uyirttiṟa miyakka miṉmi yāṉa' ((ceyyuḷiyal//44)) eṉpatu eḻuttu eṇṇappeṟāmikku viti.

iṉi iccūttirattiṟku eḻuttukkaḷi+ collākki+ kūṟiṉum piṟitāka+ kūṟiṉum māttiri tiriyāteṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, akaram eṉpuḻiyum a eṉpuḻiyum ālam eṉpuḻiyum ā eṉpuḻiyum kakaram eṉpuḻiyum^ ka eṉpuḻiyum^ kālam eṉpuḻiyum^ kā eṉpuḻiyum ōci ottu niṟkumeṉṟāl, atu muṉṉar+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāṉ+ peṟappaṭutaliṉ+ payaṉil kūṟṟāmeṉka. (20)

TE54n

akara ikara mikāra mākum.

itu cilaveḻuttukkaḷ kūṭi+ cilaveḻuttukkaḷpōla icikkumeṉa eḻuttuppōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ikaram aikāram ākum --- akaramum ikaramum^ kūṭṭi+ colla aikārampōla icikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.

utāraṇam: aivaṉam aivaṉam eṉavarum. ākumeṉṟataṉāl iḵtu ilakkaṇamaṉṟāyiṟṟu. (21)

TE55n

akara ukara mukāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ukaram aukāram ākum --- akaramum ukaramum kūṭṭiccolla aukārampōla icikkum, atu koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu. pōla eṉṟatu tokkatu.

utāraṇam: auvi auvi eṉavarum. (22)

TE56n

akarattu impar yakara+ puḷḷiyu
miye ṉeṭuñciṉi meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akarattimpar yakarappuḷḷiyum --- akarattiṉpiṉ ikaramēyaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷi vantālum, aiyeṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ vaṭivupeṟa+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aivaṉam ayvaṉam eṉavarum. meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ akarattiṉpiṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟi vakarappuḷḷiyum aukārampōla varumeṉṟu koḷka. auvi avvi eṉavarum. (23)

TE57n

ōraḷa pāku miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
tērum^ kāli moḻivayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu atikārattāṉ aikārattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum etiratu pōṟṟaleṉpataṉātal+ ceyyuḷiyalinōkki māttiri+ curukkam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: moḻivayiṉāṉa --- oru colliṭattē niṉṟa aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ, tēruṅkāli --- ārāyumiṭattu, ōraḷapākum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē --- orumāttiriyāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummiyāṉ iraṇṭumāttiri peṟutalē valiyuṭittāyiṟṟu. iṭaṉumeṉṟatu orucolliṉ mutaliṭikaṭiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum^ kuṟukum, atu ceyyuṭkaṇ ōci iṭarppaṭṭolikkumiṭattu+ kuṟukumeṉṟataṟku, uriyiṟkōṭalāl aikāram mutaliṭikaṭiyeṉṉum mūṉṟiṭattum^ kuṟukum, aukāram mutaṟkaṇ kuṟukumeṉakkoḷka.

utāraṇam: aippaci kippi iṭiyaṉ kuvaḷi eṉa varum. `aṭippiyāykōṟā' eṉavum, `puṉiyiḷaṅkoṅkiyāy varum' eṉavum piṟavāṟum varuvaṉa ceyyuḷiyaluḷ+ kāṇka. auvi kuvi eṉavarum. aukāram `kuvi nīrvēlikūṟṟu' ((veṇpā//23)) eṉa+ toṭinōkki+ kuṟukiṉavāṟum^ kāṇka. tēruṅkāli yeṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻiyum^ kuṟukum. ki pi eṉavarum. (24)

TE58n

ikara yakara miṟuti viravum.

ituvum pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ikarayakaram iṟutiviravum --- ikaramum yakaramum orumoḻiyiṉ iṟutikkaṇ ōci viravivarum, avvikāram^ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

nāy nāi eṉavarum. (25)

TE59n

paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum.

itu mēl eḻuttiṉāṉ moḻiyāmāṟu uṇartti ammoḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iviyeṉpatu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉṉīruyirum --- paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum, moḻimutal ākum --- moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṭi āṭi ili īyam uḷi ūrti eḻu ēṇi aivaṉam oḷi ōṭam auviyam eṉavarum. (26)

TE60n

uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā.

iḵtu uyirmey moḻikku mutalām eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmeyyallaṉa moḻimutal ākā --- uyirōṭukūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, uyirōṭukūṭiya meykaḷē moḻikku mutalāvaṉa eṉṟavāṟām. īṇṭu uyirmeyyeṉṟatu vēṟṟuminayaṅkarutiṟṟu. oṟṟuminayam^ karutiṉ mēliccūttirattu uyirōṭuṅkūṭi āmeṉṟal payaṉiṉṟām. (27)

TE61n

ka ta na pa ma ~eṉum ā ~aintu* eḻuttu* um*
ellā ~uyiroṭu* um^ cellumār mutal ē.

itu mēl+ potuvakiyāṉ eytuvitta irunūṟṟorupattāṟu eḻuttukkaḷi+ ciṟappuvakiyāṉ variyaṟuttu eytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: katanapama eṉum āvinteḻuttum --- katanapama eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avvintu taṉimeyyum, ellā uyiroṭum^ cellumār mutalē --- paṉṉiraṇṭu uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟku+ cellum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kali kār kiḷi kīri kuṭi kūṭu keṇṭi kēḻal kiti koṇṭal kōṭi kuvi eṉavum, tanti tāy titti tīmi tuṇi tūṇi teṟṟi tēṉ tiyal toṇṭi tōṭu tuvi eṉavum, nantu nāri nilam nīlam nukam nūl neytal nēmi nivaḷam nocci nōkkam nuvi eṉavum, paṭi pāl piṭi pīṭu pukaḻ pūmi peṭi pēṭi pital poṉ pōtu puvam eṉavum, maṭi māli miṭaṟu mīḷi mukam mūppu melivu mēṉi miyal moḻi mōtti muval eṉavum varum. (28)

TE62n

cakara+ kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
a ai auveṉu mūṉṟalam^ kaṭiyē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cakara+ kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē --- cakaramākiya taṉimeyyum muṟkūṟiyavipōla ellā uyirōṭum^ kūṭi moḻikku mutalām, a ai au eṉum mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē --- akara aikārukārameṉṟu collappaṭṭa mūṉṟu uyirum allāta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāntu ciṟṟil cīṟṟam curi cūral cekku cēval col cōṟu eṉavarum. caṭṭi cakaṭam camaḻppu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa `kaṭicollilli' ((eccaviyal//56)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. ciyam curiyam eṉpavaṟṟi vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (29)

TE63n

uū oō veṉṉu nāṉkuyir
va eṉ ṉeḻuttoṭu varuta lilli.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uū oō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir --- u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutalilli --- va eṉṟu collappaṭum^ taṉimeyyeḻuttōṭu kūṭi moḻikkumutalāy varutalilli eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, oḻintaṉa moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: vaḷi vāḷi viḷari vīṭu veḷḷi vēṭki viyam vuvutal eṉavarum (30)

TE64n

ā e, o eṉu mūvuyir ñakārattu uriya.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ā e o eṉum mūvuyir --- ā e o eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum mūṉṟu uyirum, ñakārattu uriya --- ñakāra oṟṟoṭu kūṭi moḻikku mutalātaṟku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉiya uriyavalla eṉpatām.

utāraṇam: ñālam ñeṇṭu ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.

`ñamalitantamaṉavuccūluṭumpu' ((perumpāṇ//132)) eṉpatu ticiccol. ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iḻivaḻakku. (31)

TE65n

āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu --- ākārattōṭu kūṭiyallatu yakaravoṟṟu moḻikku mutalākātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉi yāṭu yāmam eṉavarum.

yavaṉar yutti yūpam yōkam yuvaṉam eṉpaṉa vaṭacolleṉa maṟukka. (32)

TE66n

mutalā vēṉa tampeyar mutalum.

itu moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalāvum --- moḻikku mutalākā eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum, ēṉavum --- moḻikku mutalā meṉṟa oṉpatu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum, tampeyar mutalum --- tattampeyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

mutalāvum ēṉavum eṉṟa ummikaḷ tokkuniṉṟaṉa.

utāraṇam: ṅakaramum ṭakaramum ṇakaramum rakaramum lakaramum ḻakaramum ḷakaramum ṟakaramum ṉakaramum eṉa moḻikku mutalākāta oṉpatum mutalāmāṟu, ṅakkaḷintār ṭapperitu ṇannaṉṟu eṉavarum. ivvāṟē ēṉiyavaṟṟiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi ēṉaveṉṟataṉāṉ kakkaḷintār tapperitu akkuṟitu āneṭitu eṉa moḻikkumutalāmavaṟṟiyum^ tampeyar kūṟutaṟku mutalāmāṟu oṭṭikkoḷka. variyaṟukkappaṭṭu moḻikku mutalākātu niṉṟa meykkum ivvitikoḷka. avi cakarattu mūṉṟum vakarattu nāṉkum ñakarattu oṉpatum yakarattu+ patiṉoṉṟumām. (33)

TE67n

kuṟṟiya lukara muṟippeyar maruṅki
ṉoṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷi moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟi muṟiyē kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟippeyar maruṅkiṉ --- kuṟṟiyalukaramāṉatu muṉṉilimuṟi+ peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum --- taṉimeyyāy niṉṟa nakarattumēṉiṉṟa nakarattōṭukūṭi moḻikku mutalām eṉṟavāṟu.

nunti eṉavarum. itaṉāṉē muṟippeyar iṭamum nakaram paṟṟukkōṭumāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyppiṉṉar niṉṟatēṉum oṟṟumi nayattāṉ moḻikku mutaleṉṟār. itu ceyyuḷiyalinōkki+ kūṟiyatāyiṟṟu. (34)

TE68n

muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāa
tappeyar maruṅki ṉiliyiya lāṉa.

itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: appeyarmaruṅkiṉ niliyiyalāṉa --- ammuṟippeyariṭattē niṟṟalilakkaṇamāṉa kuṟṟiyalukaram, muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭāatu --- itaḻkuvittu+ kūṟumvaḻi varum muṟṟukarattōṭu avviṭattu+ kuṟṟukaram poruḷ vēṟupaṭumāṟupōla īṇṭu+ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭu nillātu eṉṟavāṟu.

kātu kaṭṭu kattu marukku teruṭṭu eṉpaṉa muṟṟukaramum kuṟṟukaramumāy+ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭuniṉṟāṟpōla tantiyeṉṟu itaḻkuvittu muṟṟakkūṟiyaviṭattum itaḻkuviyāmal+ kuṟiyakkūṟiyaviṭattum oru poruḷē tantavāṟu kāṇka. nuntāyeṉpatōveṉiṉ aḵtu itaḻkuvittē kūṟavēṇṭutaliṉ+ kuṟṟukaramaṉṟu. iyaleṉṟataṉāṉ iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum iraṇṭiṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉṟeṉṟu koḷka. itaṉāṉē moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu+ toṇṇūṟṟunāṉkeṉṟu uṇarka. (35)

TE69n

uyiru veñciya viṟuti yākum.

iḵtu uyirmoḻikku īṟāmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyir au eñciya iṟutiyākum --- uyirkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintaṉavellām moḻikku īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, aukāravuyir īṟākātāyiṟṟu. iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.

ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉa ivitāmē īṟāyiṉa. āa īi ūu ēe ōo eṉa+ kuṟilintum aḷapeṭikkaṇ īṟāyiṉa. kā tī pū cē ki kō eṉavum, viḷa kiḷi maḻu eṉavum varum. ekara okaram mēlē vilakkupa. `aḷapeṭi mikūu mikara viṟupeyar' ((col//127)) eṉpārātaliṉ, aḷapeṭippiṉ vanta kuṟṟeḻuttum^ koḷvar āciriyareṉṟu uṇarka. neṭṭeḻuttēḻum mutaṉ moḻiyāmeṉṉum^ tuṇiyē muṉṉuṇarttutaliṉ īṇṭu avi īṟāmeṉṟum uṇarttiṉār. (36)

TE70n

kavavō ṭiyiyi ṉuvu mākum.

iḵtu īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻiyāmeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: auvum --- muṉ īṟākāteṉṟa aukāramum, kavavōṭu iyiyiṉ ākum --- kakaravakarattōṭu iyinta vaḻi īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ku vu eṉavarum. eṉavē, oḻinta uyirellām^ tāmē niṉṟum patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷōṭum^ kūṭi niṉṟum īṟātal itaṉāṉ+ peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē aukāram ēṉi meykkaṇ vārāteṉa vilakkutalum peṟṟām. uyir ṅakarattōṭu kūṭi moḻikku īṟāmeṉpatu itaṉāl eytiṟṟēṉum atu moḻikku īṟākāmi tantu puṇarnturittalāṉ uṇarka. itu variyaṟi kūṟiṟṟu. (37)

TE71n

e eṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.

iḵtu ekaram^ tāṉēniṉṟa vaḻiyaṉṟi meyyōṭu kūṭiṉāl īṟākāteṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: e eṉa varum uyir mey īṟākātu --- e eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum uyir tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi yāṇṭum meykaḷōṭu iyintu īṟākātu eṉṟavāṟu. (38)

TE72n

ovvu maṟṟē navvalam^ kaṭiyē.

itu vilakkum variyaṟiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ovvum aṟṟu --- okaramum muṉcoṉṉa ekarampōla+ tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meykaḷōṭu iyintu īṟākātu, navvalaṅkaṭiyē --- nakaravoṟṟoṭu allāta iṭattil eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: no koṟṟā `noaliyaṉiṉṉāṭṭinī' eṉavarum. (39)

TE73n

ē ō eṉumuyir ñakārattu illi.

itu ciluyir cila uṭalōṭēṟi muṭiyāteṉa vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēō eṉum uyir ñakārattilli --- ēō eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa iraṇṭuyirum^ tāmēniṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭu niṉṟum īṟātalaṉṟi ñakārattōṭu īṟātalilli eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉiyuyirkaḷ ñakārattōṭu īṟāmeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: uriña uriñā uriñi uriñī uriñu uriñū ivi eccamum viṉippeyarum paṟṟivarum. aññi maññi ivi peyar. ēṉiyintum vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. uriñō eṉpatu `kaṭicollilli' eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. (40)

TE74n

u ū kāra navavoṭu navilā.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: u ūkāram --- ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ tāmē niṉṟum piṟameykaḷōṭuniṉṟum payilvataṉṟi, navavoṭu navilā --- nakara voṟṟōṭum vakaravoṟṟōṭum payilā eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ēṉiyuyirkaḷ nakaravakaraṅkaḷōṭu varumāyiṉa.

utāraṇam: nakaram poruna eṉa viṉippeyarākiyum, nā nī nē eṉa+ peyarākiyum, ni no nō eṉa viyaṅkōḷākiyum varum. poruni eṉṟum^ kāṭṭupa. vakaram uva vē eṉa viyaṅkōḷāyum, uvā cevvi vī vi eṉa+ peyarāyum varum. oruva oruvā oruvi oruvī auvi eṉṟum^ kāṭṭupa. īṇṭu vilakkāta ēṉi yuyirkaḷōṭu vanta nakaravakaraṅkaḷ akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉaveṉṟu kōṭum. ivvitiyāl iṉi navilāveṉṟataṉāṉē vakara vukaram katavu turavu kuvavu puṇarvu nukarvu novvu kavvu eṉa+ payiṉṟu varutalum^ koḷka. (41)

TE75n

uccakāram iru moḻikku urittē.

itu cakāra ukāram palacoṟku īṟāy vārātu irucoṟku īṟāmeṉṟu variyaṟi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uccakāram --- ukārattōṭu kūṭiya cakāram, irumoḻikku urittu --- iraṇṭu moḻikkē īṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. urittēyeṉṉum ēkāram moḻikkēyeṉa+ kūṭṭuka.

utāraṇam: ucu; iḵtu uḷuviṉpeyar. mucu; itu kuraṅkiṉuḷ orucāti. pacu eṉpatōveṉiṉ, aḵtu āriya+ citivu. kaccu kuccu eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukaram ēṟiya cakaram irumoḻikku īṟāmeṉavē ēṉi uyirkaḷ ēṟiya cakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāmarapiṟṟu. uca ucā vici cē kacci cō eṉa+ peyarāyum, tuñcu eñcā eñci mucī mucū eṉa eccamāyum varum. accō eṉa viyappāyum varum. iṉṉum ivi vaḻakkiṉkaṇ+ palavāmāṟum uṇarka. (42)

TE76n

uppa kāra moṉṟeṉa moḻipa
viruvayi ṉiliyum poruṭṭā kummē.

iḵtu orucolvariyaṟiyum aḵtu ōcivēṟṟumiyāl iruporuḷ tarumeṉavum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uppakāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa --- ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram orumoḻikkallatu paṉmoḻikku īṟākāteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar. iruvayiṉiliyum poruṭṭākummē --- atu tāṉ taṉviṉi piṟaviṉi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattum nilipeṟum poruṇmittām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tapu eṉavarum. itu paṭuttukkūṟa nī cāveṉa+ taṉviṉiyām. eṭuttukkūṟa nī oṉṟaṉi+ cāvappaṇṇeṉa+ piṟaviṉiyām. uppu kappu eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa kuṟṟukaram. ukarattōṭu kūṭiya pakaram oṉṟeṉavē ēṉiyuyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiya pakaram paṉmoḻikku īṟāy+ palaporuḷ tarumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu. maṟantapa tuppā eṉa eccamāyum, nampi cempū pē petumpi eṉa+ peyarāyum, pō eṉa ēvalāyum varum. ivaṟṟi+ piṟacoṟkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. ēṉi īkārapakaram iṭakkarāy vaḻaṅkum. (43)

TE77n

eñciya vellā meñcuta lilavē.

itu muṉṉar moḻikku īṟāmeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ eñci niṉṟaṉa moḻikku īṟāmāṟum moḻikku īṟākāveṉṟavi tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku īṟāmāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eñciyavum eñcutalila --- `kavavōṭiyiyiṉ' ((eḻu//70)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ patiṉōruyirum patiṉeṭṭu meykkaṇṇum vantu moḻikku īṟāmeṉṟa potuvitiyiṟ, piṉṉi vicēṭittu+ kūṟiyavaṟṟi oḻintaṉavum moḻikku īṟātaṟku oḻivila, ellām eñcutalila --- moḻikku īṟākāteṉṟa uyirmeykaḷum^ tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻivila eṉṟavāṟu.

ellāmeṉṟatu colliṉeccam^ colliyāṅku*uṇarttal eṉṉum utti. ummi virikka. īṇṭu eñciyaveṉṟatu muṉṉar utāraṇam^ kāṭṭiya ñakaramum nakaramum vakaramum cakaramum pakaramum orumoḻikkum īṟākāta ṅakaramum oḻinta paṉṉiraṇṭu meykkaṇṇum ekaramum okaramum aukāramum oḻinta oṉpatuyirum ēṟi moḻikku īṟāy varuvaṉavaṟṟiyeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: varuka pukā vīṅki pukī ceku pukū īṅkē maṅki eṅkō eṉavum, kaṭṭa kaṭā maṭi maṭī maṭu paṭū paṭi eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, maṇṇa eṇṇā kaṇṇi uṇī kaṇu kaṇṇū paṇṇi eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, ata putā pati vati atu kitū tanti antō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, kama neṉmā ammi mī cemmu koṇmū yāmi kāttumvammō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, ceya kāyā koyyū aiyi aiyō eṉavum, itaṟku ikara īkāra ukara ēkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, vā tārā pari kurī karu veru nāri eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, cila palā vali valī valu kollū vallē kali eṉavum, itaṟku ōkāram ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, toḻa viḻā nāḻi vaḻī maḻu eḻū tāḻi eṉavam, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, uḷa uḷḷā veḷḷi kuḷī uḷu eḷḷū kaḷi eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ēṟi varuvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, kaṟṟa kaṟṟā uṟi uṟī maṟu uṟū kaṟṟi eṟṟō eṉavum, itaṟku ēkāram ēṟivaruvaṉa uḷavēl+ koḷka, naṉa kaṉā vaṉṉi tuṉī muṉṉu tuṉṉū eṉṉē aṉṉi aṉṉō eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ peyarāyum viṉiyāyum varuvaṉa uṇarka. ivaṟṟuḷ+ kakaraṉakaraṅkaḷ vilakkāta oṉpatum vantaṉa. āka īṟu nūṟṟunāṟpattumūṉṟum utāraṇamillāta patiṉeṭṭum āka nūṟṟaṟupattoṉṟu. ṅakaram moḻikku īṟākāteṉpatu perumpāṉmiyātaliṉ+ kūṟiṟṟilar. iṉi ṅakaramum aukāramum ēṟāta mey patiṉintum ekaramum okaramum ēkāramum ōkāramum ukaramum ūkāramum ēṟāta meykaḷum^ tampeyar kūṟuṅkāṉ moḻikku īṟāmāṟu, ṅapperitu cuaḻakitu ñutītu eṉavarum. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. kekkuṟintatu kottītu ñevvaḻakitu ñottītu nunnaṉṟu nūpperitu vucciṟitu vūpperitu eṉa ellāvaṟṟiyam ivvāṟē oṭṭuka. iṉṉum ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉē kannaṉṟu ānaṉṟu eṉa moḻikku īṟu* āvaṉa v-um^ tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi āmeṉṟu koḷka. (44)

TE78n

TE79n

TE82n

((moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((3. piṟappiyal))

TE83n

unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi+
taliyiṉu miṭaṟṟiṉu neñciṉu nilii+
pallu mitaḻu nāvu mūkkum
aṇṇamu muḷappaṭa veṇmuṟi niliyā
yellā veḻuttum^ collum^ kāli
piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
tiṟappaṭa+ teriyum^ kāṭci yāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu piṟappu uṇarttutaliṉ+ piṟappiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. cārpiṟṟōṟṟattu eḻuttum^ taṉimeyyum moḻiyiṉaṉṟi uṇarttalākāmiyiṉ avi piṟakkum moḻiyi moḻimarapiṭi uṇartti+ piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉar viyātu itaṉi moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉar vittār.

iccūttiram eḻuttukkaḷiṉatu potuppiṟavi ittuṇi nilikkaḷattu niṉṟu pulappaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellā eḻuttum piṟappiṉ ākkam^ colluṅkāli --- tamiḻeḻuttu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum āciriyaṉ kūṟiya piṟappiṉatu tōṟṟaravi yām^ kūṟumiṭattu, untimutalā+ tōṉṟi muntu vaḷi --- koppūḻaṭiyāka+ tōṉṟi muntukiṉṟa utāṉaṉeṉṉum^ kāṟṟu, taliyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilii --- taliyiṉkaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟi niliyāṉ uṟuppuṟṟu --- pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamu meṉṟa aintuṭaṉē akkāṟṟu, niṉṟa taliyum miṭaṟum neñcum^ kūṭa eṭṭākiya muṟimiyiyuṭiya taṉmiyōṭu kūṭiya uṟuppukkaḷōṭu oṉṟuṟṟu, amiya --- iṅṅaṉam amitalāṉē, vēṟuvēṟu iyala --- avveḻuttukkaḷatu tōṟṟaravu vēṟuvēṟu pulappaṭa vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya, kāṭciyāṉa nāṭi neṟippaṭa --- ataṉi aṟivāṉ ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉampaṭa, tiṟappaṭa+ teriyum --- appiṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷellām^ kūṟupaṭa viḷaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu.

colluṅkāli vaḷi nilipeṟṟu uṟuppukkaḷuṟṟu iṅṅaṉam amitalāṉē avi vaḻaṅkutaliyuṭiya; avaṟṟiṉ vaḻakkam avaṟṟiṉ vaḻiyilē maṉam^ tiṟappaṭa+ teriyumeṉa+ kūṭṭi urittu+ koḷka. iṅṅaṉam kūṟavē muyaṟciyum muyaluṅkaruttāvum uṇmipeṟṟām. (1)

TE84n

tāmatu piṟakkum^ takāra nakāram.

itu meykaḷuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟavi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkil --- aṇṇatticcērnta palliṉataṭiyākiya iṭattē, nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa oṟṟa --- nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu ceṉṟu taṉvaṭivu mikavum uṟumpaṭi cēra, takāra nakārantām iṉitu piṟakkum --- takāra nakāram eṉṟavitām iṉitāka+ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

ta na eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. muṉṉar uṟuppuṟṟu amiya eṉṟu kūṟi īṇṭu meyyuṟa oṟṟa eṉṟār. ciṟitu oṟṟavum varuṭavum piṟappaṉa uḷavākaliṉ. (11)

TE94n

aṇari nuṉinā vaṇṇa moṟṟa
ṟaḵkā ṉaḵkā ṉāyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nuṉi nā aṇari aṇṇam oṟṟa --- nāviṉatu nuṉi mēṉōkkicceṉṟu aṇṇatti+ taṭava, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum --- ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

itu mutalāka neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟiyaṉṟi nāvatikāram paṟṟi+ kūṟukiṉṟār.

ṟa ṉa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (12)

TE95n

nuṉinā vaṇari yaṇṇam varuṭa
rakāra ḻakāra māyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nuṉi nā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa --- nāviṉatu nuṉi mēṉōkki+ ceṉṟu aṇṇatti+ taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum --- rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

ra ḻa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (13)

TE96n

nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpaṉ mutaluṟa
vāvayi ṉaṇṇa moṟṟavum varuṭavum
lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nā vīṅki viḷimpu aṇpal mutaluṟa --- nā mēṉōkkicceṉṟu taṉ viḷimpu aṇpalli ṉaṭiyilē uṟāniṟka, āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramāy --- avviṭattu avvaṇṇatti annā+ tīṇṭa lakāramāyum, āvayiṉ aṇṇam varuṭa lakāramāy --- avviṭattu avvaṇṇatti annā+ taṭava ḷakāramāyum, iraṇṭum piṟakkum --- ivviraṇṭeḻuttum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

la ḷa eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka.

ittuṇiyum nāvatikāram^ kūṟiṟṟu. (14)

TE97n

itaḻiyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: itaḻ iyintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram --- mēlitaḻum^ kīḻitaḻum^ tammiṟkūṭa+ pakāramum makāramum piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

pa ma eṉa ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. (15)

TE98n

pallita ḻiyiya vakāram piṟakkum.

itu vakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pal itaḻ iyiya vakāram piṟakkum --- mēṟpallum^ kīḻitaḻum^ kūṭa vakāramāṉatu piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

va eṉavarum. itaṟkum itaḻ iyitaliṉ makarattiṉ piṉṉar vittār. (16)

TE99n

aṇṇam^ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyici
kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭiya yakāram piṟakkum.

itu yakāram piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuvaḷi miṭaṟṟu+ cērnta ici --- untiyileḻunta kāṟṟu miṭaṟṟiṭattu+ cērnta ataṉāṉ+ piṟanta ōci, aṇṇam^ kaṇṇuṟṟu aṭiya --- aṇṇatti aṇintu uralāṇi iṭṭāṟpōla+ ceṟiya, yakāram piṟakkum --- yakāra voṟṟu+ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

āṇi --- maram. ya eṉa varum. (17)

TE100n

melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappi ṉākkam^
colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa vāyiṉu
mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟa+ tōṉṟum.

itu melleḻuttiṟku+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam^ colliya paḷḷi niliyiṉa āyiṉum --- melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟum^ tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkam^ colliya iṭattē nilipeṟṟaṉavāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷiyici yāppuṟa+ tōṉṟum --- ōci kūṟuṅkāl mūkkiṉkaṇ uḷatākiya vaḷiyiṉiciyāṉ yāppuṟa+ tōṉṟam eṉṟavāṟu.

avi aṅṅaṉamātal kūṟi+ kāṇka. yāppuṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ iṭiyiṉattiṟku miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum valliṉattiṟku+ talivaḷiyum^ koḷka. (18)

TE101n

cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉa+
tērntuveḷi+ paṭutta vēṉi mūṉṟum^
tattam^ cārpiṉ+ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
yotta kāṭciyiṉ+ ṟammiyal piyalum.

itu cārpiṟṟōṟṟaṅkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cārntu variṉ allatu --- cila eḻuttukkaḷi+ cārntu tōṉṟiṉallatu, tamakku iyalpu ila eṉa --- tamakkeṉa+ tōṉṟutaṟku ōriyalpilaveṉṟu, tērntu veḷippaṭutta tammiyalpu mūṉṟum --- ārāyntu veḷippaṭukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷ tammuṭiya piṟappiyalpu mūṉṟiṉiyum^ kūṟuṅkāl, tattam^ cārpiṉ+ piṟappoṭu civaṇi iyalum --- tattamakku uriya cārpākiya meykaḷatu ciṟappu+ piṟappiṭattē piṟattalōṭu --- porunti naṭakkum, ēṉi otta kāṭciyiṉ iyalum --- oḻinta āytam^ tamakku+ poruntiṉa neñcuvaḷiyāṉ+ piṟakkum eṉṟavāṟu.

kāṭciyeṉṟatu neñciṉi. kēṇmiyā nāku nunti eṉavum eḵku eṉavum varum.

āytattiṟku+ cārpiṭam^ `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//38)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. iṉi āytam^ talivaḷiyāṉum miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉum piṟakkumeṉpārum uḷar. moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ aḷapeṭiyum uyirmeyyum^ tammi ākkiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (19)

TE102n

ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭa+ kiḷantu
colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ+
piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi yuṟaḻcci vārattu
akatteḻu vaḷiyici yariṟapa nāṭi
yaḷapiṉ+ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittē
yaḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟattu icikku
meyteri vaḷiyici yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaṭam piṟappiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāveḻuttum^ kiḷantu veḷippaṭa --- āciriyaṉ ellāveḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkumāṟu muntunūṟkaṇṇē kūṟi veḷippaṭukkiyiṉālē, colliya paḷḷi piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi --- yāṉum avvāṟē kūṟiya eṇvaki nilattum piṟakkiṉṟa piṟappōṭē avveḻuttukkaḷi+ kūṟumiṭattu, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ uṟaḻccivārattiṉ aḷapu kōṭal --- yāṉ kūṟiyavāṟu aṉṟi untiyil tōṉṟum^ kāṟṟiṉatu tiritarum^ kūṟṟiṉ kaṇṇē māttirikūṭṭikkōṭalum, akattu eḻu vaḷiyici ariltapanāṭi+ kōṭal --- mūlātārattil eḻukiṉṟa kāṟṟiṉōciyi+ kuṟṟamaṟa nāṭikkōṭalum, antaṇar maṟittē --- pārppāratu vētattu uḷatē; annilimi āṇṭu uṇarka, aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu --- aṅṅaṉam kōṭali īṇṭu+ kūṟalākāmiyiṉ innūṟkaṭkūṟātē, eḻuntu puṟattu icikkum --- untiyiṟṟōṉṟi+ puṟattē pulappaṭṭu olikkum, meyteri vaḷiyici aḷavu nuvaṉ ṟiciṉē --- poruṭeriyum^ kāṟṟiṉatu tuṇiviṟkē yāṉ māttiri kūṟiṉēṉ; avaṟṟiṉatu māttiriyi uṇarka eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉi iraṇṭu cūttiramākkiyum urippa.

itu piṟaṉkōṭkūṟaleṉṉum uttikku iṉam. eṉṉi? untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟiṉi+ kūṟupaṭutti māttirikūṭṭi+ kōṭalum mūlātāram mutalāka+ kāṟṟeḻumāṟu kūṟalum vētattiṟku uḷateṉṟu ivvāciriyar kūṟi ammatampaṟṟi avar koḷvatōr payaṉ iṉṟeṉṟaliṉ. untiyil eḻunta kāṟṟu muṉṉar+ talikkaṭceṉṟu piṉṉar miṭaṟṟilē vantu piṉṉar neñcilē niṟṟali uṟaḻccivārattu eṉṟār, akatteḻuvaḷi yeṉavē mūlātārameṉpatu peṟṟām.

iṉ cāriyiyi attuccāriyiyōṭu kūṭṭuka. ēkāram^ tēṟṟam.

meyterivaḷiyeṉavē poruṭeriyā muṟkum vīḷiyum muyaṟciyāṉāmeṉiṉum poruṭeriyāmiyiṉ avi kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa. eṉavē, colla+ piṟantu coṟku uṟuppām ōciyi ivar eḻutteṉṟu vēṇṭuvareṉa uṇarka.

`niliyum vaḷiyum muyaṟciyu mūṉṟu
miyala naṭappa teḻutteṉa+ paṭumē.'
eṉṟārākaliṉ. (20)

((piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((4. puṇariyal))

TE103n

mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻutti
ṉiraṇṭutali yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵ
taṟunāṉ kīṟṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalu
mellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala.

eṉpatu cūttiram. moḻimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya moḻikaḷi+ potuvakiyāṉ+ puṇarkkum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ+ puṇariyaleṉṟu ivvōttiṟku+ peyarāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu muṟimiyeṉṟatu mēṟceyki yōttukkaḷuḷ+ puṇartaṟku uriyavāka īṇṭu+ kūṟiya karuvikaḷi.

iccūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttum ittuṇi yeṉṟalum, ellā moḻikkum īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirumallatu illiyeṉṟu variyaṟuttalum, īṟum mutalumāka eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu uḷavōveṉṟu aiyuṟṟārkku eḻuttu muppattu mūṉṟumē aṅṅaṉam īṟum mutalumāy niṟpateṉṟu aiyamaṟuttalum nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutal iraṇṭu taliyiṭṭa irupaḵtu īṟu aṟunāṉkākum mūṉṟu taliyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟeḻuttiṉoṭu --- moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iraṇṭi muṭiyilēyiṭṭa irupaḵtum moḻikku īṟāmeḻuttu irupattunāṉkumākiṉṟa mūṉṟi muṭiyilē yiṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttukkaḷōṭē, neṟiniṉṟu iyalum ellā moḻikkum --- vaḻakku neṟikkaṇiṉṟu naṭakkum mūvaki moḻikkum, meyyē uyireṉṟu āyīriyala iṟutiyum mutalum --- meyyum uyirumeṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷē īṟum mutalum āvaṉa eṉṟavāṟu.

irupattiraṇṭu mutalāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutal+ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. irupattunāṉku īṟāvaṉa paṉṉīruyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaramumām. meyyi muṟkūṟiṉār nālvaki+ puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻā veṉṟaṟku.

utāraṇam: maram eṉa meymmutalum meyyīṟum, ili eṉa uyir mutalum uyirīṟum, āl eṉa uyirmutalum meyyīṟum, viḷa eṉa meymmutalum uyirīṟumām. moḻiyākkam iyalpum vikāramumeṉa iraṇṭām uyir tāmē niṉṟu mutalum īṟumātal iyalpu. avi meyyōṭu kūṭiniṉṟu aṅṅaṉamātal vikāram. (1)

TE104n

avaṟṟuḷ
meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal.

itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāl taṉimey mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉi vilakkaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya meyyum uyirumeṉṟa iraṇṭiṉuḷ, meyyīṟu ellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal --- meymoḻikku īṟāyavi yellām puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē moḻikku mutalāyiṉavi yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu uyirēṟi niṟku meṉṟārāyiṟṟu. iṉṉum īṟṟu mey puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟataṉāṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi tammēl vantāl avi uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭuttu niṟkumeṉpatūum^ kūṟiṉārāyiṟṟu. ivviti muṟkūṟiyataṉṟōveṉiṉ āṇṭu+ taṉimey patiṉeṭṭum puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟum avitām uyirēṟuṅkāl+ puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkumeṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār; īṇṭu meymutal meyyīṟeṉa+ poruḷurikka vēṇṭiyamiyiṉ moḻimutaṉ meykaḷum puḷḷipeṟumōveṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyam akaṟṟa+ kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. maram eṉa+ puḷḷi peṟṟu niṉṟatu ariteṉa vantuḻi maramariteṉṟu ēṟi muṭintavāṟu kāṇka. (2)

TE105n

kuṟṟiya lukaramu maṟṟeṉa moḻipa.

itu muṉṉar+ puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalāteṉṟu meykku eytuvikkiṉṟa karuviyi etiratu pōṟṟi uyirkkum eytuvikkiṉṟa karuvi+ cūttiram.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa --- īṟṟu+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum puḷḷiyīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭi+ cēṟal puḷḷi peṟāmiyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam uyirēṟuṅkāl+ kuṟṟukaram^ keṭṭuppōka niṉṟa oṟṟiṉmēl uyirēṟiṟṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka. nākariteṉpuḻi muṉṉar+ kuṟṟukaravōciyum piṉṉar uyirōciyumpeṟṟu avviraṇṭum^ kūṭiniṉṟatallatu apporu ḷuṇarttalākāmiyiṉ, iḵtu uyirōṭuṅkūṭi niṟkumeṉṟār. (3)

TE106n

uyirmey yīṟu muyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.

itu `meyyēyuyireṉṟāyīriyala' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟa uyirkkaṇ nikaḻvatōr aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; uyirmeyyeṉpatōr īṟu uṇṭēṉum atu puṇarkkappaṭātu, atuvum uyirāyē aṭaṅkumeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmeyyīṟum --- uyirmey moḻiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉkaṇ niṉṟatum, uyirīṟṟiyaṟṟē --- uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpi yuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummiyāṉ iṭiniṉṟa uyirmeyyum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpi yuṭittu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu. ummi eccavummi īṟṟiṉum iṭiyiṉum niṉṟaṉa uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē mutal niṉṟaṉa meyyuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉṟār. itaṉāṉē mēl viḷa eṉṟāl+ pōlum uyirmeykaḷellām akaravīṟeṉṟu puṇarkkumāṟu uṇarka. varaku itaṉi mēl uyirttoṭarmoḻiyeṉpa. muṉṉar `meyyiṉ vaḻiyatu ((eḻu//18)) eṉṟatu ōreḻuttiṟkeṉṟu uṇarka. ittuṇiyum moḻimarapiṉ oḻipu kūṟiṟṟu. (4)

TE107n

uyiriṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
muyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyu
meyyiṟu coṉmu ṉuyirvaru vaḻiyu
meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ
ṟivveṉa vaṟiya+ kiḷakkum^ kāli
niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ
ṟāyī riyala puṇarnili+ cuṭṭē.

itu mēṟkūṟum puṇarccikaḷellām irumoḻippuṇarcci yallatu illiyeṉpatūum aḵtu eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum --- uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟum^ colliṉmuṉ uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, uyiriṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaruvaḻiyum --- uyir taṉakku īṟāka iṟum^ colliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum --- meytaṉakku īṟāka iṟuñcolliṉ muṉṉar uyir mutalākiya moḻivarum iṭamum, meyyiṟu coṉmuṉ meyvaruvaḻiyum --- mey taṉakku īṟāka iṟuñcolliṉ muṉṉar meymutalākiya moḻivaru iṭamum, eṉṟu puṇarnili+ cuṭṭu --- eṉṟu collappaṭṭa oṉṟiṉōṭoṉṟu kūṭum nilimiyākiya karuttiṉkaṇ, ivveṉa aṟiya+ kiḷakkuṅkāli --- avaṟṟi ittuṇiyeṉa variyaṟiyi ellārum aṟiya yām^ kūṟuṅkālattu, niṟuttacollē kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyeṉṟu āyīriyala --- muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa collum ataṉi muṭittalikkuṟittu varuñcollum eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, nāṉkuvakiyāṉum^ kūṭuṅkāl irumoḻiyallatu puṇarcciyiṉṟu eṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: āvuṇṭu āvalitu ālili ālvīḻntatu eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka. viḷaviṉi+ kuṟittāṉ eṉṟavaḻi+ cāriyiyum urupum nilimoḻiyāyē niṟkumeṉpatu nōkki ataṉi niṟutta colleṉṟum muṭikkum^ colli+ kuṟittu varukiḷavi yeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. itaṉāṉē nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyum^ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉar `meyyēyuyir' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟatu orumoḻikku, itu irumoḻikkeṉṟu uṇarka. (5)

TE108n

avaṟṟu* uḷ
niṟutta colliṉ īṟu* āku* eḻuttoṭu
kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyiya+
peyaroṭu peyari+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
peyaroṭu toḻili+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
toḻiloṭu peyari+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
toḻiloṭu toḻili+ puṇarkkum^ kāl um
mūṉṟu* ē tiripu* iṭaṉ oṉṟu* ē y-iyalpu* eṉa
v-āṅku* a+ nāṉku* ē moḻi puṇar iyalpu* ē.

itu muṉṉar eḻuttuvakiyāṉ nāṉku puṇarcci eytiya iruvaki+ collum^ colvakiyāṉum nāṉkākumeṉpatūum aṅṅaṉam puṇarvatu collum^ collum aṉṟu eḻuttum eḻuttumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- nilimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟāku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻuttu iyiya --- muṉṉar niṟuttappaṭṭa colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttōṭē ataṉi muṭikkakkaruti varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttu+ porunta, peyaroṭu peyari+ puṇarkkuṅkālum --- peyarccollōṭu peyarccolli+ kūṭṭum iṭattum, toḻiloṭu toḻili+ puṇarkkuṅkālum --- toḻiṟcollōṭu toḻiṟcolli+ kūṭṭum iṭattum, mūṉṟē tiripu iṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa āṅku annāṉkē --- tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉṟu muntunūliṉ+ kūṟiya annāṉku ilakkaṇamumē, moḻi puṇar iyalpu --- īṇṭu moḻikaḷ tammuḷ+ kūṭum ilakkaṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttaṉki, cāttaṉuṇṭāṉ, vantāṉcāttaṉ, vantāṉpōyiṉāṉ eṉa muṟiyē kāṇka. ivi nāṉku iṉattōṭum^ kūṭa+ patiṉāṟām. iṭiyum uriyum^ tāmāka nillāmiyiṉ+ peyarviṉiyē kūṟiṉār. iṭiccollum uriccollum puṇarkkum^ ceykippaṭṭuḻi+ puṇarppu+ ciṟupāṉmi. peyarppeyarum oṭṭuppeyarumeṉa iraṇṭuvakippaṭum peyar. teriniliviṉiyum^ kuṟippuviṉiyumeṉa iraṇṭuvakippaṭum^ toḻil. nilimoḻiyatu īṟṟeḻuttu muṉṉar+ piṟantu keṭṭuppōka varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu+ piṉ piṟantu keṭṭamiyāṉ muṟiyē piṟantu keṭuvaṉa oruṅku niṉṟu puṇarumā ṟiṉmiyiṉ+ puṇarcciyeṉpatu oṉṟiṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, accoṟkaḷi+ kūṟukiṉṟōrum^ kēṭkiṉṟōrum avvōciyi iṭiyaṟavupaṭāmi uḷḷattiṉkaṇṇē uṇarvarātaliṉ avvōci kēṭiṉṟi uḷḷattiṉkaṇ nilipeṟṟu+ puṇarntaṉavēyām. ākavē piṉṉar+ kaṇkūṭāka+ puṇarkkiṉṟa puṇarcciyum muṭintaṉavēyāmeṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi muyaṟkōṭu uṇṭeṉṟāl atu kuṟittuvarukiḷavi yaṉmiyiṉ+ puṇarkkappaṭātu. itutāṉ iṉṟeṉṟāl+ puṇarkkappaṭumeṉṟu uṇarka. (6)

TE109n

avitām
meypiṟi tātaṉ mikutal kuṉṟaleṉ
ṟivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu tiripum āmāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avitām^ tiriyumāṟu --- muṉṉar+ tiripeṉṟu kūṟiya avitām^ tirintu puṇarum neṟiyi, mey piṟitātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu ivveṉamoḻipa --- mey vēṟupaṭutal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu kūṟappaṭum immūṉṟu kūṟṟiyuṭiyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

immūṉṟum allātatu iyalpāmeṉṟu uṇarka. ivi vikaṟpikka+ patiṉāṟu utāraṇamām. maṭkuṭam malittali maravēr ivi peyarōṭu peyarpuṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. maṇmali eṉpatu iyalpu. coṟkēṭṭāṉ palākkuṟittāṉ maranaṭṭāṉ ivi peyarōṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. koṟṟaṉ vantāṉ iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṉāṟcāttaṉ koṭāpporuḷ ōṭunākam ivi toḻiloṭu peyarpuṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉcāttaṉ iḵtu iyalpu. vantāṟkoḷḷum pāṭappōyiṉāṉ cāñāṉṟāṉ ivi toḻiloṭu toḻil puṇarnta mūṉṟu tiripu. vantāṉ koṇṭāṉ iḵtu iyalpu. mūṉṟu tiripeṉṉātu iṭaṉeṉṟataṉāl oru puṇarccikkaṇ mūṉṟum oruṅkēyum varappeṟumeṉṟuuṇarka. makattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ. iḵtu aṅṅaṉam vantavāṟu makara īṟṟu `nāṭpeyar+ kiḷavi' ((eḻu//331)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ uṇarka. iraṇṭu varuvaṉavum^ kāṇka. (7)

TE110n

niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi y-um
aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇar-nilikku* uriya.

itu nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum varumoḻi aṭiyaṭuttum avvirumoḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttum puṇarumeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: niṟuttacollum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum --- nilimoḻiyāka niṟuttiṉacollum ataṉi+ kuṟittu varum^ collum, aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya --- tāmē puṇarātu orōvōrcol aṭiyaṭuttuvariṉum iraṇṭum aṭiyaṭuttuvariṉum puṇarnilimikku uriya eṉṟavāṟu.

aṭiyāvaṉa, ummittokiyum irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtokiyumām.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu āyirattorupaḵtu patiṉāyirattirupaḵtu eṉa varum. ivvaṭikaḷ orucollēyām. vēṟṟumi+ tokiyum uvami+ tokiyum muṭiya+ paṇputtokiyum viṉittokiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmiyiṉ orucollēyām. aṉmoḻi+ tokiyum^ taṉakku vēṟōr muṭipiṉmiyiṉ orucollēyām. ittoki+ coṟkaḷellām aṭiyāyvaruṅkālattu oru collāy varumeṉṟu uṇarka. uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum orucollēyām. (8)

TE111n

maruviṉ ṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉ moḻiyu
muriyavi yuḷavē puṇarnili+ cuṭṭē.

itu marūuccoṟkaḷum puṇarccipeṟumeṉpatūum niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy+ poruḷiyipillaṉavum puṇarcci peṟṟāṟpōla niṟkumeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maru moḻiyum --- iruvakiyāki maruviya coṟkaḷum, iṉṟokuti mayaṅkiyaṉmoḻiyum --- cevikkiṉitāka+ coṟṟiraḷiṭattu niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyumāy oṭṭiṉāṟpōlaniṉṟu poruḷuṇarttātu pirintu piṉṉar+ ceṉṟu oṭṭi+ poruḷuṇartta mayaṅkutal iyaṉṟa coṟkaḷum, puṇarnili+ cuṭṭu uriyavi uḷa --- puṇarum nilimi+ karuttiṉkaṇ uriyaṉa uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

moḻiyumeṉpataṉi maruveṉpataṉōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. iṉṟokutiyeṉṟār, pāveṉṉum uṟuppu nikaḻa+ poruḷoṭṭāmal+ cāṉṟōr coṟkaḷi+ cērttaliṉ.

utāraṇam: muṉṟil mīkaṇ ivi ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya maru. ilakkaṇam allā maru `vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum' ((eḻu//481)) eṉpuḻi+ kāṭṭutum. iṉi,

`irumpu tirittaṉṉa māyiru maruppiṉ+
paralava laṭiya virali teṟippa.' ((akam//4))

eṉpuḻi maruppiṉiraliyeṉṟu oṭṭi iraṇṭāvataṉ tokiyāy+ poruṭantu `puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉittu iyalā' ((eḻu//128)) eṉṟu uyirēṟi muṭintu mayaṅki niṉṟatu; āyiṉ, maruppiṟparaleṉṟu mey piṟitāy oṭṭi niṉṟavāṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ maruppiṉiyuṭiya paraleṉa vēṟṟumi+ tokipporuḷ uṇarttāmiyiṉ aḵtu acceyyuṭku iṉṉōci nikaḻttaṟku+ pakarattiṉmuṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintuniṉṟa tuṇiyēyāy+ puṇarcci+ payaṉiṉṟi niṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam puṇarcciyeytiṉāṟpōla māṭṭilakkaṇattiṉkaṇṇum moḻimāṟṟiṉkaṇṇum niṟṟal coṟku iyalpeṉṟaṟku aṉṟē āciriyar iṉṟokuti yeṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. `karuṅkā lōmi+ kāṇpiṉ peruñciṉi' eṉpuḻi ōmicciṉiyeṉṟu oṭṭi ōmiyiṉatu ciṉiyeṉa+ poruḷ tarukiṉṟatu iṉṉōci tarutaṟku+ kakaravoṟṟumikku+ kāṇpiṉeṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟatu. `teyva mālvari+ tirumuṉi yaruḷāl' eṉpuḻi+ teyvavari yeṉṟu oṭṭi+ teyvattaṉmiyuṭiya variyeṉa+ poruḷtarukiṉṟatu iṉṉōci tarutaṟku māleṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāl+ pōla+ kuṟintu niṉṟatu. mūṉṟu tiripum vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi eccattiṉkaṇṇum,

`eyiṟupaṭiyāka veyiṟkataviṭā
poṉṉōṭi+ pukaraṇinutaṟ
ṟuṉṉaruntiṟal+ kamaḻkaṭāttu+
kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa kaviḻmaṇimaruṅkiṉ+
peruṅkiyāṉi yirumpiṭarttaliyiruntu
maruntu il kūṟṟattu arum^ toḻil cāyā' ((puṟam//3))

eṉa māṭṭāy oṭṭiniṉṟatu kayiṟupiṇikkoṇṭa eṉpataṉōṭum oṭṭiṉāṟpōla niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu iṉṉōci peṟutaṟku. piṟa cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ ivvāṟum piṟavāṟum puṇarcciyilvaḻi+ puṇarccipeṟṟāṟpōla niṟpaḷa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ituvē ōttāka+ koḷka. (9)

TE112n

vēṟṟumi kuṟitta puṇarnili moḻiyum
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi+ puṇarmoḻi niliyu
meḻuttē cāriyi yāyiru paṇpi
ṉoḻukkal valiya puṇarum^ kāli.

itu mūvaki+ tiripiṉuḷ mikku+ puṇarum puṇarcci iruvakiya eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viḷaṅkōṭu iḵtu eḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. makaviṉ ki itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. iṉi alvaḻikkaṇ viḷakkuṟitu iḵtu eḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. paṉiyiṉkuṟi itu cāriyi peṟṟatu. itaṟku+ paṉi kuṟintateṉpatu poruḷām. iḵtu aḷavu+ peyar. oḻukkalvaliya veṉṟataṉāṉ ikkūṟiya iraṇṭum eḻuttum^ cāriyiyum uṭaṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka. avaṟṟukkōṭeṉpatu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. kalattukkuṟi yeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ iraṇṭum peṟṟatu. itaṟku+ kalaṅkuṟintateṉpatu poruḷām. iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ ivviraṇṭum uṭaṉpeṟutaliṉṟu. alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu vēṟṟumiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭutaliṉ. eḻuttuppēṟu yāppuṭimiyāṉum eḻuttiṉāṉ+ cāriyiyātalāṉum eḻuttu muṟkūṟiṉār. vēṟṟumi mēli+ cūttirattē kūṟukiṉṟār.
