alvaḻiyāvaṉa avvurupukaḷ tokkum virintum nillātu puṇarvaṉa. avi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi āṟupayaṉiliyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, viḷivēṟṟumi taṉporuḷōṭu puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, muṟṟuppeyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, uvamattokiyum ummittokiyum irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtokiyum iṭiccollum uriccollum peyarōṭum viṉiyōṭum puṇarnta puṇarcciyum, paṇputtokiyum viṉittokiyum virintuniṉṟavaḻi+ puṇarnta puṇarcciyumeṉa uṇarka. (10)

TE113n

aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉu
mavvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumi yurupē.

itu mēl vēṟṟumiyeṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyarum muṟiyum^ tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyurupu --- muṟkūṟiya vēṟṟumi+ coṟkaḷi, ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum avvāṟeṉpa --- ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum avvāṟu urupumeṉṟu colvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

mēl+ collatikārattu eḻuvāyiyum viḷiyiyum^ kūṭṭi vēṟṟumi eṭṭēṉpārāleṉiṉ ai mutaliya vēṟṟumiyāṟum^ tokkum virintum perumpāṉmiyum pulappaṭṭu niṉṟu peyarpporuḷi+ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyaṉavāka vēṟupāṭuceytu puṇarcciyeytuvikkumeṉṟaṟku īṇṭu āṟeṉṟār. āṇṭu eḻuvāyum viḷiyum^ ceyappaṭuporuḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum^ tammi vēṟupaṭuttu+ poruḷmāttiram uṇarttiniṉṟum viḷiyāy etirmukamākkiniṉṟum iṅṅaṉam^ ciṟupāṉmiyāy+ pulappaṭanillā vēṟupāṭu uṭiyavēṉum aviyum oruvāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumiyāyiṉaveṉṟaṟku āṇṭu eṭṭeṉṟāreṉa uṇarka.

TE114n

valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumi yurupiṉ+
kolvaḻi yoṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.

itu nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyali nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku --- valleḻuttu aṭiyāyniṉṟa nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭimikutal vēṇṭum --- poruntiyavaḻi valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭikkaṇ mikku+ puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

variyātu oṟṟeṉavē valloṟṟum melloṟṟum peṟṟām.

utāraṇam: maṇikku maṇikkaṇ tīkku tīkkaṇ maṉikka maṉikkaṇ eṉavum, vēykku vēykkaṇ ūrkku ūrkkaṇ pūḻkku pūḻkkaṇ eṉavum, uyirīṟu mūṉṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟu mūṉṟiṉum perumpāṉmi valloṟṟu mikkuvarum. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ nuṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa melloṟṟu mikkatu. ivaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻi makarakkēṭu urupiyaliṉ+ kūṟupa. āṅkaṇ īṅkaṇ ūṅkaṇ eṉpaṉa cuṭṭeḻuttu nīṇṭu niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟukkēṭu kūṟutaṟku oṟṟiṉṟu.

iṉi nāṉkaṉurupiṟku melloṟṟu mikāteṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi olvaḻiyeṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāmurupiṉkaṇ nampikaṇ eṉa ikara īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum naṅkikaṇ eṉa aikāra īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum^ tāykaṇ eṉa yakarīṟṟiṉkaṇṇum aracarkaṇ eṉa rakarīṟṟiṉkaṇṇum oṟṟu mikāmi koḷka.

iṉi mey piṟitātali muṉṉē kūṟātu mikutali muṟkūṟiya ataṉāṉē poṟku poṟkaṇ vēṟku vēṟkaṇ vāṭku vāṭkaṇ eṉattirintu muṭivaṉavuṅkoḷka. itaṉāṉē avaṉkaṇ avaḷkaṇ eṉa uyartiṇippeyarkkaṇ ēḻaṉurupu iyalpāy varutaluṅkoḷka. ivaṟṟiṟku+ kuṉṟiya puṇarccivarumēṉum^ koḷka.

koṟṟikku koṟṟikaṇ kōtikku kōtikaṇ eṉa viravuppeyarkkum itaṉāṉē koḷka. (12)

TE115n

āṟa ṉurupi ṉakara+ kiḷavi
yīṟā kakaramuṉi+ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu āṟāvataṟku+ tokimarapi nōkkiyatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟaṉurupiṉ akarakkiḷavi --- atuveṉṉum āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇiṉṟa akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟāku akaramuṉi+ keṭutal vēṇṭum --- neṭumutal kuṟukumoḻikaṭku `īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyum' ((eḻu//161)) eṉa vitittataṉāl uḷatākiya akarattiṉ muṉṉarttāṉ keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

tamatu namatu ematu numatu taṉatu eṉatu niṉatu eṉa varum. itu nilimoḻikku ōr akaram peṟumeṉa vitiyātu urupu akaram ēṟi muṭiyumeṉa vitittāl varum kuṟṟam uṇṭōveṉiṉ, `niṉavakūṟuvaleṉavakēṇ mati' ((puṟam//35)) eṉṟāṟpōla āṟāvataṟku uriya akara urupiṉ muṉṉarum ōr akara eḻuttuppēṟu nilimoḻikkaṇ varutal uḷatāka+ karutiṉārātaliṉ, āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum potuvāka nilimoḻikkaṇ akarappēṟu vitittu, atuveṉṉum orumiyurupu vantāl āṇṭuppeṟṟu niṉṟa akarattiṉmuṉṉar atuveṉpataṉkaṇ akaraṅkeṭukaveṉṟu īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉārātaliṉ ataṟku+ kuṟṟam uṇṭeṉṟu uṇarka. (13)

TE116n

vēṟṟumi vaḻiya peyarpuṇar niliyē.

itu vēṟṟumi peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumipeyarvaḻiya --- vēṟṟumikaḷ peyariṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉavām, puṇarnili --- avaṟṟōṭu puṇarum nilimikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttaṉi cāttaṉoṭu cāttaṟku cāttaṉiṉ cāttaṉatu cāttaṉkaṇ eṉavarum. maṟṟu itu `kūṟiya muṟiyiṉ' ((co//26)) eṉṉum vēṟṟumiyōttiṉ+ cūttirattāṉ+ peṟutumeṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyari+ puṇarttal mutaliya nālvaki+ puṇarcciyiṉiyum vēṟṟumi alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkutaliṉ+ ṟoḻiṟpiṉṉum urupu varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakkutaṟku īṇṭukkūṟiṉāreṉka. āyiṉ ivvilakkutal viṉiyiyaṉmutaṟcūttirattāṉ+ peṟutumeṉiṉ, atu mutaṉiliyi+ kūṟiṟṟeṉpatu āṇṭu uṇarka. (14)

TE117n

uyartiṇi+ peyarē aḵṟiṇi+ peyareṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnili+ cuṭṭē.

itu muṟkūṟiya peyarkaṭku+ peyarum muṟiyum^ tokiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭunilippeyar --- poruḷi oruvar karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa nilimiyiyuṭiya peyarkaḷi, uyartiṇi+ peyarē aḵṟiṇippeyareṉṟu āyiraṇṭeṉpa --- uyartiṇi+ poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa peyarum aḵṟiṇi+ poruḷi oruvaṉ karututaṟku+ kāraṇamāṉa peyarum eṉṉum avviraṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

peyariyaluḷ avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉpatu mutalāka uyartiṇippeyarum atu itu utu eṉpatu mutalāka aḵṟiṇippeyarum āmāṟu avaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇam^ kūṟukiṉṟār īṇṭu+ kuṟiyiṭṭāḷutal māttiriyē kūṟiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi+ koṟṟaṉ koṟṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum viravuppeyarum^ koṟṟaṉkuṟiyaṉ koṟṟikuṟiyaḷ koṟṟaṉ kuḷampu koṟṟi kuṟitu eṉa+ piṉvaruvaṉavaṟṟāṉ+ ṟiṇiteritaliṉ irutiṇi+ peyariṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. koṟṟaṉcevi koṟṟicevi eṉpaṉavum piṉṉar varukiṉṟa viṉikaḷāṉ+ tiṇiviḷaṅka aṭaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. iṉi `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē' ((eḻu//155)) eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟāṇṭum ōtutalpaṟṟi niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ viravuppeyar kōṭalum oṉṟu.

TE118n

avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ+ cāriyi varumē.

itu cāriyi varumiṭam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ --- a+ collappaṭṭa iruvaki+ peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattē, cāriyivarum --- cāriyiccoṟkaḷ varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭūuviṉki makaṭūviṉki palavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa+ puṇariyaṉiliyiṭi+ poruṇilikku utavi vantaṉa. cāriyi yeṉṟataṉ poruḷ vēṟāki niṉṟa irumoḻiyum^ tammiṉ+ cārtaṟporuṭṭu iyintu niṉṟatu eṉṟavāṟu. (16)

TE119n

avitām
iṉṉē vaṟṟē yattē yammē
yoṉṉē yāṉē yakkē yikkē
yaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yuḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉa veṉpa cāriyi moḻiyē.

iḵtu accāriyikaṭku+ peyarum muṟiyum^ tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avitām --- muṉṉar+ cāriyi yeṉappaṭṭa avitām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉṉeṉkiḷavi uḷappaṭa aṉṉa eṉpa --- iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉṉeṉṉum^ collōṭukūṭa oṉpatākiya attaṉmiyuṭiyaṉavum, piṟavum^ cāriyimoḻi eṉpa --- aviyoḻintaṉavum^ cāriyi+ collā meṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

piṟavāvaṉa tam nam num um ñāṉṟu keḻu ē ai eṉpaṉavām. ivaṟṟuḷ ñāṉṟu oḻintaṉa eṭuttōtuvar āciriyar. `eṭuttakaṟaliṉ kuliyalaṅkāntaḷ' itu viṉittoki; cāriyiyaṉṟu. iṉ cāriyi vaḻakkuppayiṟciyum palakāl eṭuttōtappaṭutalum potuvakiyāṉ ōtiyavaḻi+ tāṉēcēṟalumākiya ciṟappunōkki muṉvittār. vaṟṟum attum iṉpōla mutal tiriyumākalāṉum^ ceykiyoppumiyāṉum ataṉpiṉ vittār. am īṟu tiriyumēṉum vaḻakkuppayiṟciyiṉṟi nāṉkāmurupiṉkaṇ tiritaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. āṉ poruṭpuṇarccikkum urupupuṇarccikkum varumeṉṟu ataṉpiṉ vittār. akku īṟutiriyumēṉum urupupuṇarccikkaṇ vārāmiyiṉ ataṉpiṉ vittār. ikku mutal tiriyumēṉum^ ciṟupāṉmipaṟṟi ataṉpiṉ vittār. aṉ iṉpōla+ ciṟattaliṉ+ piṉvittār.

āṟaṉurupiṟkum āṉcāriyikkum iṉṉurupiṟkum iṉcāriyikkum vēṟṟumi yāteṉiṉ; avi cāriyiyāṉa iṭattu yātāṉum ōr urupēṟṟu muṭiyum; urupāyiṉa iṭattu vēṟōr urupiṉi ēlāveṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi makattukki eṉpuḻi+ takaravoṟṟum^ takaravukaramum varumeṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉeṉṟāl attu eṉavē vēṇṭutaliṉ āṇṭum attuniṉṟē keṭṭateṉṟu kōṭum. akku ikku eṉpaṉavum pirittukkūṭṭa+ kiṭakkum. tāḻakkōleṉa akku+ peṟṟuniṟṟalāṉum āṭikku eṉpuḻi+ kukaram nāṉkaṉurupākāmiyāṉum ivi cāriyiyāmāṟu uṇarka. (17)

TE120n

avaṟṟuḷ
iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉar+ keṭuta lurittu mākum.

itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyi mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram --- iṉcāriyiyatu ikaram, āviṉ iṟuti muṉṉar --- ā eṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi muṉṉar, keṭutal urittumākum --- keṭṭumuṭiyavum peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

urittumākumeṉṟataṉāṉ+ keṭātu muṭiyavum peṟumeṉṟavāṟu. iḵtu oppakkūṟaleṉṉum utti.

utāraṇam: āṉi āviṉi āṉoṭu āviṉoṭu āṟku āviṟku āṉiṉ āviṉiṉ āṉatu āviṉatu āṉkaṇ āviṉkaṇ eṉavarum.

iṉi muṉṉareṉṟataṉāṉē māviṟkum ivvāṟē koḷka. māṉi māviṉi māṉoṭu māviṉoṭu māṟku māviṟku eṉa oṭṭuka. ākāra īṟeṉṉātu āviṉiṟutiyeṉṟu ōtiṉamiyiṉ mā ilēciṉāṉ+ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.

iṉi āṉkōṭu āviṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟacāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻiyum^ koḷka. (18)

TE121n

aḷavāku moḻimuta ṉiliiya vuyirmici
ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉākiya nilittē.

iḵtu avviṉcāriyi īṟutiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavākum moḻi --- aḷavuppeyarāy+ piṉṉiṟkum moḻikku, mutal niliiya uyirmici ṉaḵkāṉ --- muṉṉarniṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ īṟṟuniṉṟa kuṟṟukarattiṉ mēl vanta iṉcāriyiyatu ṉakaram, ṟaḵkāṉākiya nilittu --- ṟakaramāy+ tiriyum nilimiyiyuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku. ivaṟṟi+ patteṉa niṟutti `niṟiyumaḷavum' ((eḻu//436)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāl iṉcāriyi koṭuttuk `kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē' ((eḻu//433)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kuṟṟukaram meyyōṭum^ keṭuttu vēṇṭum^ ceykiceytu `muṟṟaviṉvarūum' ((eḻu//433)) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭittu muṭikka.

niliiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. patiṟṟeḻuttu patiṟṟaṭukku oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu patiṟṟoṉṟu patiṟṟiraṇṭu patiṟṟoṉpatu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka. accūttirattiṉ+ `kuṟiyātākum' ((eḻu//436)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ poruṭpeyarkkum eṇṇuppeyarkkum iṉkoṭukka. (19)

TE122n

vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa+ cuṭṭumuta limmu
ṉaḵkā ṉiṟṟa lākiya paṇpē.

itu vaṟṟumutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutalimmuṉ --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuccoṉmuṉ vaṟṟu varuṅkāli, vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa aḵkāṉ niṟṟalākiya paṇpu --- avvaṟṟu+ cāriyiyiṉatu vakaramākiya oṟṟukkeṭa āṇṭu ēṟiya akaram niṟṟal ataṟku uḷatākiya kuṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aviyaṟṟi iviyaṟṟi uviyaṟṟi eṉavarum. iṉṉum ivaṟṟi, avi ivi uvi eṉa niṟuttic `cuṭṭumutalākiyaviyeṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//177)) eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṟṟum urupum^ koṭuttu vēṇṭum^ ceyki ceyka. ivvāṟē ellā urupiṟkum oṭṭuka. aviyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻiyum^ koḷka.

ākiyapaṇpu eṉṟataṉāṉē evaṉeṉpatu paṭuttalōciyāṉ+ peyarāyavaḻi evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟum urupuṅkoṭuttu vaṟṟumiciyoṟṟeṉṟu ṉakaraṅkeṭuttu akaravuyir muṉṉar vaṟṟiṉ vakaram^ keṭumeṉa+ keṭuttu evaṟṟi evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka. (20)

TE123n

ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku.

iḵtu iṉ oṉ āṉ aṉṉeṉṉum ṉakara īṟu nāṉkum^ tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉaḵkāṉ nāṉkaṉurupiṟku ṟaḵkāṉ --- ṉakāra īṟṟu nāṉku cāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkāmurupiṟṟu ṟakāramāy+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṟṟu kōoṟku orupāṟku ataṟku eṉavarum.

itaṉi `aḷavāku moḻimutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉ piṉ viyātu īṇṭu vittatu ṉakara īṟukaḷellām uṭaṉ tiriyumeṉṟaṟku. āṇṭuvippiṉ iṉcāriyiyē tiriyumeṉpatu paṭum. `oṉṟu mutalāka+ pattūrntu varūu --- mellāveṇṇum' ((eḻu//199)) eṉpataṉāṉ orupāṟku eṉpataṉi muṭikka. (21)

TE124n

āṉi ṉakaramu mataṉō raṟṟē
nāṇmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal+ ṟoḻiṟkē.

iḵtu āṉiṉ īṟu poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ tiriyumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāḷmuṉ varūum vaṉmutal+ toḻiṟku --- nāṭpeyarmuṉṉar varum valleḻutti mutalāka uṭiya toḻiṟcoṟku iṭiyē varum, āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟu --- āṉcāriyiyiṉ ṉakaramum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉ cāriyipōla ṟakaramāy+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paraṇiyāṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum. `nāḷ% muṉ+ ^tōṉṟum^ toḻil* ^nili+ kiḷavikku' ((eḻu//247)) eṉṟataṉāṉ āṉcāriyi koṭuttu+ ceyki ceyka. iṉi ummiyi iṟantatutaḻīya tākki nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉōṭu okkumeṉa+ poruḷurittu+ paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ vaḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakaramātal koḷka.

iṉi, ñāpakattāl toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram^ tiriyumeṉavē peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram^ tiritalum^ tiriyāmiyum^ koḷka. kuṟumpiṟkoṟṟaṉ paṟampiṟpāri eṉa+ tirintu vantaṉa. kurukiṉkāl eruttiṉpuṟam eṉa+ tiriyātu vantaṉa. (22)

TE125n

atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli.

iḵtu attu mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: attiṉ akaram --- attuccāriyiyiṉ akaram, akaramuṉi illi --- akara īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉar illiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

`attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē' ((eḻu//219)) eṉpataṉāṉ makappeyar attuppeṟṟu niṉṟatu makattukkiyeṉa akaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. viḷavattu+ kaṇṇeṉpuḻi+ keṭātu niṟṟal `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉuḷ `teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē' eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟupa. (23)

TE126n

ikki ṉikara mikaramuṉi yaṟṟē.

iḵtu ikku mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ikkiṉ ikaram --- ikku+ cāriyiyiṉatu ikaram, ikaramuṉi aṟṟu --- ikarīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉar muṟkūṟiya attuppōla+ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

`tiṅkaṇmuṉvariṉ' ((eḻu//248)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟṟa ikku āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ikaraṅkeṭṭu niṉṟatu. iḵtu iṭapporuṭṭu. (24)

TE127n

aiyiṉ muṉṉaru mavviya ṉiliyum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal niliyum --- ikkiṉ ikaram ikara īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉaraṉṟi aikāra īṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉarum mēṟkūṟiya keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

`tiṅkaḷ um* nāḷ um* muntu kiḷantaṉṉa' ((eḻu//286)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ cittirikku+ koṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻippeṟṟa ikku aikārattiṉ muṉṉar+ keṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. (25)

TE128n

eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
yakki ṉiṟutimeym miciyoṭum^ keṭumē
kuṟṟiya lukara muṟṟa+ tōṉṟātu.

iḵtu akku mutal oḻiya ēṉiya keṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eppeyar muṉṉarum --- evvakippaṭṭa peyarccoṉmuṉṉarum, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi --- valleḻuttu varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, akkiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟattōṉṟātu --- iṭi niṉṟa akkuccāriyiyiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiya+ tōṉṟātu, meymmiciyoṭuṅkeṭum --- akkuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟu+ taṉakku mēlniṉṟa valloṟṟōṭuṅkeṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

`oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūum' ((eḻu//418)) eṉṟataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa kuṉṟakkūki maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉpaṉavum, `vēṟṟumiyāyi ṉēṉi yiraṇṭum' ((eḻu//329)) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam eṉpaṉavum, `tamiḻeṉkiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//385)) eṉpataṉāṉ akkuppeṟṟa tamiḻakkūttu eṉpatuvum akku īṟukeṭṭavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam varutaliṉ eppeyareṉṟār. muṟṟaveṉpataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivviti koḷka. tamiḻanūl tamiḻayāḻ tamiḻavariyar eṉavarum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tamakkēṟṟa iyipu valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭittu+ koḷka. aṉṟi+ kēṭōtiya kakaravoṟṟu niṟkumeṉiṉ, cakaram^ takaram pakaram vantavaṟṟiṟku+ kakaravoṟṟākāmi uṇarka. (26)

TE129n

ammi ṉiṟuti kacata+ kāli+
taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum.

iḵtu am īṟu tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ammiṉ iṟuti --- ammuccāriyiyiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu, kacatakkāli --- kacatakkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum --- taṉvaṭivu tirintu ṅañanakkaḷām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl eṉavarum.

itu `puḷimarakkiḷavikku' ((eḻu//244)) eṉpataṉāṉ ammuppeṟṟatu. kacatakkāli+ tiriyumeṉavē pakarattiṉkaṇ tiripiṉṟāyiṟṟu. meytirinteṉṉātu taṉmey eṉṟataṉāṉ ammiṉ makaramēyaṉṟi+ tam nam num um eṉṉum^ cāriyi makaramum^ tirital koḷka. ellār taṅkiyum ellār naṅkiyum ellīr nuṅkiyum vāṉavari villuntiṅkaḷum eṉavarum. tuṟikēḻūraṉ vaḷaṅkēḻūraṉ eṉa+ keḻu v --- eṉṉum^ cāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyum^ ceyyuṇmuṭipeṉṟu koḷka. (27)

TE130n

meṉmiyu miṭimiyum varūum kāli
yiṉmi vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ammīṟu iyalpukaṇattu muṉṉar+ keṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: meṉmiyam iṭimiyum varūuṅkāli --- meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, iṉmi vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar --- ammuccāriyi iṟutimakaramiṉṟi muṭitali vēṇṭumeṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyañeri nuṉi muri yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavarum. uriyiṟkōṭaleṉpataṉāṉ+ puḷiyaviliyeṉa uyirvaruvaḻi īṟukeṭutalum puḷiyiliyeṉa ammu muḻuvatum^ keṭutalum^ koḷka. puḷiyavili yeṉṟatu `oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku' ((eḻu//132)) aṉṟu. meṉkaṇamum iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum^ tammuḷokkumēṉum ammu muḻuvatum^ keṭṭuvarutaliṉ uyiri eṭuttōtārāyiṉār. puḷiṅkāy eṉpatu marumuṭipu. (28)

TE131n

iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ
kiṉṉeṉ cāriyi yiṉmi vēṇṭum.

iḵtu iṉcāriyi aintāmurupiṉkaṇ muḻuvatum^ keṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku --- iṉṉeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumiyurupiṟku, iṉṉeṉcāriyi iṉmi vēṇṭum --- iṉṉeṉṉum^ cāriyi tāṉ iṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉ palāviṉ kaṭuviṉ taḻuviṉ cēviṉ vuviṉ eṉavarum. ivaṟṟiṟku vīḻpaḻameṉavum nīṅkiṉāṉeṉavum^ koṭuttu muṭivuṇarka. ūriṉīṅkiṉāṉ eṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. iṉi `avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉikaram' ((eḻu//120)) eṉṟataṉpiṉ itaṉi viyāta muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ iṉcāriyi keṭātu vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum niṟṟalkoḷka. pāmpiṉiṉ+ kaṭitutēḷ `kaṟpiṉiṉvaḻāa naṟpalavutavi' `akaṭucērpu porunti yaḷaviṉiṉ+ ṟiriyātu' eṉavarum. iṉi iṉmiyum vēṇṭumeṉṉum ummi tokkuniṉṟatākki ataṉāṉ ivi kōṭalum oṉṟu. (29)

TE132n

peyarum^ toḻilum pirintu* oruṅku* icippa
vēṟṟumi yurupu nilipeṟu vaḻiyum^
tōṟṟam vēṇṭā+ tokutikkaṇṇum
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi+
coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā
tiṭiniṉ ṟiyalum^ cāriyi yiyaṟki
yuṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum.

itu muṟkūṟiya cāriyikaḷellām puṇarmoḻiyuḷḷē varumeṉpatūum ammoḻitām iviyeṉpatūum avivārāta moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉpatūum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peyarum^ toḻilum --- peyarccollum^ toḻiṟcollum, pirintu icippa oruṅku icippa --- peyarum^ toḻilumāy+ pirinticippa+ peyarum peyarumāykkūṭiyicippa, vēṟṟumiyurupu nilipeṟuvaḻiyum --- vēṟṟumi ceyyum urupukaḷ tokātu nilipeṟṟa iṭattum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭā+ tokutikkaṇṇum --- avvēṟṟumiyurupukaḷ tōṟṟutal vēṇṭātu tokka iṭattum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi --- tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkōṭē porunti, coṟcitarmaruṅkiṉ --- cāriyipeṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷi+ pirittukkāṇumiṭattu, cāriyi iyaṟki vaḻivantu viḷaṅkātu iṭiniṉṟa iyalum --- accāriyiyiṉatu taṉmi accoṟkaḷiṉ piṉṉē vantu viḷaṅkātu naṭuvē niṉṟu naṭakkum, uṭimiyum iṉmiyam oṭuvayiṉ okkum --- accāriyi uṇṭātalum illiyātalum oṭuvurupiṉiṭattu ottuvarum eṉṟavāṟu.

oṭuviṟkokkum eṉavē ēṉiya ovvāvāyiṉa.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉikkuṟittāṉ, kūḻiṟku+ kuṟṟēval ceyyum ivi pirinticittu urupunili peṟṟaṉa. `aṉṉeṉcāriyi' ((eḻu//194)) eṉpataṉik `kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟuti' ((eḻu//195)) eṉpataṉi+ cēravittataṉāl iṉ cāriyi varutal koḷka. ivviraṇṭurupum^ cāriyiniṟpa+ perumpāṉmiyum^ tokāveṉṟu uṇarka. viḷaviṉikkuṟittavaṉ, kaṭicūttirattiṟku+ poṉ ivi oruṅkicippa urupu nilipeṟṟaṉa. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukki, vāṉattuvaḻukki eṉa+ cāriyipeṟṟu+ pirinticittu aintāmurupu nilipeṟṟum nilipeṟātum vantatu. vāṉattiṉ vaḻukkal, vāṉattu vaḻukkal ivi `melleḻuttuṟaḻum' ((eḻu//112)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu `vaḻakkattāṉa' eṉpataṉāṉ attukkoṭuttu makaraṅkeṭukka oruṅkicittaṉa. viḷaviṉatukōṭu, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa oruṅkicittu+ cāriyi peṟṟavaḻi āṟaṉurupu tokātum^ tokkum niṉṟatu. itaṟku+ pirinticittaliṉṟu.marattukkaṭkaṭṭiṉāṉ, marattukkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa+ pirinticitta vaḻiyum, marattukkaṭkuraṅku, marattukkuraṅku eṉa oruṅkicitta vaḻiyum^ cāriyi niṉṟavaḻi ēḻaṉurupu tokātum^ tokkam niṉṟatu. `kiḷippeyarellām' ((eḻu//307)) eṉṟataṉuḷ+ `koḷa' eṉṟataṉāṉ ṇakāram ṭakāramāyiṟṟu. `nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattōṭu civaṇum' ((eḻu//228)) eṉa vititta attu nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉṟavaḻi+ peṟātāyiṟṟu, aḵtu `oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiyavaḻakku' aṉmiyiṉ. nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttukkoṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa urupu tokkuḻi iruvaḻiyum peṟṟaṉa. ellār tammiyum eṉa+ cāriyi īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ iṭiniṟṟal perumpāṉmiyeṉṟaṟku iyalumeṉṟār. pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntal eṉa uṭimiyum iṉmiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottatu. iṉi iyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ oṭu urupiṉkaṇ+ peṟṟum peṟāmiyum varutalaṉṟi+ peṟṟēvarutalum^ koḷka. palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum. (30)

TE133n

attē vaṟṟē yāyiru moḻimē
loṟṟumey keṭuta ṟeṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
yavaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu attu vaṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻiyatu oṟṟukkēṭum varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ oṟṟuppēṟumākiya ceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl oṟṟu --- attum vaṟṟumākiya avviraṇṭu cāriyimēl niṉṟa oṟṟu, meykeṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu --- taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttumikamē --- avviru cāriyi muṉṉam varum valleḻuttu mikka muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalattukkuṟi, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavarum. `attiṭi varūum^ kalameṉ ṉaḷavē' ((eḻu//168)) `cuṭṭu mutal vakara miyu meyyum' ((eḻu//183)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ attum vaṟṟam peṟṟuvarum makara vakara īṟukaṭku īṟṟu valleḻuttuviti iṉmiyiṉ avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumeṉṟu cāriyi valleḻuttu vitittār. valleḻuttu iṉṟi+ tirintu muṭivaṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum lakāramum ḷakāramumām. makara īṟṟiṟku attum vakara īṟṟiṟku vaṟṟum varumeṉpatu accūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ peṟṟām. vaṟṟē yattē yeṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ+ puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉ muṉṉar attiṉmici yoṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: viṇṇattukkoṭkum, veyilattucceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattu+ koṇṭāṉ eṉavarum. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaram akaramuṉṉarēyaṉṟi+ piṟa uyirmuṉṉarum^ keṭum orōviṭatteṉṟu koḷka. aṇṇāttēri, tiṭṭāttukkuḷam eṉa ākārattiṉ muṉṉarum varum attiṉ akaram^ keṭṭatu. ivaṟṟi akara īṟākkiyum muṭippa. iṉi+ teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē eṉṟataṉāṉ attiṉ akaram^ teṟṟeṉa+ keṭātu niṟkum iṭamum^ koḷka. atavattukkaṇ, viḷavattukkaṇ eṉavarum. (31)

TE134n

kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
nēra+ tōṉṟu meḻuttiṉ cāriyi.

itu moḻiccāriyiyi viṭṭu eḻuttukkaṭku varuñcāriyikaḷatu peyarum muṟiyum^ tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu civaṇi --- kāramum^ karamum^ kāṉoṭu porunti, eḻuttiṉ cāriyi nērattōṉṟum --- eḻuttiṉkaṇ varum^ cāriyiyātaṟku ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭampaṭattōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

kāramum^ karamum eṭuttu+ colliyavaḻi iṉiticittalāṉum, vaḻakku+ payiṟciyuṭimiyāṉum, vaṭaveḻuttiṟkum uriyavātalāṉum^ cēra+ kūṟiṉār. kāṉ attaṉmi yiṉmiyiṉāṉ+ piṉ vittār. nērattōṉṟumeṉavē nērattōṉṟātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avi āṉam, ēṉam, ōṉam eṉka. ivi citinta vaḻakkēṉum^ kaṭiyalākāvāyiṉa. (32)

TE135n

avaṟṟuḷ
karamum^ kāṉu neṭṭeḻuttu ilavē.

iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ+ cilacāriyi cilaveḻuttōṭu vārāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ, karamum^ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttila --- karamum^ kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku varutaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, neṭṭeḻuttiṟku+ kāram varumāyiṟṟu. ākāram īkāram eṉa oṭṭuka. aikāram aukārameṉa+ cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ varumāṟu kāṇka. (33)

TE136n

varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum^ kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭiya.

iḵtu aiyam akaṟṟiyatu; eṉṉi? neṭṭeḻuttiṟku+ cilacāriyi vilakkiṉāṟpōla+ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum vilakkaṟpāṭu uṇṭōveṉa aiyam nikaḻtaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: varaṉmuṟi mūṉṟum --- varalāṟṟu muṟimiyiyuṭiya mūṉṟucāriyiyum, kuṟṟeḻuttuṭiya --- kuṟṟeḻuttuppeṟṟu varutaliyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.

akāram akaram maḵkāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. `vakāramiciyum' `akara ikaram' `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' eṉavum piṟavum^ cūttiraṅkaḷuḷ+ kāṇka. iḵkāṉ oḵkāṉ, eṉpaṉa peruvaḻakkaṉṟu. varaṉmuṟiyeṉṟataṉāṉ aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytam peṟutal koḷka. itu `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāyta+ puḷḷi' ((eḻu//38)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟātāyiṟṟu, moḻiyāy nillāmiyiṉ. (34)

TE137n

aikāra aukāram^ kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu `avaṟṟuḷ+ karamum^ kāṉum' eṉpataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aikāra aukāram^ kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum --- neṭṭeḻuttukkaḷuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum^ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

aikāṉ aukāṉ eṉavarum. ummi iṟantatutaḻīiyiṟṟu. kāratti+ karututaliṉ. (35)

TE138n

puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ ṉuyirtaṉittu iyalātu
meyyoṭum^ civaṇu mavviyal keṭuttē.

itu puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantakālattu+ puṇarum muṟimi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu --- puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ coṉmuṉṉarvanta uyir mutaṉmoḻiyiṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum^ civaṇum --- appuḷḷiyōṭum kūṭum, avviyal keṭuttu --- tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa avviyalpiṉi+ keṭuttu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, nīrōṭu kūṭiya pālpōla niṉṟateṉṟu oṟṟumi kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu itaṉāṉē uyirmeyyeṉa+ peyarpeṟṟatu.

utāraṇam: pālaritu pālāḻi ālili poruḷīṭṭam vāṉulaku vāṉūṭu vēleṟintāṉ vēlēṟṟāṉ poruḷiyam poruḷoṉṟu nāṇōṭiṟṟu colluviyam eṉa varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ iyalpallāta puḷḷi muṉṉar uyirvantālum ivviti koḷka. ataṉi ataṉoṭu nāṭuri eṉa varum. ivaṟṟic `cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu' ((eḻu//176)) `urivarukāli nāḻikkiḷavi' ((eḻu//240)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka. puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉumeṉa ummiyi māṟi eccavummiyākki+ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉarum eṉa avviti koḷka. eṉavē, `kuṟṟiyalukaramumaṟṟu' ((eḻu//105)) eṉṟataṉōṭum poruntiṟṟām. nākaritu varakaritu eṉa varum. (36)

TE139n

meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ+ ṟaṉṉuru vākum.

iḵtu uyirmey puṇarccikkaṇ uyirnīṅkiyavaḻi+ paṭuvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mey uyir nīṅkiṉ --- mey taṉṉōṭu kūṭiniṉṟa uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu+ pirintu vēṟu niṉṟatāyiṉ, taṉ uruvākum --- tāṉ muṉṉar+ peṟṟu niṉṟa puḷḷivaṭivu peṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

āl ili ataṉ ai eṉa varum.

uyir eṉṉa vaṭiviṟṟeṉṟu āciriyar kūṟāmiyiṉ uyirkkaṇ ārāycciyiṉṟu.

iṉi ekara okaraṅkaḷi+ puḷḷiyāṉ vēṟṟumi ceytaliṉ toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉa vaṭivuṭiya veṉṟu kōṭalumām. puṇarcciyuḷ uyirmeyyiṉi+ pirippārātaliṉ itu kūṟākkāl+ kuṉṟakkūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (37)

TE140n

ellā moḻikku muyirvaru vaḻiyē
yuṭampaṭu meyyi ṉuruvukoḷal variyār.

iḵtu uyirīṟum uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻvatōr karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāmoḻikkum --- nilimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumāy+ puṇarum evvaki moḻikkum, uyir varuvaḻi --- uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal variyāruṭampaṭumeyyiṉatu vaṭivi uyirīṟu kōṭali nīkkār koḷvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

avi yakaramum vakaramumeṉpatu mutaṉūl paṟṟikkōṭum;

`uṭampaṭu meyyē yakāra vakāra

muyirmutaṉ moḻivarūum^ kāli yāṉa'

eṉavum,

`iṟutiyu mutalu muyirnili variṉē

yuṟumeṉa moḻipa vuṭampaṭu meyyē'

eṉavum^ kūṟiṉārākaliṉ. uyirkaḷuḷ ikara īkāra aikāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey koḷḷum. ēkāram, yakāramum, vakāramum^ koḷḷum. allaṉavellām vakara uṭampaṭumeyyē koḷḷumeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: kiḷiyaḻakitu, kurīiyōppuvāḷ, variyāmakaḷir eṉavum; viḷavaḻakitu, palāvaḻakitu, kaṭuvaḻakitu, pūvaḻakitu, kōvaḻakitu, kuvaṭintatu eṉavum oṭṭuka. `ēe yivaḷorutti pēṭiyō veṉṟār' ēvāṭal kāṇka eṉa ēkārattiṟku iraṇṭum vantaṉa.

oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ vikārappaṭṭa moḻikkaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka. maravaṭi āyirutiṇi eṉa varum. variyāreṉṟataṉāṉ uṭampaṭumeykōṭal orutaliyaṉṟu. kiḷiaritu mūṅkāilli eṉavum varum. oṉṟeṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāl `viṇvattukkoṭkum' eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmi puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum varum. celvuḻi uṇpuḻi eṉpaṉa viṉittokiyeṉa maṟukka. (38)

TE141n

eḻuttō raṉṉa poruṭeri puṇarcci
yiciyiṉ+ ṟirita ṉiliiya paṇpē.

iḵtu eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavāmeṉa eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eḻuttōraṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci --- eḻuttu orutaṉmittāṉa poruḷviḷaṅka niṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, iciyiṉ+ ṟirital niliiya paṇpu --- eṭuttal paṭuttal nalitaleṉkiṉṟa ōcivēṟṟumiyāṉ+ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilipeṟṟa kuṇam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, cemparutti, kuṟumparampu, nākaṉṟēvaṉpōttu, tāmarakkaṇiyār, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa ivi iciyiṉ+ ṟirintaṉa. (39)

TE142n

avitām
muṉṉa+ poruḷa puṇarcci vāyi
ṉiṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttukkaṭa ṉilavē.

itu mēlataṟkōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avitām --- pala poruṭku+ potuveṉṟa puṇarmoḻikaḷ tām, muṉṉapporuḷa --- kuṟippāṉ uṇarum poruṇmiyiṉiyuṭiya, puṇarccivāyiṉ iṉṉa veṉṉum eḻuttu+ kaṭaṉ ila --- puṇarcciyiṭattu ittaṉmiya veṉṉum eḻuttumuṟimiyi uṭiyavalla eṉṟavāṟu.

cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟuḻi+ poṉṉārāycci yuḷavaḻi+ poṉṉeṉavum^ cempārāycci yuḷavaḻi+ cempeṉavum^ kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu. iciyiṉ+ ṟiritaleṉṟatu oliyeḻuttiṟkeṉavum eḻuttukkaṭaṉila veṉṟatu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavum^ koḷka. (40)

((puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((5. tokimarapu))

TE143n

kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl+ ṟōṉṟu
melleḻuttu iyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē
yaṉṉa marapiṉ moḻi vayiṉāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum mēli akattōttiṉuḷ muṭikkumvaḻi īṟukaṭōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi īṇṭu orōvōr cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ ṟokuttu muṭipu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ivvōttu+ tokimara peṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēl mūvakimoḻiyum nālvakiyāṉ+ puṇarvuḻi, mūṉṟutiripum ōriyalpum eyti vēṟṟumi alvaḻiyeṉa irupakutiyavāki eḻuttum^ cāriyiyum mikku+ puṇarumāṟu ituveṉṟu uṇartti avitām virinta cūttirapporuḷavaṉṟiyum^ tokku+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, ivvōttu+ puṇariyalōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu. i+ taliccūttiram uyirmayaṅkiyaliyum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaliyum nōkkiyatōr varumoḻi+ karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēṟṟōṉṟum iyaṟki melleḻuttu --- uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum muṉṉar niṟpa+ kacatapakkaḷi mutalākavuṭiya moḻikaḷ vantāl avaṟṟiṟku mēlē tōṉṟiniṟkum iyalpākiya melleḻuttukkaḷ, colliya muṟiyāṉ ṅañanama eṉṉum oṟṟākum --- neṭuṅkaṇakkiṉ+ poruntakkūṟiya muṟiyāṉē kacatapakkaḷukku ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟukkaḷ niraṉiṟivakiyāṉām, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉāṉa --- attaṉmittākiya muṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya moḻikaḷiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. itu `marappeyarkkiḷavi' ((eḻu//314)) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttu+ peṟṟatu. maram^ kuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ ṭiriyumāṟu `alvaḻiyellām' ((eḻu//314)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ peṟutumēṉum īṇṭu+ tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāl nilimoḻikkaṭṭōṉṟiniṉṟa oṟṟu+ tirital koḷka. aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyaṉmiyiṉ viḷakkuṟumi viḷakkuṟittāṉ eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu+ peṟāvāyiṉa; ivi ēḻāvatum iraṇṭāvatum^ tiritaliṉ. iṅṅaṉam eḻuttu+ peṟuvaṉavum^ tirivaṉavumellām varumoḻiyēpaṟṟi varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (1)

TE144n

ñanama yavaveṉu mutalāku moḻiyu
muyirmuta lākiya moḻiyu muḷappaṭa
vaṉṟi yaṉittu mellā vaḻiyu
niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpā kummē.

itu muṟkūṟiya nālvaki+ puṇarcciyuḷ iyalpu puṇaruṅkāl ikkūṟiya patiṉēḻeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu irupattunāṉkīṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum vēṟṟumiyilum alvaḻiyilum varumoḻi iyalpāy muṭika veṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñanamayava eṉum mutalāku moḻiyum --- ñanamayava eṉṟu collappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷ mutalāyniṟkum^ coṟkaḷum, uyirmutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa --- uyireḻuttu mutalāyniṉṟa coṟkaḷum^ tammiṟkūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉittum --- appatiṉēḻākiya varumoḻikaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum --- vēṟṟumiyum alvaḻiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, niṉṟacoṉmuṉ --- irupattunāṉku īṟṟavāyniṉṟa peyarccoṉmuṉṉar, iyalpākum --- tiripiṉṟi iyalpu puṇarcciyāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

uyirīṟṟiṉkaṇ ekara okaram oḻintaṉa koḷka.

utāraṇam: viḷa palā kiḷi kurī kaṭu pū cē ki cō ku eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu eṉa meymmutaṉmoḻi varuvittu, poruḷ tarutaṟku ēṟpaṉa aṟintu kūṭṭuka. cō eṉpatu araṇ. ataṟku cō ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉa+ koḷka. kuveṉpataṟku+ kuñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu eṉka. iṉi ivaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻi varuṅkāl aḻakitu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṟiṟṟu eḻuntatu ēyntatu aitu oṉṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyattatu eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ cōvukku iṭintatu īṇṭiyatu uḷḷatu ūṟiṟṟu eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiyum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ viḷa mutaliyavaṟṟi niṟutti ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku ākkam iḷami īṭṭam uyarvu ūṟṟam eḻucci ēṟṟam aiyam oḻivu ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka. ēlātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōla ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka.

iṉi+ puḷḷiyīṟṟu ūkāramum ṉakāramum mēṟkūṟupa. ēṉiya īṇṭu+ kūṟutum.

utāraṇam: uriñ verin eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻntatu nīṭiṟṟu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, ñekiḻcci nīṭṭippu aṭivu ākkam eṉavum varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. maram vēy vēr yāḻ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu aḻakitu āyiṟṟu eṉavum, varuvittu, ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ makara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ keṭutal `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. nilimoḻi+ tiripu īṇṭu+ koḷḷāmi uṇarka. yakara īṟu yakarattiṉmuṉṉar iraṇṭiṭattum^ keṭutal īṇṭu ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. vēl tev kōḷ eṉa niṟutti ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu iruvaḻiyum oṭṭuka. ṇakāra lakāra ḷakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar nakaram varumoḻiyāka vantuḻi annakaram^ tiritaliṉ attirinta utāraṇaṅkaḷ īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka. ivaṟṟuḷ tirintu varuvaṉavuḷa; avi eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ēṉiyōttukkaḷuḷ muṭikkiṉṟavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ uyirkkaṇamāyiṉ oṟṟiraṭṭiyum uṭampaṭu meypeṟṟum uyirēṟiyum muṭiyum^ karuvittiripukaḷ tiripeṉappaṭā ivviyalpiṉkaṇṇeṉṟu uṇarka. varakuñāṉṟatu varakuñāṟci eṉa+ kuṟṟukarattiṉkaṇṇum ivvāṟē koḷka. irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkum alvaḻikkum akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟi oru cūttirattāṉ+ ṟokuttu muṭittār. mēlum ivvāṟē kūṟupa. ivviyalpu varumoḻi nōkki+ kūṟiyateṉṟu uṇarka. ivviyalpupuṇarcci meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu. uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāmiyiṉ mey muṟkūṟiṉār. (2)

TE145n

avaṟṟuḷ
melleḻuttu iyaṟki yuṟaḻiṉum variyār
colliya toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiya muṭipiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku ammuṭipu vilakki+ piṟitu viti eytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉuḷ, melleḻuttiyaṟki uṟaḻiṉum variyār --- melleḻuttu iyalpiyalpātalēyaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa --- collappaṭṭa toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟukkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

ummi etirmaṟi. eṉavē, uṟaḻāmi valiyuṭittāyiṟṟu. katirñeri katirññeri nuṉi muri eṉavum, itaḻñeri itaḻññeri nuṉi muri eṉavum varum. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ōreḻuttorumoḻi īreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuḷḷum^ cila uṟaḻccipeṟṟu muṭital koḷka. pūñeri pūññeri nuṉi muri kāyñeri kāyññeri nuṉi muri eṉavarum. colliyaveṉṟataṉāṉ ōreḻuttorumoḻikaḷuḷ+ cila mikkumuṭital koḷka. kiññerittār nīṭṭiṉār maṟittār eṉavarum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē īreḻuttorumoḻikkaṇ meyññāṉam nūlmaṟantār eṉa varum. ivaṟṟi nalintu kūṟa+ piṟattaliṉ iyalpeṉpārum uḷar. pūñāṟṟiṉār eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mikātaṉa. (3)

TE146n

ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvu ñāvum
viṉiyō raṉiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu yakara ñakara mutaṉmoḻivanta iṭattu nikaḻvatōr taṉmi kūṟukiṉṟatu. ituvum puṇariyaloḻipāy+ karuvippāṟpaṭum.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum --- ṇakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta yāvum ñāvum mutalākiya viṉiccoṟkaḷ, viṉiyōraṉiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- oruviṉivanta taṉmiyi okkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē' `maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē' eṉavum `poṉyātta tārppuravi parikkumē' `poṉñātta tārppuravi parikkumē' eṉavum varum.

viṉikkaṇṇeṉavē maṇyāmi maṇñāmi eṉa+ peyarkkaṇ vārāvāyiṉa. ñāmuṟkūṟātu yāmuṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ñācceṉṟavaḻi yāccellātu yācceṉṟavaḻi ñāccellumeṉṟu koḷka. maṇñāṉṟatu eṉṟavaḻi maṇyāṉṟatu eṉṟu vārāmi uṇarka. (4)

TE147n

moḻimuta lāku mellā veḻuttum
varuvaḻi niṉṟa vāyiru puḷḷiyum
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi+ tiripiṭa ṉilavē.

itu ṇakāra īṟum ṉakāra īṟum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: moḻimutalākum ellāveḻuttum varuvaḻi --- moḻikku mutalā meṉappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum --- muṉṉarkkūṟi niṉṟa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum, vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi+ tiripiṭaṉ ilavē --- vēṟṟumi yallāta iṭattu+ tiriyumiṭam ila eṉṟavāṟu.

maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti+ kaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu nuntiyatu aṭintatu āyiṟṟu illi īṇṭiṟṟu uṇṭu ūṭṭiṟṟu evviṭattatu ēṟiṟṟu aitu oḻukiṟṟu ōṅkiṟṟu auviyatu eṉa oṭṭuka. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl ṇakārattiṟku+ ciṟupāṉmi tiripum uṇṭeṉṟu koḷka. cāṭkōl eṉa varum; itaṟku+ cāṇākiya kōleṉka. ivi `niṉṟacoṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟa vaḻi aṭaṅkāvāyiṉa; atu varumoḻipaṟṟi+ tiriyāmi kūṟiyatākaliṉ. itu nilimoḻipaṟṟi+ tiriyāmi kūṟiyatu. (5)

TE148n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻi
mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki yāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripu eyti niṉṟavaṟṟi īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttu alvaḻi+ tiriyāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉāṉa --- alvaḻikkaṇ aṅṅaṉam^ tiriyātu niṉṟa avvoṟṟukkaḷ, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarcciyiṭattum, valleḻuttalvaḻi mēṟkūṟiyaṟki --- valleḻuttallāta iṭattu mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tiriyumeṉṟārāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇ poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi nuntiyatu aḻaku ākkam iṉmi eṉa ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu oṭṭuka. ituvum^ ceyki+ cūttiram. mēl nāṉku cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟiyaṉa valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tiriyumāṟu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (6)

TE149n

laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar+
tanaveṉa variṉ+ ṟaṉavā kummē.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr varumoḻi+ karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷimuṉṉar --- lakāra ṇakārameṉṟu collavarukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, tana eṉa variṉ --- takāramum nakāramum mutaleṉṟu collumpaṭiyāka+ cila coṟkaḷ variṉ, ṟaṉavākum --- niraṉiṟiyāṉē avi ṟakāra ṉakāraṅkaḷāka+ tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu kaṉṉaṉṟu poṉṟītu poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (7)

TE150n

ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉa+ tōṉṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ --- ṇakāra ḷakāra meṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar atikārattāl takāra nakāraṅkaḷ varumeṉiṉ, ṭaṇaveṉa+ tōṉṟum --- avi niraṉiṟiyāṉē ṭakāra ṇakāraṅkaḷāy+ tirintu tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṭītu maṇṇaṉṟu muḵṭītu muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum. nilimoḻittiripu tattam īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟupa. (8)

TE151n

uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉili+ kiḷaviyum
puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉili+ kiḷaviyu
miyalpā kunavu muṟaḻā kunavumeṉ
ṟāyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.

itu muṉṉiliviṉiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya muṉṉili+ kiḷaviyum --- uyirīṟāyvanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉili+ kiḷaviyum --- puḷḷiyīṟāy vanta muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkaḷum, valleḻuttuvariṉ --- valleḻuttu mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, iyalpākunavum uṟaḻākunavumeṉṟu āyīriyala --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavumeṉa avviraṇṭu iyalpiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṟikoṟṟā koṇākoṟṟā uṇkoṟṟā tiṉkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi iyalpu. naṭakoṟṟā naṭakkoṟṟā īrkoṟṟā īrkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci. īṟeṉṟu ōtiṉamiyiṉ viṉiccollē koḷka. ivi muṉṉiṉṟāṉ toḻi luṇarttuvaṉavum, avaṉi+ toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉavu meṉa iruvakiya. i ai āy mutaliyaṉa toḻiluṇarttuvaṉa. naṭa vā mutaliyaṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum^ toḻiṟpaṭuttuvaṉa. nilkoṟṟā niṟkoṟṟā eṉa+ tirintuṟaḻntaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavu meṉṉum potuvakiyāṉ muṭikka. iyalpu muṟaḻvu meṉṟiraṇṭiyalpiṉa eṉṉātu ākunavu meṉṟataṉāṉ tukkoṟṟā nokkoṟṟā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā eṉa ōreḻuttu orumoḻi muṉṉiliviṉiccol mikkē muṭitalkoḷka. (9)

TE152n

auveṉa varūu muyiriṟu collum
ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyum^
kuṟṟiya lukarattu iṟutiyu muḷappaṭa
muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā muṉṉili moḻikkē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ+ cila ākātaṉavaṟṟi varintu uṇarttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum --- auveṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟu+ collum, ñanamava eṉṉum puḷḷiyiṟutiyum --- ñanamava eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutiyum --- kuṟṟiyalukaratti iṟutiyilēyuṭiya collum, muṉṉilimoḻikku uḷappaṭa muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā --- muṉṉar muṉṉili moḻikku+ porunta+ kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻcciyumākiya muṭipiṟku muṟṟa+ tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ nilimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭital koḷka.

utāraṇam: kuvukoṟṟā kuvukkoṟṟā, vuvukoṟṟā vuvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā uriñukkoṟṟā, porutukoṟṟā porutukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā tevvukkoṟṟā, kūṭṭukoṟṟā kūṭṭukkoṟṟā eṉa varum. (10)

TE153n

uyirī ṟākiya vuyartiṇi+ peyarum
puḷḷi yiṟuti yuyartiṇi+ peyaru
mellā vaḻiyu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu uyartiṇippeyar vaṉkaṇam meṉkaṇam iṭikkaṇam uyirkkaṇa meṉṉum nāṉku kaṇattiṉum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇippeyarum --- uyirīṟāy vanta uyartiṇi+ peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇi+ peyarum --- puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉiyuṭiya uyartiṇi+ peyarkaḷum, ellāvaḻiyum --- nāṉku kaṇattu alvaḻiyam vēṟṟumiyumākiya ellā iṭattum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷi `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭippārum uḷar. atu poruntātu, ivvāciriyar uyartiṇi+ peyarum viravuppeyarum eṭuttōtiyē muṭippārātaliṉ.

utāraṇam: nampi avaṉ eṉavum, naṅki avaḷ eṉavum niṟutti, kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ eṉavum, yāvaṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum, yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭintāṉ āyiṉāṉ auviyattāṉ eṉavum, aḻakiyaḷ āṭiṉāḷ auviyattāḷ eṉavum oṭṭuka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum, yāppu vaṉmi eṉavum, aḻaku auviyam eṉavum ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. oruvēṉ eṉa+ taṉmi+ peyarkkaṇṇum^ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ eṉavum, ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum oṭṭuka. nī muṉṉili viravu+ peyar ātaliṉ īṇṭikku* ākā.

iṉi, uyirīṟu puḷḷiyiṟuti eṉṟa mikiyāṉē uyartiṇippeyar tirintu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. kapilaparaṇar, iṟivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuvamāṇikkar eṉa ṉakara īṟu keṭṭu iyalpāy muṭintaṉa. ācīvakappaḷḷi nikkantakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi avvīṟu keṭṭu oṟṟu mikku muṭintaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam kumarakkōṭṭam, piramakōṭṭam piramakkōṭṭam eṉa ivi īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntaṉa. vaṇṇārappeṇṭir itu mikku muṭintatu. palcaṅkattār palcāṉṟōr pallaracar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa rakaravīṟum ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa akaramum^ keṭṭu+ piṟaceykikaḷum peṟṟumuṭintaṉa.

iṉi, ellāvaḻiyu meṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇi viṉiccol iyalpāyum^ tirintum muṭivaṉa ellāṅkoḷka. uṇku uṇṭu varutu cēṟu uṇpal uṇṭēṉ uṇpēṉ eṉṉum^ taṉmi viṉikaḷi+ koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu oṭṭuka. uṇṭīr cāṉṟīr pārppīr eṉa muṉṉilikkaṇṇum uṇpa uṇṭār cāṉṟār pārppār eṉa+ paṭarkkikkaṇṇum oṭṭuka. ivi iyalpu. uṇṭaṉem^ cāṉṟēm uṇṭēnām eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa tirintu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa. (11)

TE154n

avaṟṟuḷ
ikara viṟupeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭittē.

iḵtu uyartiṇippeyaruḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātateyvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ ikara iṟupeyar --- muṟkūṟiya uyartiṇippeyarkaḷuḷ ikara īṟṟuppeyar, tiripiṭaṉuṭittu --- iruvaḻiyum^ tirintu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṭṭippū kāvitippū nampippēṟu eṉa ivvuyartiṇi+ peyarkaḷ vēṟṟumikkaṇ mikku muṭintaṉa. eṭṭi kāviti eṉpaṉa tēyavaḻakkākiya ciṟappuppeyar. eṭṭimaram aṉṟu. aḵtu `eṭṭikkumaraṉiruntōṉṟaṉṉi' eṉpataṉāṉ uṇarka. ivi eṭṭiyatupū eṭṭikkuppū eṉa viriyum. iṉi nampikkollaṉ nampiccāṉṟāṉ nampittuṇi nampippiḷḷi eṉavum, ceṭṭikkūttaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉeṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ uyartiṇippeyar mikku muṭintaṉa. iṭaṉuṭitteṉṟataṉāṉ ikarīṟallātaṉavum īṟu tiriyātu niṉṟu valleḻuttuppeṟutal koḷka. naṅkippeṇ naṅkiccāṉi eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ ciṟupāṉmi aikāra īṟu mikkaṉa. ivvīṟṟaḵṟiṇippeyar mikkumuṭital uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷ+ kūṟupa. (12)

TE155n

aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē.

itu viravuppeyaruḷ iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḵṟiṇiviravuppeyar --- uyartiṇippeyarōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeyar, iyalpu māruḷa --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ummiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

uyartiṇippeyarōṭu aḵṟiṇiceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟateṉṉi? collatikārattu `irutiṇi+ coṟkumō raṉṉa vurimiyiṉ' ((col//172)) eṉṟu cūttiram^ ceyvārāleṉiṉ, atuvum poruntumāṟu kūṟutum. cāttaṉ cātti, muṭavaṉ muṭatti eṉavarum viravuppeyarkkaṇ uyartiṇikku urittāka ōtiya āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiniṉṟa īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷē aḵṟiṇi yāṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttiṟṟeṉṟal vēṇṭum; eṉṉi? aḵṟiṇikku orumippālum paṉmippālum uṇarttum īṟaṉṟi āṇpālum peṇpālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ uḷavāka āciriyar ōtāmiyiṉ; aṅṅaṉam uyartiṇi irupālum uṇarttum īṟukaḷ niṉṟē aḵṟiṇiyāṇpāliyum peṇpāliyum uṇarttutaliṉ aḵṟiṇi uyartiṇiyōṭu ceṉṟu viraviṟṟeṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ uṇmi+ taṉmi+ tōṟṟam^ kūṟuvāṉ īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟē viḷimarapiṉkaṇ+ `kiḷantaviṟuti yaḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar' ((col//150)) eṉpuḻiyum āciriyar uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viraviya viravuppeyareṉa āṇṭum uṇmi+ taṉmi+ tōṟṟam^ kūṟuvar. māṇākkaṉ iṉitu uṇartaṟku ivvāṟu viravuppeyariṉatu uṇmittaṉmi+ tōṟṟam iraṇṭu atikārattuṅkūṟi, avviravuppeyar vaḻakkiṉkaṇ irutiṇipporuḷum uṇartti irutiṇi+ collāy niṟṟaṟkum otta urimiyavāmeṉa+ pulappaṭa niṟkumāṟu kāṭṭiṉāreṉṟu uṇarka.

iṉi, avi alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāy niṟkumāṟu: --- cāttaṉ koṟṟaṉ cātti koṟṟi eṉa niṟutti+ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ kuṟiyaḷ ciṟiyaḷ tīyaḷ periyaḷ eṉavum, ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāvaṉ valiyaṉ ñāṉṟāḷ nīṇṭāḷ māṇṭāḷ yāvaḷ valiyaḷ eṉavum, aṭintāṉ auvittāṉ aṭintāḷ auvittāḷ eṉavam nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭi uṇarka. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci yāppu vaṉmi aḻaku auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ ṉakāram niṟpa+ takāra nakāram vantuḻi+ tiriyum utāraṇam īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka. iṉi+ cāttaṉkuṟitu cāttikuṟitu eṉa aḵṟiṇi muṭipēṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. ivaṟṟoṭu viṉiccol talippeyya ivi irutiṇikkum uriyavām. āṇṭu nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivataṉi īṇṭu+ tokuttār. iḵtu uyartiṇikkum. ummiyāṉ iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa ṉakāra īṟṟuḷ+ kāṭṭutum. (13)

TE156n

puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟiyāṉ+
ṟammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ
meymmi yākalu muṟaḻa+ tōṉṟalu
mammuṟi yiraṇṭu muriyavi yuḷavē
vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṉ+ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.

itu mēl uyirīṟṟiṟkum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ kūṟum muṭipu peṟātu niṟkum mūṉṟām vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcol --- mūṉṟāvataṟku uriya viṉimutaṟporuḷāṉuḷavākiya toḻiṟcol, puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉum uyiriṟukiḷavi muṉṉum variṉ --- puḷḷiyīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum uyirīṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉarum varumāyiṉ, meymmiyākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalum ammuṟiyiraṇṭum uriyavi uḷa --- avaṟṟuḷ iyalpākalum uṟaḻattōṉṟalumākiya ammuṟi yiraṇṭum peṟutaṟku uriya uḷavātalāl, vēṟṟumi maruṅkiṉ+ colliya muṟiyāṉ vēṇṭum valleḻuttumikuti --- uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷḷum vēṟṟumippuṇarccikku+ colliya muṟiyāṉ virumpum valleḻuttu mikutiyi, pōṟṟal --- īṇṭu+ koḷḷaṟka eṉṟavāṟu.

meymmi paṭṭāṅkātaliṉ iyalpām.

utāraṇam: nāy puli eṉa niṟutti+ kōṭpaṭṭāṉ cārappaṭṭāṉ tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa varuvittu iyalpāyavāṟu kāṇka. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivi uṟaḻntaṉa. ivi nāṟpatteṭṭu+ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ muṭivaṉavaṟṟi+ tokuttār.

puḷḷiyiṟuti uyiriṟukiḷavi eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeykkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ekarappēṟum uṟaḻccikku+ koṭukka. ammuṟiyiraṇṭu muriyavi yuḷavē eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilimoḻiyoṟṟu+ tiritalum^ koḷka. ivvīṟukaḷ nāykkāl tērkkāl kiḷikkāl eṉa āṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikumāṟu kāṇka. (14)

TE157n

mel=eḻuttu miku-vaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟal um
val=eḻuttu miku-vaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟal um
iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟal um
uyir mika varu-vaḻi y-uyir keṭa varutal um^
cāriyi y-uḷ-vaḻi+ cāriyi keṭutal-um^
cāriyi y-uḷ-vaḻi+ taṉ=urupu niliyal-um^
cāriyi y-iyaṟki y-uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟal um
uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutal um
aḵṟiṇi viravu+peyarkku av= iyal* ^niliyal um*
mey piṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟki y-ātal um
aṉṉa piṟa v-um^ taṉ= iyal% maruṅkiṉ
mey-peṟa+ kiḷantu poruḷ varintu* icikkum
aikāram* vēṟṟumi+ tiripu* eṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu iraṇṭām vēṟṟumi+ tiripu tokuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum --- `marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//217)) eṉṟataṉāṉ viḷaṅkuṟittāṉeṉa melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu viḷakkuṟittāṉeṉa valleḻuttu tōṉṟutalum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum --- `makaraviṟuti'(eḻu//310) eṉpataṉāṉ marakkuṟittāṉ eṉa valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu maraṅkuṟittāṉ eṉa melleḻuttu+ tōṉṟutalum, iyaṟki maruṅkiṉ mikaṟki tōṉṟalum --- `tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟkiyākum' ((eḻu//358)) eṉṟavaḻi+ tāykoli eṉa iyalpāy varumiṭattu+ tāykkoli eṉa mikuti tōṉṟalum, uyirmika varuvaḻi uyirkeṭa varutalum --- `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum'(eḻu//226) eṉavum^ `kuṟṟeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉavum `ēyeṉiṟutikku' ((eḻu//277)) eṉavum^ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ uyirmikku varumiṭattu+ palākkuṟittāṉ kaḻukkoṇarntāṉ ēkkaṭṭiṉāṉ eṉa uyirkeṭa varutalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ cāriyi keṭutalum `vaṇṭum peṇṭum' (eḻu//420 eṉpataṉāṉ+ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭu koṇarntāṉ eṉa+ cāriyi keṭṭu niṟṟalum, cāriyi uḷvaḻi+ taṉṉurupu niliyalum --- `vaṇṭum peṇṭum' eṉpataṉāṉ+ cāriyippēṟu uḷḷa iṭattu vaṇṭiṉi+ koṇarntāṉ eṉa+ taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum (itaṟku valleḻuttuppēṟu īṟṟuvakiyāṉ+ koḷka), cāriyi iyaṟki uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟalum --- `puḷimarakkiḷavikku' ((eḻu//244))eṉavum `paṉiyumariyum' ((eḻu//283)) eṉavam `pūlvēleṉṟā' ((eḻu//375)) eṉavum peṟṟa cāriyi peṟātu iyalpāyniṉṟu puḷikuṟittāṉ puḷikkuṟittāṉ paṉitaṭintāṉ paṉittaṭintāṉ pūlkuṟittāṉ pūṟkuṟittāṉ eṉa mikkum^ tirintum uṟaḻcciyāka+ tōṉṟalum, uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum --- `uyirīṟākiya vuyartiṇi+ peyarum' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy varumeṉṟavi nampiyi+ koṇarntāṉ naṅkiyi+ koṇarntāṉ eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭaṉurupu tokātē niṟṟalum (oḻiyātu eṉṟataṉāṉ makaṉ+ peṟṟāṉ makaḷ+ peṟṟāṉ eṉavum `āṭūuvaṟicol' `maḻavarōṭṭiya' `avarkkaṇ ṭemmuḷ' eṉavum oḻintum varumeṉṟu koḷka), aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyarkku avviyal niliyalum --- uyartiṇiyōṭu aḵṟiṇi viravam peyarkku+ koṟṟaṉi+ koṇarntāṉeṉa urupu tokātē niṟṟalum, (avviyal niliyalum eṉṟataṉāṉē makappeṟṟēṉeṉa viravu+ peyarkkaṇṇum^ tokutal koḷka urupiyaluḷ+ `tēruṅkāli' ((eḻu//202)) eṉṟa ilēcāṉ itaṟkam muṉṉiyataṟkum valleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka), meypiṟitākiṭattu iyaṟkiyātalum --- puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ ṇakāra ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttiyiyiṉ meypiṟitāmeṉṟa iṭattu meypiṟitākātu maṇkoṇarntāṉ poṉ koṇarntāṉ eṉa iyaṟkiyāy varutalum, aṉṉa piṟavum --- avipōlvaṉa piṟavum, (avi `ekkaṇṭu peyarum^ kāli yāḻaniṉ kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi' eṉavam `nappuṇarvillā nayaṉillōr naṭpu' eṉavum varuvaḻi ekkaṇṭu nappuṇarvu eṉṉum^ toṭakkaṅkuṟukum uyartiṇi+ peyarkaḷ melleḻuttu+ peṟutaṟku uriyaṉa valleḻuttu+ peṟutal koḷka. iṉṉum^ tiṉipiḷantāṉ mayirkuṟittāṉ taṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṟuttāṉ pukaḻntāṉ eṉa varum) taṉ iyalmaruṅkiṉ --- taṉṉiyē nōkki+ tiripu naṭakkumiṭattu, meypeṟa+ kiḷantu poruḷvarintu icikkum --- poruḷpeṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭu ēṉi vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭipiṉi+ tāṉ nīkki vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumi+ tiripeṉa moḻipa --- iraṇṭām vēṟṟumiyatu vēṟupaṭṭa puṇarcci eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē cāriyiyuḷvaḻi+ taṉṉarupu niliyātu ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉavum piṟavaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. `maṟaṅkaṭinta varuṅkaṟpiṉ' eṉavum `āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉ' eṉavum piṟāṇṭum perumpāṉmiyum varumeṉṟu koḷka. mikoṇarntāṉ mikkoṇarntāṉ vilkōḷ viṟkōḷ eṉa varum. iṉi, ivvāṟu tiriyātu akattōttiṉ+ kūṟiya potumuṭipē tamakku muṭipāka varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. avi kaṭukkuṟittāṉ ceppukkoṇarntāṉ eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉa. `tammiṉākiya toḻiṟcoṉ muṉvariṉ' ((eḻu//156)) eṉṟa atikārattāṉ viṉivantuḻiyē iṅṅaṉam perumpāṉmi tirivateṉṟu uṇarka. iṉi+ taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṟkum viṉiyōṭu muṭivuḻi+ tirital koḷka. atu `varipāy varuṭi' `pulampukkaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟkāḷi' ((puṟam//258)) eṉṟāṟpōla varum. (15)

TE158n

vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi iai eṉṉu
mīṟṟuppeyar+ kiḷavi mūvaki niliya
vavitā
miyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavu
muṟaḻā kunavu meṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟuppeyarkkum aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi --- vēṟṟumiyallā iṭattu, iai eṉṉum īṟṟuppeyarkkiḷavi mūvaki niliya --- i ai eṉṉum īṟṟiyuṭiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakiyākiya muṭipuniliyiyuṭiya, avitām --- ammuṭipukaṭām, iyalpākunavum --- iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum, valleḻuttu mikunavum --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum --- uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, eṉmaṉār pulavar --- eṉa iviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paruttikuṟitu nārikuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa ivi iyalpu. mācittiṅkaḷ cittirittiṅkaḷ alikkoṟṟaṉ pulikkoṟṟaṉ kāvikkaṇ kuvaḷikkaṇ eṉa ivi mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu kiḷikkuṟitu tiṉikuṟitu tiṉikkuṟitu eṉa ivi uṟaḻcci.

peyarkkiḷavi mūvakiniliya veṉavē peyarallāta iraṇṭīṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum uriccollum iyalpum mikutiyumākiya iruvaki niliyavām. ollikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāra īṟṟu viṉiccol mikuti. `iṉi yaṇi' ((eḻu//236)) yeṉpataṉkaṇ ikara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam eṭuttōtupa. ivaṟṟiṟku iyalpuvantuḻi+ kāṇka. `ceṉmati pāka' iḵtu ikara īṟṟu iṭiccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻi+ kāṇka. `tilli+ collē' iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉ mikuti. iyalpu vantuḻi+ kāṇka. `kaṭikā' iḵtu ikara īṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. mikuti vantuḻi+ kāṇka. paṇittōḷ iḵtu aikāra īṟṟu uriccoṉmikuti. iyalpu vantuḻi+ kāṇka. (16)

TE159n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya vikara viṟutiyu
mekaramutal viṉāvi ṉikara viṟutiyum^
cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya viye ṉiṟutiyum
yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉiye ṉiṟutiyum
valleḻuttu mikunavu muṟaḻā kunavum^
colliyaṉ maruṅki ṉuḷaveṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu ēḻāmvēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷuṇartti niṉṟa ikara aikāra īṟṟu iṭiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: colliyal maruṅkiṉ --- ikara aikāraṅkaṭku muṉṉar+ kūṟiya mūvaki yilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ iyalpi nīkki, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, ekara viṉāviṉ mutal ikara iṟutiyum --- ekaramākiya viṉāviṉi mutalākavuṭiya avvikara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- yāveṉ viṉāviṉi mutaṟkaṇuṭiya avvikāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉavum, uṟaḻākunavum --- uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum, uḷaveṉa moḻipa --- uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ utōḷikkoṇṭāṉ etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, āṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ īṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ ūṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ yāṇṭikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivi mikkaṉa. atōḷi avviṭameṉṉum poruṭṭu. avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻikoṇṭāṉ ivvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikoṇṭāṉ uvvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ evvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ eṉa uṟaḻntaṉa. cuṭṭucciṉi nīṇṭataṟkum yāviṉāviṟkum varum aikāra īṟṟukku utāraṇam akkālattu āyiṭikoṇṭāṉ āyiṭikkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum vaḻaṅkiṟṟuppōlum. iṉi, āṅkavi koṇṭāṉ āṅkavikkoṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭuvārum uḷar. avi tiripuṭiyaṉavām. colliyal eṉṟataṉāṉē piṟa aikāra īṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka. aṉṟikkūttar paṇṭiccāṉṟō reṉavum orutiṅkaḷikkuḻavi orunāḷikkuḻavi eṉavum varum. (17)

TE160n

neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutalum^
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru viraṭṭalu
maṟiya+ tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟu meykeṭutalum --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu keṭutalum. kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉ uru iraṭṭalum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉ niṉṟa oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu iraṭṭittalum, aṟiyattōṉṟiya neṟiyiyal eṉpa --- aṟiyumpaṭi vanta aṭippāṭṭiyaleṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvaṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum makāramum lakāramum ḷakāramum eṉa aivakiyām.

utāraṇam: kōṇimirntatu tāṉallaṉ nānallar vēṉaṉṟu tōṇaṉṟu eṉa nakaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ neṭiyataṉmuṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭṭatu. kōṟītu vēṟītu eṉa+ takaram varumoḻiyātaṟkaṇ lakāravoṟṟu+ keṭṭatu. ēṉiya vantuḻi+ kāṇka. ivaṟṟi `laṉaveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//146)) `ṇaṉaveṉpuḷḷimuṉ' ((eḻu//150)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭittu+ keṭumāṟu kāṇka. oṟṟiraṭṭuvaṉa ñakāra ṅakāra ḻakāram oḻintaṉa. kaṇṇaḻakitu poṉṉakal tammāṭi collaḻakitu eḷḷaḻakitu neyyakal tevvalaṉ eṉa+ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru iraṭṭiṉa.

mēli+ cūttirattu nāṉkaṉurupu piṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ oṟṟiraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻikkaṇṇateṉṟu uṇarka. kuṟiyatu piṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl tammi emmi niṉṉi eṉa neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum^ kuṟiyataṉmuṉṉaroṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, viraṉaṉṟu kuṟaṇimirntatu eṉa+ kuṟiliṇiyiṉmuṉṉar vanta oṟṟu+ keṭutalum, varāṟītu naṉṟu eṉa+ kuṟaṉeṭiṟkaṇiṉṟa oṟṟu+ keṭutalum, atukoṟōḻi eṉavum^ kuriciṟīyaṉ eṉavum^ toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟuniṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum iṭiccollōṭu oṭṭuppaṭṭuniṟṟaluṅ, kāṟṟītu eṉavum viraṟṟītu eṉavum oṟṟuniṟṟalum^ koḷka. iṉi aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ tēṉṟītu kāṇṭītu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa keṭāminiṟṟaluṅ, keṭutalum^ takaranakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻiyeṉpatūum^ koḷka. neṟiyiyaleṉṟataṉāṉ+ cuṭṭiṉmuṉ uyirmutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvaṭi avvāṭi eṉa iṭivakaravoṟṟu ilvaḻiyum iraṭṭutal koḷka. (18)

TE161n

āṟa ṉurupiṉu nāṉka ṉurupiṉum^
kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli
yīṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyu
neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.

iḵtu urupiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻikkaruvi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻimuṉ āṉa --- neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttu+ kuṟukimuṭiyum aṟuvakippaṭṭa moḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉarvanta, āṟaṉurupiṉum nāṉkaṉurupiṉum --- āṟām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum nāṉkām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭalilli --- muṉṉar nilimoḻikku iraṭṭumeṉṟa kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭivarutalilli, īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum --- nilimoḻiyīṟṟukkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟukkaḷ akarampeṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyalil `nīyeṉṉorupeyar' ((eḻu//176)) eṉavum^ `tām nāmeṉṉum' ((eḻu//188)) eṉavun, `tāṉ yāṉeṉṉum' ((eḻu//192)) eṉavum^ kūṟiyavaṟṟāṉ+ kuṟuki oṟṟiraṭṭi+ tammi nammi emmi taṉṉi niṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa varuvaṉa itaṉāṉē tamatu namatu ematu taṉatu niṉatu eṉatu eṉavum, tamakku namakku emakku taṉakku niṉakku eṉakku eṉavum oṟṟiraṭṭātu akarampeṟṟuvantaṉa. nāṉkāvataṟku oṟṟumikutal `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. āṟaṉurupākiya akaram ēṟimuṭiyāmikku+ kāraṇam `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpuḻi+ kūṟiṉām. oṟṟiraṭṭāmiyum akarappēṟum iraṇṭaṟkum ottavitiyeṉṟu uṇarka. kūṟiyaveṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukāta tam nam num eṉa varum^ cāriyikaṭkum ivviru vitiyum^ koḷka. ellārtamakkum ellānamakkum ellīr numakkum ellārtamatum ellānamatum ellīrnumatum eṉa varum. (19)

TE162n

numme ṉiṟutiyu mannili tiriyātu.

itu neṭumutalkuṟukāta nummeṉkiṉṟatum avviti peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟutiyum --- neṭumutalkuṟukā nummeṉṉum makaravīṟum, annili tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭāmiyum īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyalum eytum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: numatu numakku eṉa varum. (20)

TE163n

ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
yakaramu muyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟki.

itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyali nōkkiyatōr nilimoḻicceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟuti --- ukarappēṟṟōṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki --- yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu avvukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

av= īṟukaḷ āvaṉa puḷḷi-mayaṅkiyal uḷ ukaram peṟum eṉṟu vitikkum pala īṟukaḷ um eṉṟu koḷka. uriñ yāṉā aṉantā ātā ikalā īntā uḻuntā ūrā eyiṉā ēṟā aiyā oḻukkā ōtā auviyā eṉavum, uriñ yātu aḻaku eṉa v-um oṭṭuka. ēṉi+ puḷḷikaḷōṭum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu oṭṭuka.

`ñakāriyoṟṟiya' ((eḻu//296)) eṉpataṉāṉum `ñanamava viyiyiṉum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉpataṉāṉum yakaramum uyirum vantāl ukarampeṟātu iyalpāmeṉpatu peṟutaliṉ īṇṭu vilakkal vēṇṭāveṉiṉ, eṭuttōttil vaḻiyatē uyttuṇarcciyeṉṟu koḷka.

itu mutalāka alvaḻi kūṟukiṉṟār. (21)

TE164n

uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki
yaḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇum^ cuṭṭi
yuḷaveṉa+ paṭṭa vellā+ collum^
tattam^ kiḷavi tammaka+ paṭṭa
mutti varūum^ kālam^ tōṉṟi
ṉotta teṉpa vēyeṉ cāriyi.

iḵtu aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇumākiya peyarkaḷ tammiṉ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki --- uyirum puḷḷiyum^ tamakku īṟāy, aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇum^ cuṭṭi uḷaveṉappaṭṭa ellāccollum --- aḷaviyum niṟiyiyum eṇṇiyum^ karuti varuvaṉavuḷaveṉṟu āciriyar kūṟappaṭṭa ellāccoṟkaḷum, tattam^ kiḷavi tammakappaṭṭa --- tattamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāy+ tammiṉ+ kuṟinta coṟkaḷ, mutti varūum^ kālantōṉṟiṉ --- tammuṉṉē varuṅkālam^ tōṉṟumāyiṉ, ēyeṉ cāriyi ottatu eṉpa --- tām ēyeṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭitali+ poruntiṟṟeṉpār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

munti muttiyeṉa vikāram.

nāḻiyēyāḻākku uḻakkēyāḻākku kalaṉēpatakku eṉa aḷavuppeyar ēkārampeṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. toṭiyēkaḵcu kaḻañkēkuṉṟi koḷḷēyiyavi eṉa niṟippeyar ēkāram peṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. oṉṟēkāl kālēkāṇi kāṇiyēmuntiriki eṉa eṇṇuppeyar ēkārampeṟṟu+ tammuṉṉar+ tammiṉ+ kuṟintaṉa vantaṉa. atikārampaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē kuṟuṇināṉāḻi ainnāḻiyuḻakku eṉa ēkāramiṉṟi varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. (22)

TE165n

ari y-eṉa varūum pāl vari kiḷavikku+
purivatu aṉṟu* āl+ cāriyi y-iyaṟki.

itaṉ poruḷ: ariyeṉavarūum pālvarikiḷavikku --- ammūvakiccoṉ muṉṉar varum ariyeṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟi uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku, cāriyi yiyaṟki purivataṉṟu --- ēyeṉcāriyi peṟuntaṉmi poruntuvataṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

āl aci.

utāraṇam: uḻakkari ceviṭṭari mūvuḻakkari eṉavum, kaḵcari kaḻañcari toṭiyari koḷḷari eṉavum, oṉṟari pattari eṉavum ivi ēyeṉcāriyi peṟāvāy vantaṉa. purivataṉṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kalavariyeṉpataṉi oṟṟukkeṭuttu+ ceykiceytu muṭikka. itaṉāṉē ceviṭṭariyeṉpuḻi ṭakaravoṟṟu mikutalum^ koḷka. `oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku' ((eḻu//132)) aṉmiyiṉ+ cāriyi peṟāvāyiṉaveṉṟālōveṉiṉ avi peṟṟum peṟātum varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ kūṟiyatākalāṉum itu `tammakappaṭṭa' ((eḻu//164)) eṉa varintōtiṉamiyāṉum vilakkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. (23)

TE166n

kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kāli
niṟiya+ tōṉṟum vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu, ēyeṉcāriyi vilakki vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvarukāli --- kuṟiyeṉṉuñcol aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattiṟku, vēṟṟumiyiyaṟki --- vēṟṟumippuṇarcci muṭipiṟku urittāka+ kūṟuntaṉmi, niṟiyattōṉṟum --- nirampattōṉṟam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: urikkuṟi kalakkuṟi eṉavum, toṭikkuṟi koṭkuṟi eṉavum, kāṇikkuṟi kāṟkuṟi eṉavum varum. uriyanellum^ kuṟinellu meṉka.

vēṟṟumiyiyaṟkiyeṉavē ivi vēṟṟumiyaṉṟāyiṉa. eṉavē urikkuṟiyeṉpataṟku uriyum uḻakkumeṉa+ poruḷāyiṟṟu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa.

muṉvarukāli yeṉṟataṉāṉē kalappayaṟu kalappāku eṉṟāṟpōla+ poruṭpeyarōṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum ivvēṟṟumi muṭipu eytuvikka. pākeṉṟatu pākkiṉi. niṟiyaveṉṟataṉāṉē urikkūṟu toṭikkūṟu kāṇikkūṟu eṉa+ kūṟeṉṟaṟkum immuṭipu eytuvikka. (24)

TE167n

kuṟṟiyal ukarakku iṉṉē cāriyi.

itu vēṟṟumimuṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukarakku+ cāriyi --- kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu aḷavuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku+ kuṟiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum vaḻi varuñcāriyi, iṉṉē --- iṉcāriyiyēyām eṉṟavāṟu.

kuṟṟiyalukarakku itaṟku attu vitittu muṭikka. kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṉṉē yeṉpatum pāṭam.

uḻakkiṉkuṟi āḻākkiṉkuṟi eṉavum, kaḻañciṉkuṟi kaḵciṉkuṟi eṉavam, oṉṟiṉkuṟi pattiṉkuṟi eṉavum varum. itaṟku uḻakkum^ kuṟiyumeṉpatu poruḷ. itu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇāyiṉ uḻakkiṟkuṟiyeṉa niṟkum. (25)

TE168n

attiṭi varūum^ kalame ṉaḷavē.

ituvum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, vēṟṟumiviti vilakki attu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kalameṉ aḷavē --- kalameṉṉum aḷavuppeyar kuṟiyōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, attu iṭivarūum --- attu+ cāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

kalattukkuṟi. itaṉi `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. itaṟku+ kalamum^ kuṟiyumeṉpatu poruḷ. cāriyi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē muṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟavaḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (26)

TE169n

paṉi y-eṉ aḷavu*um^ kāl eṉ* ^niṟi y-um*
niṉiyum^ kāli y-iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

ituvum atu, vēṟṟumi viti vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyeṉ aḷavum^ kāveṉ niṟiyum --- paṉiyeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarum^ kāveṉṉum niṟippeyarum^ kuṟiyeṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, niṉiyuṅkāli iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- ārāyuṅkālattu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

paṉiyiṉkuṟi kāviṉkuṟi eṉavarum. iviyum ummittoki. niṉiyuṅkāli yeṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikku uriya vitiyeyti valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum^ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka. paṉikkuṟi kākkuṟi eṉavarum.

ittuṇiyum alvaḻimuṭipu. ivaṟṟi `vēṟṟumi alvaḻi iai' ((eḻu//158)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟātu vēṟōtiṉār, ivi aḷavum niṟiyum eṇṇumātaliṉ. (27)

TE170n

aḷaviṟku niṟiyiṟku moḻimuta lāki
yuḷaveṉa+ paṭṭa voṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
yavitām^
kacatapa veṉṟā namava veṉṟā
vakara vukaramō ṭaviyeṉa moḻipa.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ aḷaviṟkum niṟikkum moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttu iṉittu eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷaviṟkum niṟiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷaveṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttē --- aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟippeyarkkum moḻikku mutalāyuḷḷaveṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttukkaḷ, avitām^ kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā akaravukaramoṭu aviyeṉa moḻipa --- avveḻuttukkaḷ tām^ kacatapakkaḷum namavakkaḷum akara ukaramākiya aviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku ivi aḷavu. kaḻañcu cīrakam toṭi palam niṟi mā vari anti ivi niṟi. niṟikku ukaramutal+ peyar vantuḻi+ kāṇka. iṉi uḷaveṉappaṭṭa eṉṟataṉāṉē uḷaveṉappaṭātaṉavum uḷa. avi immi ōraṭi iṭā eṉa variyaṟi kūṟātaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tēya vaḻakkāy oruñār oru tuvali eṉpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟiṉār, akattōttiṉuḷ muṭipukūṟiyavaḻi atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēl+ cellātu oḻintakaṇattiṉum^ cellumeṉṟaṟku. eṇṇuppeyar variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṉ+ kūṟārāyiṉār. (28)

TE171n

īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉiviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉa+
kūṟiya kiḷavi+ pallā ṟellā
meyttali+ paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
yottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭi; eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātaṉavaṟṟiṟku ituvē ōttākaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: īṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ --- uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyākiya coṟkaḷ varumoḻiyōṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivaṟṟu iyalpu iviyeṉa+ kūṟiya kiḷavi+ pallāṟellām --- immoḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu iviyeṉakkūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉatu avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātuniṉṟa palavakimuṭipukaḷellām, meyttalippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭucivaṇi --- uṇmiyi+ talippaṭṭa vaḻakkōṭu kūṭi, puṇarmoḻinili ottavi uriya --- puṇarum moḻikaḷiṉ nilimikkaṇ+ poruntiṉavi uriyavām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: naṭañeḷḷā eṉa uyirīṟākiya muṉṉilikkiḷavi meṉkaṇattōṭu iyalpāy muṭintatu. maṇṇukoṟṟā maṇṇukkoṟṟā maṉṉukoṟṟā maṉṉukkoṟṟā uḷḷukoṟṟā uḷḷukkoṟṟā kollukoṟṟā kollukkoṟṟā eṉpaṉa puḷḷiyiṟuti muṉṉilikkiḷavi ukarampeṟṟum uṟaḻntum muṭintaṉa. uriñuñeḷḷā iḵtu `auveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//152)) eṉpataṉ oḻipu. patakkanāṉāḻi patakkamunnāḻi eṉa ivi ēyeṉcāriyi peṟātu akkuppeṟṟu ataṉ iṟuti meymmiciyoṭum^ keṭṭu+ puṇarntaṉa. vāṭṭāṉi tōṟṟaṇṭi eṉpaṉa takaram vantuḻi+ tirintu neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭātu niṉṟaṉa. cīrakari eṉpataṉi+ cīrakam ari yeṉa niṟutti+ kakaravoṟṟiṉ mēlēṟiṉa akarattiyum makaravoṟṟiyum^ keṭuttu ariyeṉpataṉ akaratti yēṟṟi muṭikka. kalavari yeṉpataṉi+ kalari eṉa muṭikka. akara makaram^ keṭuttu nākaṇi yeṉa+ piṟavum varuvaṉa vellām iccūttirattāṉ muṭikka. (29)

TE172n

palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
peyariṭi vakaram^ keṭutalu mēṉi
yoṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭi
yoṉṟiya vakaram varutalu miraṇṭu
maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ+ ṟiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.

itu marūuccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum peyariṭi vakaram^ keṭutalum --- palari yaṟiyum avareṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvareṉṉum peyar iṭiyil vakaraṅkeṭutalum, ēṉi oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉā iṭiyoṉṟiya vakaram varutalum --- oḻinta oṉṟaṉi aṟiyum atuveṉṉum^ colliṉmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāteṉṉum viṉāccol iṭiyilē uyirōṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum --- ākiya aviyiraṇṭum, maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ maṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum --- marumuṭipiṉ pakutiyiṭattu mikavum payiṉṟu tiriyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaryār eṉavum atu yāvatu eṉavum varum. avar yāvareṉpatu vakaraṅkeṭṭu avaryāreṉa niṉṟavaḻi `yāa reṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi' ((col//210)) eṉṟu viṉiyiyaluḷ+ kūṟum viṉikkuṟippu+ collām piṟaveṉiṉ, ākātu; avvakaraṅkeṭṭālum īṇṭu yāvareṉṉum peyarttaṉmiyāyē niṟṟaliṉ. atu peṟṟavāṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, īṇṭu+ palaraṟi coṉmuṉ vanta yāvareṉpataṉ vakaram^ keṭumeṉavē, ēṉi avaṉ avaḷ eṉṉum irupāṉ muṉṉum yāvareṉpatu vārāteṉṟum atu tirintu maruvāy niṟkumeṉṟum^ kūṟutalāṉum `yāvareṉṉum peyariṭi' eṉpataṉāṉum peṟṟām. itaṉāṉē avaṉ yāvar avaḷyāvar eṉṟāl+ pālvaḻuvāmeṉpatu peṟṟām. itaṉi `yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉu --- māvayiṉ mūṉṟōṭu ((col//192)) eṉa+ peyarāka ōtiyavāṟṟāṉ uṇarka. aṉṟiyum yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉ kiḷavi muppāṟkum uritteṉṟu yāreṉṉum viṉāviṉi+ kuṟippiṉi avaṉyār avaḷyār avaryār eṉa muppāṟkum oppa urimi kūṟutalāṉum atu vēṟeṉpatu peṟṟām. atu viṉiyiyaluḷ ōtiṉamiyāṉuṉam `viṉāviṟ kēṟṟal' ((col//66)) eṉa+ payaṉiliyāka ōtiṉamiyāṉum vēṟāyiṟṟu.

iṉi `yāryārkkaṇṭēyuvappar' eṉa+ palaraṟicoṉ muṉṉaraṉṟi iyalpāka vanta yāreṉpatu yāṇṭu aṭaṅkumeṉiṉ atuvum yāri yāri+ kaṇṭeṉa urupu viritaliṉ yāvari eṉṉum vakaraṅkeṭṭa peyarēyām. aṅṅaṉam nilimoḻi varumoḻiyāy niṟṟal payiṉṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. itaṉāṉē `yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum' eṉa ēṉi oṉṟaṟicollum nilimoḻiyāy niṟṟal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē yāravar yāvatatu eṉa ivviru collum nilimoḻiyāy varutal koḷka. (30)

((tokimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu))

((6. urupiyal))

TE173n

aā uū ēau veṉṉu
mappā lāṟa ṉilimoḻi muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yurupiṟ kiṉṉē cāriyi.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

urupukaḷōṭu peyar puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ivvōttu urupiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟṟokuttu+ puṇarttataṉi īṇṭu virittu+ puṇarkkiṉṟārākaliṉ, ivvōttu+ tokimarapōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu. iccūttiram akara mutaliya īṟṟāṉ varum āṟu īṟṟuccoṟkaḷ niṉṟa iṉ peṟṟu urupiṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. urupiṉ poruḷ paṭavarum puṇarcci mēṟkūṟupa.

itaṉ poruḷ: a ā u ū ē au eṉṉum appāl āṟaṉ nilimoḻi muṉṉar --- a ā u ū ē au eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa akkūṟṟu āṟiṉiyum īṟākavuṭiya nilimoḻikaḷiṉ muṉṉar varukiṉṟa, vēṟṟumi yurupiṟku iṉṉē cāriyi --- vēṟṟumi yurupukaṭku iṭiyē varum^ cāriyi iṉcāriyiyē eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷaviṉi viḷaviṉoṭu viḷaviṟku viḷaviṉatu viḷaviṉkaṇ eṉavum, palāviṉi palāviṉoṭu palāviṟku palāviṉatu palāviṉkaṇ eṉavum, kaṭuviṉi kaṭuviṉoṭu kaṭuviṟku kaṭuviṉatu kaṭuviṉkaṇ eṉavam, taḻūuviṉi taḻūuviṉoṭu taḻūuviṟku taḻūuviṉatu taḻūuviṉkaṇ eṉavum, cēviṉi cēviṉoṭu cēviṟku cēviṉatu cēviṉkaṇ eṉavum, vuviṉi vuviṉoṭu vuviṟku vuviṉatu vuviṉkaṇ eṉavum varum. ivvāṟē ceykiyaṟintu oṭṭuka.

`iṉ= eṉa varūum vēṟṟumi y-urupiṟku*
iṉ= eṉ cāriyi y-iṉmi vēṇṭum.' ((eḻu//131))

eṉavē, ēṉiya iṉpeṟumeṉṟaliṉ, `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ iyalpeṉṟatu vilakkiṟṟām. (1)

TE174n

pallavi nutaliya vakara viṟupeyar
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇa lecca miṉṟē.

iḵtu iṉcāriyi vilakki vaṟṟu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pallavi nutaliya peyariṟu akaram --- paṉmipporuḷi+ karutiṉa peyarkaḷiṉ iṟuti niṉṟa akaram, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇal eccamiṉṟu --- vaṟṟu+ cāriyiyōṭu poruntutali oḻitalilli eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pallavaṟṟi pallavaṟṟoṭu palavaṟṟi palavaṟṟoṭu cillavaṟṟi cillavaṟṟoṭu cilavaṟṟi cilavaṟṟoṭu uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷavaṟṟoṭu illavaṟṟi illavaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka.

eccamiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāṉē mēl iṉ peṟṟaṉa piṟa cāriyiyum peṟutal koḷka. nilātti tulātti makatti eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pallavi nutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ mūṉṟāmurupu vaṟṟuppeṟṟē muṭital koḷka. (2)

TE175n

yāveṉ viṉāvu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāveṉ viṉāvum --- yāveṉṟu collappaṭum ākāra īṟṟu viṉāppeyarum, āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟuppēṟṟiṉ+ ṟiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

yāvaṟṟi yāvaṟṟoṭu eṉavarum. (3)

TE176n

cuṭṭumuta lukara maṉṉoṭu civaṇi
yoṭṭiya meyyoḻittu ukaram^ keṭumē.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal ukaram aṉṉoṭu civaṇi --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol aṉ cāriyiyōṭu porunti, oṭṭiya mey oḻittu ukaram^ keṭum --- tāṉ poruntiya meyyi niṟutti ukaram^ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

ataṉi ataṉoṭu itaṉi itaṉoṭu utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉa varum. atiṉi atiṉoṭu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa maru muṭippuḻi muṭintaṉa. oṭṭiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ cuṭṭumutal ukaramaṉṟi+ piṟa ukaramum uyirvaruvaḻiyiṉ+ keṭuvaṉa koḷka. avi katavu kaḷavu kaṉavu eṉa niṟutti aḻakitu illi eṉa varuvittu ukaram^ keṭuttu muṭikka. ivaṟṟi vakara īṟākki ukaram peṟṟaṉaveṉṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟuvarum vakara īṟukaḷi oḻittu āciriyar `vakara+ kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu' ((eḻu//81)) eṉṟāṟpōla varintōtal kuṉṟakkūṟalām ātaliṉ, avi ukara īṟeṉṟu koḷka. avi celavu varavu taravu uṇavu kaṉavu eṉa vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum `puṉkaṇ ṇuṭittāl+ puṇarvu' `pāṭaṟiyātāṉi yiravu' `kaṇṇāra+ kāṇakkatavu' eṉa+ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payiṉṟuvarumāṟu uṇarka. (4)

TE177n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya viye ṉiṟuti
vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalu murittē.

iḵtu aikāra īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutalākiya aiyeṉiṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaṟṟoṭu civaṇi niṟṟalum urittu --- vaṟṟuccāriyiyōṭu porunti niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu. ummiyāṉ vaṟṟoṭu cila urupiṉkaṇ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉṟavāṟu.

aviyaṟṟi aviyaṟṟoṭu iviyaṟṟi iviyaṟṟoṭu uviyaṟṟi uviyaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iṅṅaṉam aikāram niṟka vaṟṟu vantuḻi `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' ((eḻu//122)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka.

iṉi, ummiyāṉ aviyaṟṟiṟku aviyaṟṟiṉkaṇa, eṉa nāṉkāvatum ēḻāvatum vaṟṟum iṉṉum peṟṟu vantavāṟu kāṇka. iṉi, oṉṟeṉa muṭittaleṉpataṉāṉ+ pallavi nutaliya akara īṟṟiṟkum ivviraṇṭu urupiṉkaṇ vaṟṟum iṉṉum^ koṭuttu+ palavaṟṟiṟku palavaṟṟiṉkaṇ eṉa muṭikka. itu mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum okkum. (5)

TE178n

yāveṉ viṉāvi ṉiye ṉiṟutiyu
māyiya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulava
rāvayiṉ vakara miyoṭum^ keṭumē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāviṉiyuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuccollum, āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya cuṭṭumutal aikārampōla vaṟṟuppeṟum avviyalpil tiriyāteṉṟu colluvār āciriyar, āvayiṉ vakaram aiyoṭum^ keṭum --- avvīṟṟiṭattu vakaram aikārattōṭu kūṭakkeṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

yāvaṟṟi yāvaṟṟōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. vakaram vaṟṟumiciyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuvataṉi+ kēṭu ōtiya mikiyāṉē piṟa aikāramum vaṟṟu+ peṟutal koḷka. kariyavaṟṟi kariyavaṟṟoṭu neṭiyavaṟṟi neṭiyavaṟṟoṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟi kuṟiyavaṟṟoṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivi karumi neṭumi kuṟumi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyaraṉṟi+ kariyavi neṭiyavi kuṟiyavi eṉa+ paṇpukoḷpeyarāy niṟṟaliṉ vakara aikāram^ keṭuttu vaṟṟuccāriyi koṭuttu muṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivi `aimpā laṟiyum paṇpu tokumoḻi' aṉmi uṇarka. (6)

TE179n

nīye ṉorupeyar neṭumutal kuṟuku
māvayi ṉakara moṟṟā kummē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nī eṉ orupeyar neṭumutal kuṟukum --- nīyeṉṉum orupeyar taṉmēl niṉṟa neṭitākiya īkāram^ kuṟuki ikaramām, āvayiṉ ṉakaram oṟṟākum --- avviṭattu varum ṉakaram oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: niṉṉi niṉṉoṭu niṉakku eṉa+ ceykiyaṟintu oṭṭuka. niṉakku eṉpataṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉunāṉkaṉurupiṉum' ((eḻu//161)) `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//164)) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka. niṉateṉpataṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. niṉṉeṉpatu vēṟorupeyarō eṉa+ karututali vilakkutaṟku orupeyareṉṟār. peyarkkuṟuppākumeṉṉātu mutalkuṟukumeṉṟatu appeyariṉ eḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu kuṟukka meṉṟaṟku. neṭumutaleṉavē nakaraṅkuṟukutali vilakkiṟṟu. uyirmey yoṟṟumi paṟṟi neṭiyatu mutalāyiṟṟu. `uṭimiyu miṉmiyu moṭuvayi ṉokkum' ((eḻu//132)) eṉṟataṉi nōkki oṭuviṭattu+ cāriyipeṟṟēvanta atikāratti māṟṟutaṟku+ cāriyippēṟṟiṭi eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiṉār. (7)

TE180n

ōkāra v-iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyi.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟu iṉṉavāṟu puṇarumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōkāra iṟutikku oṉṉē cāriyi --- ōkāra īṟṟiṟku iṭivarum^ cāriyi oṉcāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

kōoṉi kōoṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṉēyeṉṟa ēkārattāṉ+ perumpāṉmiyāka varum^ cāriyi oṉṉē, ciṟupāṉmi iṉ cāriyi varumeṉṟu koḷka. `oṉṟākaniṉṟa kōviṉiyaṭarkkavanta' eṉavum, kōviṉi kōviṉoṭu cōviṉi cōviṉoṭu ōviṉi ōviṉoṭu eṉavumvarum.

ōveṉpatu matakunīr tāṅkum palaki. (8)

TE181n

aā v-eṉṉu marappeyar+ kiḷavikku
attoṭum^ civaṇum ēḻaṉ urupē.

iḵtu akara ākāra īṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aā eṉṉum marappeyar+ kiḷavikku --- aāveṉṟu collappaṭum maratti uṇarttukiṉṟa peyarākiya colliṟku, ēḻaṉurupu attoṭum^ civaṇum --- ēḻāmurupu iṉṉōṭaṉṟi attōṭum poruntum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷavattukkaṇ palāvattukkaṇ eṉavarum.

`valleḻuttumutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ valleḻuttu+ koṭuttut `teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ `attiṉakara makaramuṉi' ((eḻu//125))+ keṭāmi+ ceykiceytu muṭikka. (9)

TE182n

ña na v-eṉ puḷḷikku iṉ= ē cāriyi.

itu puḷḷiyīṟṟuḷ ñakāra īṟum nakāra īṟum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñana eṉ puḷḷikku iṉṉē cāriyi --- ñanaveṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟukaṭkuvarum^ cāriyi iṉcāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñiṉi uriñiṉoṭu poruniṉi poruniṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (10)

TE183n

cuṭṭumutal vakara miyu meyyum^
keṭṭa viṟuti yiyaṟṟiri piṉṟē.

itu nāṉkumoḻikku īṟām vakara īṟṟuḷ+ cuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal vakaram --- av iv uv eṉṉum^ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟu+ col, aiyum meyyum^ keṭṭa iṟutiyiyal tiripiṉṟu --- aikāramum aikārattāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meyyum^ keṭṭu vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭinta yāvi ((eḻu//178)) eṉṉum aikāra īṟṟu+ colliyalpil tiripiṉṟi vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaṟṟi avaṟṟoṭu, ivaṟṟi ivaṟṟoṭu, uvaṟṟi uvaṟṟoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (11)

TE184n

ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu eytātatu* eytuvittatu; peyarkku* ē y-aṉṟi uriccol vakarattiṟku muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi vakaram iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- oḻinta uriccol vakaram iṉ cāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

tevviṉi tevviṉoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu uriccol= āyiṉum paṭuttal ōciyāṉ+ peyar āyiṟṟu. (12)

TE185n

maḵkāṉ puḷḷi muṉ attu* ē cāriyi.

itu makara īṟu puṇarum āṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḵkāṉ puḷḷi muṉ attu* ē cāriyi --- makaram ākiya puḷḷi y-īṟṟu+ col% muṉ varuñ cāriyi attu+ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maratti marattoṭu nukatti nukattoṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) `attiṉakaram' ((eḻu//125)) eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ muṭikka. (13)

TE186n

iṉṉiṭi varūu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

itu makara īṟṟiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉṉiṭi varūum moḻiyumāruḷa --- makara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷuḷ attēyaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭiyē vantu muṭiyum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

mār aci.

utāraṇam: urumiṉi urumiṉoṭu, tirumiṉi tirumiṉoṭu eṉa voṭṭuka. (14)

TE187n

numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟki yākum.

itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ iṟuti iyaṟkiyākum --- nummeṉṉum makara īṟu mēṟkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟātu iyalpāka muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nummi, nummoṭu, numakku, nummiṉ, numatu, nuṅkaṇ eṉa varum.

numakku numatu eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku `āṟaṉurupiṉu nāṉkaṉurupiṉum' ((eḻu//121)) `nummeṉiṟutiyumannili'(eḻu//162) `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) `āṟaṉurupiṉakarakkiḷavi' ((eḻu//115)) eṉpaṉa koṇarntu muṭikka.

nuṅkaṇeṉpataṟku mēliccūttirattu `mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ makaravoṟṟu+ keṭuttu `valleḻuttu mutaliya' eṉpataṉāṉ melloṟṟu+ koṭukka. iyaṟkiyeṉṟār cāriyi peṟāmi karuti. (15)

TE188n

tānā meṉṉu makara viṟutiyum
yāme ṉiṟutiyu mataṉō raṉṉa
āe ākum yāme ṉiṟuti
yāvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭu
mēṉi yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum.

itu makara īṟṟil muṟkūṟiya muṭipu ovvātaṉavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tām nām eṉṉum makara iṟutiyum yāmeṉ iṟutiyum ataṉōraṉṉa --- tām nāmeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum makara īṟum yāmeṉṉum makara īṟum nummeṉṉum makarīṟupōla attum iṉṉum peṟātu muṭitaliyuṭiya, yāmeṉ iṟuti ā e ākum --- yāmeṉṉum makara īṟṟuccollil ākāram ekaramām, āvayiṉ yakaramey keṭutal vēṇṭum --- avviṭattu niṉṟa yakaramākiya mey keṭutali virumpum āciriyaṉ, ēṉi iraṇṭum neṭumutal kuṟukum --- oḻinta tām nāmeṉṉum iraṇṭum neṭiyavākiya mutalkuṟuki+ tamnam eṉa niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tammi tammoṭu nammi nammoṭu emmi emmoṭu eṉa āṟu urupōṭum oṭṭuka. āṟaṉurupiṟkum nāṉkaṉurupiṟkum^ karuvi yaṟintu muṭikka.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavayiṉmeyyum^ keṭukka. taṅkaṇ naṅkaṇ eṅkaṇ eṉa ēḻaṉurupiṉkaṇ makaraṅkeṭuttu `valleḻuttu mutaliya' ((eḻu//114)) eṉpataṉāṉ melleḻuttu+ koṭukka. (16)

TE189n

ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟa+ tōṉṟu
mummi niliyu miṟuti yāṉa.

itu makara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellāmeṉṉum iṟutimuṉṉar vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟattōṉṟum --- ellāmeṉṉum makara īṟṟuccoṉ muṉṉar attum iṉṉum aṉṟi vaṟṟeṉṉum^ cāriyi muṭiya+ tōṉṟi muṭiyum. ummi niliyum iṟutiyāṉa --- āṇṭu ummeṉṉum^ cāriyi iṟutikkaṇ nilipeṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

makaram vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉa+ keṭukka.

ellāvaṟṟiyum ellāvaṟṟiṉum ellāvaṟṟukkaṇṇum eṉa varum. muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉimuṟṟukarattiṟkum ummiṉ ukaraṅkeṭuttukkoḷka. ellāvaṟṟoṭum ellāvaṟṟukkum ellāvaṟṟatum eṉa varum. muṟṟukaramātaliṉ ēṟi muṭiyā. (17)

TE190n

uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varumē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyartiṇiyāyiṉ --- ellāmeṉa niṉṟa makara īṟṟu viravuppeyar uyartiṇippeyarāmeṉiṉ, namiṭivarum --- nammeṉṉum^ cāriyi iṭiniṉṟa puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

makara īṟṟiṉi mēl vaṟṟiṉmici yoṟṟeṉakkeṭutta atikārattāṉ+ keṭukka. ellānammiyam ellānammiṉum ellānaṅkaṇum eṉa ukarampeṟṟum ellānammoṭum ellānamakkum ellānamatum eṉa ukaraṅkeṭṭum makaram niṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku ellāriyum ellāroṭum eṉpatu poruḷāka oṭṭuka. itaṟka nammuvakuttatē vēṟupāṭu. īṟākupuḷḷi akaramoṭu niṟṟal ((eḻu//161)) nāṉkāvataṟkum āṟāvataṟkum^ koḷka. (18)

TE191n

ellārum eṉṉum paṭarkki iṟuti um
ellīrum eṉṉum* muṉṉili ~iṟuti ~um
oṟṟum ukaram um^ keṭum eṉa moḻipa
niṟṟal vēṇṭum rakara+ puḷḷi
~ummi niliyum iṟutiyāṉa
tam= iṭivarūum paṭarkki mēṉa
num= iṭivarūum* muṉṉili moḻikku* ē.

itu makara īṟṟu uyartiṉippeyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkiyiṟutiyum --- ellārumeṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇi+ paṭarkki+ peyarum, ellīrumeṉṉum muṉṉiliyiṟutiyum --- ellīrumeṉṉum makara īṟṟu uyartiṇi muṉṉilippeyarum, oṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭumeṉa moḻipa --- makaravoṟṟum ataṉmuṉṉiṉṟa ukaramum^ keṭṭu muṭiyumeṉṟa colluvar pulavar, rakara+ puḷḷi niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- av vukaram ēṟiniṉṟa rakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ, iṟutiyāṉa ummi niliyum --- avvirumoḻiyi ṉiṟutikkaṇṇum ummeṉṉum^ cāriyi nilipeṟum, paṭarkki mēṉa tam iṭivarūum --- paṭarkki+ colliṭattu+ tammuccāriyi iṭivarum. muṉṉili moḻikku num iṭivarūum --- muṉṉiliccoṟku nummuccāriyi iṭivarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ellārtammiyum ellārtammiṉum ellārtaṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellārtammoṭum ellārtamakkum ellārtamatum eṉa ukaram^ keṭṭum, makaram niṟkum. ellīrnummiyum ellīrnummiṉum ellīrnuṅkaṇum eṉa ukaram peṟṟum, ellīrnummoṭum ellīrnumakkum ellīrnumatum eṉa ukaram^ keṭṭum makaram niṟkum.

muṉṉar `mey' ((eḻu//188)) eṉṟa ilēcāṟkoṇṭa makarakkēṭu ivaṟṟiṟkum mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka.

paṭarkkippeyar muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉē rakara īṟṟu+ paṭarkkippeyarum muṉṉilippeyarum makara īṟṟu+ taṉmi+ peyarum tam num nam eṉṉum^ cāriyi iṭiyē peṟṟu iṟuti ummuccāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭivaṉa koḷka. kariyār tammiyum cāṉṟārtammiyum eṉavum, kariyīrnummiyum cāṉṟīrnummiyum eṉavum, kariyēnammiyum iruvēnammiyum eṉavum ellāvurupoṭum^ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka. ukaramum oṟṟum eṉṉātataṉāṉ ikkāṭṭiyavaṟṟiṟkellām mūṉṟu urupiṉkaṇṇum ummiṉ ukaram^ keṭutal koḷka. `niṟṟalvēṇṭum rakarappuḷḷi' eṉṟataṉāṉē tammuppeṟāmi varumaviyum^ koḷka. `ellārkku mellā nikaḻpavi' eṉa varum. (19)

TE192n

tāṉyā ṉeṉṉu māyī riṟutiyu
mēṉmu+ peyaroṭum vēṟupā ṭilavē.

itu ṉakara īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉ yāṉ eṉṉum āyī riṟutiyum --- tāṉ yāṉ eṉṟu collappaṭṭa avviraṇṭu ṉakara īṟum, mēl muppeyaroṭum vēṟupāṭu ilavē --- mēl makara īṟṟuḷ+ kūṟiya mūṉṟupeyarōṭum vēṟupāṭiṉṟi+ tāṉeṉpatu kuṟukiyum yāṉeṉpataṉkaṇ ākāram ekaramāy yakaravoṟṟu+ keṭṭum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

taṉṉi eṉṉi eṉa ellāvurupōṭum oṭṭuka. ceykiyaṟintu oṟṟiraṭṭutal `neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar' eṉpataṉuḷ ilēcāṉ+ koḷka. (20)

TE193n

aḻaṉē puḻaṉē yāyiru moḻikku
mattu miṉṉu muṟaḻa+ tōṉṟa
lotta teṉpa vuṇaru mōrē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḻaṉē puḻaṉē āyirumoḻikkum --- aḻaṉ puḻaṉ ākiya avvirumoḻikkum, attum iṉṉum uṟaḻattōṉṟal ottateṉpa --- attuccāriyiyum iṉcāriyiyum māṟivara+ tōṉṟutali+ poruntiṟṟeṉpar, uṇarumōr --- aṟivōr eṉṟavāṟu.

aḻatti aḻaṉiṉi, puḻatti puḻaṉiṉi eṉa+ ceykiyaṟintu ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. ṉakaratti attiṉmici oṟṟu* eṉṟu keṭuttu `attiṉakaram' ((eḻu//125)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. tōṉṟaleṉṟataṉāṉ evaṉ eṉa niṟutti vaṟṟu+ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceyki ceytu evaṟṟi evaṟṟoṭu eṉa muṭikka. ellāvurupiṉōṭum oṭṭuka. evaṟṟi eṉpuḻi nilimoḻivakaram itaṉāṉ+ keṭukka. iṉi ottateṉṟataṉāl ekiṉ eṉa niṟutti attum iṉṉum koṭuttu+ ceyki ceytu ekiṉatti ekiṉiṉi eṉa oṭṭuka. attu iṉitu icittaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār. TE194n

aṉṉeṉ cāriyi yēḻa ṉiṟuti

TE195n

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟutimuṉṉar --- kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, muṟṟattōṉṟam iṉṉeṉ cāriyi --- muṭiyattōṉṟum iṉṉeṉṉum^ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

TE196n

neṭṭeḻuttu impa roṟṟumika+ tōṉṟu

TE198n

eṇṇi ṉiṟuti yaṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

TE199n

oṉṟumuta lāka+ pattūrntu varūu

yāṉiṭi variṉu māṉa milli
yaḵteṉ kiḷavi yāvayiṉ+ keṭumē
yuytal vēṇṭum paḵkāṉ meyyē.

iḵtu oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭu iṟutiyāka niṉṟa kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar ēḻiṉōṭum patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar vantu puṇarntu oṉṟāyniṉṟa coṟkaḷ cāriyi peṟṟu+ tiriyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

`niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam

vantiṭi niliyum' ((eḻu//437))

eṉpataṉāṉ āytam peṟṟatu.

colluṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pattūr kiḷaviyēyaṉṟi `oṉpāṉ mutaṉili' ((eḻu//463)) `oṉpāṟkoṟṟiṭimikumē' ((eḻu//475)) eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavaṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum pakarattuḷ akarampirittu aḵteṉpatukeṭuttu āṉkoṭukka. (27)

yāte ṉiṟutiyum^ cuṭṭumuta lākiya

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarallāta kuṟṟukara īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

yātaṉi yātaṉoṭu eṉavum, ataṉi ataṉoṭu, itaṉi itaṉoṭu, utaṉi utaṉoṭu eṉavum varum. (28)

TE201n

ēḻa ṉurupiṟku+ ticippeyar muṉṉar+

āvayiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kīḻcār kīḻpuṭi, mēlcār mēlpuṭi, teṉcār teṉpuṭi, vaṭacār vaṭapuṭi eṉa+ cāriyiyiṉṟi+ pala vikārappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē kīḻikkuḷam mēlikkuḷam, kīḻiccēri mēliccēri eṉa aikāram peṟutalum^ koḷka. (29)

TE202n

puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyum^
colliya valla vēṉiya vellām^
tērum^ kāli yurupoṭu civaṇi+
cāriyi niliyum^ kaṭappā ṭilavē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttāyatōr puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: colliya alla puḷḷiyiṟutiyum uyiriṟukiḷaviyum --- muṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyīṟum uyirīṟum allāta puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ collum uyirīṟṟu+ collum, ēṉiyavumellām --- muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷ tammuḷoḻintu niṉṟaṉavumellām, tēruṅkāli urupoṭu civaṇi+ cāriyiniliyum^ kaṭappāṭu ila --- ārāyuṅkālattu urupukaḷōṭu porunti+ cāriyi niṉṟu muṭiyum muṟimiyi uṭiyavalla, niṉṟum nillātum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
