ēṉiyavumeṉa ummi virikka. kūṟāta puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ aintu. avi ṇakara yakara rakara lakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām. maṇṇiṉi maṇṇi, vēyiṉi vēyi, nāriṉi nāri, kalliṉi kalli, muḷḷiṉi muḷḷi eṉavarum. uyirīṟṟuḷ oḻintatu ikaram oṉṟumēyātaliṉ, ataṉi+ piṟkūṟiṉār. kiḷiyiṉi kiḷiyi eṉa varum.

iṉi+ tāṉ yāṉ aḻaṉ puḻaṉ eṉṉum ṉakara īṟṟiṉum ēḻeṉṉum ḻakara īṟṟiṉum oḻintaṉa poṉṉiṉi poṉṉi tāḻiṉi tāḻi eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa piṟavumām. iṉi, īkāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tīyiṉi tīyi īyiṉi īyi vīyiṉi vīyi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. aikāra īṟṟuḷ oḻintaṉa tiṉiyiṉi tiṉiyi kaḻiyiṉi kaḻiyi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa piṟavumām. ēṉi īṟukaḷiṉum varuvaṉa uṇarntu koḷka.

mēlē peyarīṟṟu+ ceykiyellām^ tattam īṟṟiṉ kaṇ muṭippārātaliṉ avi īṇṭu+ kūṟalvēṇṭā.

iṉi+ tēruṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉē urupukaḷ nilimoḻiyāka niṉṟu tamporuḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum urupīṟṟu+ ceykiyellām īṇṭu muṭittukkoḷka.

utāraṇam: nampiyi+ koṇarntāṉ maṇṇiṉi+ koṇarntāṉ koṟṟaṉikkoṇarntāṉ eṉa mūvaki+ poruḷōṭum^ kūṭiniṉṟa urupiṟku oṟṟukkoṭukka. maliyoṭu porutatu, mattikiyāṉ+ puṭittāṉ, cāttaṟku+ koṭuttāṉ, ūrkku+ ceṉṟāṉ, kākkiyiṉ+ karitu, kākkiyatu pali, maṭiyuṭpaḻukkāy, taṭāviṉuṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉa urupu kāraṇamāka+ poruḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi iyalpāyum īṟutirintum oṟṟumikkum vantaṉa koḷka.

iṉi+ kaṇ kāl puṟam mutaliyaṉa peyarāyum urupāyum niṟkumātaliṉ avi urupāka+ koḷḷumvaḻi vēṟupaṭum^ ceykikaḷellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. iḵtu urupiyalātaliṉ urupoṭu civaṇi eṉa vēṇṭā, am mikiyāṉē urupupuṇarccikkaṇ+ ceṉṟa cāriyikaḷellām īṟṟu+ poruṇmuṭipu uḷvaḻi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkum^ koḷka. viḷaviṉkōṭu kiḷiyiṉkāl eṉa ellā īṟṟiṉum^ koḷka. nampiyi koṟṟaṉi eṉa uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum^ cāriyipeṟātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka. (30)

((urupiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((7. uyirmayaṅkiyal))

TE203n

akara viṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi+ kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ+
ṟatta motta voṟṟiṭi mikumē.

eṉpatu cūttiram. uyirīṟu niṉṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum^ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi+ kaṇaṅkaḷōṭum mayaṅki+ puṇarum iyalpu uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ ivvōttu uyirmayaṅkiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. mēṟpeyarōṭu urupu puṇarumāṟu kūṟi+ peyarvaruvaḻi urupu tokkuniṉṟa poruṭpuṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ urupiyalōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu.

iccūttiram akarīṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara iṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar --- akaramākiya iṟutiyiyuṭiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar, vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi+ kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ --- vēṟṟumi yallāta viṭattu+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻikaḷ varumoḻiyāy+ tōṉṟumāyiṉ, tattam otta oṟṟu iṭimikum --- tattamakku+ poruntiya akkacatapakkaḷākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viḷakkuṟitu nuṇakkuṟitu atakkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa oṭṭuka. ivi aḵṟiṇi iyal+ peyarākiya eḻuvāy viṉikkuṟippu+ paṇpākiya payaṉiliyōṭu muṭintaṉa.

ottaveṉṟamiyātu tattamotta eṉṟataṉāṉ akara īṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikkum melleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum muṭipum, akaram^ taṉṉi uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi vaṉkaṇattōṭu mikku muṭiyum muṭipum^ koḷka. taṭakki tavakkoṇṭāṉ vayakkaḷiṟu vayappuli kuḻakkaṉṟu eṉavum taṭañcevi kamañcūl eṉavum, akkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum varum. iṉi, iṭiccol valloṟṟu+ peṟṟu varuvaṉa uḷavēl avaṟṟiyum ivvilēciṉāṉ muṭittu+ koḷka. (1)

TE204n

viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvama+ kiḷaviyu
meṉave ṉeccamum^ cuṭṭi ṉiṟutiyu
māṅka veṉṉu muriyaci+ kiḷaviyu
ñāṅkar+ kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum iṭiccollum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- viṉiyi oḻipākavuṭiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, uvamakkiḷaviyum --- uvamavurupāy niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṉaveṉ eccamum --- eṉaveṉṉam vāypāṭṭāl niṉṟa akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, cuṭṭiṉ iṟutiyum --- cuṭṭākiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, āṅka eṉṉum uriyaci+ kiḷaviyum --- āṅkaveṉṉum akara īṟṟu uriyaci yiṭiccollum, ñāṅkar+ kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum --- muṉṉarkkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: {uṇa tāva cāva} eṉa niṟuttik {koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉa valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. iv viṉiyeccam oḻintaṉa ellām iṭiccolleṉṟu uṇarka. {pulipōla+ koṉṟāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉavum, {koḷḷeṉa+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ} eṉavum, {akkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ} eṉavum, āṅka+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ `āṅkakkuyilu mayilum^ kāṭṭi+ kēcavaṉi viṭuttu+ pōkiyōṉē' eṉavum varum. uvamam viṉiyeccaviṉi+ kuṟippēṉum oṉṟaṉōṭu poruvappaṭutal nōkki uvamaviyaliṉkaṇ āciriyar vēṟupaṭutti+ kūṟiṉār. eṉaveṉṉum eccamum irucolliyum iyivikkiṉṟa nilimiyāṉ iṭiccollōttiṉuḷ vēṟōtiṉār. āṅka eṉpatu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantatallāmi `āṅkaveṉṉu muriyaci' eṉṟataṉāṉum `āṅkavuriyaci' eṉṉum iṭiyiyal+ cūttirattāṉum uṇarka. ivi iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyum muṭipu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpuḻi+ kūṟiyatēyām. avi tāva pulipōla koḷḷeṉa āṅka eṉa niṟutti ñanamayava mutaliya moḻi ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu puṇartti iyalpāmāṟu oṭṭi+ koḷka. cuṭṭu mēl+ kūṟupa. (2)

TE205n

cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanama+ tōṉṟi
ṉoṭṭiya voṟṟiṭi mikutal vēṇṭum.

itu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ meṉkaṇam iyalpākum eṉa muṟkūṟiyataṉi vilakki mikku muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ñanama+ tōṉṟiṉ --- akara+ cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar ñanamakkaṇ mutalākiya moḻivariṉ, oṭṭiya oṟṟu iṭimikutal vēṇṭum --- tattamakku+ poruntiṉa oṟṟu iṭimikutali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

aññāṇ annūl ammaṇi eṉavarum.

oṭṭiyaveṉṟataṉāṉ aññeḷintatu annaṉṟu ammāṇṭatu eṉa akaram^ taṉṉiyuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum mikutal koḷka. (3)

TE206n

yavamuṉ variṉē vakara moṟṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yava muṉvariṉ --- yakara vakara mutaṉmoḻi akaraccuṭṭiṉ muṉṉē variṉ, vakaram oṟṟum --- iṭikkaṇ vakaram oṟṟām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avyāḻ avvaḷi eṉa varum.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl akaram^ taṉṉiyuṇara niṉṟa-vaḻi y-um vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḷintatu eṉa varum. (4)

TE207n

uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmuṉ variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu --- uyirkaḷ akaraccuṭṭiṉmuṉ variṉum muṟkūṟiya vakaram mikku varum iyalpiṟṟiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

a eṉa niṉṟa cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉar aṭi eṉa varuvittu vakaram oṟṟi+ taṉṉuru iraṭṭi uyirēṟṟi avvaṭi avvāṭi avvili avvīyam avvural avvūrti avveḻu avvēṇi avviyam avvoḻukkam avvōṭi avvuviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.

`neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//110)) eṉpataṉuḷ `neṟiyiyal' eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu nilimoḻi+ toḻileṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ akaram^ taṉṉi yuṇaraniṉṟavaḻiyum vakaram mikutal koḷka. avvaḻakitu eṉavarum. tiriyāteṉṟataṉāṉ mēṟcuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi vakarakkēṭu koḷka. (5)

TE208n

nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ ceyyuṭku āvatōr viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīṭa varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- akaraccuṭṭu nīṭavarutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `āy iru tiṇiyiṉ icikkumaṉa col= ē' ((col//1)) `āy iru pāl+ col' ((col//3)) eṉa varum. itu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟaliṉ vaṉkaṇam oḻintakaṇam ellāvaṟṟōṭum^ ceṉṟatu. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam vantavaḻi+ kāṇka. innīṭci irumoḻippuṇarccikkaṇ varutaliṉ `nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭal' ākāmi uṇarka. (6)

TE209n

cāva veṉṉum^ ceya ~eṉ eccattu*
iṟuti vakaram^ keṭutal-um urittu* -ē

itu mēl viṉiyeñcukiḷavi eṉṟa eccattiṟku eytātatu* eytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ:
cāva eṉṉuñ ceya ~eṉ eccattu iṟuti vakaram
-- cāva ~eṉṟu collappaṭum^ ceya ~eṉ eccattu iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa akaramum ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa vakaramum,
keṭutalum urittu
-- keṭṭuniṟṟalum urittu eṉravāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōṭṭiṭic cākkuttiṉāṉ eṉa varum cīṟiṉāṉ takarttāṉ puṭittāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. keṭātatu muṉṉar muṭittām.

itaṉi `viṉiyeñcukiḷavi' ((eḻu//204)) eṉṟataṉ piṉ viyātataṉāṉ iyalpukaṇattum innilimoḻikkēṭu koḷka. cā ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ yāttāṉ vīḻttāṉ aṭintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)

TE210n

aṉṉa veṉṉu muvama+ kiḷaviyu
maṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinili+ kiḷaviyum^
ceymmaṉa veṉṉum^ toḻiliṟu collu
mēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ+ kiḷaviyum^
ceyta veṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum^
ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
mamma veṉṉu muripporuḷ+ kiḷaviyum
palavaṟ ṟiṟuti+ peyarkkoṭi yuḷappaṭa
vaṉṟi yaṉittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ orucār peyarkkum viṉikkum iṭikkum muṉṉeytiyatu vilakkiyum eytātateytuvittum ilakkaṇam^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṉṉa eṉṉum uvama+ kiḷaviyum --- aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvamavurupākiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccollum, aṇmi cuṭṭiya viḷinili+ kiḷaviyum --- aṇiyāri+ karutiṉa viḷiyākiya nilimiyiṉiyuṭiya akara īṟṟu uyartiṇi+ peyarccollum, ceymmaṉa eṉṉum^ toḻiliṟucollum --- ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum^ toḻiṟcol+ poruḷ taruṅkāl um īṟṟāṉ iṟum^ collum, ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ+ kiḷaviyum --- oruvarāṉ ēvaṟṟoḻiṉmi karuti+ kūṟappaṭṭa ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittali uṇarttum akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyyiya eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viṉiyeccamākiya akara īṟṟu viṉiccollum, amma eṉṉum uripporuḷ+ kiḷaviyum --- amma eṉṟu collappaṭum etirmukam-ākkiya akara īṟṟu iṭiccol= um, palavaṟṟu iṟutaṭi+ peyarkkoṭi uḷappaṭa --- paṉmi+ poruḷi uṇarttum akara īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷ aintiṉiyum muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu kūṭṭikkoṭuttal uḷḷiṭṭu, aṉṟi aṉittum iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- av= eṭṭu+ col= um iyalpu* āy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: poṉṉaṉṉa kutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa itu `viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi' ((eḻu//204)) eṉpataṉāṉ mikku muṭitali vilakkiṟṟu. ūra kēḷ cel tā pō eṉa `uyirīṟākiyavuyartiṇi' eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ iyalpāymuṭivatu īṇṭu ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu akara īṟāy viḷiyēṟṟu muṭintamiyiṉ eytātateytuvittatu. uṇmaṉakutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku uṇṇumeṉaka virittum yāṉuṇmaṉa nīyuṇmaṉa avaṉuṇmaṉa eṉa niṟutti+ kūḻ cōṟu tēṉ pāl eṉa varuvittum muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟkum avvāṟē virittukkoḷka. iṅṅaṉam^ ceyyumeṉpataṉ poruṭṭākiya maṉave ṉiṟuticcol akkālam vaḻaṅkiyatātaliṉ āciriyar ataṉiyum vēṟāka eṭuttōtiṉār. yāṉum niṉṉōṭuṭaṉvaruka avaṉcelka avaḷcelka avarcelka eṉa niṟutti+ kāṭṭiṉkaṇ ceṟuviṉkaṇ tāṉikkaṇ pōriṉkaṇ eṉa varuvittu muṭikka. ivi ēvaṟporuṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭittaṉa. ēval kaṇṇiya veṉavē ēval kaṇṇātaṉavum uḷavāyiṉa. avi: nīcelka atucelka avicelka eṉa niṟutti muṟkūṟiya kāṭu mutaliyavaṟṟi varuvittu muṭikka. ivi ēval+ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyātaṉa. aḵṟiṇi ēval+ poruṇmiyi muṟṟa muṭiyāmi viṉiyiyaluḷ viyaṅkōḷ+ kaṇṇē poruḷiyalum^ ceyyuḷiyalumpaṟṟi+ kūṟutum. maṉavum viyaṅkōḷum eytātateytuvittatu. uṇṭakutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi ituvum atu. itaṟku uriya uṇṇātakutiriyeṉṉum etirmaṟi y-um nalla kutiri y-eṉṉum^ kuṟippum^ koḷka. uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ itu muṉṉar viṉiyeccam valleḻuttuppeṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eṇtiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. {amma koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā} eṉpatu iṭiccol= ātaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu. {itukēḷāy koṟṟaṉē} eṉa etirmukam-ākkiyavāṟu kāṇka. pallakutiri palakutiri cillakutiri cilakutiri uḷḷakutiri illakutiri cennāy takar paṉṟi eṉa oṭṭuka. ikkālattu+ palla cilla eṉpaṉa vaḻaṅkā. ituvum viḷakkuṟitu eṉṟāṟpōla+ palakkutiriyeṉa valleḻuttu eytiyataṉi vilakkiṟṟu. viḷinili+ kiḷaviyākiya peyar muṟkūṟātataṉāṉē ceyyumeṉpataṉ maṟiyākiya ceyyātaveṉpataṟkum ivviyalpu muṭipukoḷka. atu vārāta koṟṟaṉeṉa varum. ivviyalpu muṭipiṟku+ ceymmaṉa ciṟattaliṉ viyaṅkōṭkumuṉ vittār. ēval kaṇṇiya eṉpataṉāṉ ēval kaṇṇātatum uḷateṉṟukūṟi `maṉṉiyaperumanī' ((puṟam//61)) eṉa utāraṇam^ kāṭṭukaveṉiṉ, atu poruntātu; kūṟukiṉṟāṉ avaṉ nilipeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭumeṉṟē karuti+ kūṟutaliṉ atuvum ēvalkaṇṇiṟṟēyām. ellāvaṟṟiṉum^ ciṟanta palavaṟṟiṟut muṟkūṟukaveṉiṉ, atu vaḻakkiṟkum^ ceyyuṭkum vēṟuvēṟu muṭipuṭitteṉṟaṟkum^ ceyyuṇ muṭipu ivviyalpupōl+ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟaṟkum akara īṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟātu niṉṟa muṟṟuviṉiyum viṉikkuṟippum ivviyalpu muṭipu peṟumeṉṟaṟkum piṉ vittār. uṇṭaṉakutiri itu muṟṟuviṉi. kariyaṉa kutiri itu muṟṟuviṉikkuṟippu. iḵtu iyalpukaṇattu muṭipu `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpuḻi+ poruntuvaṉavellām^ koḷka. (8)

TE211n

vāḻiya veṉṉum^ ceyaveṉ kiḷavi
yiṟuti yakaram^ keṭutalu murittē.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vāḻiya eṉṉum^ ceya eṉ kiḷavi --- {vāḻuṅkālam neṭuṅkālam ākuka} eṉṉum poruḷit tarum vāḻiyaveṉṟu collappaṭum^ ceyaveṉecca+ kiḷavi, iṟutiyakaram^ keṭutalum urittu --- iṟutikkaṇ akaramum ataṉāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa yakaravoṟṟum^ keṭṭu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

keṭutalumeṉavē, keṭātu muṭitalē perumpāṉmiyeṉṟavāṟu.

vāḻikoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavarum. vāḻiya eṉpatē perumpāṉmi. vāḻiya yāṉ nī avaṉ avaḷ avar atu avi eṉa itu mūṉṟiṭattum^ cēṟaliṉ `uyirīṟākiya muṉṉili+ kiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//151)) eṉpuḻi muṉṉiliyiyalpām eṉṟataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. itu kuṟippuviyaṅkōḷ.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ iḵtu iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka. vāḻi ñeḷḷā eṉa varum. ivi vāḻttappaṭum poruḷ vāḻavēṇṭumeṉṉum^ karuttiṉaṉāka+ kūṟutaliṉ ēvalkaṇṇiṟṟēyām. allākkāl `vāḻttiyal vakiyē nāṟpāṟku murittē' ((ceyyuḷiyal//109)) eṉpataṟkum vāḻttiyalāka+ cāṉṟōr kūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷukkum payaṉiṉṟāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (9)

TE212n

uripporuḷ+ kiḷavi nīṭṭamum variyār.

iḵtu ammaveṉpataṟku eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uripporuḷ+ kiḷavi --- etirmukamākkum poruḷiyuṭiya ammaveṉṉum^ col, nīṭṭamum variyār --- akaramāki niṟṟalēyaṉṟi ākāramāy nīṇṭumuṭitaliyum nīkkār eṉṟavāṟu.

ammā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum. ummiyāl nīḷāmiyē perumpāṉmiyām. variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ nīṭci iyalpu kaṇattum^ koḷka. ammā ñeḷḷā nākā māṭā vaṭukā ātā eṉa oṭṭuka. (10)

TE213n

palavaṟṟu* iṟuti nīṭumoḻi yuḷavē
ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiya palavaṟṟiṟutikkaṇ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa --- palavaṟṟi uṇarttum aivakiccolliṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭu muṭiyumoḻikaḷum^ ciluḷa, ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻi āṉa --- yāṇṭuḷaveṉiṉ+ ceyyuḷātali+ karutiya oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarccippaṭum^ ceyyuṇ muṭipuṭiya moḻikaḷiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

uṭitteṉṉātu uḷaveṉṟa paṉmiyāṉ varumoḻikkaṇ+ cila eṉpatu vantu nīṭumeṉṟu koḷka. ceyyuḷāṉa eṉṉātu ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa eṉṟataṉāṟ, pala eṉpataṉ iṟuti akaram nīṇṭuḻi nilimoḻi akarappēṟum varumoḻi ñakaramākiya melleḻuttuppēṟum, varumoḻiyiṟuti nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟum makaramākiya melleḻuttuppēṟum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `palāam^ cilāa meṉmaṉār pulavar'; itaṉ coṉṉiṟu palacila eṉṉum^ cevveṇ. (11)

TE214n

toṭara liṟuti tammuṉ+ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟu* ākalu murittē.

itu palacila eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku iyalpēyaṉṟi+ tiripum uṇṭeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toṭar al iṟuti --- toṭarmoḻiyallāta īreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya palacila eṉṉum akara īṟṟu+ col, tammuṉ tām variṉ --- tammuṉṉē tām varumāyiṉ, lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟu ākalum urittu --- tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟa lakaravoṟṟu ṟakaravoṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummiyāṉ+ ṟiriyāmiyum uritteṉṟār.

utāraṇam: paṟpalakoṇṭār ciṟcilavitti eṉavarum. akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭallāta kuṟṟeḻuttu ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākuvaṉa iṉmiyiṉ toṭaraliṟuti yeṉavē īreḻuttorumoḻiyē uṇarttiṟṟu. taṉmuṉeṉṉātu tammuṉeṉṟa paṉmiyāṉ+ palacila eṉa niṉṟa iraṇṭum^ taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa.

tammuṉvariṉeṉṉātu tāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ palaviṉmuṉ pala varutalum^ cilaviṉmuṉ cilavarutalum^ koḷka. lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟāmeṉa oṟṟiṟku+ tiripu kūṟi akaraṅkeṭutal kūṟiṟṟilareṉiṉ, atu vārātataṉāl vantatumuṭittaleṉṉum utti peṟavittateṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉi ñāpakameṉpārum uḷar. aruttāpattiyāl tammuṉ tām variṉeṉavē tammuṉ piṟavariṉ lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟākātu akaram^ keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum.

utāraṇam: palkaṭal cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, palyāṉi palvēḷvi eṉavum, cilkāṭu cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, cilyāṉi cilvēḷvi eṉavum varum. uritteṉṟatu akara īṟṇorumipaṟṟi. (12)

TE215n

valleḻuttu iyaṟki yuṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum.

itu muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum uḷḷatōr muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu iyaṟki --- muṟkūṟiya palacila veṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum akara īṟṟu+ potuvitiyiṉ+ kūṟiya valleḻuttumikum iyalpu, uṟaḻattōṉṟum --- mikutalum mikāmiyumāy uṟaḻntuvarattōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: palappala palapala cilaccila cilacila eṉavarum. īṇṭum^ tammuṉ tāmvarutal koḷka. iyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ akaraṅkeṭa lakaram^ tirintum^ tiriyātum uṟaḻntum muṭitalum^ koḷka. paṟpala palpala ciṟcila cilcila eṉavarum. tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ akaraṅkeṭa lakaram melleḻuttum āytamumāka+ tirintu muṭitalum^ koḷka. paṉmīṉvēṭṭattu paṉmalar paḵṟāli paḵṟāḻici ciṉṉūl ciḵṟāḻici eṉavarum. itu muṉṉar+ tōṉṟumeṉṟu eṭuttōtiya ciṟappuvitiyāl akaraṅkeṭa niṉṟa lakaravoṟṟiṉ muṭipākaliṉ takaram varuvaḻi āytameṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu. (13)

TE216n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟi vaṉkaṇattōṭu vēṟṟumi tokkuniṉṟa muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṟkūṟiya alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi+ tattam ottavoṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iruviḷa+ koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉa varum. iruviḷa+ kuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa+ kuṇavēṟṟumikkaṇṇum^ koḷka. iruviḷa veṉpatu ōli; vēṇāṭṭakattu ōrūr; karuvūriṉakattu oru cēriyumeṉpa. iruviḷaviṉ+ koṟṟaṉ eṉa virikka. (14)

TE217n

marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu akara īṟṟu marappeyarkku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: marappeyar+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikum --- akara īṟṟu marappeyarāya col vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ataṅkōṭu viḷaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

itu `kacatapa mutaliya' ((eḻu//143)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum.

TE218n

makappeyar+ kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyi.

iḵtu akara īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: makappeyar+ kiḷavikku iṉṉē cāriyi --- akara īṟṟu maka eṉṉum peyar+ colliṟku vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ varum^ cāriyi iṉ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: makaviṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. cāriyippēṟu variyātu kūṟiyavaḻi nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ cellumeṉpatu āciriyarkku+ karuttākaliṉ makaviṉ ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa oṭṭuka.

mēl avaṇ eṉṟataṉāṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (16)

TE219n

attavaṇ variṉum varinili yiṉṟē.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttum attum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: makattu+ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

avaṇ eṉṟataṉāl makappālyāṭu eṉa valleḻuttu+ pēṟum makaviṉki eṉa mēl iṉcāriyi peṟṟuḻi iyipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvum, viḷaviṉkōṭu eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka. niliyeṉṟataṉāl makam pālyāṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka. (17)

TE220n

palavaṟṟu* iṟuti yurupiya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttum vaṟṟum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: palavaṟṟiṟuti --- palla pala cila uḷḷa illa eṉṉum palavaṟṟi yuṇarttum akara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ iṟuti, urupiyal niliyum --- urupiyaṟkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu+ puṇarntāṟpōla urupiṉatu poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vaṟṟuppeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

īṟṟu valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: pallavaṟṟukkōṭu palavaṟṟukkōṭu cilavaṟṟukkōṭu uḷḷavaṟṟukkōṭu illavaṟṟukkōṭu, cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa oṭṭuka. urupu virintuḻi niṟkumāṟu pōlaṉṟi avvurupu tokku ataṉporuḷ niṉṟu puṇaruṅkāl vēṟupāṭu uṭimiyiṉ avvēṟupāṭukaḷ īṇṭu ōtiṉār ittuṇiyu meṉṟu uṇarka. (18)

TE221n

ākāra viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ākāra iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu --- ākāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyatu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi+ tattam otta oṟṟu iṭimikum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mūṅkākkaṭitu tārākkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa oṭṭuka. (19)

TE222n

ceyyā veṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
mavviya ṟiriyā teṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu viṉiccoṉ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyā eṉṉam viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- ceyyā veṉṉam viṉiyeccamākiya collum ummiyāṉ+ peyarecca maṟiyākiya collum, avviyal tiriyātu eṉmaṉār pulavar --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam avviyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyā teṉṟu colluvār pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṇṇākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, uṇṇākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum. (20)

TE223n

ummi yeñciya virupeyar+ tokimoḻi
meymmi yāka vakara mikumē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummittoki muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ummi eñciya irupeyar+ tokimoḻi --- ummi tokkuniṉṟa irupeyarākiya tokiccoṟkaḷ, meymmiyāka akaram mikum --- meyyāka nilimoḻiyīṟṟu akaram mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uvāappatiṉāṉku irāappakal eṉavarum. meymmiyāka eṉpataṉāṉ valleḻuttu+ koṭukka. iḵtu eḻuvāyum payaṉiliyumaṉṟi ummittokiyātaliṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ valleḻuttu+ peṟātāyiṟṟu.

ummitokka eṉṉātu eñciya eṉṟa vāypāṭṭu vēṟṟumiyāṉ arāappāmpu eṉa+ paṇputtokikkam irāakkoṭitu eṉa eḻuvāymuṭipiṟkum irāakkākki eṉa+ peyareccamaṟikkum akarappēṟu koḷka. varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum akarappēṟu koḷka. iṟāavaḻutuṇaṅkāy eṉavarum. iḵtu ummittoki, arāakkuṭṭi eṉpatu paṇputtokiyam vēṟṟumittokiyumām. uvāappaṭṭiṉi eṉpatu vēṟṟumittoki. (21)

TE224n

āvu māvum viḷippeyar+ kiḷaviyum
yāveṉ viṉāvum palavaṟṟu* iṟutiyu
mēval kuṟitta vuriyaci miyāvum^
taṉṟoḻi lurikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyō
ṭaṉṟi yaṉittu miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytātateytuvittalum uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvum --- āveṉṉum peyarum, māvum --- māveṉṉum peyarum, viḷippeyar+ kiḷaviyum --- viḷittaliyuṭiya peyarākiya uyartiṇiccollum, yāveṉ viṉāvum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāppeyarum, palavaṟṟu iṟutiyum --- paṉmi+ poruḷi uṇarttum ākāra īṟṟu+ peyarecca maṟiyākiya muṟṟu viṉiccollum, ēval kuṟitta uriyaci miyāvum --- muṉṉili yēval viṉiyi+ karutivarum etirmukamākkum^ colliṉiccērnta miyā veṉṉum ākārīṟṟu iṭiccollum, taṉ toḻil urikkum viṉāviṉ kiḷaviyoṭu --- taṉatu toḻiliṉi+ collum ākāra īṟṟu+ taṉmiyākiya viṉā+ collōṭu kūṭa, aṉṟiyaṉittum --- av veḻuvakiyākiya collum, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ākuṟitu mākuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ākāra īṟṟu+ peyarākaliṉ mikku muṭivaṉa mikāveṉa eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. ūrākēḷ cel tā pō eṉa iḵtu iyalpāmeṉṟa uyartiṇippaṭarkkippeyar tirintu muṉṉiliyāy viḷiyēṟṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu. yā kuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa ituvum peyarākaliṉ eytiya iyipu valleḻuttu vilakkiyatām. uṇṇā kutirikaḷ cennāykaḷ takarkaḷ paṉṟikaḷ eṉa iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, ceyyāveṉṉum^ cūttirattu ummiyāṉ+ peṟṟa valleḻuttiṉi vilakkaliṉ. kēṇmiyā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum, uṇkā koṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum ivviṭiccoṟkaḷ muṭiyāmiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatumām. uṇkā eṉpatu yāṉuṇpēṉō eṉṉumporuṭṭu. iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ+ āyiṉ `ñanamayava'(eḻu//144) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭipeytum. (22)

TE225n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟu vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- ākāra īṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttuvantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tārā mūṅkā vaṅkā eṉa niṟutti+ kāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varuvittu valleḻuttukkoṭuttu oṭṭuka. (23)

TE226n

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉaru mōreḻuttu moḻikku
maṟiya+ tōṉṟu makara+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu avvīṟṟiṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉarum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum --- ōreḻuttu orumoḻiyākiya ākāra īṟṟiṟkum, akarakkiḷavi aṟiyattōṉṟum --- niṟumoḻikkaṇ akaramākiya eḻuttu viḷaṅka+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: palāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kāakkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum varum. ōreḻuttorumoḻi akaram peṟutal ciṟupāṉmi yeṉṟaṟku ataṉi+ piṟkūṟiṉār. itu nilimoḻi+ ceykiyātaliṉ+ palāavili palāanār eṉa iyalpu kaṇattum^ koḷka. aṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ avvakaram īriṭattum poruntiṉavaḻi+ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē aṇṇāattēri tiṭṭāattukkuḷam eṉa attu+ koṭuttum, uvāattu ñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ eṉa attum ñāṉṟum^ koṭuttum, uvāattāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum āṉum^ koṭuttum iṭāviṉuḷ+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa iṉṉum ēḻaṉurupum^ koṭuttum^ ceykiceytu muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mūṅkāviṉkāl mūṅkāviṉṟali eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttukkēṭum^ koḷka. (24)

TE227n

irā v-eṉ kiḷavikku akaram illi.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuppeyarkku oruvaḻi eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: irāveṉ kiḷavikku --- irāveṉṉum ākāra īṟṟu+ colliṟku, akaram illi --- muṟkūṟiya akaram peṟutaliṉṟi valleḻuttuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: irākkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. irāakkākki irāakkūttu eṉa+ peyareccamaṟipporuḷ tārātu irāviṭattu+ kākki irāviṭattu+ kūttu eṉa vēṟṟumi karutiyavaḻi irākkākki irākkūttu eṉa akaram peṟāteṉṟa uṇarka. (25)

TE228n

nilāveṉ kiḷavi yattoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu akaram vilakki atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nilāveṉkiḷavi attoṭu civaṇum --- nilā veṉṉum^ col attuccāriyiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nilāattukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. nilimoḻittoḻil nilimoḻi+ toḻili vilakkumātaliṉ attuvakuppa akaram vīḻntatu. itaṟku ēḻaṉurupu virikka. nilāakkatir eṉpatu `vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum' ((eḻu//225)) eṉpataṉāṉ īṟṟu valleḻuttuppeṟṟatu. nilāamuṟṟameṉpatu `oṭṭutaṟkoḻukiya vaḻakku' aṉmiyiṉ attu+ peṟātāyiṟṟu. īṇṭu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ nilāattu ñāṉṟāṉ eṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ+ um ēṟpaṉakoḷka. (26)

TE229n

yāmara+ kiḷaviyum piṭāvum^ taḷāvu
māmu+ peyaru melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu varumoḻi valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāmara+ kiḷaviyum --- yāveṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟa collum, piṭāvum --- piṭāveṉṉum^ collum, taḷāvum --- taḷāveṉṉum^ collum, ām muppeyarum melleḻuttu mikum --- ākiya mūṉṟu peyarum valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttumikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāaṅkōṭu piṭāaṅkōṭu taḷāaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. varumoḻi+ toḻilākiya melleḻuttu vakuppavē valleḻuttu vilakkiṟṟām. itaṟku vilakkāmiyiṉ akaram peṟṟatu. (27)

TE230n

valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.

iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttatu, akarattōṭu melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi --- muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāakkōṭu piṭāakkōṭu taḷāakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. māṉamilli eṉṟataṉāl immūṉṟaṟkum urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāviṉkōṭu piṭāviṉkōṭu taḷāviṉkōṭu eṉavarum. cāriyi peṟavē akaram vīḻntatu.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē yāattukkōṭu piṭāattukkōṭu taḷāattukkōṭu eṉa attuppeṟutalum^ koḷka, akaramum valleḻuttum peṟutaliṉ.

yāmarakkiḷavi yeṉpataṉik `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar' ((eḻu//226)) eṉpataṉpiṉ viyātavataṉāṉ irāviṉ+ koṇṭāṉ nilāviṉ+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa urupiṟkucceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (28)

TE231n

māmara+ kiḷaviyu māvu māvu
māmu+ peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa
vakaram valleḻuttu aviyava ṇiliyā
ṉakara moṟṟu māvu māvum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki eytātateytuvittatu. immūṉṟum valleḻuttu+ peṟā eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. māmarattukku akaramum ṅañanama oṟṟum ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟku ṉakara oṟṟum eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māmarakkiḷaviyum āvum māvum ām muppeyarum avaṟṟōraṉṉa --- māmaramākiya collum āveṉṉum^ collum māveṉṉum^ collumākiya mūṉṟupeyarum yāmaram mutaliyamūṉṟōṭum orutaṉmiyavāy melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭiyam, āvum māvum akaram avaṇniliyā ṉakaram oṟṟum --- avaṟṟuḷ āvum māvum puṇarcciyiṭattu akaram nilipeṟāvāy ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum, eṉavē aruttāpattiyāṉ māmarattiṟku akaram nilipeṟṟu ṅañanama oṟṟum peṟumāyiṟṟu; avi valleḻuttu avaṇ niliyā --- am mūṉṟu peyarum muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ puṇarcciyiṭattu nilipeṟāvāy varum eṉṟavāṟu.

avaṇiliyā eṉṟataṉi iraṇṭiṭattum^ kūṭṭuka.

utāraṇam: māaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū āṉkōṭu māṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

āvum māvum avaṟṟōraṉṉa eṉṟu ñāpakamāka+ kūṟiyavataṉāl māṅkōṭeṉa akara miṉṟiyum varum.

iṉi, avaṇ eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kāyāṅkōṭu nuṇāṅkōṭu āṇāṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟavum melleḻuttu+ peṟutalum, aṅkākkoṇṭāṉ iṅkākkoṇṭāṉ uṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṅkākkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivaṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumiyiṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum, āviṉkōṭu māviṉkōṭu eṉa+ ciṟupāṉmi iṉ peṟutalum, peṟṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka.

māṭṭēṟṟāṉ mūṉṟupeyarum valleḻuttu+ peṟātu melleḻuttu+ peṟṟavāṟum māmaram akaram peṟṟavāṟum iccūttirattiṉkaṇṇaḻivāṉ uṇarka. (29)

TE232n

āṉ oṟṟu* akaramoṭu nili y-iṭaṉ uṭittu* ē.

iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ āṉ eṉṟataṟku eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṉ oṟṟu --- ā v-eṉṉum^ coṉ muṉṉar+ peṟṟu niṉṟa ṉakara v-oṟṟu, akaramoṭu* um nili y-iṭaṉ uṭittu --- akarattōṭu kūṭi-niṟkum iṭaṉ um uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

iṭaṉ uṭittu* eṉṟavataṉāl vaṉkaṇam oḻinta kaṇattu i+ muṭipu* eṉa+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `āṉaney teḷittu nāṉa nīvi' `āṉamaṇi kaṟaṅkum^ kāṉattāṅkaṇ' eṉa varum. akaramoṭum eṉṟa ummiyāl akaramiṉṟi varutalē perumpāṉmi. āṉey teḷittu āṉmaṇi āṉvāl eṉa varum. (30)

TE233n

āṉmuṉ varūu mīkāra pakaram^
tāṉmika+ tōṉṟi+ kuṟukalu murittē.

iḵtu āṉeṉpataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṉmuṉ varūum īkāra pakaram --- āṉeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar varumoḻiyāy varukiṉṟa īkārattōṭu kūṭiya pakaramākiya moḻi, tāṉ mika+ tōṉṟi --- appakaramākiya tāṉ mikku niṟpa nilimoḻi ṉakarattiṟku+ kēṭu tōṉṟi, kuṟukalum urittu --- īkāram ikaramāka+ kuṟuki niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āppi eṉa varum.

ummi etirmaṟi yākalāṉ āṉpī eṉṟumām. (31)

TE234n

kuṟiyata ṉiṟuti+ ciṉikeṭa vukara
maṟiya varutal ceyyuḷu ḷurittē.

iḵtu ākāra īṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟiyataṉ iṟuti+ ciṉikeṭa --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu iraṇṭu māttiriyil orumāttirikeṭṭu aḵtu akaramāy niṟpa, ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- āṇṭu ukaram pulappaṭa varutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:
`iṟavu+ puṟattu aṉṉa piṇar+paṭu taṭavu mutal+
cuṟavu+ kōṭṭu* aṉṉa muḷ= ili+ tāḻi' ((naṟ//19))

`puṟavuppuṟattu aṉṉa puṉkālu kāay ((kuṟu//274))

eṉavarum. ukaram vakuppavē nilimoḻi akaram^ keṭṭatu. atikāra valleḻuttu vilakkāmiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu. iṉi nilimoḻi+ toḻil variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattiṟkum ivviti eytiṟṟākaliṉ, āṇṭu varum ukaram pulappaṭa vārāmiyum uṇarka. kaṟavuyarkoṭi aravuyarkoṭi muḻavuṟaḻtōḷ eṉa ivi kuṟiyataṉiṟuticciṉi keṭṭu varumoḻi uyir mutaṉ moḻiyāy varutaliṉ vakara uṭampaṭu mey peṟṟu ukaram peṟātu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka; mūṉṟāvatumām. (32)

TE235n

ikara viṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟuppeyarkku alvaḻimuṭipu tokimarapiṉ+ kūṟi īṇṭu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: ikara iṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar --- ikarīṟṟu+ peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṉ+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum --- vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakku+ poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kiḷikkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam eṉavarum. puli nari eṉṟāl+ pōlvaṉavum avi.

iṉi+ kiḷikuṟumi kiḷikkuṟumi eṉa+ kuṇampaṟṟi vanta uṟaḻcci muṭipu, mēl `valleḻuttu mikiṉum' ((eḻu//246)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattu `olvaḻiyaṟital' eṉpataṉāṉ+ koḷka. (33)

TE236n

iṉiyaṇi yeṉṉum^ kāliyu miṭaṉum
viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum^ cuṭṭu maṉṉa.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu, ivvīṟṟu iṭiccoṟkum viṉiccoṟkum muṭipu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṉi aṇi eṉṉum^ kāliyum iṭaṉum --- iṉiyeṉṟum aṇiyeṉṟum^ collappaṭukiṉṟa kālattiyum iṭattiyum uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccollum, viṉiyeñcukiḷaviyum --- ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya collum, cuṭṭum --- ivvīṟṟu+ cuṭṭākiya iṭiccollum, aṉṉa --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟē valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iṉikkoṇṭāṉ aṇikkoṇṭāṉ tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, ikkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavum varum. ivviṭiccoṉ mūṉṟum ippoḻutu koṇṭāṉ aṇiya iṭattē koṇṭāṉ ivviṭattukkoṟṟaṉ eṉa urupiṉporuḷpaṭavanta vēṟṟumiyātaliṉ vēṟōtimuṭittār. (34)

TE237n

iṉṟi y-eṉṉum viṉi y-eñcu* iṟuti
niṉṟa v-ikaram ukaram ātal*
^toṉṟiyal% maruṅkiṉ+ ceyyuḷ uḷ urittu* ē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu viṉiyeccattuḷ oṉṟaṟku+ ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal --- iṉṟiyeṉṟu collappaṭum viṉiyecca+ kuṟippiṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ikaram ukaramāka+ tirintu muṭital, toṉṟiyal maruṅkiṉ+ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu --- paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟiyuṭiya ceyyuḷuḷ urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `uppiṉṟu puṟki yuṇkamā koṟkiyōṉē'.

niṉṟa eṉṟataṉāl viṉiyeccattiṟkumuṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.

toṉṟiyaṉmaruṅkiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ aṉṟi yeṉpatūum^ ceyyuḷil immuṭipu eytutal koḷka. `iṭaṉaṉṟu tuṟattal valli yōrē' `vāḷaṉṟu piṭiyā vaṉkaṇāṭavar' `nāḷaṉṟupōki' ((puṟam//124)) eṉa varum. muṟṟiyalikaram^ tirintu kuṟṟiyalukaramāy niṉṟatu. (35)

TE238n

cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki muṟkiḷantu* aṟṟu* ē.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭuppeyar iyalpukaṇattōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉporuḷ: cuṭṭiṉ iyaṟki --- ikara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉ iyalpu, muṉ kiḷantaṟṟu --- muṉ akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṟku+ kūṟiya taṉmittām eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉṟatu `cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉar ñanamattōṉṟi' ((eḻu//205)) eṉpatu mutaliya nāṉku cūttirattāṉum^ kūṟiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi. avi meṉkaṇattu melleḻuttu mikutalum iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum vakaram peṟutalum^ ceyyuṭkaṇ vakaraṅkeṭṭu+ cuṭṭu nīṭalumām.

i+ ñāṇ nūl maṇi eṉa v-um, iv yāḻ iv vaṭṭu eṉa v-um, iv= aṭi iv= āṭi iv= ili iv= īyam iv= ural iv= ūrti iv= eḻu iv= ēṇi iv= aiyam iv= oṭu iv= ōkkam iv= auviyam eṉa v-um ī vayiṉāṉa eṉa v-um varum.

`ī kāṇ ^tōṉṟum em^ ciṟu nal= ūr ē'

eṉṟatu* um,

`kaḷvaṉ ō vallaṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉ kāl+cilampu
koḷḷum vili+ poruṭṭāl+ koṉṟār ē y-ītu* oṉṟu' ((cilap. ūr cūḻ vari//7))

eṉṟatu* um itu v-eṉṉum^ cuṭṭu mutal ukara īṟu* ātaliṉ atu ceyyuḷ akattu+ puṟanaṭiyāṉ muṭiyum eṉa uṇarka. (36)

TE239n

patakku muṉ variṉ ē tūṇi+ kiḷavi
mutal+ kiḷantu* eṭutta vēṟṟumi y-iyaṟṟu* ē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷuḷ aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ tokimarapiṉuḷ eytiya ēyeṉ cāriyi vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tūṇi+ kiḷavimuṉ patakku variṉ --- tūṇiyākiya aḷavuppeyariṉ muṉṉar+ patakku eṉṉum aḷavuppeyar varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkiḷantu eṭutta vēṟṟumi iyaṟṟu --- muṉpu vitanteṭutta vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tūṇippatakku eṉavarum. iḵtu ummittoki.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ aṭiyoṭu vantuḻiyum iv viti koḷka. irutūṇippatakku muttūṇippatakku eṉa oṭṭuka. kiḷanteṭutta veṉṟataṉāl tūṇikkoḷ cāmi tōri pāḷitam eṉa+ poruṭpeyar muṉ vantuḻiyum, irutūṇikkoḷ eṉa atutāṉ aṭiyaṭuttuḻiyum^ tūṇittūṇi toṭittoṭi kāṇikkāṇi pūṇippūṇi eṉa+ taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantuḻiyum viti koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē taṉmuṉṉar+ tāṉ vantuḻiyam atu tāṉ, aṭiyaṭuttu vantuḻiyum ikkuccāriyi peṟutalum^ koḷka. tūṇikkuttūṇi irutūṇikkuttūṇi eṉavarum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ paṇputtokiyum uḷa. (37)

TE240n

urivaru kāli nāḻi+ kiḷavi
iṟuti ikara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē
ṭakara moṟṟu māvayiṉāṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: urivarukāli --- nāḻimuṉṉar uri varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, nāḻikkiḷavi --- annāḻi yeṉṉum^ col, iṟuti ikaram meyyoṭum^ keṭum --- taṉ iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ikaram^ tāṉēṟiya meyyoṭum^ keṭum, āvayiṉ āṉa ṭakaram oṟṟum --- avviṭattu ṭakaram oṟṟāyvarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṭuri eṉavarum. itaṉāṉ ēkāramum vilakkuṇṭatu.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ irunāṭuri munnāṭuri eṉavum oṭṭuka.

iṟutiyikarameṉa muṉṉum ōr ikaram uḷḷatu pōla+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ īṇṭi nilimoḻiyam varumoḻiyum nilimoḻiyāy niṉṟu peyarōṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭitaluṅkoḷka. nāḻikkāyam urikkāyam cukku tōri pāḷitam eṉa varum. (38)

TE241n

paṉi y-eṉa varūum^ kāla vēṟṟumikku
attu um iṉ= um^ cāriyi y-ākum.

iḵtu ikara īṟṟu vēṟṟumiyuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyi peṟumeṉa eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyeṉa varūum^ kāla vēṟṟumikku --- paṉiyeṉṟa collavarukiṉṟa nōyaṉṟi+ kālatti uṇara niṉṟa vēṟṟumi muṭipuṭiya peyarkku, attum iṉṉum^ cāriyi ākum --- attum iṉṉum^ cāriyiyāka varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉiyattukkoṇṭāṉ paṉiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.

vēṟṟumi yeṉṟataṉāṉ iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (39)

TE242n

vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūta+ kiḷaviyu
mavviya ṉiliyal cevvi teṉpa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaḷiyeṉa varūum pūta+ kiḷaviyum --- vaḷiyeṉṟucolla varukiṉṟa iṭakkarallāta aimperum pūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟi uṇaraniṉṟa collum, avviyalniliyal cevviteṉpa --- muṉṉikkūṟiya attum iṉṉum peṟum avviyalpiṉkaṇ niṟṟal cevviteṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vaḷiyattukkoṇṭāṉ vaḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

cevviteṉṟataṉāṉ iṉpeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (40)

TE243n

utimara+ kiḷavi melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu marappeyaril oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: utimara+ kiḷavi --- utittaleṉṉum^ toḻilaṉṟi utiyeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟacol, melleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: utiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

ammuccāriyi vitikkiṉṟa puḷimarattiṉi itaṉpiṉ vittamiyāṉ utiyaṅkōṭu eṉa itaṟkum ammuppeṟutal koḷka. iḵtu ikkālattu otiyeṉama maruviṟṟu. (41)

TE244n

puḷimara+ kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷimarakkiḷavikku ammē cāriyi --- cuviyaṉṟi+ puḷiyeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa colliṟku ammeṉṉum^ cāriyi varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

cāriyi+ pēṟṟiṭi muṉṉar+ cūttirattu eḻuttuppēṟu kūṟiya vataṉāl ammuppeṟṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (42)

TE245n

ēṉi+ puḷippeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi+ puḷippeyar melleḻuttumikum --- ammarappeyaraṉṟi+ cuvippuḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyar valleḻuttu mikātu melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷiṅkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉa varum. pāḷitam pāṟcōṟu. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. (43)

TE246n

valleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli
yolvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli --- cuvippuḷi melleḻuttē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamilli, olvaḻiyaṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa --- poruntum iṭam aṟika vaḻakkiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷikkūḻ cāṟu tayir pāḷitam eṉavarum.

olvaḻi eṉṟataṉāṉ+ puḷiccāṟu pōla ēṉiya vaḻakkuppayiṟci illeṉṟu koḷka.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ iv vīṟṟukkaṇ eṭuttōttum ilēcumiṉṟi varuvaṉa vellāvaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkumāṟu ceykiyaṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. avi iṉṉiṉi+ koṇṭāṉ aṇṇaṇi+ koṇṭāṉ eṉpaṉa aṭiyaṭuttaliṉ `iṉiyaṇi' ((eḻu//236)) eṉṟavaḻi muṭiyāvāy valleḻuttu+ peṟṟaṉa. kappitanti ceṉṉitanti eṉpaṉa `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉuḷ iyalpeytātu īṇṭu varumoḻi+ takara akaram^ keṭṭu+ kappinti ceṉṉinti eṉa muṭintatu.

kūtāḷi kaṇaviri eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṟku ammu+ koṭuttu ikaraṅkeṭuttu+ kūtāḷaṅkōṭu kaṇaviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. `kūtaḷa naṟumpū' eṉa+ kuṟintum varum. iṉi ivi makarīṟāyum vaḻaṅkum. atu `veṇkūtāḷattu+ taṇpūṅkōtiyar' eṉa attuppeṟṟu makaraṅkeṭṭum^ `kaṇaviramāli yiṭūukkaḻintaṉṉa' eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭum^ kaṇaviraṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ peṟṟum niṟkum.

cuṭṭi eṉa niṟutti iṭi akal eṉa+ tantu ṭakarattil ikaraṅkeṭuttu+ kaṭṭiṭi kaṭṭakal eṉa muṭikka. paruttikku+ ceṉṟāṉeṉa īṟṟu valleḻuttum ikkum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. tuḷiyattu+ koṇṭāṉ tuḷiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. puḷiṅkāy vēṭkittaṉṟu eṉavum puḷimpaḻam eṉavum ammuppeṟātu melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭitalum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu+ keṭuttu+ kiḷiyiṉkāl puḷiyiṉkōṭu utiyiṉkōṭu eṉa muṭikka. (44)

TE247n

nāṇmuṉ+ ṟōṉṟum^ toḻiṉili+ kiḷavikku
āṉ iṭi varutal aiyam iṉṟē.

iḵtu īṟṟu valleḻuttu vilakki āṉcāriyi vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāḷ muṉ tōṉṟum^ toḻiṉili+ kiḷavikku --- ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum^ toḻil+ coṟku, āṉ iṭivarutal aiyamiṉṟu --- āṉ cāriyi iṭivantu muṭital aiyamiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paraṇiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ cōtiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. aiyamiṉṟeṉṟataṉāl iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. itaṟku+ kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka. (45)

TE248n

tiṅkaṇ muṉvari ṉikkē cāriyi.

iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiṅkaḷ muṉvariṉ ikkē cāriyi --- tiṅkaḷi uṇaraniṉṟa ikara īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ toḻiṉili+ kiḷavivariṉ varuṅcāriyi ikku+ cāriyiyām eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭikkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa iyipuvalleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. itaṟkum^ kaṇṇeṉ urupu virikka. (46)

TE249n

īkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu --- īkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īkkaṭitu tīkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (47)

TE250n

nīyeṉ peyaru miṭakkar+ peyarum
mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvari kiḷaviyu
māvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkalum eytāta teytuvittalum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nī eṉ peyarum iṭakkarppeyarum --- nī yeṉṉum peyarum iṭakkarppeyarākiya īkāra īṟṟuppeyarum, mīyeṉamarīiya iṭam vari kiḷaviyum --- mīyeṉṟu colla maruvāy vaḻaṅkiṉa ōriṭatti varintu uṇarttum collum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum --- puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nīkuṟiyi ciṟiyi tīyi periyi eṉavum, pīkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum iviyiṟṟukku+ potuvāṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vilakkuṇṭaṉa. mīkaṇ cevi tali puṟam. iḵtu ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya maruvātaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

nīyeṉpatu aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyaruḷ aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ mēl niṉkiyeṉa+ tirintu muṭitaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. mīkaṇ eṉpatu mēliṭattu+ kaṇṇeṉa vēṟṟumiyeṉiṉum iyalpupaṟṟi uṭaṉ kūṟiṉār. (48)

TE251n

iṭamvari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūu
muṭaṉili moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.

itu valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu* ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭam vari kiḷavimuṉ valleḻuttu mikūum --- iṭatti varintu uṇarttum mīyeṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉar iyalpāy muṭitalē yaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkum muṭiyum, uṭaṉili moḻiyum uḷa eṉa moḻipa --- tammil ōci yiyintu niṟṟali yuṭiya moḻikaḷum uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mīkkōḷ mīppal eṉa varum.

uṭaṉiliyeṉṟataṉāṉ mīṅkuḻi mīntōl eṉa melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. (49)

TE252n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- īkāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īkkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam, tīkkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum. (50)

TE253n

nīye ṉorupeya rurupiya ṉiliyu
māvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, valleḻuttu vilakki ṉakaravoṟṟē peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: nīyeṉ orupeyar urupiyal niliyum --- nīyeṉṉum ōreḻuttorumoḻi urupu puṇarccikkaṇ neṭumutal kuṟuki ṉakaram oṟṟi niṉṟāṟpōla īṇṭu+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭiyum, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum --- avvāṟu muṭipuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu mikā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: niṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

iḵtu īkāra īṟu ikara īṟāy ikara īṟu ṉakara īṟāy niṉṟuḻiyum `nīyeṉorupeyar' eṉṟaliṉ+ ṟirintataṉ ṟiripatuvēyāyiṟṟu. iyaṟkiyākumeṉavē nilimoḻittoḻil atikāra valleḻutti vilakkātāyiṟṟu. (51)

TE254n

ukara viṟuti yakara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ukara iṟuti akara iyaṟṟu --- ukara īṟṟu+ peyar alvaḻikkaṇ akara īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭukkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum (52)

TE255n

cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉaru mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu vaṉkaṇattōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭiṉ muṉṉarum attoḻiṟṟu ākum --- ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉmuṉṉum valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ukkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ pūtaṉ eṉavarum. (53)

TE256n

ēṉavi variṉē mēṉili yiyala.

iḵtu ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu oḻinta kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉavi variṉ --- ukara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭiṉ muṉ valleḻuttallāta meṉkaṇam mutaliya mūṉṟum variṉ, mēlnili iyala --- akara īṟṟu+ cuṭṭu muṭintāṟpōla ñanama+ tōṉṟiṉ oṟṟu mikkum yavvariṉum uyirvariṉum vakaram oṟṟiyum^ ceyyuḷil nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉavum, uvyāḻ uvvaṭṭu eṉavum, uvvaṭi uvvāṭi eṉavum, ūvayiṉāṉa eṉavum varum. (54)

TE257n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yiyalpā kummē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟuppeyar, iyalpākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atukuṟitu itukuṟitu utukuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

muṟkūṟiyavi cuṭṭumāttiri, ivi cuṭṭuppeyarāka uṇarka. (55)

TE258n

aṉṟuvaru kāli yāvā kutalum
aivaru kāli meyvarintu keṭutalum^
ceyyuṇ maruṅki ṉuritteṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu+ cuṭṭumutal+ peyarkku ōr ceyyuṇ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṉṟu varukāli āvākutalum --- atikārattāl niṉṟa cuṭṭumutal ukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉar aṉṟeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuccol varuṅkālattu a+ takaravoṟṟiṉ mēl ēṟiniṉṟa ukaram ākāramāy+ tirintu muṭitalum, ai varukāli meyvarintu keṭutalum --- ataṉmuṉṉar aiyeṉṉum^ cāriyi varuṅkālattu a+ takaravoṟṟu niṟka ataṉmēl ēṟiya ukaram^ keṭutalum, ceyyuṇmaruṅkiṉ uritteṉa moḻipa --- ceyyuṭkaṇ uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atāaṉṟamma itāaṉṟamma utāaṉṟamma `atāaṉṟeṉpa veṇpā yāppē' eṉavum, atimaṟṟamma itimaṟṟamma utimaṟṟamma eṉavum varum.

moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ muṭiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟi muṭitta leṉpataṉāl ataṉṟu itaṉṟu utaṉṟa eṉa ukaraṅkeṭṭu+ takaravoṟṟu niṟṟal koḷka. (56)

TE259n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉō raṟṟē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- ukara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- akara īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmittāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṭukkāy cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kaṭukkaṭumi eṉavum varum. (57)

TE260n

eruvum^ ceruvu mammoṭu civaṇi+
tiripiṭa ṉuṭiya teriyum^ kāli
yammiṉ makaram^ ceruvayiṉ+ keṭumē
tammoṟṟu mikūum valleḻuttu iyaṟki.

iḵtu avvīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyi vitiyum oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyi vitiyum^ cāriyi peṟātavaḻi valleḻuttu melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eruvum^ ceruvum ammoṭu civaṇi --- eruveṉṉum^ collum ceruveṉṉum^ collum ammu+ cāriyiyōṭu porunti, tiripu iṭaṉuṭiya teriyuṅkāli --- atikāra valleḻuttu+ peṟāmal tiriyum iṭaṉuṭiya ārāyuṅkālattu, ammiṉ makaram^ ceruvayiṉ+ keṭum --- āṇṭu ammuccāriyiyiṉatu īṟṟiṉ makaram^ ceruveṉṉum^ colliṭattu+ keṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu mikūum --- āṇṭu+ ceruviṉkaṇ valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum, iyaṟki+ tam oṟṟu mikūum --- ammuppeṟātavaḻi iraṇṭiṟkum^ tamakku iṉamākiya valloṟṟum melloṟṟum mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eruveṉa niṟutti+ kuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa+ tantu ammukkoṭuttu `ammiṉiṟutikacatakkāli' ((eḻu//129)) eṉpataṉāl eruvaṅkuḻi cēṟu tātu pūḻi yeṉa muṭikka. ceruveṉa niṟutti+ kaḷam cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉa+ tantu iṭi ammukkoṭuttu makaram^ keṭuttu valleḻuttukkoṭuttu+ ceruvakkaḷam cēṉi tāṉi paṟiyeṉa muṭikka. iṉi ammu+ peṟātavaḻi erukkuḻi eruṅkuḻi eṉa valleḻuttum melleḻuttum^ koṭuttu muṭikka. iṉi+ ceruviṟku ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāṉ+ cerukkaḷameṉa valleḻuttē koṭuttu muṭikka.

teriyuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ eruviṉ kuṟumi ceruviṉ kaṭumi eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻtalum eruvañāṟci ceruvañāṟci eṉa iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇ ammuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. makaram `meṉmiyu miṭimiyum'(eḻu//130) eṉpataṉāṉ+ keṭukka.

tammoṟṟumikūum eṉa uṭampoṭu puṇarttu+ cūttiram^ ceytaliṉ ukaram nīṭavarutalum^ koḷka. varūum tarūum paṭūum eṉa varum. (58)

TE261n

ḻakara vukara nīṭiṭa ṉuṭittē
yukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram peṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḻakara ukaram nīṭiṭaṉ uṭittu --- ukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷuḷ ḻakarattōṭu kūṭiya ukara īṟṟuccol nīṇṭu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu, āvayiṉ āṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻūukkatavu ciṟi tāṉi paṭi eṉa varum.

nīṭiṭaṉuṭittu eṉṟataṉāṉ nīḷātum ukaram peṟātum varumāyiṟṟu. kuḻuttōṟṟam eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉ+ paḻukkāyeṉa alvaḻikkaṇṇum ivvitiyaṉṟi varutal koḷka.

āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ+ perumpāṉmi ceyyuṭkaṇ nīṇṭu ukarampeṟṟu

`eḻūuttāṅkiya katavumalittavar

kuḻūukkaḷiṟṟu+ kuṟumpuṭittaliṉ' ((puṟam//97))

eṉavum,

`paḻūuppallaṉṉa paruvukirppāvaṭi' ((kuṟun//180))

eṉavum varutal koḷka. (59)

TE262n

oṭumara+ kiḷavi yutimara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu marappeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṭumarakkiḷavi --- oṭuveṉṉam marattiṉi uṇara niṉṟa col, utimara iyaṟṟu --- utiyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṭuṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. marameṉṟār oṭuveṉṉum nōyi nīkkutaṟku.

muṉṉar utamarattiṉpiṉṉar ammu+ peṟukiṉṟa puḷimaram vitta iyipāṉ itaṟku ammuppēṟum^ koḷka. oṭuvaṅkōṭu eṉa varum. (60)

TE263n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉiliyu
moṟṟiṭi mikāa valleḻuttu iyaṟki.

itu cuṭṭuppeyarkku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyi vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti urupiyal niliyum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya ukara īṟṟu+ coṟkaḷ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum urupupuṇarcciyiṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpilē niṉṟu aṉcāriyi peṟṟu ukaram^ keṭṭu muṭiyum, valleḻuttu iyaṟki oṟṟu iṭimikāa --- valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākiya oṟṟu iṭikkaṇ mikā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ataṉkōṭu itaṉkōṭu utaṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. oṟṟiṭimikā eṉavē cāriyi vakuppa valleḻuttu vīḻāveṉpatu peṟṟām.

valleḻuttiyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ ukara īṟṟuḷ eruvum^ ceruvum oḻittu ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kaṭuviṉkuṟi oṭuviṉkuṟi eḻuviṉpuṟam koḻuviṉ kūrmi eṉa varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē utukkāṇ eṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum^ koḷka. (61)

TE264n

ūkāra viṟuti yākāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ūkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ūkāra iṟuti ākāra iyaṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḻūukkaṭitu koṇmūkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (62)

TE265n

viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikku muṉṉili moḻikku
niṉiyum^ kāli yavvaki variyār.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉiyeccattiṟku mikku muṭiyumeṉṟaliṉ, eytātateytuvittatūum muṉṉili viṉikku iyalpum uṟaḻpum māṟṟataliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiyatūum nutaliṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṉiyeñcu kiḷavikkum --- ūkāra īṟṟu viṉiyeccamākiya coṟkum, muṉṉili moḻikkum --- muṉṉili viṉiccoṟkum, niṉiyuṅkāli avvaki variyār --- ārāyuṅkālattu avvalleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum^ kūṟṟiṉi nīkkār eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṇṇūkkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, kitūkkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉavum varum.

niṉiyuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu uyartiṇi+ peyarkkum alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu+ koṭuttu muṭikka. āṭūukkuṟiyaṉ makaṭūukkuṟiyaḷ eṉavarum. uyartiṇippeyar eṭuttōti muṭippārātaliṉ ammuṭipu peṟāmiyiṉ īṟṟu+ potuvitiyāṉ muṭiyātu ilēcāṉ muṭittām. (63)

TE266n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkuṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟu vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇum ākāra īṟṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḻūukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi, koṇmūkkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟivu eṉavarum. (64)

TE267n

kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum ōr eḻuttu moḻikku
niṟṟal vēṇṭum ukara+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ukaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuṟṟeḻuttu imparum --- kuṟṟeḻuttiṉpiṉ vanta ūkāra īṟṟu moḻikkum, ōreḻuttu moḻikkum --- ōreḻuttorumoḻiyākiya ūkāra īṟṟumoḻikkum, ukarakkiḷavi niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- ukaramākiya eḻuttu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uṭūukkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum, tūukkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavum varum.

niṟṟal eṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇi+ peyarkkum valleḻuttum ukaramum^ koṭukka. āṭūukki makaṭūukki cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum. ivi toki marapiṉuḷ iyalpātal eytiyavaṟṟi īṇṭu iruvaḻikkaṇṇum muṭittār, īṟṟu+ potu oppumi kaṇṭu. (65)

TE268n

pūve ṉorupeya rāyiyal piṉṟē
yāvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.

iḵtu ūkāra īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku ukaramum iyipu valleḻuttum vilakki+ perumpāṉmi melleḻuttum^ ciṟupāṉmi valleḻuttum peṟumeṉa eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pūveṉ oru peyar a iyalpu iṉṟu --- pūveṉṉum ūkāra īṟṟiyuṭiya oru peyar mēṟkūṟiya ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum avviyalpiṉmiyi uṭittu, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu --- avviṭattu melleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūṅkoṭi cōli tāmam pantu eṉavum, pūkkoṭi ceyki tāmam pantu eṉavum varum.

pūveṉpatu voliveṉṉum viṉikkuṟippi uṇarttātu niṟṟaṟku orupeya reṉṟār. (66)

TE269n

ūve ṉorupeya rāvoṭu civaṇum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu. ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ṉakaram vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ūveṉ orupeyar --- ūveṉa+ taciyi uṇarttiniṉṟa ōreḻuttorumoḻi, āvoṭu civaṇum --- ākāra īṟṟil āveṉṉum^ col valleḻuttu+ peṟātu ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭintāṟpōla ṉakara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ūveṉa niṟutti ṉakara oṟṟu+ koṭuttu ūṉkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka. ūveṉpatu taciyi uṇarttiniṉṟa vaḻakku āciriyar nūlceyta kālattu vaḻakku. aṉṟi+ tēyavaḻakkēṉum uṇarka. (67)

TE270n

akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalu murittē
takkavaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattu āṉa.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akkeṉ cāriyi peṟutalum urittu --- atikārattāṉ niṉṟa ūveṉṉum peyar muṟkūṟiya ṉakarattōṭu akkeṉṉum^ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭitalum urittu, vaḻakkattāṉa takkavaḻi aṟital --- ammuṭipu vaḻakkiṭattu+ takka iṭam aṟika eṉṟavāṟu.

takkavaḻiyaṟital eṉṟataṉāṉ+ cāriyipeṟṟuḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟalum muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāl vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttu+ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ūṉakkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉavarum.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ ūkāra īṟṟu+ colliṟku urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu+ keṭukka. koṇmūviṉkuḻām uṭūuviṉṟali ūviṉkuṟi eṉa varum.

TE271n

āṭūu makaṭūu vāyiru peyarkku
miṉṉiṭi variṉu māṉa milli.

itu `kuṟṟeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//267)) eṉpataṉuḷ niṟṟaleṉṟa ilēcāṉ eytiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi+ cāriyiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti uṇarttiyatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṭūu makaṭūu āyiru peyarkkum --- āṭūu makaṭūuvākiya uyartiṇippeyar iraṇṭiṟkum, iṉ iṭivariṉum māṉam illi --- muṉṉeytiya valleḻuttē yaṉṟi iṉcāriyi iṭiyē variṉum^ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṭūuviṉki makaṭūuviṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum.

māṉamilli eṉṟataṉāṉ iṉ peṟṟuḻi mēl eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (69)

TE272n

ekara vokaram peyarkkī ṟākā
muṉṉili moḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar
tēṟṟamum^ ciṟappu malvaḻi yāṉa.

iḵtu ekara okaram īṟām iṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṟṟamum^ ciṟappum alvaḻi āṉa --- teḷivuporuḷum^ ciṟappupporuḷum allāta vēṟṟumi+ poruṇmiyiṭattu aḷapeṭuttu+ kūṟutaliṉ uḷavākiya, ekara okaram peyarkku īṟākā --- ekara okaraṅkaḷ peyarkku īṟāy vārā, viṉikku īṟāy varum, muṉṉili moḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avitām^ taṉmiyiṉ um paṭarkkiyiṉ um vārā, muṉṉiliccolliṭattaṉavāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, teḷivu poruḷiṉum^ ciṟappu+ poruḷiṉum vantu peyarkku īṟām iṭiccollākiya ekara okaram mūṉṟiṭattiṟkum uriyavāmeṉṟu poruḷāyiṟṟu. eṉa iṅṅaṉam aruttāpattiyāṉ+ koṇṭataṟku ilakkaṇam mēli+ cūttirattāṉ+ kūṟupa.

utāraṇam: ēekkoṟṟā ōokkoṟṟā cāttā tēvā pūtā eṉa varum.

ivi eṉakku oru karumampaṇi eṉavum, iṅṅaṉam^ ceykiṉṟataṉi oḻiyeṉavum muṉṉiliyēval+ poruṭṭāy vantaṉa. itaṟṟu valleḻuttu+ peṟumāṟu mēlē kūṟupa. (70)

TE273n

tēṟṟa vekaramum^ ciṟappi ṉovvu
mēṟkū ṟiyaṟki valleḻuttu mikā.

iḵtu eytiyatu ikantupaṭāmal+ kāttu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṟṟa ekaramum^ ciṟappiṉ ovvum mēṟkūṟu iyaṟki --- muṉṉar aruttāpattiyāṉ+ peyarkkaṇ varumeṉṟa tēṟṟapporuṇmiyil ekaramum^ ciṟappu+ poruṇmiyil okaramum mūṉṟiṭattum varumeṉṟa ilakkaṇattaṉavām, valleḻuttu mikā --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyā, eṉavē muṉṉilikkaṇ varumeṉṟa ekara okaraṅkaḷ valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉēekoṇṭēṉ niyēekoṇṭāy avaṉēekoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, yāṉōokoṭiyaṉ nīyōokoṭiyi avaṉōokoṭiyaṉ eṉavum peyarkkaṇ īṟāy iyalpāy vantavāṟu kāṇka. itu muṉṉar eytiya ilakkaṇam ikavāmal+ kāttār. muṉ niṉṟa cūttirattiṉ muṉṉilikkum valleḻuttu mikuttu eytāta teytuvittār. iccūttirattiṟku aḷapeṭuttal `teḷiviṉēyum' ((col//261)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ+ koḷka. eṉavē, muṭivupeṟṟuḻi iṅṅaṉam iṭiccollum eṭuttōti+ puṇarppareṉpatūum peṟṟām. (71)

TE274n

ēkāra viṟuti yūkāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ēkāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēkāra iṟuti --- ēkāra īṟṟuppeyar alvaḻikkaṇ, ūkāra iyaṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēkkaṭitu cēkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavarum. (72)

TE275n

māṟukō ḷeccamum viṉāvu meṇṇum^
kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu iṭiccoṟkaḷ iyalpāy+ puṇarkaveṉa eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māṟukōḷ eccamum --- māṟukōṭaliyuṭiya eccapporuṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, viṉāvum --- viṉāpporuṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, eṇṇum --- eṇṇupporuṇmikkaṇ varum ēkāra īṟṟu iṭiccollum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu+ peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉēkoṇṭēṉ ceṉṟēṉ tantēṉ pōyiṉēṉ eṉpuḻi yāṉkoṇṭilēṉeṉa māṟukoṇṭa oḻipupaṭa niṉṟatu. nīyēkoṇṭāy ceṉṟāy tantāy pōyiṉāy eṉavum, nilaṉē nīrē tīyē vaḷiyē koṟṟaṉē cāttaṉē eṉavum varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ+ pirinili ēkāramum īṟṟaci ēkāramum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. avaruḷ ivaṉē koṇṭāṉ eṉavum, `kaḻiyē, ciṟukuraṉeytaloṭu pāṭōvātē --- kaṭalē, pāṭeḻuntolikkum' eṉavum varum. (73)

TE276n

vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉ ōr aṟṟē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- ēkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- ūkāra īṟu alvaḻipōla valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēkkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum vēkkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum varum.

vēkkuṭam vētaliyuṭiya kuṭameṉa viriyum. (74)

TE277n

ē y-eṉ iṟutikku ekaram varumē.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ekaram vitittaliṉ eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēyeṉ iṟutikku ekaram varum --- avvēṟṟumikkaṇ ēyeṉṉum iṟutikku ekaram varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēekkoṭṭil cāli tuḷi puḻi eṉa varum.

varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum varumeṉa+ koḷka. ēeñekiḻcci nērmi eṉa varum. uriyiṉ+ kōṭalāl ekāram ēṟpuḻi+ koḷka. (75)

TE278n

cēveṉ marappeya roṭumara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cē eṉ marappeyar --- peṟṟamaṉṟi+ cēveṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa peyar, oṭumara iyaṟṟu --- oṭumarampōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cēṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum. (76)

TE279n

peṟṟa māyiṉ muṟṟaviṉ vēṇṭum.

iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peṟṟam āyiṉ --- muṟkūṟiya cēveṉpatu peṟṟattiṉi uṇarttiya poḻutāyiṉ, muṟṟa iṉ vēṇṭum --- muṭiya iṉ cāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyavēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cēviṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

muṟṟa eṉṟataṉāṉē muṟkūṟiya cēveṉṉum marappeyarkkum ēveṉpataṟkum urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻtal koḷka. cēviṉkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, ēviṉ kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.

cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum iṉ peṟutal koḷka. cēviṉalam maṇivāl cēviṉimil cēviṉaṭi cēviṉōṭṭam eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉ peṟātu varutalum^ koḷka. ceyyuṭkaṇ `teṉṟaṟku vīṇikku+ cēmaṇikku+ kōkilattiṉ+ --- kaṉṟiṟku' eṉavarum. (77)

TE280n

aikāra viṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar
vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. toki marapiṉuḷ `vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi iaiyeṉṉum' ((eḻu//158)) eṉpataṉkaṇ alvaḻi muṭittār.

itaṉ poruḷ: aikāra iṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar --- aikārīṟṟu+ peyarccoṉ muṉṉar atikārattāṉ+ kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum --- vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ tamakkupporuntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉikkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ eṉṟataṉāṉ urupupuṇarccikkaṇṇum yāṉiyi+ koṇarntāṉeṉa valleḻuttu mikutal koḷka. (78)

TE281n

cuṭṭumuta liṟuti yurupiya ṉiliyum.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu vantu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutal iṟuti --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya aikāra īṟṟuppeyar, urupiyal niliyum --- urupupuṇarcciyiṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpupōla+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aviyaṟṟukkōṭu iviyaṟṟukkōṭu uviyaṟṟukkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum. itaṉi `vaḵkāṉ meykeṭa' ((eḻu//122)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. (79)

TE282n

vici mara+ kiḷavi y-um* ñemi y-um* nami y-um
ā mu+ peyar um^ cē maram* v-iyala.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vicimarakkiḷaviyum --- vicittaṟṟoḻilaṉṟi viciyeṉṉum maratti uṇaraniṉṟacollum, ñemiyum --- ñemi yeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum, namiyum --- nami yeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa collum, ā muppeyarum --- ākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, cēmara iyala --- valleḻuttu mikātu cēmarampōla melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: viciṅkōṭu ñemiṅkōṭu namiṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. ivi kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu eṉṟu uṇarka. (80)

TE283n

paṉiyu mariyu māviri+ kiḷaviyu
niṉiyum^ kāli yammoṭu civaṇu
miye ṉiṟuti yarivarintu keṭumē
meyyava ṇoḻiya veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki ammu vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyum ariyum āviri+ kiḷaviyum --- paṉiyeṉṉum peyarum ariyeṉṉum peyarum āviriyeṉṉum peyarum, niliyuṅkāli ammoṭu civaṇum --- ārāyum^ kālattu valleḻuttu mikātu ammuccāriyiyoṭu porunti muṭiyum, aiyeṉ iṟuti ari varintu keṭum --- avviṭattu aiyeṉṉum īṟu ariyeṉṉum^ colli nīkki ēṉi iraṇṭiṟkum^ keṭum, mey avaṇ oḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar --- taṉṉāṉ ūrappaṭṭa meykeṭātu accolliṭattē niṟka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉi āviri eṉaniṟutti ammu varuvittu aikāram^ keṭuttu oṟṟiṉ mēlē akaramēṟṟi+ paṉaṅkāy āviraṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. ariyeṉa niṟutti ammukkoṭuttu aikāraṅkeṭātu ariyaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa muṭikka. valleḻuttukkēṭu mēlē `kaṭiniliyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kūṟutum.

niṉiyuṅkāli yeṉṟataṉāl tūtuṇi vaḻutuṇi tilli ōli tāḻi eṉa niṟutti ammukkoṭuttu aikāram^ keṭuttu+ tūtuṇaṅkāy vaḻutuṇaṅkāy tillaṅkāy ōlampōḻ tāḻaṅkāy eṉa muṭikka. (81)

TE284n

paṉiyiṉ muṉṉa raṭṭuvaru kāli
niliyiṉ ṟāku miye ṉuyirē
yākāram varuta lāvayiṉāṉa.

itu nilimoḻicceyki nōkki eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṉiyiṉ muṉṉar aṭṭu varukāli --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi+ paṉiyeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar aṭṭeṉṉuñcol varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, niliyiṉṟu ākum aiyeṉ uyir, āvayiṉ āṉa ākāram varutal --- avviṭattu ākāram vantu ammeym mēlēṟi muṭika eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉāaṭṭu eṉa varum. itaṟku mūṉṟāvatum āṟāvatum viriyum.

āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāl ōrānayam viccāvāti eṉṉum vēṟṟumi muṭipum^ kēṭṭāmūlam pāṟāṅkal eṉṉum alvaḻi muṭipum^ koḷka. ivaṟṟuḷ vaṭamoḻikaḷi maṟuttalum oṉṟu. (82)

TE285n

koṭimuṉ variṉē yiyava ṇiṟpa+
kaṭinili yiṉṟē valleḻuttu mikuti.

itu mēl aikāram^ keṭuttu ammuppeṟuka eṉṟār, īṇṭu atu keṭātuniṟka eṉṟaliṉ eṇtiyatu ikantu paṭāmal+ kāttatu. ammu vilakki valleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatumām.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ koṭi variṉ --- paṉiyeṉṉum^ coṉmuṉṉar+ koṭiyeṉṉum^ colvariṉ, ai avaṇ niṟpa --- kēṭu ōtiya aikāram āṇṭu+ keṭātu niṟpa, valleḻuttu mikuti kaṭiniliyiṉṟu --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭital nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉikkoṭi eṉa varum. itaṟku iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum viriyum.

kaṭiniliyiṉṟu eṉṟataṉāṉ aikāra īṟṟu+ peyarkaḷellām eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum ammuccāriyiyum piṟacāriyiyum peṟṟuḻi atikāra valleḻuttu+ keṭuttu+ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. paṉiyiṉkuṟi ariyiṉkōṭu āviriyiṉkōṭu viciyiṉkōṭu ñemiyiṉkōṭu namiyiṉkōṭu eṉavum, tūtuṇiyiṉkāy vaḻutuṇiyiṉkāy uḻiyiṉkōṭu vaḻiyiṉkōṭu eṉavum varum.

paṉittiraḷ paṉintiraḷ eṉaṉum uṟaḻccimuṭipu tokimarapiṉuḷ+ puṟanaṭiyāṉ+ koḷka; alvaḻiyu mātaliṉ. aṉṟi īṇṭu avaṇeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷvārum uḷar. (83)

TE286n

tiṅkaḷ um* nāḷ um* muntu kiḷantu* aṉṉa.

iḵtu iyipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu ikku+ cāriyiyum valleḻuttu vilakki āṉcāriyiyum vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuvitiyum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvitiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiṅkaḷum nāḷum --- aikāra īṟṟu+ tiṅkaḷi uṇaraniṉṟa peyarum nāḷi uṇara niṉṟa peyarum, muntu kiḷantaṉṉa --- ikara īṟṟu+ tiṅkaḷum nāḷumpōla ikkum āṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cittirikku+ koṇṭāṉ kēṭṭiyāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. cittiri nāḷāyiṉ āṉcāriyi koṭukka. valleḻuttukkēṭu muṉṉark `kaṭiniliyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

tiṅkaḷ muṟkūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl uḻiṅkōṭu amiṅkōṭu eṉa melleḻuttu+ koṭuttum^ kaliṅkōṭu kalikkōṭu eṉa uṟaḻcci eytuvittum^ kariyavaṟṟukkōṭu kuṟiyavaṟṟukkōṭu neṭiyavaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa aikāra īṟṟu+ paṇpuppeyarkku vaṟṟukkoṭuttu aikāram^ keṭuttu vaṟṟumici yoṟṟeṉṟu oṟṟukkeṭuttum aviyattukkoṇṭāṉ aviyiṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum iṉṉuṅkoṭuttum paṉiyiṉ māṇpu kēṭṭiyiṉāṭṭiṉāṉeṉa iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉcāriyi koṭuttum muṭikka. aikāra īṟu iṉ cāriyi peṟutal tokimarapiṉuḷ+ kūṟāmiyiṉ īṇṭukkoṇṭām. (84)

TE287n

maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi vaḷiyiya ṉiliyum.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu vakuttaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuvitiyum iyipu valleḻuttu vilakki iṉ vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvitiyum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maḻiyeṉ kiḷavi --- maḻiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuccol, vaḷiyiyal niliyum --- vaḷiyeṉṉum^ col attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭinta iyalpiṉ kaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: maḻiyattukkoṇṭāṉ maḻiyiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

īṇṭu iṉpeṟṟuḻi valleḻuttukkēṭu `kaṭiniliyiṉṟu' ((eḻu//285)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka. cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum^ koḷka. maḻiyattuñāṉṟāṉ maḻiyiṉkaṇñāṉṟāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māṭṭiṉāṉ vantāṉ aṭintāṉ eṉa oṭṭuka. (85)

TE288n

ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki yeṉṉu
miye ṉiṟuti yavāmuṉ variṉē
meyyoṭum^ keṭuta leṉmaṉār pulavar
ṭakāra ṇakāra mātal vēṇṭum.

itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ vēṭki eṉṉum aiyeṉ iṟuti --- ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṭkiyeṉṉum aikāra īṟṟuccol, avā muṉ variṉ --- avāveṉṉum^ coṟku muṉṉar variṉ, meyyoṭum^ keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvikāram^ tāṉ ūrnta meyyoṭum^ kūṭa+ keṭumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar, ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum --- avviṭattu niṉṟa ṭakāra oṟṟu ṇakāra oṟṟāy+ tirital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `vēṇavānaliya veyyavuyirā' eṉa varum.

vēṭkiyāvatu poruḷkaḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam. avāvāvatu apporuḷkaḷi+ peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu mēṉmēl nikaḻum āci. eṉavē, vēṭkiyāluṇṭākiya avāveṉa mūṉṟaṉurupu virintatu. itaṉi vēṭkiyum avāvumeṉa alvaḻiyeṉpārum uḷar. iṅṅaṉam^ kūṟuvār pāṟaṅkal eṉpataṉi ammukkoṭuttu īṇṭu muṭippar. (86)

TE289n

ōkāra viṟuti yēkāra viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ōkāra iṟuti ēkāra iyaṟṟu --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ peyarccol ēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōkkaṭitu cōkkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (87)

TE290n

māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu miyamum^
kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu iṭiccoṉmuṭipu kūṟaliṉ eytātateytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: māṟukoḷ eccamum --- māṟupāṭṭiṉi+ koṇṭa eccapporuṇmiyi oḻipākavuṭiya ōkāramum, viṉāvum --- viṉāpporuṇmiyiyuṭiya ōkāramum, aiyamum --- aiyapporuṇmiyiyuṭiya ōkāramum, kūṟiya valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṉō koṇṭēṉ eṉavum, nīyō koṇṭāy eṉavum, pattō patiṉoṉṟō puṟṟō putalō eṉavum varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ `yāṉō tēṟē ṉavarpoy vaḻaṅkalarē' ((kuṟu//21)) eṉa+ piriniliyum naṉṟō tītō kaṇṭatu eṉa+ teriniliyum ōokoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ ciṟappum^ `kuṉṟuṟaḻnta kaḷiṟeṉkō koyyuḷi māveṉkō' eṉa eṇṇuniliyum valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. itaṉāṉē īṟṟaci varumēṉum uṇarka. (88)

TE291n

oḻintata ṉiliyu moḻintavaṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oḻintataṉ niliyum moḻintavaṟṟu iyaṟṟu --- oḻiyici ōkārattiṉatu niliyum muṟkūṟiya ōkāraṅkaḷiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: koḷalōkoṇṭāṉ celalōceṉṟāṉ taralōtantāṉ pōtalōpōyiṉāṉ eṉa ōci vēṟṟumiyāṉ orucol tōṉṟa+ poruḷ tantu niṟkum. (89)

TE292n

vēṟṟumikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōr aṟṟu* ē
y-okaram varutal ā vayiṉ āṉa.

iḵtu ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉporuḷ: vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ataṉōraṟṟu --- ōkāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum av vēkāra īṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum, āvayiṉāṉa okaram varutal --- avviṭattu okaram varuka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōokkaṭumi kōokkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa varum. (90)

TE293n

illoṭu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu. eṉṉi? muṉṉar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi okaram peṟukaveṉa varintu kūṟātum nilimoḻittoḻil variyātum^ kūṟaliṉ nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ cēṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: illoṭu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkiyākum --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ kōveṉṉum moḻiyiṉi illeṉṉum varumoḻiyōṭu colliṉ okaram mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōvil eṉa varum. kōveṉṟatu uyartiṇi+ peyaraṉṟōveṉiṉ, kōvantateṉṟu aḵṟiṇiyāy muṭitaliṉ aḵṟiṇippāṟpaṭṭateṉka. (91)

TE294n

urupiya ṉiliyu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
yāvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟki yākum.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyi vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: urupiyal niliyum moḻiyumāruḷa --- ōkāra īṟṟu+ cilaporuṭpuṇarccikkaṇ urupupuṇarcciyatu iyalpilēniṉṟu oṉcāriyi peṟṟumuṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, āvayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkiyākum --- avviṭattu valleḻuttiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōoṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

cāriyippēṟu varumoḻi valleḻutti vilakkāmi itaṉāṉum peṟṟām. (92)

TE295n

aukāra viṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉa
ralvaḻi yāṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
valleḻuttu mikutal varinili yiṉṟē
yavviru vīṟṟu mukaram varutal
cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē.

iḵtu aukāra īṟu iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aukāra iṟuti+ peyarnili muṉṉar --- aukāram iṟutiyākiya peyarccoṉ muṉṉar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, alvaḻiyāṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻikkaṇṇam vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, valleḻuttu mikutal variniliyiṉṟu --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu, avviru īṟṟum ukaram varutal cevviteṉpa ciṟanticiṉōr --- avvirukūṟṟu muṭipiṉkaṇṇum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaramvantu muṭital cevviteṉṟu colluvar ciṟantōr eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kuvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum.

cevviteṉṟataṉāṉ meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattum ukarampeṟutal koḷka. kuvuñemirntatu ñemircci eṉavum, vuvu valitu valimi eṉavum varum.

nilimoḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kuviṉkaṭumi eṉa urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻvum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē aikāramum ikaramum vēṟṟumikkaṇ urupu tokiyāyuḻi iyalpātal koḷka. (93)

((uyirmayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((8. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyal))

TE296n

ñakāri yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉa
rallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
valleḻuttu iyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē
yukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

eṉpatu cūttiram.

niṟutta muṟiyāṉē uyirīṟu puṇarttu+ puḷḷiyīṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭum^ ciṟupāṉmi ēṉi+ kaṇattōṭum puṇarumāṟu kūṟaliṉ ivvōttu+ puḷḷimayaṅkiyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu.

iccūttiram ñakāra īṟu vaṉkaṇattōṭu iruvaḻiyum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñakāri oṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar --- ñakāram īṟṟiṉkaṇ oṉṟākaniṉṟa toḻiṟpeyariṉmuṉṉar, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻiyiccollumiṭattum vēṟṟumiyi+ collumiṭattum, valleḻuttu kiḷappiṉ avveḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avvalleḻuttu varumoḻikkaṇ mikku muṭiyum, āvayiṉ āṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram varum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, uriñukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum. (1)

TE297n

ñanamava viyiyiṉu mukara niliyum.

iḵtu avvīṟu meṉkaṇattōṭum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattōṭum muṭiyumeṉa eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ñanamava iyiyiṉum ukaram niliyum --- aññakara īṟu vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi ñanamava mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉum nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: uriñuñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.

yakarattōṭum uyirōṭum puṇarumāṟu tokimarapiṉuḷ `ukaramoṭu puṇarum' ((eḻu//163)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiṉār. (2)

TE298n

nakara viṟutiyu mataṉō raṟṟē.

itu nakara īṟu muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟi eytāta teytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nakara iṟutiyum --- nakara īṟṟuppeyarum muṟkūṟiya kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu puṇarumvaḻi, ataṉōraṟṟu --- aññakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku ukarampeṟṟum ñanamava vantuḻi ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: porunukkaṭitu verinukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. muṭipu oppuminōkki nakara īṟu īṇṭu+ puṇarttār. īṇṭu vēṟṟumi yoḻittatu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟateṉṟu uṇarka. (3)

TE299n

vēṟṟumikku u+ keṭa vakara niliyum.

itu nilimoḻi ukaram vilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumikku --- annakara īṟu vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikku, ukkeṭa akaram niliyum --- mēleytiya ukaraṅkeṭa akarattōṭu nilipeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: porunakkaṭumi verinakkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉa varum. akaraniliyumeṉṉātu ukaraṅkeṭa eṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi iyipuvalleḻuttu vīḻvum^ ciṟupāṉmi ukarappēṟum^ koḷka. veriniṉkuṟi poruniṉkuṟi uriñiṉkuṟi eṉavum, `uyaval yāṉi verinu+ ceṉṟaṉaṉ' ((akam//65)) eṉavum varum. yāṉiyiṉatu mutukiṉmēl+ ceṉṟaṉaṉ eṉa virikka.

porun eṉpatu oru cātippeyarum porunutaleṉṉum viṉippeyarumām. (4)

TE300n

verine ṉiṟuti muḻutum^ keṭuvaḻi
varumiṭa ṉuṭittē melleḻuttu iyaṟki.

iḵtu annakara īṟṟuḷ orumoḻikku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: verin eṉ iṟuti --- verineṉṟu collappaṭum nakara īṟṟumoḻi, muḻutum^ keṭuvaḻi --- taṉ īṟṟu nakaram muṉpeṟṟa ukarattōṭu eñcāmi+ keṭṭa iṭattu, melleḻuttu iyaṟki varum iṭaṉ uṭittu --- melleḻuttuppeṟum iyalpu vantu muṭiyum iṭaṉuṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: veriṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum. melleḻuttu varumoḻi nōkki vantatu. `veyilveri niṟutta payilitaḻ+ pacuṅkuṭi' eṉpatil nakara ikaramē iṭṭeḻutupa. (5)

TE301n

āvayiṉ valleḻuttu mikutalu murittē.

iḵtu ataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āvayiṉ --- avverin eṉṉum^ col avvāṟu īṟu keṭṭu niṉṟa iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum urittu --- melleḻuttēyaṉṟi valleḻuttu mikkumuṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: verikkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum. (6)

TE302n

ṇakāra viṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ
ṭakāra mākum vēṟṟumi+ poruṭkē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ṇakāra īṟu vēṟṟumi+ poruṭkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṇakāra iṟuti --- ṇakāra īṟṟuppeyar, valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, ṭakāramākum vēṟṟumipporuṭku --- ṭakāramāka+ tirintu muṭiyum vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṭkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavarum. maṇki puṇki eṉpaṉa iraṇṭāvataṉ tiripiṉ muṭintaṉa.

kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉpaṉa mēl muṭittum. (7)

TE303n

āṇum peṇṇu maḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṇum peṇṇum --- āṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum peṇṇeṉṉum viravuppeyarum, aḵṟiṇi iyaṟki --- tokimarapiṉuḷ `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉpataṉkaṇ aḵṟiṇippeyar muṭinta iyalpupōla+ tāmum vēṟṟumikkaṇ iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṇki peṇki cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum.

itu tokimarapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉuḷ muṭinta iyalpaṉṟōveṉiṉ, ivi āṇṭu muṭintaṉapōla+ tattam marapiṉ viṉiyāṉ+ pālaṟiyappaṭuvaṉa aṉṟi irutiṇikkaṇṇum aḵṟiṇi y-āy muṭitaliṉ, aḵṟiṇi+ peyaratu iyalpōṭu māṭṭeṟintu muṭittāreṉka. āṇkaṭitu peṇkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻimuṭipu `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉpataṉkaṇ varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ muṭikka. (8)

TE304n

āṇmara+ kiḷavi yarimara viyaṟṟē.

itu tiripuvilakki+ cāriyi vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṇmara+ kiḷavi --- āṇpāli uṇarttātu āṇeṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, ari mara iyaṟṟu --- ariyeṉṉum maram ammuppeṟṟa iyalpiṟṟāy+ tāṉum ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṇaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka. āṇanār ili eṉa varum.

viravuppeya raṉṟeṉṟaṟku marameṉṟār. (9)

TE305n

viṇṇeṉa varūum^ kāya+ peyarvayi
ṉuṇmiyu murittē yatteṉ cāriyi
ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ+ ṟoḻilvaru kāli.

itu ceyyuḷuḷ tiripu vilakki+ cāriyi vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: viṇ eṉa varūum^ kāyappeyarvayiṉ --- viṇṇeṉṟu collavarukiṟa ākāyatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarkkaṇ, attu eṉ cāriyi mikutalum urittu --- atteṉṉum^ cāriyi uṇṭātalum urittu illiyātalum urittu, ceyyuḷ maruṅkil toḻilvarukāli --- ceyyuḷiṭattu+ toḻiṟcol varuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `viṇṇattukkoṭkum vaṇṇattamarar' `viṇṇattukkoṭkum viricelalūrtiyōy' eṉavum, `viṇkuttu nīḷvari veṟpa kaḷiyavō' ((nālaṭi//226)) eṉavum varum. viṇṇeṉṉum kuṟippiṉi nīkkutaṟku+ kāyameṉṟār.

viṇvattukkoṭkum eṉa uṭampaṭumey puṇarntu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. atikāra valleḻuttaṉmiyiṉ+ cāriyi valleḻuttu+ koṭukka. (10)

TE306n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ tiripu vilakki ukaramum valleḻuttum vitikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyar ellām toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyaratu iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi valleḻuttum ukaramum peṟṟum meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaram peṟṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṇukkaṭitu paṇṇukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, maṇṇukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāl toḻiṟpeyarallaṉavum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum iṉcāriyi peṟṟum puṇarvaṉa koḷka. veṇṇukkari `tāḻpeyal kaṉikural kaṭuppa+ paṇṇuppeyarttu' eṇṇuppāṟu veṇṇiṉ kari eṉa varum. (11)

TE307n

kiḷippeya rellām^ koḷattiri pilavē.

iḵtu ivaṟṟuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ tiripu vilakki iyalpu kūṟutaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷippeyarellām --- ṇakāra īṟṟuḷ ōriṉatti uṇaraniṉṟa peyarellām, koḷattiripu ila --- tiripuṭiyaveṉṟu karutumpaṭiyāka+ tiritaliṉṟāy iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: umaṇ eṉa niṟatti+ kuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa+ tantu muṭikka.

iṉi, ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa cāriyipeṟṟu muṭivaṉavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. maṇṇappattam eṇṇanōli eṉavum, kavaṇkāl paraṇkāl eṉavum koḷka.

koḷa eṉṟataṉāl ēḻām vēṟṟumi+ poruṇmi uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷ tirintu muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. aṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ iṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ uṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ īṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, avaṭkoṇṭāṉ ivaṭkoṇṭāṉ uvaṭkoṇṭāṉ evaṭkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum oṭṭuka. (12)

TE308n

vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yeṇṇe ṉuṇavuppeyar
vēṟṟumi yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟu alvaḻiyuḷ vēṟṟumi muṭipupōla+ tirintu muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi alvaḻi --- vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattu, eṇṇeṉ uṇavuppeyar --- variyaṟipporuṇmi uṇarttātu eṇṇeṉṟu collappaṭum uṇaviṉi uṇarttumpeyar, vēṟṟumi iyaṟki niliyalum urittu --- vēṟṟumiyatu tirintu muṭiyum iyalpu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ummiyāl tokimarapiṉuḷ `moḻimutalākum' ((eḻu//147)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpu perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu.

aḵtu eṇkaṭitu eṉa varum. (13)

TE309n

muraṇeṉ ṟoḻiṟpeyar mutaliya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ+ toḻiṟpeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ toḻiṟpeyarkku eytiya ukaramum valleḻuttum vilakki ivvīṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipum vēṟṟumi muṭipum eytuvittatu.

itaṉporuḷ: muraṇ eṉ toḻiṟpeyar --- māṟupāṭu uṇarttum muraṇeṉṉum^ toḻiṟpeyar, mutaliyal niliyum --- tokimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyātu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum īṇṭu vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintu muṭinta iyalpiṉkaṇṇum nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muraṇkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñekiḻntatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, muraṭkaṭumi cēṉi tāṉi paṟi eṉavum, muraṇñekiḻcci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum. itaṉit `toḻiṟpeyarellām' ((eḻu//306)) eṉṟataṉpiṉ viyāta muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ muraṇkaṭumi muraṭkaṭumi, araṇkaṭumi iraṭkaṭumi eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyum^ koḷka. (14)

TE310n

makara viṟuti vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ+
ṟuvara+ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikumē.

itu muṟiyāṉē makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: makara iṟuti vēṟṟumi āyiṉ --- makara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ, tuvara+ keṭṭu valleḻuttu mikum --- annilimoḻi makaram muṟṟakkeṭṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: marakkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. muṇṭakakkōti eṉavum varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum uyartiṇippeyarkkaṇṇum viravuppeyarkkaṇṇum makarakkēṭu koḷka. marañāṇ maranūl ivaṟṟiṟku nāṉkaṉurupu virikka. maramaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. nuṅki eṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, taṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. īṇṭu makarakkēṭē koḷka; muṭipu mēṟkūṟupa ((eḻu//320)). (15)

TE311n

akara ākāram varūum^ kāli
yīṟṟumici yakara nīṭalu murittē.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu muṭipu vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akara ākāram varūum^ kāli --- akara mutaṉmoḻiyum ākāra mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, īṟṟumici akaram nīṭalum uritatu --- makara oṟṟiṉmēl niṉṟa akaram nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu nīṭāmiyum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maram kuḷam eṉa niṟutti makaraṅkeṭuttu aṭi āmpal eṉa+ tantu rakara ḷakaraṅkaḷil niṉṟa akaram ākāramākki marāaṭi kuḷāampal eṉa muṭikka.

mēl+ `celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa' ((eḻu//312)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kuḷāampal eṉpuḻi ākāratti akaramākkuka.

ummiyāṉ maravaṭi kuḷavāmpal eṉa nīṭāmiyuṅkoḷka.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ ivvīṟṟu+ piṟavum vēṟupaṭa muṭivaṉa koḷka. kōṇākōṇam kōṇāvaṭṭam eṉavarum. ivaṟṟiṟku uḷḷeṉṉum urupu virikka.

kōṇākōṇattiṟku valleḻuttukkēṭu mēli+ cūttirattu ilēcāṉ+ koḷka. (16)

TE312n

melleḻuttu uṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē
celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkattāṉa.

itu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikutalōṭu melleḻuttum uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ, eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa --- makara īṟṟuḷ valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum moḻikaḷum uḷa, vaḻakkattu āṉa celvaḻi aṟital --- vaḻakkattiṉkaṇṇum vaḻaṅkum iṭam aṟika eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkari cēṟu tātu pūḻi eṉa varum. ivaṟṟuḷ+ kuḷaṅkari kuḷakkaripōla allaṉa otta uṟaḻcciyāy vaḻaṅkā veṉṟaṟku+ celvaḻi yaṟitaleṉṟār.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ kuḷattukkoṇṭāṉ īḻattucceṉṟāṉ kuṭattuvāy pilattuvāy eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu attu+ peṟṟaṉa. ivi `attēvaṟṟē' ((eḻu//133)) eṉpataṉāṉ oṟṟukkeṭātāyiṟṟu, aḵtu alvaḻikku+ kūṟutaliṉ. maḻakaḷiṟu eṉpatu `maḻavum^ kuḻavu miḷami+ poruḷa' eṉṟa uriccol. atu makara īṟaṉṟu. caṇpakaṅkōṭu eṉpatu vaḻakkiṭattu+ cellātu.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē makaraṅkeṭātu niṟpaṉavum^ koḷka. `pulampukkaṉavē' ((puṟam//258)) `kalampeṟu kaṇṇuḷa rokkal+ ṟaliva' ((malipaṭu//50)) eṉa varum. (17)

TE313n

illa marappeyar vicimara viyaṟṟē.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: illa marappeyar --- pukku uṟiyum illaṉṟi illameṉṉum marattiṉi uṇaraniṉṟa col, vicimara iyaṟṟu --- viciyeṉṉum marattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: illaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

mēli+ cūttirattu `vaḻakkattāṉa' ((eḻu//312)) eṉṟataṉāṉ makarakkēṭu koḷka. (18)

TE314n

alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttu ākum.

itu makaram alvaḻikkaṇ tirika eṉa muṟkūṟāmiyiṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām melleḻuttākum --- makara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām melleḻuttāyttirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maraṅkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. maram peritu eṉpuḻi+ tiripiṉṟeṉpatu āṇikūṟaleṉṉum utti.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāl alvaḻikkaṇ makara īṟu piṟavāṟṟāṉ muṭivaṉavellām muṭikka. vaṭṭattaṭukku, caturappalaki, āytappuḷḷi, vēḻakkarumpu; kalakkoḷ cukku tōri payaṟu; nīlakkaṇ eṉṉum paṇpuppeyarkaḷ makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikku muṭintaṉa. āytavulakki akaramutala ivi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu muṭintaṉa. ellāruṅkuṟiyar nāṅkuṟiyēm ivi uyartiṇippeyar makaram^ tirintu melleḻuttāy muṭintaṉa. colluṅkoṟṟaṉ uṇṇuñcāttaṉ kavaḷamāntumalikeḻunāṭaṉ porumāraṉ tāvupari paṟakkunāri ōṭunākam āṭupōr varukālam kollumyāṉi pāṭumpāṇaṉ eṉa ivi makarantirintum^ keṭṭum nilipeṟṟum vanta peyareccam.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum makaram^ keṭutalum^ keṭāmiyum^ koḷka. marañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, maram yātu valitu aṭintatu eṉavum varum. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pavaḷavāyeṉa uvamattum nila nīreṉa eṇṇiṭattum^ kēṭu koḷka. (19)

TE315n

akameṉ kiḷavikku+ kimuṉ variṉē
mutaṉili yoḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum
