varinili yiṉṟē yāciri yarkka
melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu makara īṟṟu alvaḻikkaṇ immoḻi immuṭipu eytuka eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: akam eṉ kiḷavikku+ ki muṉ variṉ --- akameṉṉum^ colliṟku+ kiyeṉṉum^ col muṉṉē varumāyiṉ, mutaṉili oḻiya muṉṉavi keṭutalum --- muṉṉiṉṟa akaram^ keṭātu niṟpa ataṉ muṉṉiṉṟa kakaramum makaravoṟṟum^ keṭṭu muṭitalum^ keṭātuniṉṟu muṭitalum, varinili iṉṟē yāciriyarkka --- nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyarkku, āvayiṉ āṉa melleḻuttu mikutal --- avi keṭṭa vaḻi melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṅki eṉavarum. akaṅki eṉa+ keṭātu muṭintavaḻi `alvaḻiyellām' ((eḻu//314)) eṉṟataṉāṉ makaram^ tirintu muṭital koḷka. itu paṇputtoki. atikārattāṉum poruṇōkkāṉum vēṟṟumi+ tokiyaṉmi uṇarka. (20)

TE316n

ilameṉ kiḷavikku+ paṭuvaru kāli
niliyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.

iḵtu ilameṉpatu muṟṟuviṉi+ collākātu kuṟippākiya uriccollāy niṟkuṅkāl alvaḻikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ilam eṉ kiḷavikku --- illāmiyeṉṉum kuṟippākiya uriccoṟku, paṭuvarukāli --- uṇṭātaleṉṉum viṉikkuṟippuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ceyyuḷāṉa niliyalum urittu --- ceyyuḷiṭattu makarakkēṭum^ tiripumiṉṟi niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, ummiyāṉ+ piṟacol varuṅkālattu+ kēṭum^ tiripum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uritteṉa+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: `ilampaṭu pulava rēṟṟaki niṟiya' itaṟku illāmi uṇṭākiṉṟa pulavareṉa+ poruḷ kūṟuka. `ilampāṭu nāṇuttarum' eṉkiṉṟatōveṉiṉ, illāmi uṇṭātal nāṇuttarumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuka. itaṉi neṟpāṭu periteṉṟāṟpōla+ koḷka. itu poruḷilameṉa muṟṟuviṉi+ collāmāṟum uṇarka. ilaniṉṟateṉa+ keṭṭavāṟum, ilaṅkeṭaviyantāṉ ilañciṟitāka ilantīteṉṟu eṉa+ kacatakkaḷ varumvaḻi+ tirintavāṟum^ kāṇka.

`ellā' ((eḻu//314)) meṉṟataṉāṉ ilamvaruvatupōlum ilam yāriṭattu eṉa vakara yakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.

itaṉi ilattāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉa vēṟṟumiyeṉṟārāl uriyāciriyareṉiṉ, paṟṟappaṭṭa pulavareṉpatu peyareccamātaliṉ+ paṟṟaveṉṉum^ toḻil tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa mutaṉiliccolli+ cūttirattu āciriyar eṭuttōtiṟṟilarātalāṉum paṭuveṉpatu tāṉum pulavareṉṉum peyarōṭu muṭiyaṅkāl iraṇṭukālamum kāṭṭum īṟukaḷ tokka mutaṉili+ collāy niṟṟaliṉ, ataṉi eṭuttōtiṉārātalāṉum āciriyarkku aṅṅaṉam kūṟutal karuttaṉmi uṇarka.

aṉṟiyum paṟṟappaṭṭa eṉpuḻi iraṇṭu mutaṉili kūṭi oṉṟāy niṉṟu paṟṟutali+ ceyyappaṭṭa eṉa+ poruḷ tārāmiyāṉum alvaḻi yatikāramātalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmi uṇarka. (24)

TE317n

attoṭu civaṇu māyirattu iṟuti
yotta veṇṇu muṉvaru kāli.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ eṇṇuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku+ tokimarapiṉuḷ `uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki' ((eḻu//164)) eṉpataṉāṉ eytiya ēyeṉcāriyi vilakki attu vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyirattu iṟuti --- āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyariṉ makaram, otta eṇ muṉvarukālitaṉakku akappaṭum moḻiyāy+ poruntiṉa eṇṇuppeyar taṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, attoṭu civaṇum --- tokimarapiṉ+ kūṟiya ēyeṉ cāriyi oḻittu attuccāriyiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyirattoṉṟu āyirattoṉpatu eṉa oṉṟumutal oṉpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. makaratti attiṉmiciyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭuttu `attiṉakara makaramuṉiyilli' ((eḻu//125)) eṉṟu muṭikka. āyirattu orupatu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum oṭṭuka.

nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ āyirattukkuṟi kūṟu mutal eṉpaṉavum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē āyirappatteṉpuḻi makaraṅkeṭuttu valloṟṟu mikuttu muṭikka. (22)

TE318n

aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉu mataṉō raṟṟē.

iḵtu `aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilikku uriya' ((eḻu//110)) eṉṟamiyiṉ avveṇṇu+ peyari aṭiyaṭuttu muṭikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aṭiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum --- avvāyira meṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar aṭiyaṭutta moḻiyōṭu variṉum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- muṟkūṟiyataṉōṭu orutaṉmittāy attu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyirattoṉṟu iraṇṭu irupatiṉāyirattoṉṟu āṟāyirattoṉṟu nūṟāyirattoṉṟu munnūṟāyirattoṉṟu ainnūṟāyirattoṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. muṉṉar ilēciṉāṉ muṭintavaṟṟiyam aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka. patiṉāyirattukkuṟi puṟam kūṟu mutal eṉavum, nūṟāyirappattu eṉavum varum. (23)

TE319n

aḷavu niṟiyu vēṟṟumi yiyala.

iḵtu avveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum --- atikārattāl āyiram^ tāṉē niṉṟuḻiyum aṭiyaṭuttu niṉṟuḻiyum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl, vēṟṟumi iyala --- makara īṟṟu vēṟṟumiyōṭu ottu valleḻuttu vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttumikkum iyalpukaṇam vantuḻit `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṉum ilēcāṉ eytiya makaram^ keṭṭum puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āyiram patiṉāyiram nūṟāyiram eṉa niṟutti+ kalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭil akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḵcu kaḻañcu toṭi tulām palam eṉavum^ tantu oṭṭuka.

vēṟṟumiyiyala eṉavē tām vēṟṟumiyallavāyiṉa. (24)

TE320n

paṭarkki+ peyaru muṉṉili+ peyarum^
toṭakkam^ kuṟukum peyarnili+ kiḷaviyum
vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyu
melleḻuttu mikuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu uyartiṇippeyarum viravuppeyarum urupiyaluḷ muṭintavāṟē īṇṭu+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum muṭikaveṉa eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: paṭarkki+ peyarum muṉṉilippeyarum --- ellārumeṉṉum paṭarkkippeyarum ellīrumeṉṉum muṉṉilippeyarum, toṭakkam^ kuṟukum peyarnili+ kiḷaviyum --- kiḷittoṭarcci+ poruḷavāy neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum^ tām nām yāmeṉṉum peyarākiya nilimiyiyuṭiya collum, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum --- vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇṇāyiṉ urupupuṇarccikku+ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum, āvayiṉ āṉa melleḻuttu mikutal --- mēl neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ellāru meṉpataṉi makaroṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭuttu rakarappuḷḷiyi niṟutti+ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉa+ tantu iṭiyilē tammuccāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyiyum^ koṭuttu ellārtaṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. itaṟku `ammiṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//129)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟacāriyikkaṇ makara oṟṟu+ tirintu `ṅañanavākum' eṉa+ ceykiceytu muṭikka. ellīru meṉpataṟku iṭiyilē nummuccāriyiyum iṟutiyilē ummuccāriyiyum^ koṭuttu muṟkūṟiya ceykikaḷellām^ ceytu ellīrnuṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa muṭikka. tām nām eṉpaṉavaṟṟi `ēṉi yiraṇṭu neṭumutal kuṟukum' ((eḻu//188)) eṉa+ kuṟukki `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉpataṉāṉ makaram^ keṭuttu+ taṅki naṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka. yām eṉpataṉi ākāratti ekaramākki yakara oṟṟi+ keṭuttu `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāl makaraṅkeṭuttu eṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa muṭikka. toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku iccūttirattāṉ melleḻuttu mikukka.

urupiyal niliyum eṉpataṉāṉ vēṟṟumiyātal peṟāniṟkavum piṉṉum vēṟṟumiyāyiṉeṉṟa mikiyāṉē paṭarkkippeyarum muṉṉilippeyarum iyalpukaṇattu ñakāramum ṅakāramum vantuḻi+ tammuccāriyiyum nummuccāriyiyum peṟutalum makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟutalum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. ellārtam^ ñāṇum ellīrnum^ ñāṇum nūlum eṉa varum.

iṉi+ toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum avviraṇṭu ilēcāṉum makaraṅkeṭutalum oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. taññāṇ naññāṇ eññāṇ nūl eṉa varum.

iṉṉum āvayiṉāṉa eṉṟataṉāṉē ellārtam ellīrnum eṉa niṉṟavaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar ēṉi maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉpaṉa vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭāmiyum ummuppeṟutalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē toṭakkam^ kuṟukuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ tammaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaram^ keṭāmiyum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tamakāṇam namakāṇam emakāṇam numakāṇam eṉa urupīṟṟu+ ceykikaḷum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē num eṉpataṟku makaratti melloṟṟākki nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varutalum, nuññāṇ eṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutalum, numvali eṉa makaram^ keṭātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ellārkiyum ellīrkiyum eṉa+ tammum nummum peṟātu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. (25)

TE321n

allatu kiḷappi ṉiyaṟki yākum.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟu peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ iyaṟkiyākum --- ammūṉṟu peyarum alvaḻiyi+ collumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

īṇṭu iyaṟkiyeṉṟatu cāriyi peṟāmi nōkki. ivaṟṟiṉ īṟutirital `alvaḻiyellām' eḻu//314) eṉpataṉuḷ ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ellārum^ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, ellīrum^ kuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavum, tām^ kuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, tāṅkuṟiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉavum, nāṅkuṟiyam ciṟiyam tīyam periyam eṉavum, yāṅkuṟiyēm ciṟiyēm tīyēm periyēm eṉavum varum.

iṉi ellārum vantār yāttār aṭintār, ellīrum vantīr yāttīr aṭintīr eṉavum, tāmvantār yāttār aṭintār eṉavum, nām varutum yāttum aṭitum, yāmvaruvēm yāppēm aṭivēm eṉavum ēṉikkaṇaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar makaram^ tiriyātu niṟṟalum `uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇippeyarum'(eḻu//153) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. (26)

TE322n

allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
ellām eṉum peyar urupiyal niliyum
vēṟṟumi y-alvaḻi+ cāriyi niliyātu.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟuḷ viravuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum urupiyalōṭu māṭṭeṟintu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- alvaḻikkaṇ+ colliṉum vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ+ colliṉum, ellāmeṉum peyar urupiyal niliyum --- ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyar urupupuṇarcciyiṉ iyalpilē niṉṟu muṭiyum, vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi+ cāriyi niliyātu --- appeyar vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarcciyallāta iṭattu vaṟṟu+ cāriyi nillātāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyaṉiliyum eṉṟa māṭṭēṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ummuppeṟṟu niṟṟalum poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟu niṟṟalum uṇarttiṟṟu.

ummuccāriyi oṉṟumē peṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa alvaḻiyiṉiyum vaṟṟum ummumpeṟṟu muṭikiṉṟa vēṟṟumiyōṭu uṭaṉōti atuvum vaṟṟuppeṟumāṟupōla māṭṭeṟinta mikiyāṉē vaṉkaṇattu alvaḻikkaṇ nilimoḻi makarakkēṭum varumoḻi valleḻuttuppēṟum meṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟum peṟātum varutalum ēṉi+ kaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟum makaraṅkeṭātu ummuppeṟātum varutalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: ellākkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavum, ellāñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ellāñāṉṟaṉa nīṇṭaṉa māṇṭaṉa eṉavum, ellāyāḻum vaṭṭum aṭiyum eṉavum, ellāmvāṭiṉa āṭiṉa eṉavum varum. iṉi vēṟṟumikkaṇ ellāvaṟṟukkōṭum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa ivi vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku makaram vaṟṟiṉmiciyoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukka. iṉi meṉkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟuñāṇum nūlum maṇiyum eṉavum, ēṉikkaṇattukkaṇ ellāvaṟṟiyāppum valiyum aṭiyum eṉavum varum. ēṉikkaṇamum vaṟṟum ummum peṟṟaṉa. (27)

TE323n

melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.

iḵtu orucār valleḻutti vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mel=eḻuttu mikiṉum māṉam illi --- avvellāmeṉpatu alvaḻikkaṇ mēl ilēciṉāṉ+ kūṟiya valleḻuttēyaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikku muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, valleḻuttumikutalē perumpāṉmiyāyiṟṟu. muṟkūṟiya ceykimēlē itu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ makarakkēṭum ummuppēṟum^ koḷka.

ellāṅkuṟiyavum ciṟiyavum tīyavum periyavum eṉavarum.

māṉamilli eṉṟataṉāṉ uyartiṇikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttumikku iṟuti ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu ummuppeṟṟu muṭitalum^ koḷka. ellākkollarum cāṉṟārum taccarum pārppārum kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉavum ellā ñāṉṟārum nāykarum maṇiyakārarum vaṇikarum aracarum eṉavum varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē uyartiṇikkaṇ ellām^ kuṟiyarum ciṟiyarum tīyarum periyarum eṉa makaraṅkeṭṭu melleḻuttu mikku ummuppeṟutalum ellāṅkuṟiyar ciṟiyar tīyar periyar eṉavum, kuṟiyīr kuṟiyam eṉavum ummu+ peṟātu varutalum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iṭikkaṇattum uyirkkaṇattum makaraṅkeṭātu ummiṉṟi varutalum^ koḷka. ellāmvantēm aṭintēm eṉavarum. (28)

TE324n

uyartiṇi yāyi ṉurupiya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu ellāmeṉpataṟku uyartiṇi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyartiṇiyāyiṉ urupiyal niliyum --- ellāmeṉpatu uyartiṇiyāy niṟkumāyiṉ urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy iṭikkaṇ nammum iṟutikkaṇ ummumpeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

urupiyaluḷ `ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar --- vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi' ((eḻu//389)) vakuttataṉāṉ vaṟṟiṉ miciyoṟṟeṉṟu makaraṅkeṭutta atikārattāṉ `uyartiṇi yāyi ṉammiṭi varum' ((eḻu//190)) eṉa nammiṉ muṉṉum makaraṅkeṭuttār, ataṉōṭu īṇṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ atu koṇṭu īṇṭum makaraṅkeṭukka. `ammiṉiṟuti'(eḻu//129) eṉpuḻit `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ nammuccāriyiyiṉatu makarantirital koḷka.

ellānaṅkiyum ceviyum taliyum puṟamum eṉa oṭṭuka.

varumoḻi variyātu kūṟaliṉ ellānaññāṟciyum nīṭciyum eṉa ēṟpaṉavaṟṟōṭu muṭipu aṟintu oṭṭuka. (29)

TE325n

numme ṉorupeyar melleḻuttu mikumē.

itu makara īṟṟiṟku valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttu vitittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nummeṉ orupeyar melleḻuttu mikum --- nummeṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa viravuppeyar poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

nuṅki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. `makaraviṟuti' ((eḻu//310)) eṉpataṉāṉ makaraṅkeṭukka.

oṉṟeṉa muṭitta leṉpataṉāṉ uṅki eṉa varuvatūum^ koḷka. `tuvara' ((eḻu//310)) eṉṟataṉāṉ ñakaranakaraṅkaḷ vantuḻi makaraṅkeṭutalum orupeyar eṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu mikatalum^ koḷka. nuññāṇ nūl eṉavarum. iṉṉum orupeyar eṉṟataṉāṉ nummaṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa makaraṅkeṭāmiyum koḷka. (30)

TE326n

allataṉ maruṅkiṉ+ collum^ kāli
yukkeṭa niṉṟa meyvayi ṉīvara
iyiṭi nilii yīṟukeṭa rakara
niṟṟal vēṇṭum puḷḷiyoṭu puṇarntē
yappāṉ moḻivayi ṉiyaṟki yākum.

itu nummeṉpataṟku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allataṉ maruṅkiṉ+ colluṅkāli --- nummeṉpataṉi alvaḻikkaṇ+ kūṟumiṭattu, u keṭaniṉṟa meyvayiṉ ī vara --- nakarattuḷ ukaraṅkeṭṭu+ pōka oḻintu niṉṟa nakaravoṟṟiṭattē īkāram vantu niṟpa, i iṭinili i īṟukeṭa --- ōr ikaram iṭiyilē vantu nilipeṟṟu makaramākiya īṟukeṭṭuppōka, puḷḷi rakaram niṟṟal vēṇṭum --- āṇṭu+ puḷḷi peṟṟu oru rakaram vantu niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ, appāl moḻivayiṉ --- akkūṟṟiṉiyuṭiya nilimoḻiyiṭattu, iyaṟkiyākum --- varuñcol iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nīyirkuṟiyīr ciṟiyīr tīyīr periyīr eṉavarum.

colluṅkāli yeṉṟataṉāṉē nīyirñāṉṟīr nīṇṭīr māṇṭīr yāttīr vāṭiṉīr aṭintīr eṉa ēṉikkaṇattilum oṭṭuka. (31)

TE327n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ makaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkum alvaḻikkaṇ melleḻuttāy+ tirintum varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki ñakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar pōla niṟkumeṉa+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- makara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cemmukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, cemmukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum. `tummu+ ceṟuppa' veṉpatum atu. ivi `kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar+ taṉṉuru viraṭṭal' ((eḻu//160)).

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ ukarampeṟātu nāṭṭaṅkaṭitu āṭṭaṅkaṭitu eṉa melleḻuttāy alvaḻikkaṇ tiritalum nāṭṭakkaṭumi āṭṭakkaṭumi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ valleḻuttu mikutalum^ koḷka. (32)

TE328n

īmum^ kammu murumeṉ kiḷaviyu
māmu+ peyaru mavaṟṟō raṉṉa.

itu poruṭpeyaruḷ+ cila toḻiṟpeyarōṭu ottu muṭika eṉa eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īmum^ kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum āmuppeyarum --- īmeṉṉum^ collum kammeṉṉum^ collum urumeṉṉum^ collumākiya ammūṉṟu peyarum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa --- muṟkūṟiya toḻiṟpeyarōṭu orutaṉmiyavāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum iyalpukaṇattu ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

īm eṉpatu cuṭukāṭu; kam --- toḻil.

utāraṇam: īmukkaṭitu kammukkaṭitu urumukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, īmukkaṭumi kammukkaṭumi urumukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum oṭṭuka.

kiḷaviyeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum uyir varuvaḻi ukarampeṟātu īmaṭivu īmaṭintatu eṉa niṟṟalum^ koḷka.

taṉṉiṉamuṭitta leṉpataṉāl ammu tammu nammu eṉa+ cāriyikkaṇṇum ukaram varutal koḷka. (33)

TE329n

vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yiraṇṭum^
tōṟṟam vēṇṭu makkeṉ cāriyi.

itu mēl muṭipukūṟiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ vēṟōr muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ --- vēṟṟumipporuṭpuṇarcciyāyiṉ, ēṉi iraṇṭum tōṟṟam vēṇṭum akkeṉ cāriyi --- iṟutiyil urumoḻinta iraṇṭum akkeṉṉum^ cāriyi tōṉṟi muṭitalivēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: īmakkuṭam kammakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, ñāṟci neruppu māṭci viṟaku eṉavum oṭṭuka.

akku vakuppavē nilimoḻi+ toḻilākiya ukaraṅkeṭṭu muṟkūṟiya valleḻuttu vilakkappaṭāmiyiṉ niṉṟu muṭintatu.

vaṉkaṇattiṟku muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟiyatu kuṇavēṟṟumikkeṉṟum īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu poruḷ+ puṇarccikkeṉṟum^ koḷka. (34)

TE330n

vakāra miciyu makāram^ kuṟukum.

itu muṉṉar `ariyaḷavu kuṟukal' ((eḻu//13)) eṉavum, `ṉakāri muṉṉar' ((eḻu//52)) eṉavum^ kūṟiya makaram irumoḻikkaṇṇum^ kuṟukumeṉa ataṉ īṟṟakattu eytātateytuvikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vakāramiciyum makāram^ kuṟukum --- makaram orumoḻikkaṇṇēyaṉṟi vakārattiṉmēlum^ kuṟukum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nilam valitu varumvaṇṇakkaṉ eṉa varum. (35)

TE331n

nāḷ+peyar+ kiḷavi mēl+kiḷantu* aṉṉa
attu* um āṉ mici vari-nili y-iṉṟu* ē
y-oṟṟu mey keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu nāṭpeyarkku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṭpeyarkkiḷavi mēṟkiḷantaṉṉa --- makara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar ikara īṟṟu nāṭpeyar pōla āṉ cāriyipeṟṟu muṭiyum, attum āṉmicivarinili iṉṟu --- attuccāriyi āṉcāriyi mēlum piṟacāriyi mēlum varutal nīkkunilimiyiṉṟu, oṟṟu meykeṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- āṇṭu nilimoḻi makara oṟṟu+ keṭuka eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

ummiyi āṉmiciyumeṉa māṟuka.

utāraṇam: makattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ōṇattāṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉka. ēṉi nāṭkaḷōṭum oṭṭuka. makara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu `atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli' ((eḻu//125)) eṉa akaram^ keṭuttu+ `kuṟṟiyalukaramumaṟṟeṉa' ((eḻu//105)) eṉa āṉēṟṟi `āṉi ṉakaramum' ((eḻu//124)) eṉṟataṉāṉ ṟakaramākki muṭikka. makattuñāṉṟu koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa ñāṉṟeṉṉum^ cāriyimēl attu vantatu.

variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ immuṭipu nāṉkukaṇattum^ koḷka. makattāṉ ñāṟṟiṉāṉ niṟuttiṉāṉ māyntāṉ vantāṉ aṭintāṉ eṉa varum. (36)

TE332n

ṉakāra viṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ
ṟakāra mākum vēṟṟumi+ poruṭkē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ṉakara iṟuti vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ṉakāra iṟuti valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṟakāramākum --- ṉakāra īṟṟuppeyar valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṟakāramākum, vēṟṟumi+ poruṭku --- vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: poṟkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum. (37)

TE333n

maṉṉum^ ciṉṉu māṉu mīṉum
piṉṉu muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu
maṉṉa viyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu avvīṟṟu acinili iṭiccoṟkaḷum ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟkaḷum viṉiyeccamum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: maṉṉum^ ciṉṉum āṉum īṉum piṉṉum muṉṉum viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyum --- maṉṉeṉṉum^ collum^ ciṉṉeṉṉum^ collum āṉeṉṉum^ collum īṉeṉṉum^ collum viṉiyeccamākiya collum, aṉṉa iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiyavāy ṉakaram ṟakaramāy muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `atumaṉ+ koṇkaṉ ṟērē' `kāppum pūṇṭiciṉ+ kaṭiyum pōkal' ((akam//7)) eṉavum, āṟkoṇṭāṉ īṟkoṇṭāṉ piṟkoṇṭāṉ muṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, variṟkoḷḷum cellum tarum pōm eṉavum varum.

peyarāntaṉmiyavākiya āṉ īṉ eṉpaṉavaṟṟi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ āṉkoṇṭāṉ īṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ tiriyāmiyum^ koḷka.

piṉmuṉ eṉpaṉa peyarum urupum viṉiyeccamumāy niṟṟaliṉ+ peyar īṇṭu+ kūṟiṉār. ēṉiya urupiyaluḷḷum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi yeṉpataṉkaṇṇum muṭiyum. appeyari muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ+ piṉkoṇṭāṉ muṉkoṇṭāṉ eṉa+ tiriyāmiyum^ koḷka.

iyalaveṉṟataṉāl ūṉeṉṉum^ cuṭṭu ūṉ koṇṭāṉeṉa iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. (38)

TE334n

cuṭṭumutal vayiṉu mekaramutal vayiṉu
mappaṇpu niliyu miyaṟkiya veṉpa.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷuṇarttum iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu mutal vayiṉum --- cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉum^ collum, ekara mutal vayiṉum --- ekaramākiya mutal viṉāviṉiyuṭiya vayiṉeṉṉum^ collum, appaṇpuniliyum iyaṟkiya eṉpa --- mēl ṉakaram ṟakaramāmeṉṟa taṉmi nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpiyuṭiya veṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ ivvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ uvvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ evvayiṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉavarum.

iyaṟkiya eṉṟataṉāl tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. (39)

TE335n

kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.

itu ṉakāram^ tiriyātu iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kuyiṉeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum --- kuyiṉeṉṉuñcol tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kuyiṉkuḻām celavu tōṟṟam paṟivu eṉavarum. kuyiṉeṉpatu mēkam. aḵtu aḵṟiṇi+ peyar.

tokimarapiṉuḷ uyartiṇippeyarum viravuppeyarum iyalpākaveṉṟār.

kuyiṉ viṉiyumām.

iyaṟki eṉṟataṉāṉ+ kāṉkōḻi nōṉkuṇam vāṉkari eṉa varuvaṉavum^ koḷka. (40)

TE336n

ekiṉmara māyi ṉāṇmara viyaṟṟē.

itu tiripuvilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ekiṉ maramāyiṉ --- ekiṉeṉpatu puḷḷaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, āṇmara iyaṟṟu --- āṇmarattiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy ammuppeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ekiṉaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum. (41)

TE337n

ēṉi yekiṉē yakaram varumē
valleḻuttu iyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum.

ituvum atu. tiripu vilakki akaram vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi ekiṉē akaram varum --- maramallāta ekiṉ nilimoḻikkaṇ akarampeṟṟu muṭiyum, valleḻuttiyaṟki mikutal vēṇṭum --- āṇṭu varumoḻi valleḻuttiyalpu mikku muṭitali vēṇṭum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ekiṉaṅkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

mēliccūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi yāppu aṭivu eṉa varum.

mēliccūttirattōṭu itaṉi oṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ iyalpu kaṇattum akarappēṟu koḷka. ekiṉañāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi yāppu aṭivu eṉa varum.

iyaṟki yeṉṟataṉāṉ akarattōṭu melleḻuttu+ pēṟum^ koḷka. ekiṉaṅkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

iṉi+ ciṟupāṉmi ekiṉcēval ekiṉaccēval peṭi eṉpaṉa āṟaṉurupu virivuḻi īṇṭi ilēcāṉ muṭikka. paṇpu karutiyavaḻi ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka. (42)

TE338n

kiḷi+peyar ellām^ kiḷi+peyar iyala.

itu ṉakaram^ tiritali vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷippeyarellām --- ṉakara īṟṟu+ kiḷippeyarellām, kiḷippeyar iyala --- ṇakara īṟṟu+ kiḷippeyarpōla+ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eyiṉkuṭi cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum. eyiṉ vantatu eṉṟu aḵṟiṇikkum eytutaliṉ toki marapiṉuḷ muṭiyātāyiṟṟu, āṇṭu uyartiṇikkē kūṟutaliṉ.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉē eyiṉakkaṉṉi piḷḷi eṉa akkum valleḻuttum peṟutalum, eyiṉavāḻvu eṉa valleḻuttu+ peṟāmiyum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē pārppaṉakkaṉṉi kumari cēri piḷḷi eṉa ākāraṅkuṟuki akkum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum pārppaṉavāḻkki eṉa valleḻuttu+ koṭātum muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum veḷḷāḷaṉeṉa niṉṟataṉi aṉkeṭuttu+ pirittu ḷakāravoṟṟiṉi ṇakāravoṟṟākki veḷḷāṇkumari piḷḷi māntar vāḻkki oḻukkam eṉa muṭikka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mutaleḻutti nīṭṭi ḷakāra voṟṟiṉi+ keṭuttu vēḷāṇeṉa muṭikka.

itaṉāṉē poruna vāḻkkiyum muṭikka.

vēṭṭuvakkumari yeṉpatu marūuvaḻakku. (43)

TE339n

mīṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvē.

ituvum atu, taṉ tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: mīṉeṉkiḷavi valleḻuttu uṟaḻvu --- mīṉeṉṉum^ col tiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mīṉkaṇ mīṟkaṇ, mīṉciṉi mīṟciṉi, mīṉṟali mīṟṟali, mīṉpuṟam mīṟpuṟam eṉa varum. (44)

TE340n

tēṉeṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ
mēṉili yottalum valleḻuttu mikutalu
māmuṟi yiraṇṭu murimiyu muṭittē
valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yilli.

ituvum atu. mēlataṉōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tēṉ eṉ kiḷavi valleḻuttu iyiyiṉ --- tēṉeṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy variṉ, mēl nili ottalum --- mīṉeṉpataṟku+ kūṟiya tiripuṟaḻcci nili ottu muṭitalum, valleḻuttu mikutalum --- varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalumākiya, āmuṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu --- ammuṟimiyiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭiṉiyum urittātaliyum uṭittu, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi iṟutiyilli --- valleḻuttu mikku varumiṭattu iṟutiyiṉiṉṟa ṉakaram^ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

urimiyumeṉṉum ummi `melleḻuttu mikiṉum' ((eḻu//341)) eṉa mēlvarukiṉṟataṉi nōkkiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉkuṭam tēṟkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa mēṉili ottaṉa. tēkkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa ṉakaraṅkeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. (45)

TE341n

melleḻuttu mikiṉu māṉa milli.

ituvum atu. uṟaḻcciyum valleḻuttum vilakki melleḻuttu vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu mikiṉum māṉamilli --- muṟkūṟiya tēṉeṉkiḷavi valleḻuttu vantāl avvalleḻuttu mikutalē yaṉṟi melleḻuttu mikiṉum^ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

ṉakarakkēṭu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: tēṅkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavarum. (46)

TE342n

melleḻuttu iyiyi ṉiṟutiyo ṭuṟaḻum.

itu tokimarapiṉuḷ `vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum valleḻuttalvaḻi' ((eḻu//148)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpi vilakki uṟaḻumeṉṟaliṉ eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ --- attēṉeṉkiḷavi melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ, iṟutiyoṭu uṟaḻum --- nilimoḻiyiṟutiyiṉ ṉakara voṟṟu+ keṭutalum^ keṭāmiyumākiya uṟaḻcciyāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉñeri tēñeri tēṉuṉi tēnuṉi tēṉmoḻi tēmoḻi eṉa varum.

mēl `āmuṟi' ((eḻu//340)) eṉṟataṉāl tēñeri tēññeri tēnuṉi tēnnuṉi tēmoḻi tēmmoḻi eṉa ṉakaram^ keṭṭu+ tattam melleḻuttu mikkum mikātum muṭintaṉavum^ koḷka.

iṉi mēl `māṉamilli' ((eḻu//341)) eṉṟataṉāṉ īṟu keṭṭu melleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koṇṭu tēñeri tēnuṉi tēmoḻi eṉpaṉa kāṭṭiṉ avi muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉka. (47)

TE343n

iṟāal+ ^tōṟṟam iyaṟki y-ākum.

iḵtu a+ tēṉ eṉpataṟku uyir+kaṇattu oru moḻi muṭipu vēṟṟumi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṟāal+ ^tōṟṟam --- tēṉ eṉṉum^ col iṟāl eṉṉum varumoḻiyatu tōṟṟattukkaṇ, iyaṟki y-ākum --- nilimoḻiyiṉ ṉakaram^ keṭātu* ē niṉṟu iyalpu* āy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēṉ iṟāl eṉa varum. (48)

TE344n

oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalu murittē.

ituvum ataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟumiku takaramoṭu niṟṟalum urittu --- attēṉeṉpatu iṟāloṭu puṇarumiṭattu+ piṟitumōr takarattōṭu niṉṟu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

`valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi yiṟuti yilli' ((eḻu//340)) eṉṟataṉāṉ nili moḻi īṟu keṭukka. takaram mikumeṉṉātu oṟṟumiku takarameṉṟataṉāṉ īroṟṟākkuka.

utāraṇam: tēttiṟāl eṉa varum.

mēli+ cūttirattōṭu itaṉi yoṉṟāka ōtātataṉāṉ+ piṟavarumoḻikkaṇṇum immuṭipu koḷka. tēttaṭi tēttī eṉa varum.

tōṟṟameṉṟataṉāl tēṉaṭi tēṉī eṉṉum iyalpum^ koḷka. (49)

TE345n

miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum^ kaṉṉu
mannāl+ collum^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum varukeṉa eytuvitta muṭipi vilakki+ toḻil+ peyariyalpāmeṉa+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum^ kaṉṉum a+ nāṟcollum --- miṉṉeṉṉum^ collum piṉṉeṉṉum^ collum paṉṉeṉṉum^ collum^ kaṉṉeṉṉum^ collumākiya annāṉku collum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉum ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

`miṉṉuccey viḷakkattu+ piṉṉuppiṇi yaviḻnta' eṉavum, paṉṉukkaṭitu kaṉṉukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, miṉṉukkaṭumi piṉṉukkaṭumi paṉṉukkaṭumi kaṉṉukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉavum varum.

toḻiṟpeyarellām^ toḻiṟpeyariyala eṉṟu ōtātu kiḷantōtiṉār, ivi toḻiṉilikkaṇṇaṉṟi vēṟu tam poruḷuṇara niṉṟavaḻiyum immuṭipu eytumeṉṟaṟku.

miṉṉeṉpatu miṉṉutaṟṟoḻilum `miṉṉunimirntaṉṉa' eṉa miṉṉeṉappaṭuvatōr poruḷum uṇarttum. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. (50)

TE346n

vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉēṉi yekiṉoṭu
tōṟṟa mokkum^ kaṉṉeṉ kiḷavi.

itu nilimoḻikkaṇ ukaramvilakki akaram vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ ēṉi ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam okkum --- vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ oḻinta maramallāta ekiṉoṭu tōṟṟam ottu akaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭiyum, kaṉ eṉ kiḷavi --- kaṉṉeṉṉum^ col eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṉṉakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉa varum. kaṉṉakkaṭumi eṉa+ kuṇa vēṟṟumiyum^ ciṟupāṉmi koḷka.

tōṟṟameṉṟataṉāl alvaḻikkaṇ vaṉkaṇattu akaramum melleḻuttum ēṉikkaṇattu akaramum^ koḷka. kaṉṉaṅkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavuṉṉañāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum varum. kaṉṉaṅkaṭumi eṉa+ kuṇavēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivviti koḷka.

`poṉṉakar varippiṉ+ kaṉṉam^ tūkki' ((aiṅkuṟu//247)) eṉpatōveṉiṉ, atu makara īṟṟu+ poruṭpeyar. (51)

TE347n

iyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tanti muṟivariṉ
mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa vakara niliyu
meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum avviyal+ peyarē.

iḵtu `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeya riyalpumā ruḷavē' ((eḻu//155)) eṉṟataṟku īṇṭu+ tiripu kūṟaliṉ eṇtāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tanti muṟi variṉ --- iv vīṟṟu viravuppeyaruḷ iyaṟpeyariṉ muṉṉar+ tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyar varumoḻiyāy varumāyiṉ, mutaṟkaṇ meykeṭa akaram niliyum --- attanti yeṉpataṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa takaravoṟṟukkeṭa ataṉ mēlēṟiniṉṟa akaram^ keṭātu niṟkum, avviyaṟpeyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭum --- annilimoḻiyākiya iyaṟpeyar aṉṉeṉṉum^ collil akaram ēṟiniṉṟa meyyi oḻittu avvaṉtāṉ keṭṭumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cāttanti koṟṟanti eṉa varum.

mutaṟkaṇ meyyeṉṟataṉāl cāttaṉṟanti koṟṟaṉṟanti eṉṉum iyalpu muṭipum^ koḷka. (52)

TE348n

ātaṉum pūtaṉum^ kūṟiya viyalpoṭu
peyaroṟ ṟakaram^ tuvara+ keṭumē.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ātaṉum pūtaṉum --- muṟkūṟiya iyaṟpeyaruḷ ātaṉum pūtaṉum eṉṉum iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tantiyeṉṉum muṟippeyaroṭu muṭiyuṅkāl, kūṟiya iyalpoṭu --- muṟkūṟiya nilimoḻi aṉ keṭutalum varumoḻi+ takaravoṟṟu+ keṭutalumākiya ceykikaḷuṭaṉē, peyaroṟṟu akaram^ tuvarakkeṭum --- nilimoḻippeyaril aṉkeṭa niṉṟa takaravoṟṟum varumoḻiyil takaravoṟṟukkeṭa niṉṟa akaramum muṟṟakkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ānti pūnti eṉa varum.

iyalpeṉṟataṉāṉ+ peyaroṟṟum akaramum keṭātē niṟṟalum^ koḷka. ātanti pūtanti eṉavarum.

iṉi+ tuvaraveṉṟataṉāṉ aḻāṉ puḻāṉ eṉa niṟutti+ tanti eṉa varuvittu nilimoḻi ṉakaramum varumoḻi+ takaramum akaramum keṭuttu aḻānti puḻānti eṉa muṭikka. (53)

TE349n

ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi yiyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ciṟappoṭu varuvaḻi --- avviyaṟpeyar paṇpu aṭuttu varumvaḻi, iyaṟkiyākum --- muṟkūṟiya iruvaki+ ceykiyum^ tavirntu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: peruñcāttaṉṟanti peruṅkoṟṟaṉṟanti eṉa varum. koṟṟam^ koṟṟaṉṟanti cāttam^ koṟṟaṉṟanti eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa paṇpaṉṟi aṭi aṭuttaṉavātaliṉ+ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka. (57)

TE350n

appeyar meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭu vaḻiyu
niṟṟalu murittē yammeṉ cāriyi
makkaṇ muṟitokūu maruṅki ṉāṉa.

itu mēlataṟku vēṟōr varumoḻikkaṇ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: appeyar makkaḷ āṉamuṟitokūum maruṅkiṉum --- avviyaṟpeyar muṉṉar+ tantiyaṉṟi makaṉākiya muṟippeyar vantu tokumiṭattiṉum, meyyoḻittu aṉkeṭuvaḻi ammeṉ cāriyi niṟṟalum urittu --- avviyal+ peyaril tāṉēṟiya meyniṟka aṉkeṭṭu ammuccāriyi vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

āṉa eṉpataṉi makkaḷōṭum, ummiyi maruṅkiṉōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. muṟitokūu maruṅkiṉeṉṟatu iṉṉāṟku makaṉeṉṉum muṟippeyarāy+ cērumiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: koṟṟaṅkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṅkoṟṟaṉ eṉa nilimoḻi aṉ keṭṭuḻi ammu vantatu. ivaṟṟiṟku atuveṉurupu viriyātu ataṉ uṭimipporuḷ virikka. itu muṟippeyar.

iṉi ummiyāṉ+ koṟṟaṅkuṭi cāttaṅkuṭi eṉa+ piṟa peyar tokkaṉavum^ koḷka.

meyyoḻittu eṉṟataṉāṉē koṟṟamaṅkalam cāttamaṅkalam eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ ammiṉ makaraṅkeṭutalum vēṭṭamaṅkalam vēṭṭaṅkuṭi eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ nilimoḻiyoṟṟu iraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. (55)

TE351n

tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉu meṉṉu
māmuṟi yiyaṟpeyar tiripiṭa ṉilavē.

itu mēlataṟku oruvaḻi eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉum eṉṉum āmuṟi iyaṟpeyar --- avviyaṟpeyaruḷ tāṉum pēṉum^ kōṉumeṉṉum ammuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya iyaṟpeyarkaḷ tantiyoṭum makkaḷ muṟimiyoṭum puṇarumvaḻi, tiripiṭaṉila --- muṟkūṟiya tiripukaḷiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāṉṟanti pēṉṟanti kōṉṟanti eṉavum, tāṉkoṟṟaṉ pēṉkoṟṟaṉ kōṉkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum varum. pēṉ kōṉ eṉpaṉa muṟkālattu vaḻakku. ivi tokimarapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇi viravu+ peyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpuḻi iyalpāyiṉavēṉum īṇṭu ivvīṟṟiṟku+ tiripu kūṟutaliṉ ataṉiyum vilakki iyalpāmeṉpatūum^ kūṟiṉār. (56)

TE352n

tāṉyā ṉeṉumpeya rurupiya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, toki marapiṉuḷ `aḵṟiṇi viravuppeyar' ((eḻu//155)) eṉpataṉāṉē iyalpāy niṉṟa tāṉ eṉpataṉiyum `uyirīṟākiya' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉāl iyalpāy niṉṟa yāṉeṉpataṉiyum avviyalpuvilakki urupiyalōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāṉ yāṉ eṉum peyar --- tāṉeṉṉum viravuppeyarum yāṉeṉṉum uyartiṇippeyarum, urupiyal niliyum --- urupiyaliṉ+ kūṟiya iyalpilē nilipeṟṟu+ tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟuki+ taṉṉeṉṟum yāṉeṉpatu ākāram ekaramāy yakaraṅkeṭṭu eṉṉeṉṟum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: taṉki eṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum.

varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ taṉñāṇ eṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉavarum. (57)

TE353n

vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi+ kuṟukalum^ tiritalum^
tōṟṟa milli yeṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumi alvaḻi --- muṟkūṟiya tāṉ yāṉeṉpaṉa vēṟṟumi+ puṇarcciyallātaviṭattu, kuṟukalum^ tiritalum^ tōṟṟamilli eṉmaṉār pulavar --- tāṉeṉpatu neṭumutal kuṟukutalum yāṉeṉpatu avvāṟu tiritalum^ tōṟṟamiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāṉ kuṟiyaṉ ciṟiyaṉ tīyaṉ periyaṉ ñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ valiyaṉ eṉavum, yāṉ kuṟiyēṉ ciṟiyēṉ tīyēṉ periyēṉ ñāṉṟēṉ nīṇṭēṉ māṇṭēṉ valiyēṉ eṉavum varum.

tōṟṟa meṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇ avvāṟaṉṟi ṉakaram^ tiritalum^ koḷka. taṟpukaḻ taṟpāṭi, eṟpukaḻ eṟpāṭi eṉa varum. (58)

TE354n

aḻaṉe ṉiṟutikeṭa valleḻuttu mikumē.

itu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṉakaram^ tiriyātu keṭuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḻaṉ eṉ iṟuti keṭa --- aḻaṉeṉṉuñcol taṉ īṟṟu ṉakaram^ keṭa, valleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:aḻakkuṭam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉa varum. aḻakkuṭameṉpatu piṇakkuṭatti. (59)

TE355n

muṉṉeṉ kiḷavi muṉṉar+ tōṉṟu
milleṉ kiḷavimici ṟakara moṟṟa
ṟolliyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapē.

itu `maruviṉṟokuti' ((eḻu//111)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa marūukkaḷil oṉṟaṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ eṉ kiḷavi muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum il eṉ kiḷavimici --- muṉṉeṉṉum^ colliṉ muṉṉē varum illeṉṉum^ colliṉmēlē, ṟakaram oṟṟal --- oru ṟakaravoṟṟu vantuniṉṟu muṭital, tolliyal maruṅkiṉ marīiyamarapu --- paḻiyatākiya iyalpiṉiyuṭiya vaḻakkiṭattu maruvivanta ilakkaṇa muṭipu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam:muṉṟil eṉa varum. iṉmuṉ eṉa niṟkaṟpālatu muṉṟileṉṟu talitaṭumāṟutaliṉ marūuvāyiṟṟu. muṉṉeṉpataṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutal ilakkaṇamēṉum atuvaṉṟi+ taṉakku iṉamāyatōr ṟakaravoṟṟu+ peṟutaliṉ vēṟu muṭipāyiṟṟu. (60)

TE356n

poṉṉeṉ kiḷavi yīṟukeṭa muṟiyiṉ
muṉṉar+ tōṉṟum lakāra makāram^
ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ+ ṟoṭariya lāṉa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟuppeya roṉṟaṟku+ ceyyuḷmuṭipu kūṟutaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: poṉ eṉ kiḷavi ī,ṟa keṭa --- poṉṉeṉṉum^ col taṉṉīṟākiya ṉakaraṅkeṭāniṟka, muṉṉar lakāra makāram muṟiyiṉ+ ṟōṉṟum --- ataṉ muṉṉar lakaramum makaravoṟṟum muṟiyāṉē vantu niṟkum, ceyyuḷ maruṅkil toṭariyalāṉa --- aṅṅaṉam niṟpatu ceyyuḷiṭattu+ coṟkaḷ tammuḷ toṭarccippaṭum iyalpiṉkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

muṟiyiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ makaram oṟṟātal koḷka.

utāraṇam: `polampaṭa+ polinta koycuvaṟpuravi' eṉavarum.

toṭariyalāṉ eṉṟataṉāṉē vaṉkaṇattukkaṇṇum lakaram niṟka makaram valleḻuttiṟkēṟṟa melleḻuttāka+ tirital koḷka. `polaṅkalañcumanta pūṇṭāṅkiḷamuli' ((akam//16)) `polañcuṭarāḻi pūṇṭatērē' `polantēr+ kuṭṭuvaṉ' eṉa varum.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē `polanaṟunteriyal' ((puṟam//26)) `polamalarāviri' eṉṟāṟpōla makaraṅkeṭṭu+ piṟakaṇattu muṭitalum^ koḷka. (61)

TE357n

yakara viṟuti vēṟṟumi+ poruḷvayiṉ
valleḻuttu iyiyi ṉavveḻuttu mikumē.

itu muṟiyāṉē yakara īṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yakara iṟuti vēṟṟumi+ poruḷvayiṉ --- yakara īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ, av valleḻuttu mikum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantu iyiyiṉ av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāykkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. (62)

TE358n

tāyeṉ kiḷavi yiyaṟki yākum.

itu viravupporuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiya valleḻuttu vilakki+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāyeṉ kiḷavi iyaṟkiyākum --- tāyeṉṉuñcol valleḻuttu mikātu iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tāyki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

mēli+ cūttirattāṉ mikutiyum^ kūṟutaliṉ aḵṟiṇi viravu+ peyaruḷ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu. (63)

TE359n

makaṉviṉi kiḷappiṉ mutaṉili yiyaṟṟē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu, tāyeṉpatu aṭiyaṭuttuḻi valleḻuttu mikuka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: makaṉ viṉikiḷappiṉ --- tāyeṉṉum^ col taṉakku aṭiyāy muṉvanta makaṉatu viṉiyi+ piṉṉāka oruvaṉ kūṟumiṭattu, mutal nili iyaṟṟu --- iv vīṟṟuḷ mutaṟkaṇ+ kūṟiya nilimiyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: makaṉṟāykkalām ceru tuṟattal pakittal eṉa varum. makaṉ tāyōṭu kalāytta kalām eṉa viriyum. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ēṟkum urupu virikka. viṉi īṇṭu+ pakimēṟṟu. (64)

TE360n

melleḻuttu uṟaḻu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu uṟaḻum moḻiyumāruḷa --- yakara īṟṟuḷ atikāra valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttu mikku uṟaḻntu muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vēykkuṟi vēyṅkuṟi ceyki tali puṟam eṉa varum. (65)

TE361n

alvaḻi yellā miyalpeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu alvaḻikku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻi ellām iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- yakara īṟṟu alvaḻiyellām iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāykaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāl avvāykkoṇṭāṉ ivvāykkoṇṭāṉ uvvāykkoṇṭāṉ evvāykkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa vurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa muṭivaṉavum, tāykkoṇṭāṉ tūyppeytāṉ eṉṟāṟpōlum viṉiyeccamum, poyccol meyccol eyppaṉṟi eṉṟāṟpōlum paṇputtokiyum, vēykaṭitu vēykkaṭitu eṉṉum alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci muṭivum^ koḷka. (66)

TE362n

rakāra viṟuti yakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē rakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: rakāra iṟuti --- rakāra īṟṟuppeyar vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, yakāra iyaṟṟu --- yakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu vantuḻi av valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tērkkāl celavu tali puṟam eṉa varum.

im māṭṭēṟṟiṉi yakara īṟṟu vēṟṟumi alvaḻiyeṉṉum iraṇṭiyum^ karuti māṭṭeṟintāreṉpār alvaḻi muṭipum īṇṭu+ kāṭṭuvar. yām ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ+ kāṭṭutum.

itu ḻakara īṟṟiṟkum okkum.

māṭṭēṟṟāṉ uṟaḻcciyum^ koḷka. vērṅkuṟi vērkkuṟi eṉa varum. (67)

TE363n

ārum vetirum^ cārum pīru
melleḻuttu mikutaṉ meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟiṟku eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ārum vetirum^ cārum pīrum --- āreṉṉum^ collum vetireṉṉuñcollum^ cāreṉṉum^ collum pīreṉṉum^ collum, melleḻuttu mikutal meypeṟa+ tōṉṟum --- melleḻuttu mikkumuṭital meymmi peṟa+ tōṉṟum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ārṅkōṭu vetirṅkōṭu cārṅkōṭu pīrṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

pīr marameṉpār pīrṅkōṭeṉpar. `pīrvāy+ pirinta nīr niṟi muṟiceytu' eṉṟāṟpōla+ cāṉṟōr palarum^ ceyyuḷ ceytavāṟu kāṇ.

meypeṟa eṉṟataṉāṉ `āram^ kaṇṇi yaṭupōr+ cōḻar' ((akam//93)) eṉa ār ammuppeṟutalum `mārippīrattalar cilakoṇṭē ((kuṟu//98)) eṉa+ pīr attuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. itaṉi atikāra+ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭippārum uḷar.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē kūrṅkatirvēl īrṅkōti eṉṟāl+ pōlavum^ kutirṅkōṭu vilarṅkōṭu ayirṅkōṭu tuvarṅkōṭu cilirṅkōṭu eṉṟāṟpōlavum melleḻuttu mikuvaṉa koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē tuvaraṅkōṭu eṉa ammuppeṟutalum^ koḷka. (68)

TE364n

cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻ vayiṉ valikkum.

iḵtu eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cār eṉ kiḷavi kāḻ vayiṉ valikkum --- cār eṉpatu kāḻ eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarum iṭattu valleḻuttu mikku+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: cār+kāḻ eṉa varum. cāriṉatu vittu* eṉpatu* ē poruḷ. itaṉi vayiram eṉiṉ+ kiḷantōtuvār eṉṟu uṇarka. (69)

TE365n

pīr eṉ kiḷavi y-ammoṭu* um^ civaṇum.

iḵtu eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: pīr eṉ kiḷavi ammoṭu* um^ civaṇum --- pīr eṉṉum^ col melleḻuttu* ē y-aṉṟi ammu+ peṟṟu* um muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pīraṅkoṭi cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, `poṉpōl+ pīramoṭu pūtta putaṉmalar' ((neṭunalvāṭi//14)) eṉavum varum. ummi iṟantatutaḻīiyiṟṟu. (70)

TE366n

lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra viyaṟṟē

itu muṟiyāṉē lakāra īṟṟi vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: lakāra iṟuti ṉakāra iyaṟṟu --- lakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṉakāra īṟṟu iyalpiṟṟāya, lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṟkuṟi ciṟi tali puṟam neṟkatir cōṟu tali puṟam eṉa varum. (71)

TE367n

melleḻuttu iyiyi ṉakāra mākum.

itu ṉakāramāmeṉṟaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṉakāramākum --- avvīṟu meṉkaṇam vantu iyiyiṉ ṉakāramāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṉñeri nuṉi muri eṉa varum.

i+ cūttirattiṉi vēṟṟumi yiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttu+ kōṭaṟkaṇ+ ciṅkanōkkāka vittamiyāṉ alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka. kaṉñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum.

TE368n

alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.

iḵtu av vīṟṟu alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa --- iv vīṟṟu alvaḻikaḷellām^ tantiripu valleḻuttiṉōṭu uṟaḻntu muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kalkuṟitu kaṟkuṟitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kalkuṟumi kaṟkuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉa+ kuṇampaṟṟi vanta vēṟṟumikkum uṟaḻcci koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē viṉiccollīṟu tirintaṉavum uruvu tirintaṉavum^ koḷka. vantāṉāṟkoṟṟaṉ poruvāṉāṟpōkāṉ eṉavum, attāṟkoṇṭāṉ ittāṟkoṇṭāṉ uttāṟkoṇṭāṉ ettāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum varum.

akkāṟkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟāṟpōla+ piṟavum muṭipu uḷḷaṉavellām itaṉāṉ muṭittukkoḷka. (73)

TE369n

takaram varuvaḻi yāyta niliyalum
pukariṉ ṟeṉmaṉār pulami yōrē.

itu lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tiriyumeṉṟaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varuvaḻi āytam niliyalum --- takaram mutalākiya moḻi vantāl lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tirintuniṟṟalum, pukar iṉṟu eṉmaṉār pulamiyōr --- kuṟṟamiṉṟeṉṟu colluvār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaḵṟītu kaṟṟītu eṉa varum.

pukariṉṟeṉṟataṉāl `neṭiyataṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//370)) eṉpataṉuḷ vēṟītu vēṟṟītu eṉṉum uṟaḻcci muṭipum^ koḷka. (74)

TE370n

neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpumā ruḷavē.

iḵtu `alvaḻiyellāmuṟaḻ' ((eḻu//168)) eṉṟataṉi vilakki iyalpāka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpumāruḷa --- neṭṭeḻuttiṉ īṟṟu lakāra īṟu kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa lakārampōla+ tirintu uṟaḻtalēyaṉṟi iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pālkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. iyalpākātu tirintaṉa, vēlkaṭitu vēṟkaṭitu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. (75)

TE371n

nellum^ cellum^ kollum^ collu
mallatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi yiyala.

iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ uṟaḻntumuṭika eṉṟataṉi vēṟṟumi muṭipeṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: nellum^ cellum^ kollum^ collum --- nelleṉṉum^ collum^ celleṉṉum^ collum^ kolleṉṉum^ collum^ colleṉṉum^ collumākiya i+ nāṉku collum, allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumi iyala --- alvaḻiyi+ collumiṭattum^ tām vēṟṟumi muṭipiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy lakaram ṟakaramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ummi ciṟappu.

utāraṇam: neṟkāyttatu ceṟkaṭitu koṟkaṭitu coṟkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (76)

TE372n

illeṉ kiḷavi y-iṉmi ceppiṉ
valleḻuttu mikutalu miyiṭi varutalu
miyaṟki yātalu mākāram varutalum^
koḷattaku marapi ṉākiṭa ṉuṭittē.

iḵtu ivvīṟṟu viṉikkuṟippu+ colluḷ oṉṟaṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: illeṉ kiḷavi iṉmi ceppiṉ --- illeṉṉuñcol iruppiṭamākiya illi uṇarttātu oru poruḷiṉatu illāmiyi uṇarttum iṭattu, valleḻuttu mikutalum --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi avvalleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum, ai iṭivarutalum --- aikāram iṭiyē varutalum, iyaṟkiyātalum --- iraṇṭum vārātu iyalpāy muṭitalum, ākāram varutalum --- ākāramvantu muṭitalumākiya innāṉku muṭipum, koḷattaku marapiṉ --- coṟku muṭipāka+ koḷattakum muṟiyāṉē, ākiṭa ṉuṭittu --- taṉmuṭipām iṭaṉ uṭittu eṉṟavāṟu.

koḷattaku marapiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi vantuḻi aikāram varutalum, aikāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikutalum mikāmiyum, ākāram vantuḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: illeṉa niṟutti+ koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa+ tantu valleḻuttum aikāramum^ koṭuttu illi+ koṟṟaṉeṉa ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

iṉṉum avvāṟē niṟutti aikāramē koṭuttu illi koṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ teḷivu poruḷ eṉa valleḻuttu mikātu muṭikka.

iṉṉum^ koḷattaku marapiṉeṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉi+ kaṇattiṉ muṉṉum aikāramē koṭuttu illiñāṇ nūl maṇi vāṉam āaiṭa eṉa oṭṭuka.

iḵtu illeṉpatōr mutaṉili niṉṟu varumoḻiyōṭu iṅṅaṉam puṇarntateṉpatu uṇartaṟku illeṉkiḷaviyeṉṟum iyaṟkiyātalumeṉṟum^ kūṟiṉār. immuṭipu viṉiyiyaluḷ viravuviṉikkaṇ `iṉmi ceppal' eṉpuḻi `illi il' ((col//222)) eṉṟu urikūṟiyavataṉāṉum, avaṉilli eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa utāraṇamāka ellā āciriyarum^ kāṭṭiyavaṟṟāṉum uṇarka.

itaṉāṉē iṅṅaṉam puṇarttacollaṉṟi illiyeṉa aikāra īṟāy niṟpatōr col iṉmiyum āyiṉ, iṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ivvāṟē puṇarkkaveṉiṉ avi varumoḻiyiṉṟi oru collāy niṟṟaliṉ+ puṇarkkārāyiṉār.

iṉi iyalpu varumāṟu: --- eṇṇil kuṇam ceyki tuṭi poruḷ eṉavum, poyyil ñāṉam miyil vāṇmukam eṉavum varum.

iṉi ākāram varumāṟu: --- illākkoṟṟaṉ cāttaṉ tēvaṉ poruḷ eṉa ākāram valleḻuttu+ peṟṟaṉa. piṉ+ kūṟiya iraṇṭum illeṉṉum viṉikkuṟippu mutaṉiliyaṭiyāka+ tōṉṟiya peyarecca maṟi tokkum virintum niṉṟaṉa.

iyalpumuṉ+ kūṟātataṉāl immuṭipiṟku vēṇṭum^ ceyki ceyka. tāviṉīṭci eṉṟāṟpōla vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭum^ ceyki ceytu muṭikka. (77)

TE373n

valleṉ kiḷavi toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.

iḵtu iruvaḻiyum^ tirintum uṟaḻntum varumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ+ piṟitu viti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: val eṉ kiḷavi --- valleṉṉum^ col alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakārattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum. (78)

TE374n

nāyum palakiyum varūum^ kāli
yāvayi ṉukaram^ keṭutalu murittē
yukaram^ keṭuvaḻi yakara niliyum.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉporuḷ: nāyum palakiyum varūum^ kāli --- valleṉpataṉmuṉ nāyeṉṉum^ collum palakiyeṉṉum^ collum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āvayiṉ ukaram^ keṭutalum urittu --- avviṭattu ukaram^ keṭātu niṟṟalēyaṉṟi+ keṭṭu muṭiyavum peṟum, ukaram^ keṭuvaḻi akaram niliyum --- avvukaram^ keṭumiṭattu akaram nilipeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vallanāy vallappalaki eṉa varum.

ummi etirmaṟiyākalāṉ ukaram^ keṭātēniṉṟu vallunāy valluppalaki eṉa varutalum^ koḷka.

akaram niliyumeṉṉātu ukaram^ keṭumeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa varumoḻikkaṇṇum ivvakarappēṟu koḷka. vallakkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavarum. (79)

TE375n

pūlvē leṉṟā āleṉ kiḷaviyo
ṭāmu+ peyarkku mammiṭi varumē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pūl vēl eṉṟā āleṉkiḷaviyoṭu āmuppeyarkkum --- pūleṉṉum^ collum vēleṉṉum^ collum āleṉṉum^ collumākiya ammūṉṟupeyarkkum, am iṭi varum --- vēṟṟumikkaṇ tiripiṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭivantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūlaṅkōṭu vēlaṅkōṭu ālaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum oṭṭuka. pūlañeri vēlañeri ālañeri nīḻal viṟaku eṉavarum. eṉṟā eṉa eṇṇiṭiyiṭṭamiyāṉ+ pūlāṅkōṭu pūlāṅkaḻi eṉa ākāram pūlukku+ koḷka. (80)

TE376n

toḻil+ peyar ellām^ toḻil+ peyar iyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarkku alvaḻikkaṇṇum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum^ tiripum uṟaḻcciyum vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarōṭu māṭṭeṟitaliṉ eytiyatuvilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- lakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻil+ peyariṉ iyalpiṉavāy iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pullukkaṭitu kallukkaṭitu vallukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, vallukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku+ pullutal kallutal vallutal eṉa+ poruḷurikka.

iṉi ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ ṟoḻiṟpeyarviti eytātu piṟa viti eytuvaṉavum^ koḷka. kaṉṉalkaṭitu piṉṉalkaṭitu kaṉṉaṟkaṭumi piṉṉaṟkaṭumi eṉavum varum. itaṉāṉē meṉkaṇam vantuḻi+ piṉṉaṉñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu piṉṉaṉ ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉa oṭṭuka.

iṉi āṭal pāṭal kūṭal nīṭal mutaliyaṉavum alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyum vēṟṟumikkaṇ tirintum muṭital itaṉāṉ+ koḷka. (81)

TE377n

veyileṉ kiḷavi maḻiyiya ṉiliyum.

itu tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: veyil eṉ kiḷavi mayiyiyal niliyum --- veyileṉṉum^ col maḻiyeṉṉum^ coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟumuṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

maḻiyeṉpataṉi `vaḷiyeṉa varūum' ((eḻu//242)) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum vaḷiyeṉpataṉi `paṉiyeṉa varūum' ((eḻu//241)) eṉpataṉuṭaṉum māṭṭeṟintavāṟu kāṇka.

utāraṇam: veyilattu+ koṇṭāṉ veyiliṟkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu attumici yoṟṟu+ keṭātuniṉṟa iṭam. iḵtu `avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu' ((eḻu//133)) mikkatu atikāra valleḻuttiṉmiyiṉ iyalpukaṇattum^ koḷka, cāriyi varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ. (82)

TE378n

cuṭṭumuta lākiya vakara viṟuti
muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta vurupiya ṉiliyum.

itu muṟiyāṉē vakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭumutalākiya vakara iṟuti --- vakara īṟṟu+ peyar nāṉkaṉuḷ+ cuṭṭeḻuttiṉi mutalākavuṭiya vakara īṟṟuppeyar mūṉṟum, muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta urupiyal niliyum --- muṟpaṭakkūṟiya urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avaṟṟukkōṭu ivaṟṟukkōṭu uvaṟṟukkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum.

muṟpaṭa+ kiḷanta eṉṟataṉāṉē vaṟṟiṉōṭu iṉṉum peṟutal koḷka. avaṟṟiṉkōṭu ivaṟṟiṉkōṭu uvaṟṟiṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉa oṭṭuka. iḵtu ēṉi+ kaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. (83)

TE379n

vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.

itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vēṟṟumiyalvaḻi āytamākum --- accuṭṭumutal vakaram vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattu āytamāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aḵkaṭiya iḵkaṭiya uḵkaṭiya ciṟiya tīya periya eṉa varum. ivvaḻakku ikkālattu aritu. (84)

TE380n

melleḻuttu iyiyi ṉavveḻuttu ākum.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ avveḻuttākum --- avvakara īṟu meṉkaṇamvantu iyiyumāyiṉ avvakaravoṟṟu avvammelleḻuttāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aññāṇ iññāṇ uññāṇ nūl maṇi eṉa varum. (85)

TE381n

ēṉavi puṇari ṉiyalpeṉa moḻipa.

ituvum atu, av vīṟu ēṉi+ kaṇaṅkaḷōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉavi puṇariṉ --- accuṭṭumutal vakara īṟṟōṭu iṭikkaṇamum uyirkkaṇamum vantu puṇarumāyiṉ, iyalpeṉa moḻipa --- avvakaram^ tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: avyāḻ ivyāḻ uvyāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉoṭṭuka.

īṇṭu+ kūṟiyatu nilimoḻikkeṉṟum āṇṭu `niṉṟa coṉmuṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟatu varumoḻikkeṉṟum uṇarka. (86)

TE382n

ēṉi vakaram^ toḻiṟpeya riyaṟṟē.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉivakaram --- `vakarakkiḷavi nāṉmoḻi yīṟṟatu'((eḻu//81)) eṉṟataṉuḷ oḻintuniṉṟa uriccollākiya vakaram iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyaṟṟu --- ñakara īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyar iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tevvukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tevvukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.

uriyiṟkōṭaleṉpataṉāl temmuṉi eṉa vakaravoṟṟu makaravoṟṟāka+ tirital koḷka. (87)

TE383n

ḻakāra viṟuti rakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟutta muṟiyāṉē ḻakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḻakāra iṟuti rakāra iyaṟṟu --- ḻakāra īṟṟuppeyar vaṉkaṇam vantāl vēṟṟumikkaṇ rakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pūḻkkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam eṉa varum. (88)

TE384n

tāḻeṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇari
ṉakkiṭi varuta lurittu mākum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, valleḻuttiṉōṭu akku vakukattaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: tāḻ eṉ kiḷavi kōloṭu puṇariṉ --- tāḻeṉṉuñcol+ kōleṉṉum^ colloṭu puṇarumiṭattu, akku iṭivarutalum urittākum --- valleḻuttu mikutalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyi iṭiyē vantu niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē, akkuppeṟātu valleḻuttu mikutal valiyuṭittāyiṟṟu.

utāraṇam: tāḻakkōl tāḻkkōl eṉa varum.

itu tāḻi+ tiṟakkum^ kōl eṉa viriyum (89)

TE385n

tamiḻeṉ kiḷaviyu mataṉō raṟṟē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tamiḻ eṉ kiḷaviyum --- tamiḻeṉṉum^ collum, ataṉōraṟṟu --- valleḻuttu mikku muṭitalēyaṉṟi akkuccāriyiyum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ataṉōraṟṟē eṉṟataṉāl itaṟku+ tamiḻkkūtteṉa valleḻuttu mikutalē valiyuṭittu.

utāraṇam: tamiḻakkūttu cēri tōṭṭam paḷḷi eṉa varum. tamiḻiyuṭiya kūttu eṉa virikka. tamiḻavariyar eṉṟāṟpōla valleḻuttuppeṟātu akku+ peṟṟaṉa, `uṇarakkūṟiya' ((eḻu//405)) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ+ koḷka. tamiḻanāṭu tamiḻnāṭu eṉa ēṉikkaṇattu muṭipu `eppeyarmuṉṉarum' ((eḻu//128)) eṉpataṉuḷ `muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittām. (90)

TE386n

kumiḻeṉ kiḷavi marappeya rāyiṉ+
pīreṉ kiḷaviyo ṭōriyaṟ ṟākum.

itu valleḻuttu vilakki melleḻuttum ammum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kumiḻ eṉ kiḷavi marappeyar āyiṉ --- kumiḻeṉṉum^ col+ kumiḻttaleṉṉum^ toḻilaṉṟi marappeyarāyiṉ, pīr eṉ kiḷaviyoṭu ōr iyaṟṟu ākum --- pīreṉṉum^ colloṭu ōriyalpiṟṟāy oruvaḻi melleḻuttum oruvaḻi ammum peṟṟumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kumiḻṅkōṭu kumiḻaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉa varum.

ōriyaṟṟeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟavaṟṟiṟkum immuṭipu koḷka. makiḻṅkōṭu makiḻaṅkōṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. (91)

TE387n

pāḻeṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvē.

itu valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttu+ peṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pāḻ eṉ kiḷavi melleḻuttu uṟaḻvu --- pāḻeṉṉum^ collīṟu valleḻuttiṉōṭu melleḻuttuppeṟṟu uṟaḻntu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: pāḻkkiṇaṟu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa oṭṭuka. itu pāḻuṭkiṇaṟu eṉa viriyum, pāḻtta kiṇaṟu eṉa viṉittoki muṭiyāmiyiṉ. (92)

TE388n

ēḻeṉ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyar ivvāṟu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēḻ eṉ kiḷavi --- ēḻeṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar iṟuti, urupiyal niliyum --- urupu puṇarccikkaṇ+ kūṟiya iyalpiṉkaṇṇē nilipeṟṟu aṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aḵtu `aṉṉeṉcāriyi yēḻaṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//194)) eṉpatām.

utāraṇam: ēḻaṉkāyam cukku tōri payaṟu eṉa varum. iyipu valleḻuttu ōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ vīḻkka. iḵtu ēḻaṉāṉ+ koṇṭa kāyam eṉa viriyum. (93)

TE389n

aḷavu niṟiyu meṇṇum varuvaḻi
neṭumutal kuṟukalu mukaram varutalum^
kaṭinili yiṉṟē yāciri yaṟka.

itu mēlataṟku eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyam eṇṇum varuvaḻi --- avvēḻeṉpataṉmuṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, neṭumutal kuṟukalum ukaram varutalum^ kaṭiniliyiṉṟē āciriyaṟka --- muṉṉiṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttiṉ māttiri kuṟukalum āṇṭu ukaram varutalum nīkku nilimiyiṉṟu āciriyaṟku eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻukalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum, eḻukaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum, eḻumūṉṟu eḻunāṉku eṉavum varum.

niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇattu+ poruṭpeyarkkum immuṭipu koḷka. eḻukaṭal cili tici piṟappu eṉa varum. (94)

TE390n

patteṉ kiḷavi yoṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi
niṟṟal vēṇṭu māyta+ puḷḷi.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti vakuttatu, varumoḻi nōkki vitittaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: pattu eṉ kiḷavi oṟṟiṭi keṭuvaḻi --- avvēḻaṉōṭu patteṉpatu puṇarumiṭattu appatteṉkiḷavi iṭiyoṟṟu+ keṭuvaḻi, āytappuḷḷi niṟṟalvēṇṭum --- āytamākiya puḷḷi niṟṟali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eḻupaḵtu eṉa varum. (95)

TE391n

āyiram varuvaḻi yukaram^ keṭumē.

itu neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram varuvaḻi --- ēḻeṉpataṉ muṉ āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyar varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, ukaraṅkeṭum --- neṭumutal kuṟukiniṉṟu ukarampeṟātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻāyiram eṉa varum. (96)

TE392n

nūṟūrntu varūu māyira+ kiḷavikku+
kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukka miṉṟē.

iḵtu eytiyatu muḻuvatūum vilakkiṟṟu, ukaraṅkeṭṭu ataṉmēlē neṭumutal kuṟukātu eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu ūrntu varūum āyira+ kiḷavikku --- avvēḻeṉpatu nūṟeṉṉum^ coṉmēl varum āyirameṉṉum^ colliṟku, kūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟukkamiṉṟu --- muṟkūṟiya neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutaliṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻnūṟāyiram eṉa varum.

kūṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutalkuṟuki ukarampeṟṟu eḻunūṟāyirameṉavum varum.

itaṉāṉē ēḻāyirameṉa mēl mutaṉili kuṟukāmiyum^ koḷka.

itaṉāṉē eḻuñāyiṟu eḻunāḷ eḻuvaki eṉa iyalpu kaṇattu neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟutalum^ koḷka. (97)

TE393n

aiyam palleṉa varūu miṟuti
yalpeya reṇṇiṉu māyiya ṉiliyum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti --- avvēḻaṉ muṉṉar aiyeṉṟum ammeṉṟum palleṉṟum varukiṉṟa iṟutikaḷiyuṭiya, alpeyar eṇṇiṉum --- poruṭpeyarallāta eṇṇuppeyarākiya tāmari veḷḷam āmpal eṉpaṉa vantālum, ā iyal niliyum --- neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu avviyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻtāmari ēḻveḷḷam ēḻāmpal eṉa varum. (98)

TE394n

uyirmuṉ variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirmuṉ variṉum --- avvēḻeṉpataṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum eṇṇuppeyarumākiya uyirmutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ā iyal tiriyātu --- neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram peṟātu muṭiyum iyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēḻakal ēḻuḻakku ēḻoṉṟu ēḻiraṇṭu eṉa varum. (99)

TE395n

kīḻeṉ kiḷavi yuṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku vēṟṟumikkaṇ uṟaḻcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kīḻ eṉ kiḷavi uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟum --- kīḻeṉṉum^ col uṟaḻcciyāy+ tōṉṟi muṭiyam eṉṟavāṟu.

tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṉ neṭumutal kuṟukātu valleḻuttuppeṟṟum peṟātum varumeṉṟa iraṇṭum uṟaḻcciyāy varumeṉṟu koḷka. iyipu valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ+ koḷka.

utāraṇam: kīḻkkuḷam kīḻkuḷam cēri tōṭṭam pāṭi eṉa varum. (100)

TE396n

ḷakāra viṟuti ṇakāra viyaṟṟē.

itu niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē ḷakāra īṟṟuccol vēṟṟumikkaṇ+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ḷakāra iṟuti ṇakāra iyaṟṟu --- ḷakāra īṟṟu+ peyar ṇakāra īṟṟiṉ iyalpiṟṟāy vaṉkaṇam vantuḻi ṭakāramāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṭkuṟi ciṟi tali puṟam eṉa varum. (101)

TE397n

melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṇakāra mākum.

itu mēlataṟku meṉkaṇattumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melleḻuttu iyiyiṉ ṇakāramākum --- ḷakāra īṟu melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy vantu iyiyiṉ ṇakāramāy+ tirintumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṇñeri nuṉi maram eṉa varum.

itaṉi vēṟṟumiyiṟutikkaṇ alvaḻiyatu eṭuttukkōṭaṟkaṇ+ ciṅkanōkkāka vittaliṉ alvaḻikkum immuṭipu koḷka. muṇñerintatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉa varum. (102)

TE398n

alvaḻi yellā muṟaḻeṉa moḻipa.

itu mēlataṟku alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: alvaḻiyellām --- ḷakāra īṟu alvaḻikkaṇellām, uṟaḻeṉa moḻipa --- tiriyātum ṭakāramāy+ tirintum uṟaḻntu muṭiyumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muḷkaṭitu muṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ kuṇa vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum ivvuṟaḻcci koḷka. muḷkuṟumi muṭkuṟumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi vāḷkaṭumi vāṭkaṭumi eṉavum oṭṭuka.

itaṉāṉē atōṭkoṇṭāṉ itōṭkoṇṭāṉ utōṭkoṇṭāṉ etōṭkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉeṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉavum^ koḷka. (103)

TE399n

āyta niliyalum varinili yiṉṟē
takaram varūum^ kāli yāṉa.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu. takaram varuvaḻi uṟaḻcciyēyaṉṟi āytam āka+ tirintu uṟaḻka eṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: takaram varūum^ kāliyāṉa --- takara mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, āytam niliyalum varinili yiṉṟu --- ḷakāram ṭakāramāka+ tiritalēyaṉṟi āytamāka+ tirintu niṟṟalum nīkkum nilimiyiṉṟu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muḵṭītu muṭṭītu eṉavarum (104)

TE400n

neṭiyata ṉiṟuti yiyalpā kunavum
vēṟṟumi yalvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum
pōṟṟal vēṇṭu moḻiyumā ruḷavē.

itu mēlataṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: neṭiyataṉ iṟuti iyalpu ākunavum --- av vīṟṟu neṭiyataṉ iṟuti tiriyātu iyalpāy muṭivaṉavaṟṟiyum, vēṟṟumi alvaḻi vēṟṟumi niliyalum --- vēṟṟumiyallāta iṭattu vēṟṟumiyiṉ iyalpiyuṭiyaṉavāy+ tirintu muṭitaliyum, pōṟṟal vēṇṭum moḻiyumāruḷa --- pōṟṟutal vēṇṭum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kōḷkaṭitu vāḷkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, `puṭṭēmpa+ puyaṉmāṟi' ((paṭṭiṉappāmāli//4)) eṉavum varum.

pōṟṟalvēṇṭum eṉṟataṉāl utaḷaṅkāy cetiḷ pū tōl eṉa ammu+ peṟutalum^ koḷka. utaḷeṉpatu yāṭṭiṉi uṇarttuṅkāl muṟkūṟiya muṭipūkaḷ iruvaḻikkum ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka. utaṭkōṭu utaḷkaṭitu utaṇaṉṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. `mōttiyum^ takaru mutaḷu mapparum' ((marapiyal//47)) eṉṟār marapiyalil. (105)

TE401n

toḻiṟpeya rellām^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

iḵtu iv vīṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarkku iruvaḻiyum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: toḻiṟpeyarellām --- ḷakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarellām iruvaḻiyum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tuḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, tuḷḷukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.

ellā meṉṟataṉāṉē iruvaḻiyum^ toḻiṟpeyarkaḷ ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟātu tirintum tiriyātum muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. kōḷkaṭitu kōṭkaṭitu kōḷkaṭumi kōṭkaṭumi eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa piṟavum varum.

iṉi vāḷkaṭitu vāṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum vāḷkaṭumi vāṭkaṭumi eṉavum^ kāṭṭuka. vāḷ --- kollutal. (106)

TE402n

iruḷeṉ kiḷavi veyiliya ṉiliyum.

itu tiripuvilakki attum iṉṉum vakuttaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iruḷ eṉ kiḷavi --- iruḷeṉṉum col vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ, veyiliyal niliyum --- veyileṉṉum coṟpōla attum iṉṉum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: iruḷattukkoṇṭāṉ iruḷiṉ+ koṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

cāriyi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iyalpu kaṇattum oṭṭuka. iruḷattuñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ iruḷiṉñāṉṟāṉ nīṇṭāṉ māṇṭāṉ eṉa varum. (107)

TE403n

puḷḷum vaḷḷum^ toḻiṟpeya riyala.

ituvum eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu, tiripum iyalpum vilakki+ toḻiṟpeyarviti vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: puḷḷum vaḷḷum --- puḷḷeṉṉum^ collum vaḷḷeṉṉum^ collum iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, toḻiṟpeyar iyala --- ñakāra īṟṟu+ toḻiṟpeyarpōla vaṉkaṇattu ukaramum valleḻuttum, meṉkaṇattum iṭikkaṇattu vakarattum ukaramum peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puḷḷukkaṭitu vaḷḷukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu valitu eṉavum, puḷḷukkaṭumi vaḷḷukkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi ñāṟci nīṭci māṭci vaṉmi eṉavum varum.

itaṉit `toḻiṟpeyarellām' ((eḻu//401)) eṉpataṉpiṉ viyātataṉāl iruvaḻiyum vēṟṟumittiripu eyti muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. puṭkaṭitu vaṭkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉavum, puṭkaṭumi vaṭkaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum, puṇñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu eṉavum, puṇñāṟci nīṭci māṭci eṉavum varum. puḷḷuvalitu puḷvalitu puḷḷu vaṉmi puḷvaṉmi eṉa vakarattiṉ muṉṉar ukaram peṟṟum peṟātum varutaliṉ `niṉṟacoṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭiyāmi uṇarka. itu vaḷḷiṟkum okkum. (108)

TE404n

makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu. `uyirīṟākiya uyartiṇippeyar' ((eḻu//153)) eṉpataṉuḷ+ kūṟiya iyalpu vilakki+ tiripu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccol iṟuti --- makkaḷeṉṉum peyarccolliṟuti iyalpēyaṉṟi, takkavaḻi aṟintu valittalum urittu --- takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

takkavaḻiyeṉṟār perumpāṉmi makkaḷ uṭampu uyir nīṅki+ kiṭantakālattiṉ aḵtu immuṭivupeṟum eṉṟaṟku.

utāraṇam: makkaṭki cevi tali puṟam. `ikkiṭantatu makkaṭṭali' eṉpataṉāṉ avvāṟātal koḷka. makkaḷ ki cevi tali puṟam eṉa+ tiriyātu niṉṟatu uyiruṇmi peṟṟu.

iṉi+ ciṟupāṉmi makkaṭpaṇpu makkaṭcuṭṭu eṉavum varum. (109)

TE405n

uṇara+ kūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ+
kaṇṭuceyaṟ kuriyavi kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē.

iḵtu ivvōttiṉkaṇ eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟiṟkellām ituvē ōttākakkoṇṭu cāriyi peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ cāriyiyum, eḻuttu+ peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku eḻuttum^ koṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka eṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṇarakkūṟiya puṇariyaṉ maruṅkiṉ --- uṇara+ kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟu varumoḻiyōṭu puṇarum iyalpiṭattu, kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavi --- mēl muṭitta muṭipaṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ+ kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku uriyavi tōṉṟiyavaḻi, kaṇṇiṉar koḷal --- avaṟṟiyum^ karutikkoṇṭu ēṟṟavāṟē muṭikka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: maṇṇappattam eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ṇakara īṟu akku+ peṟṟatu. maṇṇaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammu+ peṟṟatu. poṉṉappattam eṉa ṉakara īṟu akku+ peṟṟatu. poṉṉaṅkaṭṭi eṉa ammu+ peṟṟatu. kāṉaṅkōḻi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammu+ peṟṟatu. maṇṇāṅkaṭṭi kāṉāṅkōḻi eṉpaṉa marūu. vēyiṉṟali eṉa yakara īṟṟu urupiṟku+ ceṉṟacāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu+ keṭukka. nīr kuṟitu eṉa rakara īṟu alvaḻikkaṇ iyalpāyiṟṟu. vērkuṟitu vērkkuṟitu itu rakara īṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci. vaṭacārkkūri mēlcārkkūri ivi valleḻuttu mikka marūu muṭipu. ampar+ koṇṭāṉ imparkkoṇṭāṉ umparkkoṇṭāṉ emparkkoṇṭāṉ eṉa ivvīṟu ēḻaṉurupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantaṉa valloṟṟu+ peṟṟaṉa. takarkkuṭṭi pukarppōttu eṉpaṉa paṇputtoki karutiṟṟēl īṇṭu muṭikka. vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ muṉṉar muṭiyum. viḻaleṉṉum lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ṟakaramākātu ṉakaramāy muṭital koḷka. viḻaṉkāṭu ceṟu tāḷ puṟam eṉa varum. nallampāṟi ucilaṅkōṭu eliyālaṅkāy puṭōlaṅkāy eṉa av vīṟu ammuppeṟṟatu. kallāmpāṟi eṉpatu marūu. aḻalattu+ koṇṭāṉ eṉa avvīṟu attuppeṟṟatu. aḻukkaṟpōr puḻukkaṟcōṟu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻittiripu. yāḻkuṟitu eṉpatu ḻakara īṟṟu alvaḻi yiyalpu. vīḻkkuṟitu eṉpaṉa av vīṟṟu alvaḻi yuṟaḻcci. tāḻampāvi eṉpatu av vīṟṟu alvaḻi akku+ peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉkōṭu ceyki tali puṟam eṉa av vīṟṟu urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṭceṉṟuḻi valleḻuttu vīḻkka. muṉṉāḷivāḻvu muṉṉāḷipparicu orunāḷikkuḻavi orutiṅkaḷikkuḻavi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa ḷakāra īṟu aikāramum ataṉōṭu valleḻuttum peṟutal koḷka. piṟavum ivvōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa vellām^ koṇarntu itaṉāṉ muṭikka. kuḷattiṉpuṟam marattiṉpuṟam eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka.

iṉik `kaṭicollilli' ((col//452)) eṉpataṉāṉ vaḻakkiṉkaṇṇum^ ceyyuṭkaṇṇum vantu tirintu muṭiyum^ coṟkaḷum uḷa. avaṟṟi+ kaṇṇiṉar koḷalē eṉpataṉāṉ maṇṇukkuppōṉāṉ poṉṉukku viṟṟāṉ poruḷukkuppōṉāṉ nellukkuviṟṟāṉ koḷḷukkukkoṇṭāṉ patiṉēḻu eṉṟāṟpōla vaḻakkiṉkaṇ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum, `viṇṇukkumēl' `maṇṇukkunāppaṇ'

`pallukku+ tōṟṟa paṉimulli piṅkiḷikaḷ
collukku+ tōṟṟiṉṉam^ tōṉṟiṉavā --- ṉellukku
nūṟōoo nūṟeṉpā ṇuṭaṅkiṭikkum vaṉmulikku
māṟōmā laṉṟaḷanta maṇ.'

eṉṟāṟpōla+ ceyyuṭkaṇ ukaram peṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum muṭikka.

paṟku neṟku eṉpaṉa mutaliyavum^ koḷka. ivi urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vārātu urupiṉkaṇ vantaṉavēṉum īṇṭu+ kāṭṭiṉām, āṇṭu+ `puḷḷiyiṟutiyum' ((eḻu//202)) eṉṉum urupiyal+ cūttirattu ilēcu kōṭaṟku iṭaṉiṉṟeṉṟu karuti. iṉi accūttirattut `tēruṅkāli' eṉṟataṉāṉ muṭittalum oṉṟu. (110)

((puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu))

((9. kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal))

TE406n

īreḻuttu orumoḻi yuyirttoṭa riṭittoṭa
rāyta+ toṭarmoḻi vaṉṟoṭar meṉṟoṭa
rāyiru mūṉṟē yukaram^ kuṟukiṭaṉ.

eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu+ kuṟṟiyalukarameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa eḻuttu+ poruṭpeyarōṭum eṇṇuppeyar mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum puṇarum muṟimi uṇarttiṉamiyiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu. itu `meyyēyuyireṉṟāyīriyala' ((eḻu//103)) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ uyiriṉatu vikāramāy niṉṟa kuṟṟukaratti iruvaḻikkaṇṇum puṇarkkiṉṟamiyiṉ mēli ōttiṉōṭu iyipuṭittāyiṟṟu. ittaliccūttiram moḻimarapiṉakattu iruvakittu eṉṟa kuṟṟukaram itaṉakattu iṉittu moḻiyiṟuti varumeṉṟu avaṟṟiṟku+ peyarum muṟiyum tokiyum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. appeyarpeyar, ammuṟimuṟi, attokitoki `toṭarmoḻiyīṟṟu' ((eḻu//36)) varumeṉṟu āṇṭu+ kūṟiyavataṉi īṇṭu aintuvakippaṭutti ataṉōṭu `neṭṭeḻuttimparum' ((eḻu//36)) eṉṟatu oṉṟēyātaliṉ ataṉiyum^ kūṭṭi aṟuvakitteṉṟār.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttu orumoḻi --- iraṇṭeḻuttāṉākiya orumoḻiyum, uyirttoṭar --- uyirmēlvarum meyyi+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, iṭittoṭar --- iṭiyoṟṟu mēlvarum meyyi+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyta+ toṭarmoḻi --- āytamākiya eḻuttu mēlvarum meyyi+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, vaṉṟoṭar --- valloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyi+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, meṉṟoṭar --- melloṟṟu mēlvarum meyyi+ toṭarntu niṉṟa collum, āyirumūṉṟē --- ākiya av vāṟucollumē, ukaram^ kuṟuku iṭaṉ --- kuṟṟiyalukaram^ kuṟukivarum iṭaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāku, varaku, teḷku, eḵku, kokku, kuraṅku eṉa varum.

itaṉi ēḻeṉṟu koḷvārkku+ piṇṇākku cuṇṇāmpu āmaṇakku mutaliyaṉa muṭiyāmi uṇarka. (1)

TE407n

avaṟṟuḷ
īroṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi yiṭittoṭa rākā.

iḵtu avvāṟaṉuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avaṟṟuḷ --- avvāṟaṉuḷ, īroṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi --- iraṇṭoṟṟu iṭikkaṇ toṭarntu niṟkum^ colliṟku iṭiyiṉa oṟṟu mutal niṉṟāl, iṭittoṭarākā --- mēl iṭiyiṉam^ toṭarntunillā, valliṉamum melliṉamum toṭarntu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ārkku, īrkku, noympu, moympu eṉa varum. (2)

TE408n

allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum
ellā v-iṟutiyum ukara niliyum.

iḵtu `iṭippaṭiṟkuṟuku miṭaṉumāruṇṭē' ((eḻu//37)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ puṇarmoḻikkaṇ arimāttiriyiṉum^ kuṟukumeṉa eytiyataṉi vilakki `avviya ṉiliyu mēṉi mūṉṟē' ((eḻu//12)) eṉṟa vitiyē peṟumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉum --- alvaḻiyi+ collumiṭattum, vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum --- vēṟṟumippuṇarccikkaṇṇum, ellā iṟutiyum ukaram niliyum --- āṟu īṟṟiṉ kaṇṇum ukaram^ taṉ arimāttiriyi+ peṟṟuniṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

varumoḻiyāṉallatu alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumiyum viḷaṅkāmiyiṉ `allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum' eṉavē irumoḻi+ puṇarcciyeṉpatu peṟṟām. ivvirumoḻikkaṇ+ paḻiya arimāttiripeṟṟē niṟkumeṉṟār. aṉṟi irumoḻi+ puṇarccikkaṇ orumāttiri peṟumeṉṟārkku+ paṉmoḻi+ puṇarcciyākiya ceyyuḷilakkaṇam^ kuṟṟukarattāṉ nērpaci niriyaci kōṭalum avaṟṟāṉ aṟupatu vañciccīr kōṭalum pattoṉpatiṉāyirattu irunūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟṟoru toṭikōṭalum iṉṟāy muṟṟiyalukaramākavē koḷḷavēṇṭutaliṉ māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉṉum kuṟṟam^ taṅkumeṉṟu uṇarka.

utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu nākukaṭumi varakukaṭumi eṉa varum. ivi tam arimāttiri peṟṟaṉa. ēṉiyavaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

iṉi itu `mālyāṟupōntu kālcurantupāyntu' eṉa+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum arimāttiri peṟṟatu eṉṉākkāl vañciccī riṉṟāmāṟu uṇarka. (3)

TE409n

valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu varuvaḻi+
tolli yiyaṟki niliyalu murittē.

itu muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ arimāttiri peṟum eṉṟataṉi vilakki `iṭippaṭiṉ+ kuṟukumiṭaṉum' ((eḻu//37)) eṉṟataṉāṉ arimāttiriyiṉum^ kuṟukumeṉṟu āṇṭu vitittatu īṇṭu valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇē varumeṉkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi --- valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukaram, valleḻuttu varuvaḻi --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu, tolli iyaṟki niliyalum urittu --- `iṭippaṭiṟkuṟukum' ((eḻu//37)) eṉpataṉāṉ+ kūṟiya arimāttiriyiṉum^ kuṟuki niṟkum eṉṟa iyalpilē niṟṟalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

ummi etirmaṟi.

utāraṇam: kokkukkaṭitu kokkukkaṭumi eṉa ari māttiriyiṉ+ kuṟintavāṟu kuraṅkukaṭiteṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu paṭuttu+ cevikaruviyāka uṇarka.

muṉṉiṉṟa cūttirattu ukaraniṟiyumeṉṟu pāṭamōti ataṟku ukaram arimāttiriyiṉ+ ciṟitu mikku niṟkumeṉṟu poruḷkūṟi iccūttirattiṟku+ paḻiya arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkumeṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar. (4)

TE410n

yakaram varuvaḻi yikaram kuṟuku
mukara+ kiḷavi tuvara+ tōṉṟātu.

itu kuṟṟiyalikaram puṇarmoḻiyakattu varumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yakaram varuvaḻi ukarakkiḷavi tuvara+ tōṉṟātu --- yakaramutaṉmoḻi varumoḻi y-āy varumiṭattu nilimoḻi+ kuṟṟukaraveḻuttu muṟṟattōṉṟātu, ikaram^ kuṟukum --- āṇṭu ōr ikaram vantu arimāttiri peṟṟu niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākiyātu varakiyātu teḷkiyātu eḵkiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu eṉa varum. `tuvara' eṉṟār, āṟu īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum ukaram^ keṭumeṉṟaṟku. (5)

TE411n

īreḻuttu moḻiyu muyirttoṭar moḻiyum
vēṟṟumi yāyi ṉoṟṟiṭi yiṉamika+
tōṟṟam vēṇṭum valleḻuttu mikuti.

itu muṟkūṟiya āṟaṉuḷ muṉṉarniṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum vēṟṟumi+ poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttu moḻiyum uyirttoṭar moḻiyum --- īreḻuttu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum uyirttoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṟkum, vēṟṟumiyāyiṉ --- vēṟṟumi+ poruḷ+ puṇarcciyāyiṉ, iṉa oṟṟu iṭimika --- iṉamākiya oṟṟu iṭiyilēmika, valleḻuttu mikuti tōṟṟam vēṇṭum --- valleḻuttu mikuti tōṉṟi muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṭṭukkāl cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, muyiṟṟukkāl ciṉi tali puṟam eṉavum varum. kayiṟṟu+ puṟam vayiṟṟuttī eṉpaṉavumām.

tōṟṟam eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉikkaṇattum immuṭipu koḷka. yāṭṭu ñāṟci niṇam maṇi vāl ataḷ eṉavum, muyiṟṟu ñāṟci niṇam muṭṭi valimi aṭi āṭṭam eṉavum varum. (6)

TE412n

oṟṟiṭi yiṉamikā moḻiyumā ruḷavē
yattiṟattu illi valleḻuttu mikalē.

iḵtu eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟu iṭi iṉam mikā moḻiyu māruḷa --- muṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ iṉavoṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyāta moḻikaḷum uḷa, valleḻuttu mikal attiṟattilli --- valleḻuttu mikkumuṭital akkūṟṟuḷilli eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākukāl cevi tali puṟam eṉavum, varaku katir ciṉi tāḷ patar eṉavum varum.

attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ urupiṟku eytiya cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipuvalleḻuttu vīḻkka. yāṭṭiṉkāl muyiṟṟiṉkāl nākiṉkāl varakiṉkatir eṉavarum.

attiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉikkaṇattum oṟṟiṭi mikāmi koḷka. nākuñāṟci nīṭci māṭci valimi eṉa oṭṭuka. (7)

TE413n

iṭiyoṟṟu+ toṭaru māyta+ toṭaru
naṭiyā yiyala veṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu iṭiniṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iṭiyoṟṟu+ toṭarum āyta+ toṭarum --- iṭiyoṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, naṭi ā iyala eṉmaṉār pulavar --- naṭipeṟa naṭakkumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya avviyalpu muṭipiṉiyuṭiya eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: teḷkukāl ciṟi tali puṟam eṉavum, eḵku kaṭumi ciṟumi tīmi perumi eṉavum varum. (8)

TE414n

vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyu meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟiṭi mikumē
melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi melloṟ ṟellām
valloṟ ṟiṟuti kiḷiyoṟ ṟākum.

itu piṉṉiṉṟa iraṇṭaṟkum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyum meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum --- vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikkuṟṟukara īṟum, meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, vanta valleḻuttu oṟṟiṭi mikum --- varumoḻiyāy vanta valleḻuttiṉatu oṟṟu iṭiyilē mikkumuṭiyum, melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi melloṟṟellām --- avviraṇṭaṉuḷ melloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa melloṟṟellām, iṟuti valloṟṟu --- iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa valloṟṟum, kiḷi oṟṟu ākum --- kiḷiyākiya valloṟṟumāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

iṟuti valloṟṟu varutalāvatu kuṟṟukaram ēṟiniṉṟa valloṟṟu+ tāṉē muṉṉarvantu niṟṟalām. kiḷivalloṟṟu varutalāvatu ṇakārattiṟku ṭakāramum ṉakārattiṟku ṟakāramum puṇarcciyum piṟappum nōkki+ kiḷiyāmātaliṉ, avi muṉṉar vantu niṟṟalām.

utāraṇam: kokkukkāl ciṟaku tali puṟam, kuraṅku+ kāl cevi tali puṟam, kurakkukkāl cevi tali puṟam, eṭkukkuṭṭi cevi tali puṟam, eṟpukkāṭu curam tali puṟam eṉa varum. aṟputtaḷi eṉpatu aṉpiṉāṉ+ ceyta taḷiyeṉa vēṟṟumiyum aṉpākiya taḷiyeṉa alvaḻiyumām.

vanta eṉṟataṉāṉ ivviraṇṭiṟkum urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyi poruṭkaṇ+ ceṉṟavaḻi iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. kokkiṉkāl kuraṅkiṉkāl eṉavarum.

ellāmeṉṟataṉāṉ+ paṟampiṟpāri kuṟumpiṟcāṉṟār eṉa melloṟṟu+ tiriyāmiyum^ koḷka.

oṟṟeṉṟa mikutiyāṉ iyalpukaṇattukkaṇṇum kurakku ñāṟci niṇam mukam viral ukir eṉa melloṟṟu+ tirintu varumāṟu koḷka. cilappatikārameṉpatum atu.

vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ varutal `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyum. (9)

TE415n

marappeyar+ kiḷavikku ammē cāriyi.

iḵtu eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu, ammu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: marappeyarkkiḷavikku ammē cāriyi --- kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu marappeyarkku varuñcāriyi ammu+ cāriyi eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: tēkkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavarum.

kamukaṅkāy teṅkaṅkāy cīḻkampul kampampul payaṟṟaṅkāy eṉṟāṟpōlum pulliṉiyum marameṉa aṭakki māṟukoḷa+ kūṟaleṉa+ taḻīikkoṇṭa citiveṉpatām iccūttiram eṉṟu uṇarka. (10)

TE416n

melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyaru muḷavē.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻikku eytiyatu orumaruṅku maṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: melloṟṟu valiyā marappeyarum uḷa --- melloṟṟu valloṟṟāka+ tiriyātu melloṟṟāy muṭiyum marappeyarum uḷa eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: puṉkaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum, kuruntaṅkōṭu cetiḷ tōl pū eṉavum varum.

valiyāmarappeyarumuḷa eṉavē valikkum marappeyarum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka. vēppaṅkōṭu kaṭappaṅkāy īccaṅkuli eṉa varum. (11)

TE417n

īreḻuttu moḻiyum valloṟṟu+ toṭaru
mammiṭi varaṟku muriyavi yuḷavē
yammara poḻuku moḻivayi ṉāṉa.

iḵtu īreḻuttu orumoḻikkum vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkum eytāta teytuvittatu, muṉṉar eytiyataṉi vilakki ammu vakuttaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: īreḻuttumoḻiyum valloṟṟu+ toṭarum --- īreḻuttu orumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukaramum, amiṭivaraṟkum uriyavi uḷa --- muṉ muṭittuppōnta muṭipukaḷaṉṟi ammuccāriyi iṭiyēvantu muṭitaṟku uriyaṉavum uḷa; yāṇṭeṉiṉ, ammarapu oḻukum moḻivayiṉāṉa --- avvilakkaṇam naṭakkum moḻiyiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ēṟaṅkōḷ cūtampōr vaṭṭampōr puṟṟampaḻañcōṟu eṉa varum.

ummi etirmaṟiyākaliṉ, ammuppeṟātaṉa nākukāl kokkukkāl eṉa muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉavēyām.

ammarapoḻukum eṉṟataṉāl aracakkaṉṉi muracakkaṭippu eṉa akkum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum aracavāḻkki muracavāḻkki eṉa akkukkoṭuttum muṭikka.

iṉṉum ataṉāṉē iruṭṭattukkoṇṭāṉ viḷakkattukkoṇṭāṉ eṉa attum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttum mayilāppiṟkoṟṟaṉ paṟampiṟpāri eṉa iṉ koṭuttum^ kariyataṉkōṭu neṭiyataṉkōṭu eṉa aṉ koṭuttum muṭikka. (12)

TE418n

oṟṟunili tiriyā takkoṭu varūu
makkiḷi moḻiyu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṟṟu nilitiriyātu akkoṭuvarūum --- oṟṟu muṉṉiṉṟa nilitiriyātu akkuccāriyiyōṭum piṟa cāriyiyōṭum varum, akkiḷi moḻiyumuḷa eṉa moḻipa --- akkiḷiyāṉa coṟkaḷum uḷa eṉṟu colluvar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

itaṟku ummiyi muṉṉar māṟuka.

utāraṇam: kuṉṟakkūki maṉṟappeṇṇi eṉa varum. ummiyāṉ+ koṅkattuḻavu vaṅkattuvāṇikam eṉa attum peṟṟaṉa.

niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu nilitiriyā atikārattukkaṇ varum^ cāriyikku iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. akkiḷi yeṉṟār, iraṇṭu cāriyi toṭarntu muṭivaṉavum uḷaveṉṟaṟku. pārppaṉakkuḻavi cēri tōṭṭam piḷḷi eṉa aṉṉum akkum vantaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟku uṭimi virikka. pārppiṉuṭkuḻavi eṉṟumām. pārppāṉākiya kuḻavi eṉṟāl īṇṭu muṭiyāteṉṟu uṇarka. pārppaṉamakaḷ pārppaṉa vaṉiti eṉpaṉavum pārppāṉ cāti uṇarttiṉa. (13)

TE419n

eṇṇuppeyar+ kiḷavi yurupiya ṉiliyum.

itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu poruḷ+ peyar muṭikkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṇṇuppeyar+ kiḷavi --- eṇṇuppeyarākiya coṟkaḷ poruṭpeyarōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, urupiyal niliyum --- urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟu+ puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉṟaṉkāyam iraṇṭaṉkāyam cukku tōri payaṟu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉṟaṉāṟkoṇṭa kāyameṉa viriyum. varumoḻi variyātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, iyalpu kaṇattukkaṇṇum^ koḷka. oṉṟaṉñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi eṉa varum. mēli+ cūttirattu `nili' ((eḻu//418)) eṉṟataṉāṉ iyipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (14)

TE420n

vaṇṭum peṇṭu miṉṉoṭu civaṇum.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟa muṭivu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: vaṇṭum peṇṭum iṉṉoṭu civaṇum --- vaṇṭeṉṉum^ collum peṇṭeṉṉum^ collum iṉcāriyiyōṭu porunti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: vaṇṭiṉkāl peṇṭiṉkāl eṉa varum. itaṟku muṟkūṟiya ilēciṉāṉ valleḻuttu vīḻkka. (15)

TE421n

peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum variyār.

itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: peṇṭu eṉ kiḷavikku aṉṉum variyār --- peṇṭeṉṉuñcoṟku iṉṉēyaṉṟi aṉcāriyi varutaliyum nīkkār āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: peṇṭaṉki cevi tali puṟam eṉa varum. (16)

TE422n

yāte ṉiṟutiyum^ cuṭṭumuta lākiya
vāyta viṟutiyu murupiya ṉiliyum.

iḵtu īreḻuttorumoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattuḷ oṉṟaṟkum^ cuṭṭumutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum vēṟumuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: yātu eṉ iṟutiyum^ cuṭṭumutalākiya āyta iṟutiyum --- yāteṉṉum īṟum^ cuṭṭeḻuttu mutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟum, urupiyal niliyum --- urupu puṇarcciyiṉ iyalpiṉkaṇṇē niṉṟu aṉpeṟṟu+ cuṭṭumutaliṟuti āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yātaṉkōṭu ataṉkōṭu itaṉkōṭu utaṉkōṭu cevi tali puṟam eṉavarum. āytaṅkeṭāmuṉṉē aṉṉiṉ akaratti+ kuṟṟukarattiṉmēl ēṟṟuka, āytam^ keṭṭāl atu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ. (17)

TE423n

muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu āyta+ puḷḷi
maṉṉal vēṇṭu malvaḻi yāṉa.

itu muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ+ cuṭṭumutalukarattiṟku oruvaḻi alvaḻimuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉṉuyir varumiṭattu --- cuṭṭumutalākiya āytattoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟṟiṉmuṉṉē uyirmutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, āytappuḷḷi maṉṉal vēṇṭum --- āytavoṟṟu muṉpōla+ keṭātu nilipeṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, alvaḻiyāṉa --- alvaḻikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aḵtu iḵtu uḵtu eṉa niṟutti aṭi āṭi ili īyam ural ūrti eḻu ēṇi aiyam oṭukkam ōkkam auviyam eṉa oṭṭuka.

maṉṉeṉṟataṉāṉ vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum iv viti koḷka. aḵtaṭiva aḵtoṭṭam eṉa oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāmurupu virikka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ēṉi ilakkaṇam muṭiyumāṟu aṟintu muṭikka. (18)

TE424n

ēṉimuṉ variṉē tāṉili yiṉṟē.

itu mēlavaṟṟiṟku+ piṟakaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: ēṉi muṉ variṉ --- muṟkūṟiya īṟukaḷiṉ muṉṉar uyirkkaṇamallaṉa varumāyiṉ, tāṉ niliyiṉṟu --- avvāytam keṭṭumuṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: atukaṭitu itukaṭitu utukaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu ñāṉṟatu nīṇṭatu māṇṭatu yātu valitu eṉa oṭṭuka. (19)

TE425n

allatu kiḷappi ṉellā moḻiyum^
colliya paṇpi ṉiyaṟki yākum.

iḵtu āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukarattiṟkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: allatu kiḷappiṉ --- alvaḻiyi+ collumiṭattu, ellā moḻiyum --- āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaramum, colliya paṇpiṉ iyaṟkiyākum --- mēl āciriyaṉ kūṟiya kuṇattiyaṭiya iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nākukaṭitu varakukaṭitu teḷkukaṭitu eḵkukaṭitu kuraṅkukaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. ēṉikkaṇattukkaṇ `niṉṟa coṉmu ṉiyalpākum' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

ellāmoḻiyum eṉṟataṉāl viṉiccollum viṉikkuṟippuccollum iyalpāy muṭital koḷka. kiṭantatu kutiri karitukutiri eṉa varum.

colliya eṉṟataṉāṉ irupeyaroṭṭu+ paṇputtoki vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku valleḻuttuppeṟṟa muṭitalum iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ iṉavoṟṟumikku muṭitalum^ koḷka. karaṭṭukkāṇam kuruṭṭukkōḻi tiruṭṭuppuliyaṉ kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟi veḷiṟṟuppaṉi eyiṟṟuppal eṉavum, varaṭṭāṭu kuruṭṭerutu eṉavum varum.

paṇpiṉeṉṟataṉāl melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāram peṟṟu muṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟāy aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭivaṉavum, melloṟṟu valloṟṟākātu aikāramum valleḻuttum peṟṟumuṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. ōryāṭṭiyāṉi aiyāṭṭiyerutu eṉavum, aṟṟikkūttar iṟṟikkūttar eṉavum, maṉṟittūti maṉṟippāṉi paṇṭiccāṉṟār eṉavum varum. (20)

TE426n

valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē.

iḵtu avvāṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakuttatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: valloṟṟu+ toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikum --- valloṟṟuttoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟu valleḻuttu varuvaḻi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kokkukkaṭitu pākkukkaṭitu paṭṭukkaṭitu ciṟitu tītu peritu eṉa varum. (21)

TE427n

cuṭṭucciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum
yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyu
māyiya ṟiriyā valleḻuttu iyaṟki.

ituvum av āṟu īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉkaṇ ēḻām vēṟṟumi iṭapporuḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭiccoṟku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: cuṭṭu+ ciṉi nīṭiya meṉṟoṭar moḻiyum --- cuṭṭākiya ciṉiyeḻuttu nīṇṭa meṉṟoṭar+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, yāviṉā mutaliya meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyum --- yāveṉṉum viṉāmutalākiya meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟukara īṟum, valleḻuttiyaṟki ā iyal tiriyā --- valleḻuttu+ peṟṟa muṭiyum^ taṉmiyākiya avviyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: āṅkukkoṇṭāṉ īṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ūṅkukkoṇṭāṉ yāṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

iyaṟkiyeṉṟataṉāṉ meṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam iyalpāyum vaṉṟoṭarmoḻi+ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟṟu viṉiyeccam mikkum muṭivaṉa koḷka. iruntu koṇṭāṉ āṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ tantu tīrntāṉ vantu pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum, cettukkiṭantāṉ ceṟṟucceytāṉ uyttukkoṇṭāṉ naṭṭuppōṉāṉ eṉavum varum. (22)

TE428n

yāviṉā moḻiyē yiyalpu mākum.

itu mēlaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟukiṉṟatu. valleḻuttu vilakki iyalpāmeṉṟaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: yāviṉā moḻiyē iyalpumākum --- avaṟṟuḷ yāveṉṉam viṉāviyuṭiya col muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi iyalpāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: yāṅkukoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum. iḵtu eppaṭiyeṉṉum viṉāpporuḷi uṇarttiṟṟu. ummiyāṉ mikkumuṭitalē valiyuṭittu. ēkāram pirinili. (23)

TE429n

annāṉ moḻiyum^ tannili tiriyā.

itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻicceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: annāṉmoḻiyum --- cuṭṭumutaṉmūṉṟum yāmutaṉ moḻiyumākiya annāṉkumoḻiyum, tam nili tiriyā --- tam melloṟṟāya taṉmi tirintu valloṟṟākātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muṟkāṭṭiyavē. tanniliyeṉṟataṉāṉ melloṟṟu+ tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikkumuṭivaṉa piṟavum^ koḷka. aṅkukkoṇṭāṉ iṅkukkoṇṭāṉ uṅkukkoṇṭāṉ eṅkukkoṇṭāṉ ceṉṟāṉ tantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉa varum.

iṉi muṉṉar yā moḻiyeṉṉātu viṉāveṉṟataṉāṉ ēḻāvataṉ iṭapporuṭṭākiya piṟavum iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum^ koḷka. muntukoṇṭāṉ paṇṭukoṇṭāṉ iṉṟukoṇṭāṉ aṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉṟukoṇṭāṉ eṉavarum. (24)

TE420n

uṇṭeṉ kiḷavi yuṇmi ceppiṉ
munti yiṟuti meyyoṭum^ keṭutalu
mēṉili yoṟṟē ḷakāra mātalu
māmuṟi yiraṇṭu murimiyu muṭittē
valleḻuttu varūum^ kāli yāṉa.

itu meṉṟoṭarmoḻiyuḷ viṉikkuṟippāy niṉṟatōr col+ paṇpi uṇarttuṅkāl vēṟumuṭivu peṟutal kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uṇṭeṉkiḷavi uṇmiceppiṉ --- uṇṭeṉṉuñcol viṉikkuṟippiyuṇarttātu oruporuḷ tōṉṟuṅkāl tōṉṟi atu keṭuntuṇiyum uṇṭāy niṟkiṉṟa taṉmiyākiya paṇpi uṇarttiniṟkumāyiṉ, munti iṟuti meyyoṭuṅkeṭutalum --- muṉṉarniṉṟa kuṟṟukarantāṉ ēṟiniṉṟa meyyoṭuṅkeṭutalum, mēṉili oṟṟē ḷakāramātalum --- ataṟku mēl niṉṟa ṇakāra oṟṟu ḷakāra oṟṟātalumākiya, ā muṟi iraṇṭum urimiyum uṭittu --- ammuṟiyiṉiyuṭiya iraṇṭu niliyum urittu. aḵtu urittaṉṟi muṉṉar niṉṟa niliyilē kēṭum^ tiripum iṉṟi niṟṟalum uṭittu, valleḻuttu varūum^ kāliyāṉa --- valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu eṉṟavāṟu.
