valleḻuttu atikārattāṉ vārāniṟpa valleḻuttu varūuṅkāli eṉṟataṉāṉ avvirumuṭipum uḷa, paṇpi yuṇarttum pakaram varum moḻikkaṇṇēyeṉpatūum ēṉi+ kacatakkaḷilum iyalpukaṇattiṉum uṇṭeṉa niṉṟu viṉikkuṟippāyum^ ciṟupāṉmi paṇpāyum niṟkumeṉpatūum^ koḷka.

utāraṇam: uḷporuḷ uṇṭuporuḷ eṉa varum. itu poruṇmi cuṭṭātu uṇmittaṉmi+ paṇpi īṇṭu uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi uṇṭukāṇam uṇṭucākkāṭu uṇṭutāmari uṇṭuñāṉam nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu aṭi āṭi eṉa varuvaṉavellām^ kēṭum^ tiripumiṉṟi viṉikkuṟippāyum ciṟupāṉmi paṇpāyum niṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu viṉiyiyaluḷ viṉikkuṟippu ōtum vaḻi uṇarka.

uḷporuḷeṉpatu paṇputtoki muṭipaṉṟōveṉiṉ, aḵtu ōci oṟṟumipaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu. iḵtu ōci iṭiyaṟavupaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatupōlum. (25)

TE431n

irutici puṇari ṉēyiṭi varumē.

itu kuṟṟukara īṟṟu+ ticippeyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: irutici puṇariṉ --- iraṇṭu perunticiyum^ tammiṉ+ puṇariṉ, ē iṭi varum --- ēyeṉṉum cāriyi iṭi niṉṟu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: teṟkēvaṭakku kiḻakkēmēṟku ivi ummittoki. (26)

TE432n

tiripuvēṟu kiḷappi ṉoṟṟu mukaramum^
keṭutal vēṇṭu meṉmaṉār pulava
roṟṟumey tirintu ṉakāra mākum
teṟkoṭu puṇarum kāli yāṉa.

itu perunticikaḷōṭu kōṇatticikaḷ puṇarttaliṉ eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: tiripuvēṟu kiḷappiṉ --- apperum^ ticikaḷōṭu kōṇatticikaḷi vēṟāka+ puṇarkkumiṭattu, oṟṟum ukaramum^ keṭutal vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvukaram ēṟi niṉṟa oṟṟum avvīṟṟu ukaramum^ keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, teṟkoṭu puṇaruṅkāli --- atu teṟkeṉṉum^ ticiyoṭu puṇaruṅkālattu, āṉa oṟṟu mey tirintu ṉakāramākum --- atticikku+ poruntiniṉṟa ṟakāra oṟṟu+ taṉvaṭivu tirintu ṉakara oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

tirinteṉṟataṉāṉ vaṭakkeṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa kakara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu muṭittukkoḷka.

utāraṇam: vaṭakiḻakku vaṭamēṟku teṉkiḻakku teṉmēṟku eṉa varum.

vēṟeṉṟataṉāl ticippeyarōṭu poruṭpeyarvariṉum immuṭipu koḷka. vaṭakaṭal vaṭacuram vaṭavēṅkaṭam teṉkumari teṉcuram teṉṉilaṅki eṉa varum.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ uyir keṭṭum^ tirintum mey keṭṭum muṭivaṉavum uḷa, ticippeyar muṉṉar+ poruṭpeyar vantuḻiyeṉṟu uṇarka. kiḻakku eṉpatu kari kūri eṉpavaṟṟōṭu puṇarumiṭattu+ kīḻkari kīḻkūri eṉa nilimoḻi yiṟuti ukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭṭu ataṉmēlniṉṟa kakara oṟṟum ḻakarattil akaramum^ keṭṭu mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭintaṉa. mēṟku, kari, kūri, mīkari mīkūri eṉa nilimoḻi īṟṟu ukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu ataṉmēl niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟum^ keṭṭu ēkāram īkāramāki muṭintaṉa. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē mēṉmāṭu mēlpāl mēliccēri eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavum^ ceykiyaṟintu muṭikka. (27)

TE433n

oṉṟumuta lāka veṭṭa ṉiṟuti
yellā veṇṇum pattaṉ muṉvariṉ+
kuṟṟiya lukara meyyoṭum^ keṭumē
muṟṟa viṉvarūu miraṇṭalam^ kaṭiyē.

niṟutta muṟiyāṉē āṟu īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaramum puṇarumāṟu uṇartti iṉi avvīṟṟu eṇṇuppeyar muṭikkiṉṟār; iḵtu avaṟṟuḷ+ patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarōṭu eṇṇuppeyarvantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalāka eṭṭaṉ iṟuti ellā eṇṇum --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇmutalāka eṭṭeṉṉum eṇṇīṟāyuḷḷa ellā eṇṇuppeyarkaḷum, pattaṉ muṉvariṉ --- patteṉṉum eṇṇuppeyariṉ muṉvariṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭum --- appatteṉṉum^ colliṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram^ tāṉ ēṟi niṉṟa meyyōṭum^ keṭum, iraṇṭalaṅkaṭi muṟṟa iṉvarūum --- āṇṭu iraṇṭallāta eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṭattu muṭiya iṉcāriyi iṭivantu puṇarum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉoṉṟu patiṉmūṉṟu patiṉāṉku patiṉintu patiṉēḻu patiṉeṭṭu eṉa varum.

nilimoḻi muṟkūṟātataṉāṉ+ piṟaveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar+ piṟapeyar vantuḻiyum iṉpeṟutal koḷka. oṉpatiṉkūṟu oṉpatiṉpāl eṉa varum. muṟṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ+ patiṉāṉkeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram varumoḻikkaṇ+ karuviceytu keṭuttu muṭikka. (28)

TE434n

patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa ṉakāra miraṭṭa
lotta teṉpa viraṇṭuvaru kāli.

itu mēl iṉpeṟāteṉṟu vilakkiya iraṇṭaṟku+ piṟituviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa ṉakāram iraṭṭal --- patteṉṉum^ colliṉ niṉṟa takara oṟṟu+ keṭa ṉakara oṟṟu iraṭṭittu varutal, iraṇṭu varukāli ottateṉpa --- iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ varuṅkālattiṉ+ poruntiṟṟeṉpar āciriyar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉa varum.

`kuṟṟiyalukara meyyoṭuṅkeṭum' ((eḻu//433)) eṉṟaviti itaṟkum mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka. (29)

TE435n

āyiram variṉu māyiya ṟiriyātu.

iḵtu āyirameṉṉum eṇṇuppeyarvariṉ varum muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉum āyiyal tiriyātu --- muṟkūṟiya pattaṉ muṉṉar oṉṟu mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟi āyirameṉṉumeṇ vantālum īṟukeṭṭu iṉpeṟṟu muṭiyum iyalpil tiriyātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉāyiram eṉa varum.

ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu. (30)

TE436n

niṟiyu maḷavum varūum^ kāliyum^
kuṟiyā tāku miṉṉeṉ cāriyi.

iḵtu eṇṇuppeyarōṭu niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum puṇarkiṉṟa puṇarcci kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: niṟiyum aḷavum varūuṅkāliyum --- muṟkūṟiya patteṉpataṉ muṉṉar niṟippeyarum aḷavuppeyarum varuṅkālattum, iṉṉeṉcāriyi kuṟiyātākum --- avviṉṉeṉṉum^ cāriyi kuṟiyātu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: patiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum, patiṉkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum, patiṟṟakal patiṟṟuḻakku eṉavum varum.

kuṟiyātāku meṉṟataṉāṉ+ poruṭpeyarum eṇṇu+ peyarum niṟippeyarum vantuḻiyum iṉ koṭuttu vēṇṭuñceyki ceytu muṭikka. patiṟṟuvēli yāṇṭu aṭukku muḻam eṉavum, patiṉṟiṅkaḷ eṉavum, patiṟṟuttoṭi eṉavum varum. patiṟṟoṉṟu eṉpatupōla iraṇṭumutal+ pattaḷavum oṭṭuka.

iv vīṟṟiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal `aḷavākumoḻimutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉuḷ `niliiya' eṉṟataṉāl muṭikka. ivaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum ukaram varutalum valleḻuttu+ peṟutalum `oṉṟumutalāka' ((eḻu//433)) eṉpataṉuḷ `muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ koḷka.

TE437n

oṉṟumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
niṉṟa patta ṉoṟṟukkeṭa vāytam
vantiṭi niliyu miyaṟkittu eṉpa
kūṟiya viyaṟki kuṟṟiya lukara
māṟa ṉiṟuti yalvaḻi yāṉa.

iḵtu eṇṇu+ peyarōṭu patteṉṉum eṇṇu+ peyarkku muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉ iṟutimuṉṉar --- oṉṟumutal oṉpatu īṟāka+ kūṟukiṉṟa eṇṇuppeyar variṉ muṉṉar, niṉṟa pattaṉ oṟṟukkeṭa --- varumoḻiyāka vantuniṉṟa patteṉṉum^ colliṉatu takara oṟṟu+ keṭa, āytam vantu iṭiniliyum iyaṟkittu eṉpa --- āytamāṉatu vantu iṭiyē nilipeṟum iyalpiyuṭitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar, āṟaṉ iṟuti alvaḻiyāṉa --- avaṟṟuḷ āṟeṉṉum īṟallātaviṭattu, kuṟṟiyalukaram^ kūṟiya iyaṟki --- kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟkūṟiya iyaṟkiyāy meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

iṅṅaṉam varumāṟu mēṟcūttiraṅkaḷuḷ+ kāṭṭutum.

vanteṉṟataṉāl āytamāka+ tiriyātu takara oṟṟukkeṭṭu orupatu eṉṟu niṟṟalum^ koḷka. (22)

TE438n

mutalī reṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakara māku
mukaram varuta lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ+ cilavaṟṟiṟku nilimoḻicceyki kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalī reṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakaram ākum --- avaṟṟiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉuṭi ṉakaroṟṟum ṇakaroṟṟum rakara oṟṟāka+ tirintuniṟkum, āvayiṉāṉa ukaram varutal --- avviṭattu ukaram varuka eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupaḵtu eṉa varum. oṉṟeṉpataṉ īṟṟu+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu ṉakara oṟṟiṉi rakara oṟṟākki ukaramēṟṟi oruveṉa niṟutti niṉṟa patteṉpataṉ takara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu āytamākki+ paḵteṉa varuvittu orupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka. mēlvaruvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum^ cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ+ kūṟum^ ciṟappuviti oḻintavaṟṟiṟku ituvē muṭipāka+ koḷka. (33)

TE439n

iṭinili rakaram iraṇṭu* eṉ eṇṇiṟku
naṭi maruṅku* iṉṟu* ē poruḷ vayiṉāṉa.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iraṇṭe ṉeṇṇiṟku iṭinili rakaram --- iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṟku iṭiniṉṟa rakaram, poruḷvayiṉāṉa --- ammoḻi poruḷpeṟum iṭattu, naṭimaruṅkiṉṟu --- naṭakkum iṭamiṉṟi+ keṭum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: irupaḵtu eṉa varum. itaṟku rakaravuyirmey itaṉāṉ+ keṭuttu ēṉiya kūṟiyavāṟē kūṭṭi muṭikka.

poruḷeṉavē eṇṇallā+ peyarum^ koḷka. irukaṭal iruviṉi irupiṟappu eṉa varum. (34)

TE440n

mūṉṟu māṟu neṭumutal kuṟukum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukum --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇum āṟeṉṉu meṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟuki muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aṟu eṉa+ kuṟukki+ paḵtu eṉa varuvittu aṟupaḵtu eṉa muṭikka.

TE441n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē pakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉ oṟṟē pakāramākum --- mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu+ pakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muppaḵtu eṉa varum. (36)

TE442n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē ṟakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉ oṟṟē ṟakāramākum --- nāṉkeṉṉum eṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṟakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṟpaḵtu eṉa varum.(37)

TE443n

ainta ṉoṟṟē makāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē makāramākum --- ainteṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu makara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aimpaḵtu eṉa varum.

ēḻu kuṟṟiyalukara īṟaṉmi urupiyaluḷ+ kāṇka.(38)

TE444n

eṭṭa ṉoṟṟē ṇakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṭṭaṉoṟṟē ṇakāramākum --- eṭṭeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇ niṉṟa ṭakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: eṇpaḵtu eṉa varum. (39)

TE445n

oṉpā ṉokaramici+ takara moṟṟu
munti yoṟṟē ṇakāra miraṭṭum
paḵteṉ kiḷavi yāytapaka raṅkeṭa
niṟṟal vēṇṭu mūkāra+ kiḷavi
yoṟṟiya takaram ṟakara mākum.

iḵtu eytāta teytuvittatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ okaramici+ takaram oṟṟum --- oṉpaḵtu eṉa niṟutti+ paḵtu eṉa varuvittu muṭikkuṅkāl, nilimoḻiyākiya oṉpaḵteṉṉum eṇṇiṉatu okarattiṟku muṉṉāka oru takara oṟṟu+ tōṉṟi niṟkum, munti oṟṟē ṇakāram iraṭṭum --- muṉ coṉṉa okarattiṟku muṉṉar niṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ṇakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, paḵteṉ kiḷavi āyta pakaraṅkeṭa --- varumoḻi y-ākiya paḵteṉṉum^ col taṉkaṇ āytamum pakaramum^ keṭṭuppōka, ūkārakkiḷavi niṟṟalvēṇṭum --- nilimoḻiyil iraṭṭiya ṇakarattiṉ piṉṉar ūkāramākiya eḻuttu vantu niṟṟali āciriyaṉ virumpum, oṟṟiya takaram ṟakaramākum --- varumoḻiyākiya patteṉpataṉ īṟṟiṉmēlēṟiya ukaraṅkeṭātu pirintu niṟpa oṟṟāy niṉṟa takaram ṟakara oṟṟāy niṟkum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: toṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. itaṉi oṟṟāy vantuniṉṟa takara oṟṟiṉmēl nilimoḻi okaratti ēṟṟi+ tovvākki ṇakara oṟṟu iraṭṭi ataṉmēl varumoḻikkaṇ+ pakaramum āytamuṅkeṭa vanta ūkāramēṟṟi+ toṇṇūvākki+ pakaravāytameṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāṉ nilimoḻikkaṇ+ pakaramum āytamum^ keṭuttu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭuttu varumoḻi iṟuti+ takara oṟṟu+ tirintu niṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉmēlē niṉṟa ukaramēṟṟi+ toṇṇūṟeṉa muṭikka. (40)

TE446n

aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyu niṟiyiṉ kiḷaviyum^
kiḷanta viyala tōṉṟum^ kāli.

itu mēṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉeṇkaḷōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum niṟiyiṉ kiḷaviyum^ tōṉṟuṅkāli --- muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷantaṟiyappaṭum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantu tōṉṟuṅkālattu, kiḷanta iyala --- āṟaṉ īṟu alvaḻi+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantu iṭinili rakaraṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki nāṉkaṉoṟṟu vaṉkaṇattu ṟakaramāy eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakaramāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orukalam irukalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum, orukaḻañcu irukaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa muṉṉar muṭittum. ivi mutalīreṇṇiṉ ceyki.

tōṉṟuṅkāli yeṉṟataṉāṉ ivveṇṇiṉ muṉṉar eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātuniṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷellām ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eytuvittu muṭittukkoḷka. orumūṉṟu orunāṉku irumūṉṟu irunāṉku orukāl irukāl orumuntiriki irumuntiriki orumukkāl irumukkāl eṉpaṉa piṟavum^ koṇarntu oṭṭuka. iṉi+ piṟavit eytuvaṉa ōroṉṟu ōriraṇṭu ōrintu ōrāṟu ōrēḻu ōreṭṭu ōroṉpatu eṉavum, īroṉṟu īriraṇṭu īrintu īrāṟu īrēḻu īreṭṭu īroṉpatu eṉavum, mūvoṉṟu mūviraṇṭu mūvintu mūvāṟu mūvēḻu mūveṭṭu mūvoṉpatu eṉavum, `mutalīreṇṇiṉmuṉuyir' ((eḻu//455)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ uyirkku eytiya piṟavitiyum `mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalumurittu' ((eḻu//457)) eṉṟa piṟavitiyum peṟṟu+ piṟaceykikaḷum peṟṟu muṭintaṉa. nāloṉṟu nāliraṇṭu nālintu nālāṟu nālēḻu nāleṭṭu nāloṉpatu eṉpaṉa `nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum' ((eḻu//453)) eṉṟa vitipeṟṟu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum ituvāṟēyaṉṟi aḷavum niṟiyumaṉṟi varuvaṉavellām ivvilēcāṉ muṭikka. (41)

TE447n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vanta tokkum.

itu mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vantatokkum --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu varumoḻiyāy vanta aḷavuppeyar niṟippeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vanta valloṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mukkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, mukkaḻañcu kaḵcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

`nāṉkaṉoṟṟē ṟakāramākum' ((eḻu//442)) eṉṟa muṉṉi māṭṭēṟu niṟṟaliṉ nāṟkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, nāṟkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. (42)

TE448n

ainta ṉoṟṟē melleḻuttu ākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē melleḻuttākum --- aintāvataṉkaṇ niṉṟa nakara oṟṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttukku ēṟṟa melleḻuttāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aiṅkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi eṉavum, aiṅkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. ēkāram īṟṟaci. (43)

TE449n

kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūum^ kāli.

itu muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟaṟkum aintaṟkum varumoḻi variyaṟukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kacatapa mutaṉmoḻi varūum^ kāli --- mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu vantatoppatūum aintaṉoṟṟu melleḻuttāvatūum avvaḷavuppeyar oṉpatiṉum vaṉkaṇamākiya kacatapakkaḷ mutaṉmoḻiyāy vanta iṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

atu muṉṉar+ kāṭṭiṉām. āṟu `neṭumutalkuṟukum' ((eḻu//440)) eṉṟa māṭṭēṟṟāṉē āṟu neṭumutal kuṟuki niṉṟatu.

utāraṇam: aṟukalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum, aṟukaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. akal uḻakka eṉpaṉa mēṟkāṭṭiṉām. ēḻu kuṟṟukara īṟaṉmiyiṉ māṭṭēṟu ēlātāyiṟṟu. (44)

TE450n

namava veṉṉu mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi
yakaram variṉu meṭṭaṉmu ṉiyalpē.

itu vēṇṭātu kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭikkiṉṟatu. `ñanamayava' ((eḻu//144)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ+ kūṟiyavaṟṟi+ kūṟutaliṉ.

itaṉ poruḷ: eṭṭaṉmuṉ --- eṭṭeṉpataṉ muṉṉar, namava eṉṉum mūṉṟoṭu civaṇi akaram variṉum --- aḷavuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar meṉkaṇattu iraṇṭum iṭikkaṇattu oṉṟumākaya namava eṉṉum mūṉṟaṉōṭu porunti uyirkkaṇattu akaram variṉum, ummiyāṉ uyirkkaṇattu ukaram variṉum^ kūṟāta valleḻuttukkaḷ variṉum, iyalpu --- muṟkūṟiyavāṟē ṭakāram ṇakāramāy vēṟōr vikāramiṉṟi iyalpāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

namava veṉṉum mūṉṟum vantāṟpōla akaramvariṉu meṉpatu poruḷ.

utāraṇam: eṇṇāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi eṉavum eṇṇakal eṇṇuḻakku eṉavum, eṇkalam cāṭi, tūti pāṉi eṉavum varum.

oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāṉ vaṉkaṇattu niṟippeyarum^ koḷka. eṇkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉa varum.

ivvēṇṭā kūṟalāṉ eṇṇakaleṉa+ kuṟṟukara īṟāy+ kēṭum^ tirivumpeṟṟu uyirvarumoḻiyāṉa toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. (45)

TE451n

aintu mūṉṟu namavaru kāli
vanta tokku moṟṟiya ṉiliyē.

ituvum mēl māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvāmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintum mūṉṟum nama varukāli --- ainteṉṉu meṇṇum mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇum nakara mutaṉmoḻiyum makara mutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, oṟṟiyalnili --- nilimoḻikkaṇ niṉṟa oṟṟu naṭakkum nilimi kūṟiṉ, vantatu okkum --- mēl+ kūṟiyavāṟē makaramum pakaramumākātu varumoḻimutal vanta oṟṟōṭu otta oṟṟāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ainnāḻi aimmaṇṭi munnāḻi mummaṇṭi eṉa varum.

mūṉṟum aintum eṉṉāta muṟiyaṉṟi+ kūṟṟiṉāl nāṉāḻi nāṉmaṇṭi eṉpuḻi nilimoḻi ṉakaram ṟakaramākātu niṉṟavāṟē niṉṟu muṭitalum varumoḻi mutaṉiṉṟa nakaram ṉakaramāka+ tiriya nilimoḻi nakaram^ keṭutalum^ koḷka. (46)

TE452n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakaram varuvaḻi+
tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvā kummē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu, vakaram varuvaḻi --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi varumiṭattu, tōṉṟiya vakarattu uruvākum --- avvarumoḻiyāy+ tōṉṟiya vakarattiṉ vaṭivāy muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvaṭṭi eṉa varum.

tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāṉē mutal nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟiṉṟi mūvaṭṭi eṉṟumām. (47)

TE451n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟu --- nāṉkāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakāramākum --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl lakara oṟṟākattirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nālvaṭṭi eṉa varum. (48)

TE454n

ainta ṉoṟṟē muntiyatu keṭumē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟu --- aintā meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu, muntiyatu keṭum --- vakaramutaṉmoḻi vantāl muṉṉiṉṟa vaṭivu keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aivaṭṭi eṉa varum.

muntiyeṉṟataṉāl nakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu vakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu aivvaṭṭiyeṉa+ ciṟupāṉmi varum. (49)

TE455n

mūtalī reṇṇiṉmu ṉuyirvaru kāli+
tavaleṉa moḻipa vukara+ kiḷavi
mutaṉili nīṭa lāvayi ṉāṉa.

itu māṭṭēṟṟāṉ eytiya ukarattiṟku+ kēṭukūṟi mutaṉīḷka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituvit kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutalīreṇṇiṉ muṉ uyir varukāli --- oru iru eṉa muṭintuniṉṟa iraṇṭeṇṇiṉ muṉṉar uyir mutaṉmoḻi varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ukarakkiḷavi tavaleṉa moḻipa --- nilimoḻiyukaramākiya eḻuttu+ keṭutalāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar, āvayiṉāṉa mutaṉili nīṭal --- avviraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōrakal īrakal ōruḻakku īruḻakku eṉa varum (50)

TE456n

mūṉṟu nāṉku minteṉ kiḷaviyum^
tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟki yākum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟum nāṉkum ainteṉ kiḷaviyum --- mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇum nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇum ainteṉṉumeṇṇum, tōṉṟiya vakarattu iyaṟkiyākum --- muṉṉar+ tōṉṟiniṉṟa vakaram varumoḻikku+ kūṟiya iyalpāka mūṉṟiṉkaṇ vakara oṟṟāyum, nāṉkiṉkaṇ lakara oṟṟāyum aintiṉkaṇ oṟṟukkeṭṭum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvakal muvvuḻakku eṉa itaṟku+ tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. nālakal nāluḻakku aiyakal aiyuḻakku eṉa varum.

tōṉṟiya eṉṟataṉāl mēl mūṉṟeṉpatu mutalnīṇṭa iṭattu nilimoḻi ṉakaravoṟṟukkeṭuttukkoḷka. iyaṟkiyeṉṟataṉāl toṭarmoḻikkaṇ oṟṟiraṭṭutal koḷka. `mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē' mutaliya mūṉṟu cūttiramum^ koṇarntu muṭikka. (51)

TE457n

mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalu murittē
yuḻakkeṉ kiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa.

itu muṉṉar+ kuṟukumeṉṟataṉi nīṇṭu muṭika eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉ mutaṉili nīṭalum urittu --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa eḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭitalum urittu, aḵtiyāṇṭeṉiṉ, uḻakku eṉkiḷavi vaḻakkattāṉa --- uḻakkeṉṉum^ coṉ muṭiyum vaḻakkiṭattu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: mūvuḻakka eṉa varum.

vaḻakkattāṉa eṉpataṉāṉ akaleṉ kiḷavikku mutaṉili nīṭalum^ koḷka. mūvakal eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē nilimoḻi ṉakara oṟṟu+ keṭukka.

mūḻakku mūḻākkeṉṉum marumuṭipu ivvōttiṉ puṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka. (52)

TE458n

āṟeṉ kiḷavi mutaṉī ṭummē.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟeṉkiḷavi --- āṟeṉṉumeṇṇu+ peyar akal uḻakku eṉpaṉa variṉ, mutal nīṭum --- muṉṉar+ kuṟukiniṉṟa mutaleḻuttu nīṇṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

atuveṉṉātu āṟeṉṟār, tirintataṉ ṟiripatu eṉṉum nayattāl.

utāraṇam: āṟakal āṟuḻakku eṉa varum. (53)

TE459n

oṉpā ṉiṟuti yurupunili tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭum^ cāriyi moḻiyē.

itu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭātu niṉṟu iṉpeṟuka eṉṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟituviti vakukkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti urupunili tiriyātu --- aḷavum niṟiyam varuvaḻi oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟukaram^ taṉvaṭivu nilitiriyātu niṉṟu, cāriyi moḻi iṉ peṟalvēṇṭum --- cāriyi+ collākiya iṉ peṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉpatiṉkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum, kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

cāriyimoḻi yeṉṟataṉāṉ iṉṟōṭu ukaramum valleḻuttum^ koṭuttu+ ceykiceytu muṭikka. oṉpatiṟṟukkalam cāṭi eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.

urupeṉpataṉāṉ oṉpatiṟaṟeṉa oṟṟiraṭṭutal ellāvaṟṟiṟkum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē oṉpatiṉāḻiyeṉpuḻi vanta iṉṉiṉ ṉakarakkēṭum^ koḷka. `aḷavākumoḻimutal(eḻu//121) eṉpataṉāṉum `niliiya' eṉṉum ilēcāṉum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. (54)

TE460n

nūṟumuṉ variṉum^ kūṟiya viyalpē.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉpataṉi+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: muṉ --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, nūṟuvariṉum --- nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar vantālum, kūṟiya iyalpu --- mēl+ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻi+ kūṟiya iyalpu eyti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

atu kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭuṅkeṭṭu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟuki mutalīreṇṇi ṉoṟṟu rakakaramāy ukaram peṟṟu iṭinili rakaram iraṇṭaṉkaṇ+ keṭṭu muṭitalām.

utāraṇam: orunūṟu irunūṟu aṟunūṟu eṇṇūṟu eṉa varum. ivi māṭṭēṟṟāṉ muṭintaṉa. māṭṭēṟu ovvātaṉa mēṟkūṟi muṭippa. (55)

TE461n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē nakāra mākum.

itu māṭṭēṟṟōṭu ovvātataṟku vēṟu muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē nakāramākum --- mūṉṟāmeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakaravoṟṟu nakaravoṟṟākum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: munnūṟu eṉa varum. (56)

TE462n

nāṉku mintu moṟṟumey tiriyā.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkum aintum oṟṟumey tiriyā --- nāṉkeṉṉumeṇṇum ainteṉṉumeṇṇum tammoṟṟukkaḷ nilitiriyātu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nāṉūṟu ainnūṟu eṉa varum.

meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ nāṉūṟeṉpuḻi varumoḻi nakarattuḷ ūkāram pirittu `laṉaveṉavarūum' ((eḻu//149)) eṉpataṉāṉ ṉakaravoṟṟākki ūkāramēṟṟi nilimoḻi ṉakaram^ keṭuttu+ koḷka. (57)

TE463n

oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntu kiḷantu* aṟṟu* ē
munti y-oṟṟu* ē ḷakāram iraṭṭum*
nūṟu* eṉ kiḷavi nakāram* mey-keṭa
ū v-ā v-ākum iyaṟkittu eṉpa
v-āy iṭi varutal ikāra rakāram
īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum.

ituvum atu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ mutaṉili muntukiḷantaṟṟu --- oṉpateṉṉu meṇṇiṉ mutaṉiṉṟa okaram mēl+ patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumvaḻi+ kūṟiyavāṟupōla oru takaram oṟṟi ataṉmēl ēṟimuṭiyum, munti oṟṟē ḷakāram iraṭṭum --- avvokarattiṉ muṉṉiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu ḷakara oṟṟāy iraṭṭittu niṟkum, nūṟeṉkiḷavi nakāra meykeṭa ū āvākum iyaṟkitteṉpa --- varumoḻiyākiya nūṟeṉṉumeṇṇuppeyar nakaramākiya meykeṭa ataṉmēl ēṟiya ūkāram ākāramām iyalpiyuṭitteṉpar pulavar, āyiṭi ikāra rakāram varutal --- ammoḻiyiṭi ōr ikaramum rakāramum varuka, īṟu mey keṭuttu makaram oṟṟum --- īṟākiya kuṟṟukarattiṉiyum aḵtu ēṟiniṉṟa ṟakara oṟṟiṉiyum keṭuttu ōr makara oṟṟu vantu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

mey yeṉpataṉāṉ nilimoḻikkaṇ+ pakaram^ keṭukka.

utāraṇam: toḷḷāyiram eṉa varum.

itaṉi oṉpateṉṉum okarattiṉmuṉṉar vanta takara oṟṟiṉmēlē okarattiyēṟṟi+ pakaram^ keṭuttu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttuniṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟiṉi iraṇṭu ḷakara oṟṟākki nūṟeṉpataṉ nakaraṅkeṭuttu ūkāram ākāramākki ḷakarattiṉ mēlēṟṟi ākārattiṉmuṉ uṭampaṭu meyyakāram varuvittu ṟakara ukaram^ keṭuttu makara oṟṟu varuvittu muṭikka. (58)

TE464n

āyira+ kiḷavi varūum^ kāli
mutalī reṇṇi ṉukaram^ keṭumē.

iḵtu avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu āyirammuṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyirakkiḷavi varūum^ kāli --- āyirameṉṉum^ col oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉvaruṅkālattu, mutal īreṇṇiṉ ukaram keṭum --- oru iru eṉṉum iraṇṭeṇṇiṉkaṇ+ peṟṟu niṉṟa ukaram^ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

ukaram^ keṭumeṉavē ēṉiyaṉa muṉṉar+ kūṟiyavāṟē niṟṟal peṟṟām.

utāraṇam: orāyiri irāyiram eṉa varum. (59)

TE465n

mutaṉili nīṭiṉu māṉa milli.

iḵtu eytiyataṉmēl+ ciṟappuviti kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉili nīṭiṉum māṉamilli --- ammutalīreṇṇiṉ mutaṟkaṇiṉṟa okāra ikāraṅkaḷ nīṇṭu muṭiyiṉum^ kuṟṟamilli eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: ōrāyiram īrāyiram eṉa varum. (60)

TE466n

mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vakāra mākum.

itu mūṉṟeṉṉumeṇ āyirattōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mūṉṟaṉoṟṟē vakāramākum --- mūṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu vakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: muvvāyiram eṉa varum.

muṉṉiṉ+ cūttirattu nili eṉṟataṉāṉ itaṉi mutaṉili nīṭṭi vakara oṟṟu+ keṭuttu mūvāyiram eṉa muṭikka. (61)

TE467n

nāṉka ṉoṟṟē lakāra mākum.

itu nāṉkeṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nāṉkaṉoṟṟē lakāramākum --- nāṉkeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa ṉakara oṟṟu lakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nālāyiram eṉa varum. (62)

TE468n

ainta ṉoṟṟē yakāra mākum.

iḵtu ainteṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aintaṉoṟṟē yakāramākum --- ainteṉṉumeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa nakara oṟṟu yakara oṟṟāka+ tirintu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

aiyāyiram eṉa varum. (63)

TE469n

āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ+ kuṟṟiya lukara
mīṟumey yoḻiya+ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu āṟeṉṉu meṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram --- āṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, mey oḻiya īṟu keṭutal vēṇṭum --- atu tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakara oṟṟu+ keṭātu niṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu tāṉ keṭṭu+ puṇartali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: aṟāyiram eṉa varum.

muṉṉar `neṭumutal kuṟukum' ((eḻu//440)) eṉṟa vaḻi aṟuveṉa niṉṟa muṟṟukarattiṟkē īṇṭu+ kēṭu kūṟiṉāreṉpatu peṟṟām, eṉṉi? kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ ēṟi muṭitaliṉ. itu kuṟṟukaram^ tirintu muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ īṇṭu muṭipu kūṟiṉār. muṟṟiyalukaram īṟu meyyoḻiya+ keṭumeṉavē kuṟṟukaram^ keṭātu ēṟimuṭiyumeṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṉ+ peṟutum. āṟāyiram eṉa varum.

maruṅkeṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟa poruṭpeyarkkaṇṇum neṭumutal kuṟukātu niṉṟu muṭital koḷka. āṟākuvatē eṉa varum. (64)

TE470n

oṉpā ṉiṟuti yuruvunili tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭum^ cāriyi marapē.

iḵtu oṉpateṉṉumeṇ ataṉōṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉpāṉ iṟuti --- oṉpateṉṉumeṇṇiṉ iṟuti+ kuṟṟukaram, uruvunili tiriyātu --- taṉvaṭivunili tirintu keṭātu, cāriyi marapu iṉ peṟalvēṇṭum --- cāriyiyākiya marapiṉiyuṭiya iṉ peṟṟu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉa varum.

uruveṉṟum ciṉiyeṉṟum^ cāriyi marapeṉṟum^ kūṟiya mikiyāl āyiramallāta piṟaveṇṇiṉkaṇṇum poruṭpeyariṭattum iṉṉum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟu muṭiyum muṭipu koḷka. oṉpatiṟṟukkōṭi oṉpatiṟṟoṉpatu oṉpatiṟṟuttaṭakki oṉpatiṟṟeḻuttu eṉavarum.

iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē vēṟoru muṭipiṉmiyiṉ+ kūṟātoḻinta eṇṇāyira meṉṟavaḻi oṟṟiraṭṭutalum īṇṭu+ kūṟiyavaṟṟiṟku oṟṟiraṭṭutalum^ koḷka. `aḷavāku moḻi mutal' ((eḻu//121)) eṉpataṉuḷ `niliiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka. (65)

TE471n

nūṟā yiramuṉ varūum^ kāli
nūṟa ṉiyaṟki mutaṉili+ kiḷavi.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉumeṇ aṭiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟāyiram muṉ varūum^ kāli --- nūṟāyirameṉṉum aṭiyaṭuttamoḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉvarumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, mutaṉili+ kiḷavi nūṟaṉ iyaṟki --- oṉṟeṉṉum mutaṉili+ kiḷavi oṉṟu muṉ nūṟeṉṉumeṇṇōṭu muṭintāṟpōla vikārameyti muṭiyum. eṉavē vaḻinilikkiḷaviyākiya iraṇṭu mutaliya eṇkaḷ vikārameytiyum eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orunūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ēṉiyaṉa irunūṟāyiram iraṇṭu nūṟāyiram, munnūṟāyiram mūṉṟu nūṟāyiram, nāṉūṟāyiram nāṉku nūṟāyiram, ainnūṟāyiram aintu nūṟāyiram, aṟunūṟāyiram āṟu nūṟāyiram, eṇṇūṟāyiram eṭṭu nūṟāyiram, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram eṉa varum. ivvikāra+ paṭṭaṉavaṟṟiṟku+ kuṟṟukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu mutalīreṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākki ukaram varuvittu mūṉṟum āṟum neṭumutal kuṟukki mūṉṟaṉoṟṟu nakāramākki nāṉkum aintum oṟṟu meytiriyā vākki eṭṭaṉoṟṟu ṇakāramākki ilēcukaḷāṉ+ koṇṭa ceykikaḷil vēṇṭuvaṉavum^ koṇarntu muṭikka.

ēṟpuḻikkōṭa leṉpataṉāṉ+ toḷḷāyirameṉṟa muṭipiṉōṭu māṭṭēṟu ceṉṟatēṉum avvāṟu muṭiyāteṉṟu koḷka. muṉṉeṉpataṉāṉ iṉcāriyi peṟṟu oṉpatiṉūṟāyiram eṉṟumām. niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ mūṉṟum āṟum iyalpāka muṭivuḻi neṭumutal kuṟukāmi koḷka. (66)

TE472n

nūṟeṉ kiḷavi yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ
kīṟuciṉi yoḻiya viṉavoṟṟu mikumē.

itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷi+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: nūṟu eṉ kiḷavi --- nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇuppeyar, oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṟku --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu, īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya --- īṟākiya kuṟṟukaram^ taṉṉāṉ+ paṟṟappaṭṭa meyyoṭum^ keṭātu niṟpa, iṉa oṟṟu mikum --- acciṉikku iṉamākiya ṟakara oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa varum. iraṇṭu mutal oṉpatu aḷavum^ ceyki yaṟintu oṭṭuka.

īṟuciṉi eṉṟu ōtiya mikiyāṉ nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu piṟa eṇṇum piṟaporuṭpeyarum ivvitiyum piṟavitiyum eyti muṭital koḷka. nūṟṟuppattu nūṟṟukkōṭi nūṟṟuttoṇṇūṟu eṉavum, nūṟṟukkuṟi nūṟṟitaḻttāmari nūṟṟukkāṇam nūṟṟukkāṉmaṇṭapam eṉavum iṉa oṟṟu mikkaṉakoḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē irunūṟṟoṉṟu iraṇṭu nūṟṟoṉṟu eṉa nūṟu aṭiyaṭuttavaḻiyum^ koḷka. (67)

TE473n

aviyūr pattiṉu mattoḻiṟ ṟākum.

iḵtu annūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ aṭiyaṭuttavaḻi+ puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: avi ūrpattiṉum --- annūṟeṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷi ūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattum, attoḻiṟṟākum --- īṟu ciṉiyoḻiya iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟorupaḵtu irupaḵtu muppaḵtu nāṟpaḵtu aimpaḵtu aṟupaḵtu eḻupaḵtu eṇpaḵtu eṉavarum. maṟṟu nūṟṟoṉpatu avi ūrappaṭṭu vantatu aṉmi uṇarka.

ākumeṉṟataṉāṉ orunuṟṟorupaḵtu irunūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilimoḻi aṭiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipum^ koḷka. (68)

TE474n

aḷavu* um* niṟi y-um āy iyal* tiriyātu
kuṟṟiyalukaram um val=eḻuttu iyaṟki y-um*
muṉ+kiḷantaṉṉa v-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyātu --- nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṉ+ ṟiriyātu iṉa oṟṟu mikku muṭiyum, kuṟṟiyalukaramum valleḻuttiyaṟkiyum --- avviṭattu+ kuṟṟiyalukaram^ keṭāmiyum iṉa oṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭarmoḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ varumoḻi valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum, muṟkiḷantaṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar --- `valloṟṟu+ toṭar moḻi valleḻuttu mikumē' ((eḻu//426)) eṉa vaṉṟoṭarmoḻikku+ kūṟiya taṉmiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: nūṟṟukkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum kaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum.

tiriyāteṉṟataṉāl nūṟeṉpatu aṭiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷka. aḵtu orunūṟṟukkalam irunūṟṟukkalam eṉa varum. (69)

TE475n

oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
yoṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭi mikumē
niṉṟa vāytam^ keṭutal vēṇṭum.

iḵtu oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷi+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi --- oṉṟu mutal eṭṭu īṟāka+ patteṉṉam eṇ ēṟi orucollāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷ, oṉṟumutal oṉpāṟku --- oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ varumoḻiyāy vantu puṇarum iṭattu, niṉṟa āytam^ keṭutal vēṇṭum --- paḵteṉpataṉkaṇ niṉṟa āytaṅkeṭṭu muṭitali virumpum āciriyaṉ, oṟṟu iṭimikum --- āṇṭu iṉa oṟṟākiya oru takara oṟṟu iṭimikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupattoṉṟu irupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu irupattiraṇṭu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. ivaṟṟuḷ orupattoṉṟu orupattiraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇkaḷ atikārattāl niṉṟa nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aṭuttu varumeṉṟu uṇarka. (70)

TE476n

āyiram variṉē yiṉṉeṉ cāriyi
yāvayi ṉoṟṟiṭi mikuta lilli.

iḵtu orupaḵtu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu āyiratti+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: āyiram variṉ iṉṉeṉ cāriyi --- avvoṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi āyirattōṭu puṇarum iṭattu iṉ cāriyi peṟum, āvayiṉ oṟṟu iṭi mikutal illi --- avviṭattu+ takara oṟṟu iṭivantu mikātu eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupatiṉāyiram irupatiṉāyiram eṉa eṇpatiṉkāṟum oṭṭuka. ivi nūṟṟorupatiṉāyiram eṉavum varum.

āvayiṉ eṉṟataṉāl nūṟāyirattorupattīrāyiram eṉṟāṟpōla attu+ peṟutalum piṟavum^ koḷka. (71)

TE477n

aḷavu niṟiyu māyiya ṟiriyā.

iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: aḷavum niṟiyum āyiyal tiriyā --- orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭimikātu iṉcāriyi peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: orupatiṉkalam cāṭi tūti pāṉi nāḻi maṇṭi vaṭṭi akal uḻakku eṉavum, orupatiṉkaḻañcu toṭi palam eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟiṟku nūṟu aṭiyaṭuttu oṭṭuka.

tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ orupatiṟṟukkalam irupatiṟṟukkalam eṉṉum toṭakkattaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ iṉṉiṉ akaram ṟakaramāka+ tirintu iraṭṭutalum ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟutalum^ koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē orupatiṉāḻi eṉṟavaḻi varumoḻi nakaram^ tirintuḻi nilimoḻi ṉakarakkēṭum^ koḷka.

aḷavu niṟiyu mataṉō raṉṉa eṉṟu pāṭam ōtuvār muṉṉar+ cūttirattu `āvayiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉum ataṉ muṉṉarccūttirattu `niṉṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉum ivaṟṟi muṭippar. (72)

TE478n

mutaṉili y-eṇṇiṉ muṉ val=eḻuttu variṉum*
ña na ma+ tōṉṟiṉum ya va vantu* iyiyiṉum*
mutaṉili y-iyaṟki y-eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyari+ puṇarkkiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉili eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, ñanama tōṉṟiṉum --- ñanamakkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, yavavantu iyiyiṉum --- yavakkaḷākiya iṭiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum, mutaṉili iyaṟki eṉmaṉār pulavar --- avvoṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷ muṉṉeytiya muṭipu nilimi eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

eṉavē vaḻiniliyeṇṇākiya iraṇṭu mutalākiya eṇkaḷ ammūṉṟukaṇamum mutaṉmoḻiyāy variṉ mutaṉili muṭipāki vikāram eytiyam eytātu iyalpāyum muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: orukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum, irukal rēṇṭukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉavum oṭṭuka. ivveṇkaḷiṉ+ kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭṭu mutalīreṇṇiṉ oṟṟu rakāramāy ukaram vantatu. irukal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṭinili rakāram^ keṭukka. mukkal mūṉṟukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. itaṟku neṭumutal kuṟukki `mūṉṟa ṉoṟṟē vantatokkum' ((eḻu//447)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭikka. muṉṉar eṇṇuppeyarum aḷavuppeyarum niṟippeyarum varuvaḻi+ kūṟiya vikāraṅkaḷiṉ+ poruṭpeyarkkum ēṟpaṉa koṇarntu muṭittu ellāvaṟṟiṟkum niliyeṉṟataṉāṉ oṟṟu+ tirintu muṭikka. avi mūṉṟaṟku yakaram varuvaḻi vakara oṟṟātalumām. nāṟkal nāṉkukakal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi, nāṉñāṇ nāṉkuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; aiṅkal aintukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi, aiññāṇ aintu ñāṇ nūl maṇi, aiyāḻ aintu yāḻ, aivaṭṭu aintu vaṭṭu; aṟukal āṟukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; eṇkal eṭṭukkal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi, eṇñāṇ eṭṭuñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu; oṉpatukal cuṉi tuṭi paṟi ñāṇ nūl maṇi yāḻ vaṭṭu eṉa oṭṭuka. oṉpatiṉkal eṉa+ ceṉṟatēṉum vaḻakkiṉmiyiṉ oḻikka. iṉṉum māṭṭēṟiṉṟi varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām muṭipu `nili' yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṭikka. (73)

TE479n

ataṉili yuyirkkum yāvaru kāli
mutaṉili yokara mōvā kummē
rakarattu ukaram^ tuvara+ keṭumē.

iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi muṭiyumāṟum mēṟkūṟiya yakāram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum^ kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: mutaṉilikku --- oṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉṉum eṇṇiṟku, uyirum yāvum varukāli --- uyirmutaṉ moḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu, ataṉ nili --- ammutaṉiliyiṉ taṉmi ivvāṟām, okaram ōvākum --- okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum, rakarattu ukaram^ tuvara+ keṭum --- rakarattu mēṉiṉṟa ukaram muṟṟa+ keṭṭu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

nāṉkāvataṉi mutaṉiliyōṭu kūṭṭi ataṉkaṇ niṉṟa ummiyi uyirōṭum yāvōṭum^ kūṭṭuka. eṉavē vaḻiniliyeṇkaḷ uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu muṟkūṟiyavāṟē iruvāṟṟāṉum muṭiyum.

utāraṇam: ōraṭi ōrākam ōrili ōrīṭṭam ōruli ōrūcal ōreḻu ōrēṭu ōriyam, ōroḻukku ōrōli ōruviyam eṉa varum. kuṟṟiyalukaram meyyoṭum^ keṭuttu mutaleṇṇiṉoṟṟu rakaramākkuka. ōr yāḻ ōr yāṉi eṉa varum. tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ikaratti nīṭṭi rakarattuḷ ukaratti+ keṭuttu īraci īr yāṉi eṉavum, mūṉṟeṉṉum eṇṇiṉ ṉakaravoṟṟu+ keṭuttu mūvaci mūyāṉi eṉavum muṭikka. ivi ceyyuṇ muṭipu. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iṅṅaṉam varuvaṉa piṟavum aṟintu muṭittukkoḷka. (74)

TE480n

iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
makara vaḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.

iḵtu iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavu mutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum uriya māveṉpatu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar --- iraṇṭeṉṉumeṇ mutalāka oṉpateṉṉumeṇ īṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar, vaḻaṅku iyal mā eṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ --- vaḻakkiṉ kaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavumutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uriya māveṉṉum^ col varumoḻiyāy variṉ, makara aḷavoṭu nikaralum urittu --- avveṇṇuppeyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭiyeṉṉum aḷavuppeyarōṭu ottu vikārappaṭṭu muṭitalum urittu; ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalum urittu eṉṟavāṟu.

vaḻakkiyal vaḻaṅkiyaleṉ vikāram. makara aḷavu makara mutaṉmoḻiyākiya aḷavuppeyareṉa+ paṇputtoki. aḵtu `aḷantaṟi kiḷaviyum' ((eḻu//419)) eṉpataṉuḷ oru maṇṭi eṉa muṭittatām.

utāraṇam: irumā mummā nāṉmā aimmā aṟumā eṇmā oṉpatiṉmā eṉa muṉṉarkkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ vikārappaṭutti muṭikka. iṉi ummiyāṉ vikārappaṭuttātu iraṇṭumā mūṉṟumā nāṉkumā aintumā āṟumā eṭṭumā oṉpatumā eṉa muṭikka. puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ `aḷavuniṟiyum' ((eḻu//289)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattāṉ neṭumutal kuṟuki ukaram vantu puṇarumāṟu kūṟiṉār. ataṉāṉ īṇṭu ēḻumā eṉa muṭikka. ēḻmāveṉa muṭital vaḻakkiṉṟu.

iraṇṭumutal oṉpāṉeṉṟu eṭuttōtiṉamiyiṉ, oṉṟaṟku orumāveṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā veṉṉum muṭipu illiyāyiṟṟu.

vaḻaṅkiyaṉmā eṉṟār vilaṅkumāvi nīkkutaṟku. (75)

TE481n

laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
ṉummum^ keḻuvu muḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭi+ tōṉṟi+
ceyyuḷ+ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum
vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.

itu puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintuniṉṟa ceyyuṇmuṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: laṉa eṉa varūum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉ --- lakāra ṉakārameṉṟu kūṟa varukiṉṟa puḷḷiyīṟṟu+ coṟkaḷiṉ muṉṉar ummum^ keḻuvum uḷappaṭa --- ummeṉṉum^ cāriyiyum^ keḻuveṉṉum^ cāriyiyum uṭpaṭa, piṟavum aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭittōṉṟi --- piṟacāriyiyum appeṟṟippaṭṭa marapiṉiyuṭiya moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuḷ toṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa niliyum --- ceyyuyaṭcoṟkaḷi+ toṭarpu paṭutti+ kūṟumiṭattu+ poruḷpaṭa niṟkum, vēṟṟumi kuṟitta poruḷvayiṉāṉa --- vēṟṟumiyi+ kuṟitta poruḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `vāṉavari villum^ tiṅkaḷum pōlum' itaṟku ummeṉṉum^ cāriyiyiṉ makaratti `ammiṉiṟuti' ((eḻu//129)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattuḷ `taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ+ piṟacāriyiyum^ tiriyumeṉa nakara oṟṟākki nilimoḻi lakara oṟṟiṉ mēl uyirēṟṟi muṭikka. villuntiṅkaḷum pōlumeṉpataṟku villiṭittiṅkaḷ pōlumeṉa ēḻaṉurupu virittu+ poruḷurikka. `kalkeḻukāṉavar nalkuṟumakaḷē' ((kuṟun//71)) itaṟku+ kalli+ keḻīiyiṉa eṉa urikka. `mānitikkiḻavaṉumpōṉm' ((akam//66)) itaṟku+ kiḻavaṉi+ pōṉmeṉa urikka. ivvummi ciṟappaṉṟu. `kāṉkeḻunāṭu' ((akam//98)) itaṟku+ kāṉikkeḻīiya eṉa urikka.

iṉi aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyiṭi eṉṟataṉāṉ+ keḻuveṉṟatu piṟacolliṭattē `paṇikeḻu peruntiṟal+ palvēṉ maṉṉar' `tuṟikeḻumānti' ((naṟṟiṇi//35)) eṉa iyalpāka varuvaṉavum `vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar' ((akam//17)) `payaṅkeḻumāmaḻi' ((puṟam//266)) eṉa nilimoḻi yīṟṟeḻuttu+ tiriya varuvaṉavum^ koḷka.

iṉṉum itaṉāṉē iccāriyiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekara nīṭciyum^ koḷka. `pūkkēḻ toṭali nuṭaṅka veḻuntu' ((akam//28)) `tuṟikē ḻūraṉ koṭumi nāṇi' ((aiṅkuṟu//11)) ivaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭāvatum ēṟpuḻi mūṉṟāvatum virikka. `ceṅkēḻ meṉkoṭi' ((akam//80)) eṉpuḻi+ keḻuveṉṉum uriccol `eḻuttu+ pirinticittal' ((col//365)) eṉpataṉāṉ nīṇṭateṉṟu uṇarka. meyyeṉṟataṉāṉ+ pūkkēteṉpuḻi valloṟṟu mikutal koḷka.

iṉṉum^ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ+ piṟacāriyi peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟkellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittukkoḷka. (76)

TE482n

uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki+
kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉu miciyiṉum^ tōṉṟi
neṟippaṭa vārā+ kuṟiccol+ kiḷaviyu
muyartiṇi yaḵṟiṇi yāyiru maruṅki
ṉimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum^
ceyyum^ ceyta veṉṉum^ kiḷaviyiṉ
mey=oruṅku* iyalum^ toḻiṟoku moḻiyum^
tammiyal kiḷappiṉ+ ṟammuṉ+ ṟāmvarūu
meṇṇiṉ ṟokuti yuḷappaṭa+ piṟavu
maṉṉavi yellā maruviṉ pāttiya
puṇariya ṉiliyiṭi yuṇara+ tōṉṟā.

iḵtu ivvatikārattāṉ+ puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ iviyeṉa avaṟṟi eṭuttu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki --- kūṟuṅkāl uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāka niṟpatōr collāki, kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum iciyiṉuntōṉṟi --- kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum iciyiṉāṉum piṟantu, neṟippaṭa vārā+ kuṟiccol+ kiḷaviyum --- oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmi kuṟinta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi āyiru maruṅkiṉ --- uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyeṉṉum avviraṇṭiṭattum uḷavākiya, aimpālaṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyum --- oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu pala eṉṉum aintupāliṉiyum aṟitaṟku+ kāraṇamākiya paṇpukoḷpeyar tokum^ tokiccollum, ceyyum^ ceyta eṉṉum^ kiḷaviyiṉ --- ceyyum^ ceyta eṉṉum peyarecca+ coṟkaḷiṉuṭiya, mey oruṅku iyalum^ toḻil tokumoḻiyum --- kālaṅkāṭṭum ummum akaramum orucoṟkaṇṇē cēra naṭakkum puṭipeyarcci tokku niṟkum^ coṟkaḷum, tamiyal kiḷappiṉ --- tamatu taṉmi kūṟumiṭattu, tammuṉ tām varūum eṇṇiṉṟokuti uḷappaṭa --- niṟuttacollum^ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumāy vārātu tammuṉṉar+ tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇuppeyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa, aṉṉa piṟavum ellām --- attaṉmiyākiya piṟavumellām, maruviṉ pāttiya --- ulakattu maruvi naṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyi+ tam ilakkaṇamākavuṭiya, puṇariyal nili iṭi yuṇara+ tōṉṟā --- oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmi iṭam viḷaṅka+ tōṉṟā eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: kaṇ viṇṇaviṇittatu viṇviṇittatu ivi kuṟippuriccol; āṭi veḷḷa viḷarttatu veḷ viḷarttatu ivi paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla volittatu ollolittatu ivi iciyuriccol. `ollolinīr pāyvatē pōlum^ tuṟivaṉ' eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum. ivi uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakkalākāmiyiṉ neṟippaṭavārā eṉṟār. viṇṇaviṇittatu teṟippu+ tōṉṟa+ teṟittateṉṟum viṇviṇittatu teṟippu+ teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṉ+ ṟaṉmi kuṟinta collāyiṟṟu. `viṉiyēkuṟippē' ((col//260)) eṉṉum^ cūttirattiṉ+ kūṟiya eṉa eṉpataṉi ivaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭiccollātaliṉ viṇṇeṉa viṇittatu eṉa+ puṇarkkappaṭumāṟu uṇarka.

iṉi+ karumpārppāṉ karumpārppaṉi karumpārppār karuṅkutiri karuṅkutirikaḷ eṉa varum ivaṟṟuḷ+ kariyaṉākiya pārppāṉ kariyaḷākiya pārppaṉi kariyarākiya pārppār kariyatākiya kutiri kariyaṉavākiya kutirikaḷ eṉa aimpāliṉiyum uṇarttum paṇpukoḷpeyar tokkavāṟu kāṇka. ivaṟṟuḷ+ karumi eṉṉum paṇpuppeyar tokkatēl+ karumiyākiya pārppāṉeṉa virittal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam viriyāmiyiṉ+ paṇpukoḷ peyar tokkateṉṟu uṇarka.

veṟṟili veṟṟuppili veṟṟaṭi veṟṟeṉattoṭuttal eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavaṟṟuḷ veṟuvitākiya iliyeṉpatu pākkum^ kōṭṭu nūṟum^ kūṭātatāya paṇpuṇartti īṟu tokutaliṉ maruviṉ pāttiyavāy niṉṟu oṟṟaṭuttatu . veṟuvitākiya uppiliyeṉṟatu ciṟitum uppiliyeṉa niṉṟatu. ēṉiyavum aṉṉa. iṅṅaṉam aimpālum^ tokuttaṟku uriya mutaṉiliyātaliṉ+ puṇarttalākāmi kūṟiṉār.

iṉi āṭaraṅku ceykuṉṟu puṇarpoḻutu arivāḷ kolyāṉi celcelavu eṉa nilam mutalākiya peyareccam^ tokka viṉittokikaḷi virikkuṅkāl, āṭiṉavaraṅku eṉa+ ceyta eṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta akara īṟu iṟappu uṇarttiyum, āṭāniṉṟavaraṅku āṭumaraṅku eṉa+ ceyyumeṉṉum peyareccattu īṟu virinta ummīṟu nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttiyum, avaṟṟāṉāya puṭipeyarcciyi+ tōṟṟuvittu iraṇṭu peyareccamum orucoṟkaṇ oruṅku tokku niṟṟaliṉ ataṉi oru peyareccattiṉkaṇ aṭakki+ puṇarkkalākāmiyiṉ+ puṇarkkalākāteṉṟār. ummiṟuti nikaḻvum etirvum uṇarttumāṟu `viṉiyiṉṟokuti' ((col//415)) eṉṉum eccaviyal+ cūttirattuḷ+ kūṟutum. ivvum īṟu iraṇṭu kālamum oruṅkuṇarttutaṉ+ ciṟappunōkki+ ceyta eṉpataṉi āciriyar muṟkūṟārāyiṉar.

iṉi+ pattu eṉa niṟutti+ patteṉa+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭātu pappatteṉavum paḵpatteṉavum vaḻaṅkumāṟu uṇarka. orōvoṉṟeṉpatum atu. atutāṉē ōroṉṟōroṉṟāka+ koṭu eṉṟāl+ puṇarkkappaṭum.

iṉi aṉṉapiṟavum eṉṟataṉāṉē uṇṭāṉeṉpuḻi uṇṇeṉṉum mutaṉiliyum^ kālaṅkāṭṭum ṭakaramum iṭaṉum pālum uṇarttum āṉum oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarkkappaṭā, avi niṟutta collum^ kuṟittuvarukiḷaviyum aṉmiyiṉ, kariyaṉeṉpuḻi+ karuveṉa niṟutti aṉṉeṉa+ tantu puṇarkkappaṭā, atu iṉṉaṉeṉṉum poruṭarutaliṉ. ēṉiviṉiccoṟkaḷum ivvāṟē pirittu+ puṇarkkalākāmi uṇarka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē koḷḷeṉakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpuḻi+ koḷḷeṉpataṉi eṉa eṉpataṉōṭu puṇarkkappaṭāmiyum ūraṉ veṟpaṉ mutaliya viṉippeyayarkaḷum piṟavum puṇarkkappaṭāmiyum^ koḷka. ivvāciriyar puṇarkkappaṭāta iccoṟkaḷiyum vaṭanūṟkaṇmuṭitta aṉakaṉ aṉapāyaṉ akaḷaṅkaṉ mutaliya vaṭacoṟkaḷiyum piṉṉuḷḷōr muṭittal mutaṉūlōṭu māṟukoḷa+ kūṟalāmeṉṟu uṇarka. (77)

TE483n

kiḷanta valla ceyyuḷuḷ+ ṭirinavum
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
viḷampiya viyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟiṉ
vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉa roḻukka
ṉaṉmati nāṭṭattu eṉmaṉār pulavar.

iḵtu ivvatikārattu eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum muṭiyātu niṉṟavaṟṟi yellām itaṉāṉē muṭikka eṉa atikāra+ puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.

itaṉ poruḷ: kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum --- muṉṉar eṭuttōtappaṭṭaṉa allāta coṟkaḷ ceyyuḷiṭattu+ tirintu muṭivaṉavum, vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum --- nālvaki vaḻakkum naṭakkumiṭattu maruvutalōṭu tirintu muṭivaṉavum, viḷampiya iyaṟkiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟiṉ --- muṉṉar+ kūṟiya ilakkaṇa muṟimiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟumāyiṉ avaṟṟi, naṉmati nāṭṭattu --- nalla aṟiviṉatu ārāycciyālē, vaḻaṅkiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ --- vaḻakku muṭintu naṭakkumiṭattē, uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal eṉmaṉār pulavar --- muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṟintu naṭāttuka eṉṟa kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.

utāraṇam: `taṭavuttiri' eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum^ `taṭavunili' ((puṟam//140)) eṉa ukaram peṟṟum akara īṟṟu uriccol vantatu. atavattaṅkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumikkaṇ akara īṟu attu+ peṟṟatu. kacatapa+ tōṉṟiṉ eṉa akara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar+ takaraṅkoṭukka.

`naṟavam^ kaṇṇi naṟpōr+ cempiyaṉ
kurava ṉīṭiya koṉṟiyam^ kāṉal'

eṉa ākāra iṟuti kuṟiyataṉiṟuti+ ciṉikeṭṭuḻi iruvaḻiyum ammuppeṟṟaṉa.

`muḷavumā tolicciya pinniṇa+ piḷavi+
piṇavunāy muṭukkiya taṭiyoṭu virii' ((malipaṭu//176))

eṉa avvīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammuppeṟāta muṭivupeṟṟaṉa. `tiṇvaḷi vicitta muḻavo ṭākuḷi' ((malipaṭu//3)) `cuṟaveṟimīṉ' `iravaḻaṅkuciṟuneṟi' ((akam//318)) ivi ukaram peṟāmal vantaṉa. kaḷḷiyam^ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali' ((akam//67)) eṉa ikarīṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟaṉa. īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ iṉpeṟṟatu. `nalloḻukkam^ kākkum^ tiruvattavar' ((nālaṭi//57)) eṉa ukara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attu+ peṟṟatu, `eppeṟṟamāṉ piṇipōla' ((cintā//2615)) eṉa ēkāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ēkāram peṟātu vantatu. `kittuṇṭām pōḻtē' ((nālaṭi//19)) `kittillar nallar' eṉavum `puṉṉiṅkāṉal' ((akam//80)) `mulliyantoṭiyal' eṉavum aikāra īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. `aṇṇaṉkōyil vaṇṇamē' ((cintā//nāmakaḷ//126)) eṉa ōkāra īṟu yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟatu.

iṉi, `añceviniṟiyavāliṉa' ((mullip//89)) eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ+ kakaramum akaramum^ keṭṭaṉa. `maravam pāvi vayiṟāra+ parukiya' `māvanākam vaṇaṅki māṟcuṇam' eṉa iruvaḻiyum makaram vikārappaṭṭu ammu+ peṟṟaṉa. `kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka' `poṉṉam^ tikiri muṉcamat turuṭṭi' `poṉṉam^ kuvaṭṭiṟ poli veyti' eṉa ṉakara īṟu iruvaḻiyum ammu+ peṟṟaṉa. `vērpiṇi vetirattu+ kālporunālici' eṉa rakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ attuppeṟṟatu.`nāvalantaṇpoḻil' `kāṉalamperuntuṟi' eṉa lakara īṟu vēṟṟumikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟaṉa. `neytalam^ ciṟupaṟi' iḵtu alvaḻikkaṇ ammuppeṟṟatu. `āyiṭi yirupērāṇmi ceytapūcal' ((kuṟun//43)) eṉpuḻi āyiṭiyeṉpatu urupātaliṉ `nīṭavarutal' ((eḻu//208)) eṉpataṉāṉ muṭiyātu nīṇṭu vakara oṟṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. temmuṉi eṉa+ tevveṉpuḻi vakaraṅkeṭṭu makara oṟṟu+ peṟṟu muṭintatu. a eṉṉum^ cuṭṭu `aṉṟiyaṉittum' eṉa+ tirintatu. `mutirkōṅkiṉ muki' ((kuṟiñcikkali//20)) eṉavum^ `kāymāṇṭa teṅkiṉ paḻam' ((cintā//31)) eṉavum^ kuṟṟukara īṟu iṉpeṟutalum^ koḷka. `toṇṭutaliyiṭṭa pattukkuṟi' eṉa ṇakaram iraṭṭātu takara oṟṟu ṭakara oṟṟāy+ kuṟṟiyalukaram ēṟi muṭintatu.

iṅṅaṉam^ ceyyuḷuḷ+ piṟavum^ tirivaṉa uḷavēṉum ippuṟaṉaṭiyāṉ muṭikka. arumaruntāṉeṉpatu rakaravukaraṅkeṭṭu arumantāṉeṉa muṭintatu. cōḻaṉāṭu cōṇāṭu eṉa aṉkeṭṭu muṭintatu. pāṇṭināṭum atu. toṇṭimānāṭu toṇṭināṭeṉa īṟṟeḻuttu+ cilakeṭṭu muṭintatu, maliyamāṉāṭu malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻuttoḻintaṉa palavum^ keṭṭu muṭintatu. potuvileṉpatu potiyileṉa ukaram^ tirintu ikaramāy yakara uṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu. piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa ellām ippuṟaṉaṭiyāṉ amittukkoḷka. (78)

((kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((eḻuttatikāram muṟṟiṟṟu))

((TOC))

nūliṉ marapu moḻimarapu nuṇpiṟappu
mēli+ puṇarcci tokimarapu --- pālā
murupiyaliṉ piṉṉuyir puḷḷi mayakkam^
teḷivariya kuṟṟukaram^ ceppu.

eḻuttati kārattu+ cūttiraṅka ḷellā
moḻukkiya oṉpatu ōttu uḷḷum --- vaḻukkiṉṟi
nāṉūṟ ṟirunāṟpāṉ mūṉṟeṉṟu nāvalarkaṇ
mēṉūṟṟu vittār virittu.
